Uniform title: kaulāvalīnirṇaya
Author : jñānānandaparamahaṃsa
Editor : Arthur Avalon

########################################################################### 



|| śrīḥ ||

śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracitaḥ

kaulāvalīnirṇayaḥ

prathama ullāsaḥ 


maṅgalācaraṇam


natvā śrīgurupādapadmayugalaṃ siddhaiḥ sadā sevitaṃ 
śāktānāṃ paribhāvamokṣajanakaṃ kaulāvalīnirṇayam |
vakṣye'haṃ kulavardhanaṃ sukṛtibhiḥ sevyaṃ sadā kaulikai-
rbhāvaireva manoharaiśca caraṇābjaikaprasādālayam || 1 ||

vāñchākalpataruṃ tanoti śivayorvākyānusārārthato |
jñānānandagirirmaheśacaraṇābjaikaprasādālayaḥ || 2 ||


yasyāḥ prasādato lebhe śāstrārthaṃ tattvanirṇayam | 
tāṃ namāmi mahādevīṃ bāṇīṃ vāgvibhavapradām || 3 ||


saṃgrahārthe tantragranthanirūpaṇam


saṃvīkṣya yāmalaṃ rudrabrahmaviṣṇukasaṃjñakam | 
yāmalaṃ śaktisaṃjñañca bhāvacūḍāmaṇiṃ tathā || 4 ||

tantracūḍāmaṇiñcaiva kulacūḍāmaṇiṃ tathā |
kulasāraṃ kuloḍḍīśaṃ kulāmṛtakulārṇavam || 5 ||

p. 2)

kālikākulasarvasvaṃ kulasadbhāvameva ca |
kālītantrakulānandaṃ kulacakraṃ tathaiva ca || 6 ||

kālikalpaṃ mahākaulaṃ kumārītantrameva ca |
samayākhya kālītantraṃ phetkārīṃ pheravīṃ tathā || 7 ||

śrīkramaṃ yoginītantraṃ śrīhaṃsaparameśvaram |
svatantraṃ tantrarājañca jñānamālāṃ tataḥ param || 8 ||

vāyavīṃ haṃsatantrañca tārākalpañca mālinīm |
mantranirṇayatantrañca yoginīhṛdayaṃ tathā || 9 ||

kulārṇavañca gāndharvamuḍḍīyānañca toḍalam |
śivaśāsanatantrañca mandaraṃ plāvanīṃ tathā || 10 ||

vīrābaliñca vārāhīṃ vīratantrañca kubjikām |
nīlatantraṃ matsyasūktaṃ lalitātantrameva ca || 11 ||

p. 3)

śambhunirṇayatantrañca vāmakeśvaratantrakam |
vaṭukasaṃhitāñcaiva tārārṇavaṃ tataḥ param || 12 ||

guptārṇavaṃ caṇḍaroṣaṃ māyāṃ nīlamaṇiṃ tathā |
samayākhyaṃ tantrasāraṃ tato mantravimarśinīm || 13 ||

jñānasāraṃ yogavatīṃ bhairavītantrameva ca |
bhairavākhyaṃ mahātantraṃ siddhasārasvataṃ tataḥ || 14 ||

tripurārṇavatantrañca uttaraṃ tantrameva ca | 
chinnātantraṃ vātulañca dakṣiṇāmūrttisaṃhitām || 15 ||

nayottaraṃ hyekavīrāṃ dṛṣṭvā vividhasaṃgrahān | 
guruṇāñca mataṃ jñātvā sārātsārataraṃ mahat || 16 ||


tantraśāstragopanīyatve yuktiḥ


dvaitajñānavihīnānāṃ mano'bhīṣṭapradāyakam |
atiyatnena goptavyaṃ mātṛmaithunavat sadā || 17 ||

yasmai kasmai na dātavyaṃ na duṣṭāya kathañcana | 
p. 4) na dadyāt paraśiṣyāya vañcakāya tathaiva ca || 18 ||

sarvathā gopayedenaṃ yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ |


yogyaśiṣyāyadeyam


prakṛtācārayuktāya gurusevāparāya ca || 19 ||

kulīnāya mahocchāya devībhaktaparāya ca |
śiṣyāya bhaktiyuktāya sādhakāya pradāpayet || 20 ||

vinā parīkṣya yo dadyāt yasmai kasmai kathañcana |
sa bhakṣyo yoginīvṛndairmṛte ca narakaṃ vrajet || 21 ||

tasmācca sādhakaśreṣṭho gopayet sarvathā punaḥ |
pustakaṃ vīkṣya yo mūḍho na labdho guruvaktrataḥ || 22 ||

kuryāllobhavaśenaiva so'pi naśyati niścitam |
tasya karmāṇi sarvāṇi narakāya bhavanti hi || 23 ||

yoginīnāṃ bhaved bhakṣyo nārakī brahmaghātakaḥ |
tasmājjñātvā gurorvaktrāt sādhane yatnamācaret || 24 ||

p. 5) 

śivaḥ brahmasvarūpī

asti devaḥ parabrahmasvarūpī niṣkalaḥ śivaḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā ca sarveśo nirmalodayaḥ || 25 ||

svayaṃjyotiranādyanto nirvikāraḥ parātparaḥ |
nirguṇaḥ saccidānandastadaṃśā jīvasaṃjñakāḥ || 26 ||

anādyavidyopahitā yathāgnau visphuliṅgakāḥ |
sarvepyupādhisaṃbhinnāste karmabhiranādibhiḥ || 27 ||


mānavakartavyakathanam


caturvidhaśarīrāṇi dhṛtvā dhṛtvā sahasraśaḥ | 
sukṛtairmānavo bhūtvā jñānī cenmokṣamāpnuyāt || 28 ||

caturaśītilakṣeṣu śarīreṣu śarīriṇām |
na mānuṣyaṃ vinā'nyatra tattvajñānantu labhyate || 29 ||

tatra janmasahasreṣu janmaikamapi bhāgyataḥ |
kadācillabhate janturmānuṣyaṃ puṇyasañcayāt || 30 ||

sopānabhūtaṃ mokṣasya mānuṣyaṃ prāpya durlabham |
yastārayati nātmānaṃ tasmāt pāpataro'tra kaḥ || 31 ||

tataścāpyuttamaṃ janma labdhvā cendriyasauṣṭhavam |
na vettātmahitaṃ yastu sa bhavedātmaghātakaḥ || 32 ||

vinā dehena kasyāpi puruṣārtho na vidyate |
tasmād dehadhanaṃ rakṣan puṇyakarmāṇi sādhayet || 33 ||


jñānaprāptyarthaṃ karmakartavyam


tāvat karmāṇi kurvīta yāvajjñānaṃ na jāyate |
jñānino'jñānino vāpi yāvad dehasya rakṣaṇam || 34 ||

tāvadvarṇasamācāraḥ karttavyaḥ karmamuktaye |
karmaṇonmīlite jñāne jñānena śivatāṃ vrajet || 35 ||

tattvajñānanirūpaṇam

kuryādanityakarmāṇi nityakarmāṇi vā caret |
sarvakarmāṇi santyaktuṃ na śakyaṃ dehadhāriṇā || 36 ||


p. 7) 

svakāryeṣu pravartante karaṇānīti cintayan |
ahambhāvamapāsyaivaṃ yaḥ kuryāt sa na lipyate || 37 ||

tyajet karmaphalaṃ yo vā sa tyāgītyabhidhīyate | 
kriyamāṇāni karmāṇi jñānaprāpteranantaram || 38 ||

na ca spṛśanti tattvajñaṃ jalaṃ padmadalaṃ yathā |
tattvaniṣṭhasya karmāṇi puṇyāpuṇyāṇi saṃkṣayam || 39 ||

prayānti naiva narakaṃ tattvaniṣṭhāḥ kadācana | 
utpannasahajānandatattvajñānarataḥ sadā || 40 ||

sarvasaṅkalpasantyaktaḥ sa vidvān karma santyajet |
vṛthaiva yaiḥ parityaktaṃ karmakāṇḍamapaṇḍitaiḥ || 41 ||


pāṣaṇḍāḥ paṇḍitammanyāste yānti narakaṃ kila | 


prāptajñānasya karmakāṇḍe anadhikāritva kathanam


phalaṃ prāpya yathā vṛkṣaḥ puṣpaṃ tyajati nispṛhaḥ || 42 ||

tattvaṃ prāpya tathā yogī tyajet karmaparigraham |

p. 8)

aśvamedhaśatenāpi brahmahatyāśatena ca || 43 ||

puṇyapāpairna lipyante yeṣāṃ brahma hṛdi sthitam |
pṛthivyāṃ yāni karmāṇi jihvopasthanimittataḥ || 44 ||

jihvopasthaparityāgī karmaṇā kiṃ kariṣyati |

jñānasya dvaividhyam

āgamotthaṃ vivekotthaṃ dvidhā jñānaṃ pracakṣate || 45 ||

śabdabrahmāgamamayaṃ paraṃ brahma vivekajam |
advaitaṃ kecidicchanti dvaitamicchanti cāpare || 46 ||

paraṃ tattvaṃ na jānanti dvaitādvaitavivarjitam |
dve pade bandhamokṣāya mameti nirmameti ca || 47 ||

mameti badhyate janturvimuktirnirmameti ca |

vidyā'vidyālakṣaṇam

vidyā'vidyādvayoryogāt pakṣadvayamudīritam || 48 ||

tat karma yacca bandhāya sā'vidyā parikīrttitā |
yanna bandhāya tat karma sā vidyā parikīrttitā || 49 ||

p. 3)

yāvat saṅkalpakarmāsti tāvadeva hi bandhanam |
yāvattadeva naivāsti tāvadeva hi mokṣaṇam || 50 ||

japahomārcanaṃ tīrthaṃ vedaśāstrāgamādikam |
tāvadeva hi sarvañca yāvattattvaṃ na vindati || 51 ||

ataḥ prayatnato dhīraḥ sarvāvasthāsu sarvadā |
tattvaniṣṭho bhaveccaiva yadīcchenmokṣamātmanaḥ || 52 ||

tatkāraṇamahaṃ vakṣye kṣipraṃ yena prajāyate |
yathāvidhi gurorvaktrādvidyāṃ saṃgṛhya sādhanam || 53 ||

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ sāpekṣaṃ pūrvapūrvataḥ |
anyathā na bhajeditthaṃ karotyāpatparamparā || 54 ||

iti jñātvā mahāprājñaḥ krameṇa samupācaret |
prātarutthāya śirasi sahasradalapaṅkaje || 55 ||

p. 10)

taruṇādityakiñjalkabidhormaṇḍalasaṃyute |
dhyāyennijaguruṃ dhīro rajatācalasannibham || 56 ||

gurudhyānakathanam

vīrāsanasamāsīnaṃ śuklābharaṇabhūṣitam |
śuklamālyāmbaradharaṃ varadābhayapāṇikam || 57 ||

vāmoruśaktisahitaṃ kāruṇyenāvalokitam |
priyayā savyahastena dhṛtacārukalevaram || 58 ||

vāmenotpaladhāriṇyā raktābharaṇabhūṣayā |
jñānānandasamāyuktaṃ smarettannāmapūrvakam || 59 ||

gurupūjānirūpaṇam

mānasairupacāraistu saṃpūjya kalpayet sudhīḥ | 
gandhaṃ bhūmyātmakaṃ dadyād bhāvapuṣpaṃ tataḥ param || 60 ||

dhūpaṃ vāyvātmakaṃ deyaṃ tejasā dīpameva ca |
naivedyamamṛtaṃ dadyāt pānīyaṃ varuṇātmakam || 61 ||

tattanmudrāvidhānena sampūjyā'tha guruṃ yajet |
darśayitvā yonimudrāṃ gurumantraṃ japettataḥ || 62 ||

p. 11)

gurumantravidhānam

hsauḥ padaṃ samālikhya sahakṣamalavarānilān |
vahniyuktāṃstridhā likhya ādyadīrghatrayānvitān || 63 ||

bindunādakalāyuktān śrīparānte ca pāvakam |
athārādhyapadaṃ caiva sarvamūrdhvapadaṃ tataḥ || 64 ||

nātha sarvaguruprānte guruḥ svayaṃ tato guruḥ |
śrīgurunāthaśabdānte sahakṣamalavarānilān || 65 ||

vahnidīrghapratiyutān nādabindukalānvitān |
piṇḍīkṛtaṃ likhedādyaṃ caturthañca tato likhet || 66 ||

tṛtīyaṃ pūrvavallekhyaṃ dvitīyañca tathaiva ca |
tataḥ śrīśambhugurvante punarādyaṃ samālikhet || 67 ||

ādyakūṭaṃ caturthañca punarādyaṃ tṛtīyakam |
catvāriṃśanmārgavarṇairmaṇḍito'yaṃ gurormanuḥ || 68 ||

yathāśaktijapaṃ kuryādathavā vāgbhavaṃ japet |
dakṣakarṇayuto gauro vahnirudreṇa bhūṣitaḥ || 69 ||

varṇadvayātmako mantraścaturvargaphalapradaḥ |
gakāraḥ siddhidaḥ prokto rephaḥ pāpasya hārakaḥ || 70 ||

p. 12)

ukāro viṣṇuravyaktastritayātmā guruḥ paraḥ |
gurorvāmakare tattu samarpyātha stutiṃ paṭhet || 71 ||

gurustutim

ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjanaśalākayā |
cakṣurunmīlitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ || 72 ||

akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ vyāptaṃ yena carācaram |
tatpadaṃ darśitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ || 73 ||

viśvātmakaḥ paraḥ śambhurviśvottīrṇo'smyahaṃ dṛḍham |
iti yasyājñayā tasmāttasya śrīpādukāsmṛtiḥ || 74 ||

sahasrāre padme vigataśaṭhaśāstrāntaragataṃ 
sadā devairvandyaṃ bhavabhayavināśaikakaraṇam |
varābhī pāṇibhyāṃ prakaṭitasamandasmitamukhaṃ 
namāmīdṛgrūpaṃ paramagururūpaṃ sakaruṇam || 75 ||

p. 13)

śāntaṃ śuklakalevaraṃ trinayanaṃ kāntāṅgabhūṣaṃ śivaṃ vāñchābhītikaraṃ pracaṇḍatimirājñānaprakāśaṃ ravim |
nityaṃ naumi raviṃ tathābdhitaraṇe taṃ karṇadhāraṃ guruṃ 
yasya śrīpadapaṅkajodbhavasudhā siddhaiḥ sadā pīyate || 76 ||

sadā vidhormaṇḍalamadhyasaṃsthaṃ namāmi nityaṃ gurupādapadmam |
prasādato yasya mayā pralabdhaṃ savāsanājñānavināśabījam || 77 ||

padmodbhave samakalāpatayo'pi nityaṃ 
yasyāṅghripadmamamalaṃ paribhāvayanti |
kāruṇyavāridhiraśeṣaguṇaikarāśiḥ 
so'yaṃ guruḥ śirasi śuklasarojamadhye || 78 ||

pravaddhānāṃ pāśaiḥ sakalaguṇamāyāmayarasai-
rnimagnānāṃ nityaṃ bhavajalanidherantaragataḥ |
kṛpāleśo bandhaṃ pariharati yasyāṅghritaragaḥ 
pravande sānandaṃ tamapi gururūpaṃ sakaruṇam || 79 ||


kavitvaṃ pāṇḍityaṃ tribhuvanapatitvaṃ na hi kadā 
na vā svargasiddhiṃ na suravarasāmyañca niyatam |
na vāñchāmo mokṣaṃ puraharapadaṃ naiva śivatāṃ 
yadi syācceto me niravadhi guroḥpādakamalam || 80 ||

stavenānena saṃstutyaṃ gurorājñāṃ labhettataḥ |
dhyāyet kuṇḍalinī śaktiṃ mūlādhāranivāsinīm || 81 ||


kuṇḍalinī nirūpaṇam


nijeṣṭadevatārupāṃ viṣatantutanīyasīm |
suptāhisādṛśākārāṃ sārdhatrivalayānvitām || 82 ||

koṭisaudāminībhāsāṃ svayambhuliṅgaveṣṭinīm |
samutthāpya mahādevīṃ prāṇamantreṇa sādhakaḥ || 83 ||

bhedayitvā ca ṣaṭcakraṃ sahasrārasthiteśvare |
sthāpayitvā ca tasyordhvaṃ dhyāyet kulagurūn kramāt ||  84 ||

prahlādānandanāthañca sanakānandanāthakam |
kumārānandanāthañca vaśiṣṭhānandanāthakam || 85 ||

krodhānandasukhānandau jñānānandaṃ tataḥ param |
bodhānandamathābhyarcya krameṇānena sādhakaḥ || 86 ||

parāmṛtarasollāsahṛdayān ghūrṇalocanān |
parāliṅganasambhinnacūrṇitāśeṣatāmasān || 87 ||

kulaśiṣyaiḥ parivṛtān sukhāntaḥkaraṇodyatān |
varābhayakarāśeṣakulatattvārthavedinaḥ || 88 ||

saśaktiṃ sakulañcaiva kathitañca sukhāvaham |
evaṃ kulagurūn natvā'mṛtavṛṣṭiṃ vidhāya ca || 89 ||

tarpayitvā ca tāṃ devīṃ punarmūle samānayet |
tasyāḥ prabhāsamūhaiśca vyāptaṃ dehaṃ vibhāvayet || 90 ||

ahaṃ devī na cānyo'smi brahmaivāhaṃ na śokabhāk |
saccidānandarūpo'hamityātmānaṃ vibhāvayet || 91 ||

bahirgatvā yadā paśyet kulavṛkṣaṃ susādhakaḥ |
namaskuryāt prayatnena kulaṃ kulapatiṃ tathā || 92 ||

kulavṛkṣanāmāni

aśokaḥ keśaraḥ karṇikāraścūtastilastathā |
nameruśca piyālaśca sindhuvārakadambakau || 93 ||

maruvakaścampakaśākhī kulavṛkṣāśca dvādaśa |
athavā kulavṛkṣāṃśca kathayāmi susiddhaye || 94 ||

śleṣmātakakarañjāni nimbāśvatthakadambakāḥ |
vilvo vaṭoḍumbaraśca ciñceti daśamī smṛtā || 95 ||

tiṣṭhanti kulayoginyaḥ kulavṛkṣeṣu sarvadā | 
na svapet kulavṛkṣādho na copadravamācaret || 96 ||

dṛṣṭvā bhaktyā namaskuryāt chedayenna kadācana | 
kāmabījaṃ kāmadevaṃ sarvānte janamālikhet || 97 ||

priyāya hṛdayānto'yaṃ manurdantaviśuddhaye |
dantādikaṃ viśudhyātha svakalpoktaṃ samācaret || 98 ||

p. 17) 

prātaḥkṛtyamakṛtvā tu yo devīṃ bhaktito'rcayet |
tasya pūjā tu viphalā śaucahīnā yathā kriyā || 99 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye prathamollāsaḥ || 1 ||



dvitīya ullāsaḥ 


ātmaśuddhinirūpaṇam


atha snānaṃ pravakṣyāmi śāktānāṃ siddhihetave |

snānavidhiḥ

yathāvidhi jalaṃ prāpya ātmānaṃ devatālayam || 1 ||

vicintya praṇameddhīro varuṇaṃ vāridaivatam |
unmajya tata utthāya kuladarbhaṃ vidhārayet || 2 ||

pavitrīdhāraṇam

aśūnyau ca karau kuryyāt suvarṇa rajataiḥ kuśaiḥ |
tatra śāktaiḥ svayaṃ grāhyaṃ na ca vanyāḥ kuśāḥ kuśāḥ || 3 ||

anāmayorbhavet svarṇaṃ tarjanyo rajataṃ tathā |
eṣa eva bhaveddarbho na darbho vanasambhavaḥ || 4 ||

vinaiva dūrvayā devyāḥ pūjā nāsti kadācana |
tasmāddūrvā grahītavyā sarvapuṣpamayī yataḥ || 5 ||

p. 19)

haste dūrvāṅkuraṃ kṛtvā sarvakarma samācaret |
ācāmedātmatattvādyaiḥ praṇavādyaiḥ svadhāntikaiḥ || 6 ||

mantraistridhā tathā vaktraṃ nāsākṣiśotranābhihṛn | 
mastakāni spṛśen mantrī kuryācchrotrākṣibandhanam || 7 ||

bhūtvā pūrvamukho mantrī uttarābhimukho'pi vā |
mūlānte cātmatattvāya vidyātattvāya tatparam || 8 ||

śivatattvāya ca proktvā krameṇa sādhakottamaḥ |
svāhāntamadbhirācāmet pūrvottaramukhaḥ sudhīḥ || 9 ||

kulapātraṃ sadūrvañca satilaṃ sajalaṃ tataḥ |
ebhiḥ saṅkalpya vīrendraḥ samantraṃ vāriṇi nyaset || 10 ||

sūryasya maṇḍalāttirthamāvāhyā'ṅkuśamudrayā |
sañjapya daśadhā mūlaṃ tattoyaṃ mūrdhni saṃkṣipet || 11 ||

kumbhākhyamudrayā mūlaṃ smaran sādhakapuṅgavaḥ |
aṅgulībhirmukhaṃ śrotraṃ nāsāṃ netre ca rodhayan || 12 ||

p. 20)

mūlaṃ smaran tridhonmajya gātrāṇi parimārjayet |
tilakaṃ mūlamantreṇa śikhāñca parikalpayet || 13 ||

trirācamya tato mantrī ṣaḍaṅgāni nyasettataḥ |
gṛhītvā vāmahaste tu jalaṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā || 14 ||

samācchādya tato vyoma vāyuvahnistataḥ param |
kṣauṇīvaruṇabījañca trivāramabhimantrayet || 15 ||

mūlena saptavārantu mudrayā tattvasaṃjñayā |
svamūrdhni ca kṣipettoyaṃ śeṣaṃ dakṣe nidhāya ca || 16 ||

dhyātvā tejomayaṃ toyaṃ savyanāsāpuṭena ca |
tata uttolya dehasthaṃ pāpaṃ prakṣālya vāmayā || 17 ||

protsārya kalpite vajraśilāyāṃ saṃkṣipet sudhīḥ |
prakṣālya hastayugalamācamettadanantaram || 18 ||

māyāprāṇātmakaṃ mantraṃ mārtaṇḍabhairavāya ca |
prakāśaśaktiyuktāya arghyaṃ svāhā tato vadet || 19 ||

sūryāyā'rghyaṃ vidhāyātha śaktisandhyā prakīrtitā |
vibhāvya purato yantraṃ tatra devīṃ gaṇānvitām || 20 ||

p. 21) 

gṛhītvā gandhatoyañca kulapātrāttataḥ param |
tarpayed devatāvaktre vidhivat sādhakottamaḥ || 21 ||

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya tadante devatābhidhām |
dvitīyāntāmahaṃ paścāt tarpayāmi namaḥ padam || 22 ||

mūlānte tarpayāmīti svāhāntaṃ tarpaṇaṃ bhavet |
guruṃ saparivārañca āyudhaṃ vāhanaṃ tathā || 23 ||

śaktitastarpayitvā ca gāyatrīṃ śaktito japet |
dhyātvā sāvaraṇāṃ devīṃ sūryamaṇḍalavāsinīm || 24 ||

mūlānte udyadādityavartinyaistadanantaram |
śivacaitanya tasyānte mayyai svāheti saṃsmaran || 25 ||

arghyaṃ dattvā tatastasyai tāmānīyāṃkuśena ca |
mudrayā hṛdi saṃsthāpya jalādutthāya sādhakaḥ || 26 ||


vastradhāraṇam


mūlena kulavastre dve paridhāya kulena ca |
mokṣārthī raktavastreṇa bhogārthī śvetavāsasā || 27 ||

māraṇe kṛṣṇavāsastu vaśye raktaṃ sadā gṛhī |
uccāṭane vyāghracarmaṃ vṛkṣatvak śubhakarmaṇi || 28 ||

p. 22)

paridhāya tato maunī hṛdi mantraṃ parāmṛśan |
prakṣālya hastapādau ca pūjāsthānaṃ samāviśet || 29 ||

snānaṃ vinā pūjā viphalā

iti snānaṃ samuddiṣṭamadhikārapradāyakam |
snānatarpaṇaśūnyasya nādhikāraśca pūjane || 30 ||

vividhairupacāraiśca bhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ |
vinā snānena yā pūjā viphalā siddhihānidā || 31 ||

tasmāt snānādikaṃ kṛtvā raktavastraṃ tataḥ param |


pūjāsthānanirūpaṇam


ekaliṅge śmaśāne ca śūnyāgāre catuṣpathe || 32 ||

vilvamūle prāntare vā nadyāṃ parvatamastake |
ujjaṭe nirjane vāpi bhūgṛhe devatālaye || 33 ||

aśvatthasannidhau vāpi vaṭamūle triśūlake |
haṭṭāgāre nijāgāre paśudṛṣṭivivarjite || 34 ||

nṛpālamaṇḍape vāpi gahvare vāpi yantrite |
niśchidramaṇḍape vāpi kartavyaṃ pūjanaṃ sadā || 35 ||

p. 23)

ekaliṅganiruktiḥ

pūjāsthānaṃ mahādevyāḥ kathitaṃ pīṭhamuttamam |
pañcakośāntare yatra na liṅgāntaramīkṣate || 36 ||

tadekaliṅgamityāhustatra siddhiranuttamā |


catuṣpathaniruktiḥ


catuṣpathaṃ vijānīyāt yatrāste caṇḍikāśilā || 37 ||

tatra yatnena gantavyaṃ japtavyaṃ siddhikāṅkṣibhiḥ |
dvāradeśe tato gatvā pūjayeddāradevatāḥ || 38 ||

kārayettatra vādyādinṛtyagītaṃ tathaiva ca |
siddhārthākṣatadūrvābhistilamiśraiḥ susādhakaḥ || 39 ||

apasarpantu te bhūtā ye bhūtā bhuvi saṃsthitāḥ |
ye bhūtā vighnakartāraste naśyantu śivājñayā || 40 ||

vāgbhavādyaṃ samuccārya nārācamudrayā sudhīḥ |
kulabhūtaṃ samutsārya yāgabhūmiṃ tato viśet || 41 ||

p. 24)

kūrmacakraṃ prakurvīta yathātantravidhānataḥ |
rekhācatuṣṭayaṃ kāryaṃ indravāruṇayogataḥ || 42 ||

uttarād yāmyaparyantaṃ navakoṣṭhaṃ yathā bhavet |
indrāgniyāmyanair-ṛtyapratīcyāṃ pañcavargakam || 43 ||

vāyukuberaśambhau ca yādivarṇāṃścatuścatuḥ |
lakṣau kramāt samālikhya sādhakaḥ siddhihetave || 44 ||

madhye koṣṭhe tataḥ kuryāt pūrvavannavakoṣṭhakam |
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ ṣoḍaśasvaramālikhet || 45 ||

akārādikrameṇaiva yugmayugmaprabhedataḥ |
kṣetrādyarṇastu yatrāste śīrṣaṃ tasya viniścitam || 46 ||

dīpasthānaṃ tadeva syāttatra siddhiranuttamā |
nirdhano jāyate patsu pārśvayorvyādhireva ca || 47 ||

pucche hāniḥ sadā jñeyā dīpasthānaṃ sadāśrayet |
kūrmacakraṃ vinā yo hi japahomaṃ samācaret || 48 ||

tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ vindyāt abhicārāya kalpate |


pañcaśuddhikathanam


pañcaśuddhivihīnena yatkṛtaṃ na ca tat kṛtam || 49 ||

yāvanna kurute tattu tāvad devārcanaṃ kutaḥ |
pañcaśuddhiṃ vinā pūjā abhicārāya kalpate || 50 ||

bhūmiśuddhirātmaśuddhirdravyānāṃ śodhanaṃ tathā |
mantraśuddhirdevaśuddhiḥ pañcaśuddhiritīritā || 51 ||

pañcaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi yathākramavidhānataḥ |
sāmānyārghyaṃ tataḥ kuryāt prokṣaṇārthaṃ ca sādhakaḥ || 52 ||

caturasraṃ tato vṛttaṃ trikoṇaṃ vāmato likhet |
ādhāraṃ sthāpayettatra saṃpūjya vahnimaṇḍalam || 53 ||

astreṇa prokṣitaṃ pātraṃ sthāpayitvā ca pūjayet |
sūryamaṇḍalamuccārya hṛnmantreṇa prapūjayet || 54 ||

vilikhya maṇḍalaṃ toye pūrvavat pūjayettataḥ |
indumaṇḍalamabhyarcya prakṣiped gandhapuṣpake || 55 ||

p. 26)

pūrvavattīrthamāvāhya ṣaḍaṅgāni yajettataḥ |
agnīśāsuravāyavyamadhye dikṣvaṅgapūjanam || 56 ||

mūlamantraṃ japedaṣṭau tajjalena prasecayet | 
astrāntamūlamuccārya ātmānaṃ yāgabhūmiṣu || 57 ||

pṛthivyarghyaṃ tato dattvā nair-ṛtyāṃ sādhakottamaḥ |
praṇavaṃ bhagavatyeva dharaṇīṃ ṅeyutāṃ vadet || 58 ||

dharaṇīvedhase svāhā anenārghyaṃ vidhāya ca |
brahāṇaṃ pūjayitvā ca puruṣaṃ vāstusaṃjñakam || 59 ||

sammārjanānulepādyairdarpaṇodaravatprabham |
vitānaṃ dhūpadīpādyaiḥ puṣpamālā'bhiśobhitam || 60 ||

pañcavarṇarajaścitraṃ bhūmeḥ śuddhiritīritā |
vilikhya maṇḍalaṃ bhūmau pūrvavat sādhakāgraṇīḥ || 61 ||

āsanaṃ sthāpayettatra yathā tantrānusārataḥ |
athāsanaṃ pravakṣyāmi śāktānāṃ sthitikāmyayā || 62 ||


mṛdvādivividhāsanakathanam


mṛducūḍakamāsīnaḥ anyeṣu komaleṣu vā |
viṣṭare vā samāsīnaḥ sādhayet siddhimuttamām || 63 ||

arvāk ṣaṇmāsato garbhacyutamāhurmṛduṃ budhāḥ |
cūḍopanayanairhīnaṃ mṛdumacūḍakaṃ viduḥ || 64 ||

nivṛttacūḍako bālo hīnopanayanaḥ pumān |
yo mṛtaḥ pañcame varṣe tameva komalaṃ viduḥ || 65 ||


vividhāsanaphalakathanam


mṛdvāsanaṃ vinā yo hi pūjayeccaṇḍikāṃ śivām |
tāvat kālaṃ nārakī syād yāvadāhūtasaṃplavam || 66 ||

pañcāśadbhiḥ kuśairbrahma tadardhena tu viṣṭaraḥ |
kauśeyaṃ bhogadaṃ raktakambalaṃ siddhidaṃ smṛtam || 67 ||

vastraṃ rogaharaṃ jñeyaṃ mokṣaṃ śrīrvyāghracarmaṇi | 
ūrṇā duḥkhaharī proktā jñānasiddhirmṛgājine || 68 ||

p. 28)

sannyāsī brahmacārī tu viśeṣaḥ kṛṣṇacarmaṇi |
kāṣṭhāsanaṃ na kurvīta yatra daurbhāgyavān bhavet || 69 ||

atyuccaṃ cātinīcañca magnañca parivarjayet |
manoharaṃ mṛdu ślakṣṇamāsanaṃ parikīrtitam || 70 ||


āsanaśodhanam


eṣāmanyatamaṃ prāpya prokṣayettadanantaram |
sāmānyārghyasya toyena puṭāñjalividhānataḥ || 71 ||

merupṛṣṭha ṛṣiḥ proktaḥ sutalaṃ chanda īritam |
kūrmo'dhidevatā cātra āsanasthāpanāya ca || 72 ||

viniyogaḥ parijñeyo'pyabhilapya paṭhettataḥ |
pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokā devipadaṃ tato vadet || 73 ||

tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtā tvañca dhāraya māṃ tataḥ param |
nityaṃ pavitraṃ kuru ca āsanañca samuccaret || 74 ||

anena praṇavādyena tatra kuśatrayaṃ kṣipet |
ādhāraśaktiṃ saṃprocya ṅeyutaṃ kamalāsanam || 75 ||

hṛdantaṃ manumuccārya māyādyena prapūjayet |
āsane saṃviśet paścāt vīrāsanena sādhakaḥ || 76 ||


p. 29)


dakṣagulphaṃ hi vāmorau tadadho vāmagulphakam |
kuryādṛjuvapurgrīvaḥ śarīraṃ ṛjuvaccaret || 77 ||

samagrīvaṃ vidhāyātha proktaṃ vīrāsanaṃ param |
kāmākhyādiṅmukhaḥ susthaḥ prāṅmukho vā prapūjayet || 78 ||

sāmānyārghyasya toyena prokṣayedyāgabhūmiṣu |
iti te kathito hyatra āsanavidhiḥ pūjane || 79 ||

snāto nicchidragehe kulavimalajalairarghyapātraṃ vidhāya bhaktiśraddhānvito'sau nijakulakamale yantrarājaṃ vilikhya | 
sindūrairbhūṣitābhiḥ sukulayuvatibhiḥ pūjayitvā japedya- 
stasya śrīrnetrapadme vilasati satataṃ vaktra candre ca vāṇī || 80 ||

dīkṣitābhiḥ kulīnābhiryuvatībhiḥ kulātmabhiḥ |
devatāgurubhaktābhiḥ sañcitaṃ yāgabhūmiṣu || 81 ||

nānāvidhāni puṣpāṇi gandhāni vividhāni ca |
karpūrāgurudhūpādivāsitaṃ paṭavāsitam || 82 ||


p. 30)


tāmbūlaṃ peyadravyañca dhūpadīpādikañca yat |
sarvālaṅkārabhūṣābhirbhūṣitaḥ sādhakottamaḥ || 83 ||

mūlavidyājaptatoyaiḥ prokṣayet yāgavastu ca |
sarvaṃ svadakṣiṇe sthāpya vāme kumbhaṃ niveśayet || 84 ||

paścime devatāyāśca kula dravyāṇi dhārayet |
(athavā) 
naivedyaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme purato vā na pṛṣṭhataḥ || 85 ||

athavā vāmato dadyānnatu pṛṣṭhe kadācana |
dīpaṃ dakṣiṇato dadyāt purato vā na vāmataḥ || 86 ||

vāmahastatale dhūpamagrato vā na dakṣiṇe |
nivedayet purobhāge gandhapuṣpādibhūṣaṇam || 87 ||

malinaṃ bhūmisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ kṛmikeśādidūṣitam |
aṅgasaṃspṛṣṭamāghrātaṃ tyajet puṣpaṃ sadā budhaḥ || 88 ||

naiva puṣpaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā na dadyāt kalikāmapi |
raktamarghyaṃ śvetadūrvāṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ kuruṇṭakam || 89 ||

na dadyācca mahādevyai yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ |
sugandhiśvetalauhityakusumairarcayet kuśaiḥ || 90 ||


p. 31)


vilvairmaruvakādyaiśca tulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
patrairna cārcayed devīṃ vilvapatravivarjitaiḥ || 91 ||

nirgandhairnārcayet puṣpaiḥ sugandhaiḥ sarvavastubhiḥ |
javābandhūkamandāraiḥ sindūraiśca mahotpalaiḥ || 92 ||

saṃpūjayecca nirgandhaistathā ca raktacandanaiḥ |
nandyāvarttaṃ tilapatraṃ tathā keśarapatrakam || 93 ||

madanaṃ maruvakañcaiva dāḍimaṃ kundapuṣpakam |
nānopahārabalibhirnānāpuṣpaimanoharaiḥ || 94 ||

apāmārgadalairbhṛṅgaistulasīvarjitaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
kulapuṣpeṇa dhūpena kuladīpena tāṃ sudhīḥ || 95 ||

caṇḍikāṃ pūjayeddhīmānanyathā siddhirodhakṛt | 
na dadyānmālatīpuṣpaṃ tārākālīprapūjakaḥ || 96 ||

unmattañca na dātavyaṃ sādhakena mahātmanā |
na paryuṣitadoṣo'sti jalajotpalacampake || 97 ||

agastyavakule caiva vilvagaṅgājale tathā | 
nijadevasya puṣpāṇi pṛthakpātre nidhāpayet || 98 ||

ekībhāvaṃ na karttavyaṃ yadīcchecchubhamātmanaḥ |
naivedyādīni sarvāṇi tathā jānīhi niścitam || 99 ||

pūjākāle tu saṃprāpte vāsudevaṃ smarettu yaḥ |
pūjāphalaṃ na cāpnoti narakaṃ pratipadyate || 100 ||

vaiṣṇavaṃ kusumaṃ spṛṣṭvā pūjākāle ca sarvadā |
hastaprakṣālanācchaucamācāmastu tadācaret || 100 ||


śaktiṃ vinā pūjā viphalā


śuciḥ sāvahito bhūtvā bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
saṃsthāpya vāmabhāge tu śaktiṃ svāmiparāyaṇām || 102 ||

raktavastraparīdhānāṃ dīkṣitāṃ cāruhāsinīm |
vinā śaktyā tu yā pūjā viphalā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 103 ||

tasmācchaktiyuto vīro bhavecca yatnapūrvvakam | 
yā śaktiḥ sā mahādevī hararūpastu sādhakaḥ || 104 ||

anyonyacintanāccaiva devatvamupajāyate |
prāṇaṃ binā yathā dehī spandituṃ naiva śakyate || 105 ||

śaktiṃ vināpi pūjāyāṃ nādhikārī bhavettadā |


hetuyuktena pūjā kāryā


vinā dravyādhivāsena na japenna smaret sadā || 106 ||

vinā hetukamāsvādya kṣobhayukto bhavennaraḥ |
na pūjāṃ na japaṃ kuryānna dhyānaṃ na ca cintanam || 107 ||

tasmādbhuktvā ca pītvā ca pūjayet parameśvarīm |
satyametad vijānīyādvākyaṃ śaṅkarabhāṣitam || 108 ||

na kramacyutidoṣo'sti nācāradoṣadūṣaṇam | 
na bhuktvā ca na pītvā ca na smarenna japettadā || 109 ||

ye smaranti narā mūḍhāsteṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ pade pade |
ānandena vinā yastu caṇḍikāṃ paripūjayet || 110 ||

rogī duḥkhī bhavet so'pi mṛte ca narakaṃ vrajet |
tasmādādau prakarttavyaṃ samvidāsavayojanam || 111 ||

saṃvidaḥ śreṣṭhatvam

samvidāsavayormadhye samvideva garīyasī |
vijayāgrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā dhyānaṃ yaḥ kurute naraḥ || 112 ||

tadā dhyānamayī mūrtiḥ pratyakṣā tasya jāyate |
samvitprayogasteneha pūjādau sādhakottamaiḥ || 113 ||

karttavyā ca mahāpūjā karuṇāmayananditaiḥ |
tasyā bhedaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathārthaṃ tantravartmanā || 114 ||


tasyā cāturvidhyam


śvetapuṣpā ca brahmāṇī raktā tu kṣatriyā smṛtā |
vaiśyā pītaprasūnā ca śūdrā kṛṣṇaprasūnikā || 115 ||


tāsāṃ śodhanamantrāḥ


āsāṃ śodhanamantrañca kathayāmi pṛthak pṛthak |
samvide brahmasambhūte brahmaputri sadānaghe || 116 ||

bhairavāṇāñca tṛptyarthaṃ prasannā bhava sarvadā |
oṃ brahmāṇyai namaḥ svāhā brahmāṇīṃ śodhayediti || 117 ||


p. 35)


siddhimūlakriye devi hīnabādhaprabādhini |
rājaprajāvaśaṅkari śatru kaṇṭhatriśūlini || 118 ||

oṃ aiṁ kṣatriyāyai namaḥ svāhā kṣatriyāyāḥ smṛto manuḥ |
ajñānendhanadīptāgne jñānāgne jñānarūpiṇi || 119 ||

ānandasyāhutiṃ prītiṃ samyagjñānaṃ prayaccha me |
hrīṁ vaiśyāyai namaḥ svāhā śūdrāṃ saṃśodhayedatha || 120 ||

oṃ namasyāmi namasyāmi yogamārgapradarśini |
trailokyavijaye mātaḥ samādhiphaladā bhava || 121 ||

klīṁ (śrīṁ) śūdrāyai namaḥ svāhā kathitaṃ mantramuttamam |
oṃ amṛte amṛtodbhave amṛtavarṣiṇi padantataḥ || 122 ||

amṛtamākarṣayadvandvaṃ siddhiṃ dehi tataḥ param |
trailokyaṃ me tato brūyādvaśamānaya tatparam || 123 ||

dviṭhānto'yaṃ manuḥ proktaścatuṣkāṇāñca śodhane |
(athavā) 
hasakṣamalavarayūṁ hasarauñca tataḥ param || 124 ||

ānandabhairavāyeti vaṣaḍantaḥ paro manuḥ |
anayorekatareṇaiva viśodhya vijayāṃ tataḥ || 125 ||


vijayāgrahaṇavidhiḥ


saptadhā mūlamantrañca tāsāmupari saṃjapet |
āvāhanyādimudrāñca dhenuyoniñca darśayet || 126 ||

tritālachoṭikābhiśca digbandhanaṃ samācaret |

p. 36)

divyadṛṣṭyā padāghātairvighnāṃśca vinivārayet || 127 ||

saptadhā tarpayen mūrdhni guruvarga susādhakaḥ |
tattvamudrāvidhānena hṛdi devīṃ gaṇānvitām || 128 ||

tarpayitvā mahādevīṃ śaktibhyo'pi dadettataḥ |
gṛhītvā vāmahastena paṭhedamuṃ manuṃ tataḥ || 129 ||

aiṁ vadavadapadaṃ proktvā vāgvādini tataḥ param |
mama jihvāgre sthireti bhava sarvapadaṃ likhet || 130 ||

sattvavaśaṅkari svāhā mantreṇa tadanantaram | 
svīkuryāt sādhakaśreṣṭho mumukṣuḥ prasamīkṣayā || 131 ||


vijayāsevanasyānukalpakathanam


mārgaśīrṣādibhirmāsaiḥ kramāt saṃsevyatāṃ tribhiḥ |
dugdhaghṛtamadhusurāvarṇānāmanupānakam || 132 ||

ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ pañcakālakam |
kramāt saṃsevanīyā ca siddhidā siddhimūlikā || 133 ||


vijayāsevanasyamantraguptiḥ


tathā tathā prakartavyaṃ samvidāsavabhojanam |
yathā yathā na hi bhavet prākaṭyaṃ paśumelake || 134 ||

nirjane caiva kartavyaṃ sadaiva gurusannidhau |
na pituḥ sannidhāne vā na mātuḥ sannidhau tathā || 135 ||

kimvā pakṣipataṅgānāṃ darśane naiva kārayet |
kulapuṣpaṃ kuladravyaṃ kulapūjāṃ kulaṃ japam || 136 ||

guruṃ kulapatiñcaiva kulamālāṃ kulākulam |
kulacakraṃ kuladhyānaṃ sarvathā na prakāśayet || 137 ||

prakāśātsiddhihāniḥ syāt prakāśādbandhanādikam |
prakāśānmantranāśaḥ syāt prakāśāddevahiṃsanam || 138 ||

prakāśānmṛtyulābhaḥ syānna prakāśyaṃ kadācana |
pūjākāle tu saṃprāpte yadi ko'pyatra gacchati || 139 ||

darśayedvaiṣṇavīṃ mudrāṃ viṣṇunyāsaṃ tathā stavam |
prakāśādyadi guptiḥ syāttatprakāśaṃ na dūṣaṇam || 140 ||


p. 38)


gopanādyadi vyaktiḥ syānna ca vyaktiḥ kadācana |
varaṃ pūjā na kartavyā na ca vyaktiḥ kadācana || 141 ||

pūjāvidhānam

raktamālyāmbaradharo yoṣidyukto mahāśayaḥ |
tāmbūlapūritamukho dhūpāmodasugandhitaḥ || 142 ||

devo bhūtvā yajeddevaṃ nādevo devamarcayet |
astreṇa gandhapuṣpābhyāṃ surabhīkṛtahastakaḥ || 143 ||

mārjayet sādhakaśreṣṭho mūlamantraṃ parasparam |
nirmañchanaṃ tridhā kṛtvā astreṇa vāmapāṇinā || 144 ||

kṣipettaddūrataḥ puṣpaṃ mantrī nārācamudrayā |
aṅguṣṭhāgre tu tarjanyā saṃyojyādhordhvarekhayā || 145 ||

anyāṃgulīstathordhvañcanārācaḥ syāt prasāryate |
dhyātvā guruṃ nyasedvāme dakṣiṇe gaṇapatiṃ tathā || 146 ||

nijadevīṃ tataḥ paścānmadhyadeśe ca sādhakaḥ |
pārṣṇighātakarāsphoṭa samudañcitavakrakaiḥ || 147 ||

p. 39) 

tatra tālatrayaṃ dadyāt saśabdaṃ sampradāyataḥ |

prāṇāyāmavidhānam

prāṇāyāmairvinā yasya japahomārcanādikāḥ || 148 ||

na bhavantyeva saphalā yatnenāpi kṛtāḥ kriyāḥ |
japasya purataḥ kāryaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ samāhitaiḥ || 149 ||

anyathā niṣphalāḥ sarvā japahomārcanādikāḥ |
ṛtucandrairvedarasairnetrarāmairyathākramam || 150 ||

mātrābhiḥ praṇavaṃ japtvā pūrakumbhakarecakaiḥ |
vāmā ca madhyamā caiva tathā dakṣiṇanāḍibhiḥ || 151 ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikāṅguṣṭhairyannāsāpuṭadhāraṇam |
prāṇāyāmaḥ sa vijñeyastarjanīmadhyame vinā || 152 ||

(yadvā) 

prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryānmūlena praṇavena vā |
uccārayaṃstu praṇavaṃ vāmenaikena mantravit || 153 ||

udaraṃ pūrayitvā tu vāyunā yāvadāsthitiḥ |
prāṇāyāmo bhavatyevaṃ pūrako dehapūrakaḥ || 154 ||

pidhāya sarvagātrāṇi niśvāsocchvāsavarjitaḥ |
sampūrṇakumbhavattiṣṭhet prāṇāyāma sa kumbhakaḥ || 155 ||

muñcedvāyuṃ tathaikena śvāsenaikena mantravit | 
niśvāsayogayuktastu vāyuṃ vāhyena recayet || 156 ||

recakastveva saṃproktaḥ prāṇāyāmo na saṃśayaḥ |
jānupradakṣiṇīkṛtya na drutaṃ na vilambitam || 157 ||

kriyate cāṅgulisphoṭaṃ sā mātrā parikīrtitā |
tato dvādaśamātrābhiḥ pūrakaḥ kriyate budhaiḥ || 158 ||

tasmād dviguṇamātrābhiḥ kriyate recakaḥ śuciḥ |
tasmād dviguṇamātrābhiḥ kriyate kumbhakastathā || 159 ||


bhūtaśuddhikathanam


prāṇāyāmatrayeṇaivamekaikaṃ tritayātmakam |
liṅgaṃ kāryaṃ hṛdisthaṃ tu caitanyaṃ sarvasākṣiṇam || 160 ||

praṇavena viniḥsārya brahmarandhrādbahirbudhaḥ |
tenaiva saṃpuṭīkṛtya mastakopari vinyaset || 161 ||

caitanyarahitaṃ dehaṃ bāhyañcābhyantaraṃ tathā |
nābhimaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ vāyumaṇḍalamadhyagam || 162 ||

yaṃ bījaṃ dhūṣaraṃ taptaṃ dhyātvā taṃ taṃ viśodhayet |
agnimaṇḍalasaṃsthena raṃbījenāgnirūpiṇā || 163 ||

nirdahedatha tadbhasmarāśidehākṛtirbhavet |
bhrūmadhyasthaṃ hṛdisthaṃ vā dhyāyedvaruṇamaṇḍalam || 164 ||

ardhacandrākṛtiṃ śvetaṃ padmadvayasadakṣiṇam |
tatrasthena vakāreṇa plāvayenmadhurūpiṇā || 165 ||

jīvaṃ pārthivaṃ nyasya cintayeddhemamaṇḍalam |
praṇavena dvidhākṛtya tatra caitanyarūpiṇā || 166 ||

mastakopari vinyasya caitanyena niyojayet |
amṛtaplāvitaḥ piṇḍaḥ sa martyo dhautakalmaṣaḥ || 167 ||

ātmānaṃ cintayet paścāddevatārupiṇaṃ tataḥ | 
prāṇāyāmañca kathitaṃ bhūtaśuddhiṃ tathāparam || 168 ||

athavānyaprakāreṇa bhūtaśuddhiśca kathyate |
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ tataścaret || 169 ||

p. 42)

bhūtaśuddhiṃ vinā pūjā viphalā


bhūtaśuddhiṃ vinā karma japahomārcanādikam |
tāvattadviphalaṃ sarvaṃ prakāśenāpyanuṣṭhitam || 170 ||

bhūtaśuddhiṃ vinā yastu nyāsapūjāṃ karoti hi |
nyāsajālaṃ vṛthā tasya pūjā sā viphalā bhavet || 171 ||

tasmāttu sādhakaśreṣṭho bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret |
mūlādhārāttataḥ prāṇaṃ brahmamārgeṇa tāntrikaḥ || 172 ||

haṃsena puṣkarasthāne paramātmani yojayet |
saṃhārakramayogena pañcatattvaṃ samuddharet || 173 ||

śoṣadāhaplavān kṛtvā vāyvagnisalilākṣaraiḥ |
anena vidhinā mantrī bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 174 ||


svadehe devatājīvanyāsaḥ


ātmānaṃ devatārūpaṃ vibhāvya sādhakottamaḥ |
svadehe devatājīvaṃ vinyasettadanantaram || 175 ||

puṣpairvānāmayā vāpi manasā vā nyasedbudhaḥ |
jīvanyāse manuḥ prokto jīvanyāseṣu yojayet || 176 ||

jīvanyasamantraḥ

mukhavṛttaṃ samuccārya haṃsastu viparītakaḥ |
kramāt prāṇā iha prāṇāstathā jīva iha sthitaḥ || 177 ||

amuṣyāḥ sarvendriyāṇi bhūyo'muṣyāḥ padantataḥ |
vāṅmanonayanaśrotraghrāṇaprāṇapadānyatha || 178 ||

paścādihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu ṭhadvayam |
athavā pāśabījañca lajjā cāṅkuśameva ca || 179 ||

prāṇamantraṃ tataḥ paścāccheṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret |
jīvanyāsamanuḥ prokto jīvanyāseṣu yojayet || 180 ||

tataḥ kuryāt svakalpoktaṃ nyāsādikaṃ susādhakaḥ |
ṣaḍaṅgānāñca mudrāñca sthānañca mantramucyate || 181 ||

tridvyekadaśarātridvisaṃkhyāṅguligatakramāt |
hṛdi maste śikhāyāñca kavace locanāstrake || 182 ||

namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ huñca vauṣaṭ phaṭ yojayet kramāt |
ṛṣiśchandaśca saṃyuktaṃ devatābījaśaktikam || 183 ||

kīlitaṃ ṅeyutaṃ sarvaṃ vinyasedeṣu sādhakaḥ |
śirovadanahṛdguhyapādasarvāṅgake nyaset || 184 ||

aṅgaṣaṭkaṃ tataḥ kuryāt pūrvoktenaiva vartmanā |
pañcāśadakṣaranyāse yadviśeṣastaducyate || 185 ||

lalāṭe'nāmikāmadhye vinyasenmukhavartmake |
tarjanīmadhyamānāmā vṛddhānāmā ca netrayoḥ || 186 ||

aṅguṣṭhaṃ karṇayornyasya kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakau nasoḥ |
madhyāstisro gaṇḍayośca madhyamāmoṣṭhayornyaset || 187 ||

anāmāṃ dantayornyasya madhyamāmuttamāṅgake |
mukhe'nāmāṃ madhyamāñca haste pāde ca pārśvayoḥ || 188 ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikāmadhyāstāstu pṛṣṭhe ca vinyaset |
tāḥ sāṅguṣṭhā nābhideśe sarvāḥ kukṣau ca vinyaset || 189 ||

hṛdaye ca talaṃ pūrvaṃ aṃśayośca kakutsthale |
hatpūrvaṃ hastapat kukṣimukheṣu talameva ca || 190 ||

etāstu mātṛkāmudrāḥ krameṇa parikīrttitāḥ |
ajñātvā vinyasedyastu nyāsaḥ syāttasya niṣphalaḥ || 191 ||

pañcāśadakṣaranyāsaḥ krameṇaiva prakāśitaḥ |
omādyantyo namo'nto vā sabindurbinduvarjitaḥ || 192 ||

māyālakṣmībījapūrvā nyastavyā ucyate budhaiḥ |
athavā kevalaṃ sargabinduyuktāṃ kalānvitām || 193 ||

tārttīyarudraśaktyantāṃ smarādikeśarānvitām |
śaktipūrvāṃ śriyā yuktāṃ sudhāsyāṃ vā tanau nyaset || 194 ||

śaktiśrīkāmabījāntāṃ vedādiśaktimātṛkām |
ajapā paramātmā ca dviṭhaprapañcayāgakaḥ || 195 ||

ravisaṃkhyātmikāṃ proktā mātṛkāntasvarūpiṇīm |
yasya yatra rucirgacchettadrūpāttattanau ca tām || 196 ||

vinyasya tanmayo mantrī yāti vāgīśvarīmayam |
spṛṣṭvā vānāmayā vāpi manasā vā nyasedbudhaḥ || 197 ||

ṛṣyādivyāpakāntañca svakalpoktaṃ samāpayet |
prāṇāyāmādibhirnyāsaiḥ ātmaśuddhiśca jāyate || 198 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye dvitīyollāsaḥ || 2 ||


tṛtīya ullasāāḥ


antaryāganirūpaṇam

ātmaśuddhiṃ samākhyātā antaryāgaśca kathyate |
antaryāgavidhiṃ kṛtvā bahiryāgaṃ samācaret || 1 ||

bahiryāge nādhikārī antaryāgavivarjitaḥ |
bahiryāgaphalaṃ nāsti vināntaryajanaṃ kadā || 2 ||

tasmāt prayatnato vīraścāntaryāgaṃ samārabhet | 


mānasapūjākathanam


śivaśaktisamāyogāt sudhāvṛṣṭiṃ vidhāya ca || 3 ||

tarpayitvā mahādevīṃ padme padme susādhakaḥ | 
mūlādhāre samānīya so'haṃ sañcintayettataḥ || 4 ||

ityantaryajanaṃ proktaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
yadvāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye sādhakasya susiddhaye || 5 ||

svakīyahṛdaye dhyāyet sudhāsāgaramuttamam |
ratnadvīpaṃ tu tanmadhye suvarṇabālukāmayam || 6 ||

parito bhāvayenmantrī pārijātaṃ manoharam |
tatra kalpadrumaṃ dhyāyet pañcāśadakṣarātmakam || 7 ||

tanmūle bhāvayenmantrī nānāratnopaśobhitam |
udyadādityasaṅkāśaṃ vyāptaṃ brahmāṇḍamaṇḍalam || 8 ||

śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ jyotirmandiramuttamam |
caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ hemaprākāraśobhitam || 9 ||

bahucāmaraghaṇṭādivitānairupaśobhitam |
mandavāyusamākrāntaṃ gandhadhūpairalaṅkṛtam || 10 ||

tanmadhye vedikāṃ dhyāyennānāratnopaśobhitam |
suvarṇasūtraracitaṃ cintayecchatramuttamam || 11 ||

vibhāvayenmahāyantraṃ pīyūṣapūrapūritam |
pādyapātratrayaṃ kuryādarghyapātratrayaṃ tathā || 12 ||

ṣaḍācamanapātrāṇi madhuparkatrayaṃ tataḥ |
ekaṃ tu bhogapātraṃ tu sādhakaḥ ṣoḍaśe dale || 13 ||

p. 49) 

sahasrārāmṛtenaiva pātrāṇi pūrayettathā |
pīṭhapūjāṃ tataḥ kuryāt svakalpoktavidhāntaḥ || 14 ||


pīṭhapūjāvidhānam


sarvopari tato dhyāyet paścimānanapaṅkajam |
sravantamamṛtaṃ nityaṃ divyāṅgakamalāntare || 15 ||

śivaśaktisamāyogādamṛtānandanandinīm |
svasvarūpāṃ samāvāhya parivāragaṇaiḥ saha || 16 ||


mānasapūjopacārāḥ


mānasairupacāraiśca dīyate dravyamuttamam |
āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pṛṣṭvā kuśalaṃ tadanantaram || 17 ||

pādyaṃ caraṇayordadyānmaulau cārghyaṃ nivedayet |
sahasradalabhṛṅgārāllambikānālavicyutam || 18 ||

paramāmṛtapānīyaṃ bhāvapuṣpaiḥ samanvitam |
svarṇapātre jalaṃ kṛtvā dadyādācamanaṃ mukhe || 19 ||

madhuparkaṃ mukhe dadyāt punarācamanīyakam |

p. 50)

caturviṃśatitattvena gandhaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 20 ||

svayambhukusumaṃ dadyāt kuṅkumaṃ raktacandanam |
saptāṣṭabhāvapuṣpaiśca saṃpūjya dhūpadīpakam || 21 ||

manaḥkalpitanaivedyaṃ dadyāddevyai manoramam |
tarpayecca tridhā devīṃ yathoktavidhinā tataḥ || 22 ||

ṣaḍaṅgaṃ gurupaṅktiñca pūjayedaṅgadevatāḥ |
pūjayitvā prayatnena snānīyaṃ dāpayedyathā || 23 ||

svavāme maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā ratnasiṃhāsanānvitam |
tatra devīṃ samānīya nānāgandhasamanvitam || 24 ||

udvartanādikaṃ kṛtvā tailaṃ nārāyaṇādikam |
dattvā devyai prayatnena snānīyaṃ dāpayettataḥ || 25 ||

suvarṇakoṭikumbhaiśca gandhatoyasamanvitaiḥ |
nānātīrthajalenaiva snāpayet paradevatām || 26 ||

dukulairmārjitaṃ gātraṃ dukūlaṃ paridhāpayet |
nayane tejasastattvaṃ proñchayettena vai tanum || 27 ||

ākāśatattvaṃ śrotre ca tena vastraṃ prakalpayet |
vidhivadvandanaṃ kṛtvā keśasaṃskāramācaret || 28 ||

p. 51)

nānāratnasamāyuktakaṅkatyā sādhakottamaḥ | 
paṭṭagucchaṃ keśapāśe nānāratnasamākulam || 29 ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ dadyāt sindūraṃ keśamadhyake |
nāgendradantaracitaṃ śaṅkhaṃ dadyānmanoharam || 30 ||

haste keyūrakañcaiva kaṅkaṇaṃ kaṭakantathā |
pādāṅgurīyakaṃ dadyānnānāratnopaśobhitam || 31 ||

pādayornūpūraṃ dadyāt kaṭyāñca kṣudraghaṇṭikām |
śiroratnaṃ pradadyācca mukuṭaṃ ratnanirmitam || 32 ||

tāṭaṅkakuṇḍalaṃ karṇabhūṣaṇaṃ supariṣkṛtam |
nayane kajjalaṃ dadyānnāsāgre gajamauktikam || 33 ||

grīvāpatraṃ kaṇṭhabhūṣāṃ muktāmaṇivibhūṣitam |
ānandahāratritayaṃ aṅgurīyakaratnakam || 34 ||

p. 52)

sarvāṅgalepanaṃ kāryaṃ gandhacandanasihlakaiḥ |
kāñcanāñcitakañcūlīmalaktaṃ radanacchade || 35 ||

suvarṇapādukāṃ dattvā pravāladantacihnitām |
maṇḍapeṣu samānīya ratnanirmitadolayā || 36 ||

punaḥ pādyādikaṃ dattvā pūjayettāṃ sanātanīm |
gandhādikaṃ tato dattvā bhāvapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet || 37 ||


mānasapuṣpanāmāni

amāyamanahaṅkāramarāgamamadaṃ tathā |
amohakamadambhañca adveṣākṣobhakau tathā || 38 ||

amātsaryamalobhañca daśapuṣpaṃ vidurbudhāḥ |
ahiṃsā paramaṃ puṣpaṃ puṣpamindriyanigraham || 39 ||

dayāpuṣpaṃ kṣamāpuṣpaṃ jñānapuṣpañca pañcamam | 
iti pañcadaśairbhāvapuṣpaiḥ saṃpūjayecchivām || 40 ||

karavīraṃ javā droṇaṃ campakaṃ nāgakeśaram |
aparāñca kadambañca pārijātādikaṃ tathā || 41 ||

dadyāddevyai prayatnena mālyaṃ gandhasamanvitam |
sumekhalāṃ padmamālāṃ puṣpaṃ nānāvidhaṃ tathā || 42 ||

p. 53)

saptadvīpasamudbhūtaṃ dadyāddevyai susādhakaḥ |
kuṇḍagolodbhavañcaiva dhūpaṃ dadyāt samīraṇam || 43 ||

sahasrārakarṇikā pātraṃ tailaṃ syācca parāmṛtam |
mūlādhāre varttirūpaṃ cidagniḥ syāt pradīpakam || 44 ||

āmūlādbrahmarandhrāntaṃ suṣumnāhatavāyunā |
anāhatadhvanimayīṃ ghaṇṭāmetāṃ nivedayet || 45 ||

naivedyaṃ ṣaḍrasopetamambaraṃ cāmaraṃ tathā |
sūryañca darpaṇañcandramaṇḍalaṃ chatramuttamam || 46 ||

sudhāmbhodhiṃ māṃsaśailaṃ matsyarāśiṃ phalāni ca |
supakvañcarukañcaiva ghṛtāktaṃ paramānnakam || 47 ||

bhakṣyabhojyaṃ tathā coṣyaṃ lehyaṃ peyañca carvaṇam |
dadyāccheṣārghyaṃtoyena punarācamanīyakam || 48 ||

bāhyacakraṃ tato dadyānmūlādhārotthavāyunā |
rajaḥsattvaguṇāḍhyañca karpūracūrṇāmiśritam || 49 ||

ratnapātre pariṣkṛtya tāmbūlañca nivedayet |
tarpayitvā punardevīṃ pūjayeccāṅgadevatāḥ || 50 ||

vidhāya tarpaṇaṃ teṣāṃ devīṃ saṃpūjayet punaḥ |
vidhivat pūjanaṃ kṛtvā tarpayettadanantaram || 51 ||

antarmātṛkayā japtā samarpya ca stutiṃ paṭhet |
puṣpaśayyāñca saṃskuryāttatra devīṃ sureśvarīm || 52 ||

manonarttakatālaiśca śṛṅgārādirasodbhavaiḥ |
gītairnānāvidhairdevīṃ toṣayet sādhakottamaḥ || 53 ||

tasmāt kāmeśvarī devī prītā kāmeśvaraśca saḥ |
manasyaikyaṃ vibhāvyātha sādhakaḥ siddhibhāg bhavet || 54 ||

ityantaryajanaṃ proktaṃ bhuktimuktipradāyakam |
raktapadmasahasrāṇi manasā yaḥ prayacchati || 55 ||

kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi kalpakoṭiśatāni ca |
sthitvā devīpure śrīmān tato rājā kṣitau bhavet || 56 ||

p. 55)

manasāpi mahādevyai naivedyaṃ dātumicchati |
yo naro bhaktisaṃyuktaḥ sa dīrghāyuḥ sukhībhavet || 57 ||

mansāpi ca yo bhaktyā devyai kuryāt pradakṣiṇam |
devīgṛhe vaset so'pi narakāṇi na paśyati || 58 ||

namanaṃ manasā devyai yo bhaktyā kurute naraḥ |
so'pi lokān vijityāśu śivaloke mahīyate || 59 ||


prakārāntaryajanam


athāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalapradam |
gurudhyānaṃ prakurvīta yathāpūrvaṃ viśāladhīḥ || 60 ||

snāyācca vimale tīrthe puṣkare hṛdayāśrite | 
bindutīrthe'thavā snāyāt punarjanma na vidyate || 61 ||

iḍāsuṣumne śivatīrthake'smin 
jñānāmbupūrṇe vahataḥ śarīre |
brahmāmbubhiḥ snāti tayoḥ sadā yaḥ 
kiṃ tasya gāṅgairapi puṣkarairvā || 62 ||

p. 56)

iti snānaṃ samuddiṣṭamatha sandhyāṃ samācaret |


sandhyānirūpaṇam


śivaśaktyoḥ samāyogo yasminkāle pravartate || 63 ||

sā sandhyā kulaniṣṭhānāṃ samādhisthaiḥ pragīyate |
candrārkānalasaṃghaṭṭādgalitaṃ yat parāmṛtam || 64 ||

tenāmṛtena divyena tarpayet paradevatām |
tarpaṇaṃ kathitaṃ divyamarghyasādhanamucyate || 65 ||

brahmarandhrādadhobhāge yaccāndraṃ pātramuttamam |
kalāsāreṇa saṃpūrya tarpayettena khecarīm || 66 ||


prasaṅgataḥ ṣaṭcakrakathanam


ādhāre liṅganābhau hṛdayasarasije tālumūle lalāṭe 
dve patre ṣoḍaśāre dvidaśadaśadale dvādaśārddhe catuṣke |
vāsānte bālamadhye ḍaphakaṭhasahite kaṇṭhadeśe svarāṃśca 
hakṣau kodaṇḍamadhye nyasatu vimaladhīrnyāsasampattisiddhau || 67 ||


p. 57)


ityantarmātṛkāvarṇān dhyāyet kaṇṭhacchadakramāt |
ṣaḍaṅgamatha kuryāttu yena devīmayo bhavet || 68 ||

hanyamānahṛdartho'yaṃ hṛdayaṃ syāccidātmakam |
kriyate tat paratvena hṛnmantreṇa tataḥ param || 69 ||

sarvāṅgādiguṇottuṅge samvidrūpe parātmani |
kriyate viṣayāhāraḥ śiromantreṇa deśikaiḥ || 70 ||

hṛcchivo rūpaṃ ciddhāma mamatā bhāvanā dṛḍhā | 
kriyate nijadehasya śikhāmantreṇa deśikaiḥ || 71 ||

mantrātmakasya dehasya mantravācyena tejasā |
sarvato mantravarṇena kriyate tasya saṃvṛtiḥ || 72 ||

p. 58)

yaddadāti paraṃ jñānaṃ saṃvidrūpe parātmani |
hṛdayādimayaṃ tejaḥ syādetannetrasaṃjñitam || 73 ||

ādhyātmikādirūpaṃ yat sādhakasya vināśayet |
avidyājātamantraṃ tat paraṃ dhāma samīritam || 74 ||

iti nyāsaṃ vidhāyātha tato dhyāyeccidātmakam |
śaktidvayapuṭāntaḥsthahalakṣatrayasaṃsthitam || 75 ||

jyotintattvamayaṃ dhyāyet kulākulaniyojanāt |
atha śṛṅgāṭamadhyasthaśaktidvayapuṭīkṛtam || 76 ||

sadā samarasaṃ dhyeyaṃ dhyānaṃ tat kulayoginām |
(yadvā) 
kiraṇasthaṃ tadagrasthaṃ candrabhāskaramadhyagam || 77 ||

mahāśūnye layaṃ kṛtvā pūrṇastiṣṭhati yogirāṭ |
nirālambe pade śūnye yatteja upajāyate || 78 ||

tadgarbhamabhyasennityaṃ dhyānametaddhi yoginām |
atha pūjāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathātantrānusārataḥ || 79 ||


bhagavatī pūjāvidhi


arcayan viṣayaiḥ puṣpaistatkṣaṇāttanmayo bhavet |
nyāsastanmayatābuddhiḥ so'haṃbhāvena pūjayet || 80 ||

pūrvoktabhāvapuṣpāni dadyāddevyai susādhakaḥ |
atha vakṣye japaṃ tatra padmasthavarṇamālayā || 81 ||


japavidhānam


mālā pañcāśikā proktā sūtraṃ śaktiśivātmakam |
grathitā kuṇḍalīśaktiḥ kalānte merusaṃsthitiḥ || 82 ||

sabinduṃ varṇamuccāryya mūlamantraṃ smarettataḥ |
anulomavilomābhyāṃ yathāśakti japañcaret || 83 ||

japtvā samarpayet paścāt svakalpoktavidhānataḥ |
stutibhiḥ sakalaṃ tatra samarpaṇaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 84 ||


homavidhikathanam


atha homavidhiṃ vakṣye yena cinmayatāṃ labhet |
ātmānamaparicchinnaṃ jñātavyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ || 85 ||

p. 60)

ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
etadrūpantu cit kuṇḍaṃ caturasraṃ vibhāvayet || 86 ||

ānandamekhalāramyaṃ bindutribalayānvitam |
ardhamātrāyonirūpaṃ brahmānandamayaṃ bhavet || 87 ||

paradevamaye sarvajñānadīpavijṛmbhite |
samvidagnau huneddhavyaṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 88 ||

śabdākhyaṃ mātṛkārūpamakṣavarṇavirājitam |
akṣarāṇi hutānyatra niḥśabdaṃ brahma jāyate || 89 ||

kṛtyākṛtye pāpapuṇye saṅkalpāṃśca vikalpakān |
dharmādharmau sādhakendro havistvena prakalpayet || 90 ||


mānasahomaḥ


cidagnau juhuyānmantrī mūlāntena yathākramāt |
nābhimaṇḍalacaitanyarupāgnau manasā śrucā || 91 ||

jñānapradīpite nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham |
vahnijāyāntamantreṇa dadyācca prathamāhutim || 92 ||

dharmādharmahavirdīpta ātmāgnau manasā śrucā | 
suṣumnāvartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyamaham || 93 ||

vahnijāyāntamantreṇa dvitīyāhutimācaret |
prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanīśrucā || 94 ||

dharmādharmau kalāsnehapūrṇavahnau juhomyaham |
vahnijāyāntamantreṇa tṛtīyāmāhutiñcaret || 95 ||

antarnirantaranibandhanamedhamāne māyāndhakāraparipanthini samvidagnau |
kasmiṃścidadbhutamarīcivikāśi bhūmau 
viśvaṃ juhomi vasudhādiśi vāvasānam || 96 ||

idantu pātrabharitaṃ mahottāpaparāmṛtam |
pūrṇāhutimaye vahnau pūrṇahomaṃ juhomyaham || 97 ||

ityantaryajanaṃ kṛtvā sākṣādbrahmamayo bhavet || 98 ||
mahāntaryajanaṃ vakṣye brahmayajñasvarūpakam || 99 ||


mahāntaryajanam


brahmayajñaṃ mahāyajñaṃ brahmajñānī yajet sadā |
manasā ca mano dṛṣṭvā yathā sindhugatā nadī || 100 ||

tathā sarvaśarīrāṇi mahāśūnye niyojayet |
yajñakarttā mahāyogī sarvamantraṃ japet punaḥ || 101 ||

p. 62)

ātmasthaḥ sarvayajñastu bahiryajñavivarjitaḥ |
karmayajño manoyajñaḥ prāṇayajño hutāśanaḥ || 102 ||

mantrayajñaḥ suṣumnāntaḥ sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet |
brahmārpaṇaṃ brahmahavirbrahmāgnau brahmaṇā hutam || 103 ||

brahmaiva tena gantavyaṃ brahmakarmasamādhinā |
brahmavat sarvamārgeṣu sarvakarma samabhyaset || 104 ||


bahiryāgavidhānam


atha vakṣye bahiryāgavidhānaṃ tantravartmanā |
eṣāmanyatamaṃ kṛtvā yantrarājaṃ samālikhet || 105 ||


cakrarājalekhanavidhiḥ


svārṇe vā rājate tāmre pāṣāṇe cāṣṭadhātuṣu |
(yadvā) 
gaṇḍakītīrapāṣāṇe tathaiva rucipīṭhake || 106 ||

śrīkhaṇḍasambhave pīṭhe raktacandanasambhave |
yadvā suvarṇarūpyādau tāmre vā darpaṇe tathā || 107 ||

kāśmīraprabhave bhūmau yantraṃ kuryād yathāvidhi |
gomayena ca saṃlipte bhūpradeśe manorame || 108 ||

sindūrarajasā  vāpi raktābhiḥ kuṅkumasya vā |
(yadvā) 
śuddhe same bhūrjapatre athavānye tāmrapaṭṭake || 109 ||

śuṣke same bhūgṛhe vā sphaṭike svarṇapaṭṭake |
ratne vā kulaśaktau vā yantraṃ kuryāt susādhakaḥ || 110 ||

paṭṭālakaṃ bilipyeta vakṣamāṇaikavastunā |
svayambhu kusumaṃ kuṇḍagolotthaṃ rocanāguruṃ || 111 ||

kāśmīraṃ mṛganābhiñca madyañca malayodbhavam |
eṣa gandhaḥ samākhyātaḥ sarvadā caṇḍikāpriyaḥ || 112 ||

etena gandhayogena cakrarājaṃ samālikhet |
(yadvā) 
svayambhukusumenaiva kuṇḍagolena vā punaḥ || 113 ||

tadā tasya sarvasiddhirjāyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
(yadvā) 
candanāgurucandreṇa sindūrarajasāpi vā || 114 ||

kastūrīghusṛṇairdravyai rocanālākṣayāpi vā |
(yadvā) 
kuṇḍagolodbhavairdravyaiḥ svayambhukusumena ca || 115 ||

p. 64)

rocanālākṣayā yuktaiḥ kuṅkumāgurucandanaiḥ |
suvarṇaratna lekhanyā sarvakāryārthasiddhaye || 116 ||

(yadvā) 
puṣpeṇa vilikhedvāpi yantrarājaṃ susādhakaḥ | 
candrasūryātapaṃ yā vā athavā bilvakaṇṭakaiḥ || 117 ||

candrasūryātapahomodbhavayā vilvakaṇṭakaiḥ | 
eṣāmekatamaṃ labdhā svīyayantraṃ samuddharet || 118 ||

sārdhatrikoṭitīrtheṣu snātvā yat phalamāpnuyāt |
tat phalaṃ labhate vīraḥ kṛtveṣṭacakradarśanam || 119 ||

nālocya kulajaṃ vartma na jñātvā sūtradhāraṇaṃ |
na jñātvā suṣamāṃ rekhāṃ nālikhya suṣamaṃ mukhaṃ || 120 ||

tasya sarvaṃ hareddevo yo'smin yantre prapūjakaḥ |
atha vakṣye ca śāktānāṃ sāmānyaṃ yantramūttamam || 121 ||


sāmānyayantranirmāṇavidhiḥ


yatrāparājitāpuṣpaṃ javāpuṣpañca vidyate |
karavīre raktaśukle droṇe vā yantrarājake || 122 ||

tatra devī vasennityaṃ tadyantre caṇḍikārcanaṃ |
paśorālokanaṃ na syāttathā kuryāt prayatnataḥ || 123 ||

yadi daivāt paśoragre likhanaṃ vidyate kvacit |
devyaṅgakṣatirevātra kriyate pāpabuddhinā || 124 ||

uttarāśāmukho bhūtvā yadā cakraṃ samuddharet |
uttarāśā tadā sāpi pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 125 ||


p. 66)


īśakoṇaṃ tadeva syādāgneyañca na saṃśayaḥ |
paścimāśāmukho mantrī yathācakraṃ samuddharet || 126 ||

paścimāśā tadā jñeyā pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ |
vāyukoṇaṃ tadāgneyamīśānaṃ rākṣasaṃ bhavet || 127 ||

dakṣiṇābhimukho mantrī yadā cakraṃ samuddharet |
pūrvāśaiva tadā sā dik rakṣaḥkoṇantu vahnikam || 128 ||

pūrvāśābhimukho bhūtvā yadā cakraṃ samuddharet |
pūrvāśāpi tadā jñeyā pūrvāśaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 129 ||

āgneyañca tathāgneyaṃ īśakoṇañca īśakam |
yadāśābhimukho mantrī pūjayeccaṇḍikāṃ śubhām || 130 ||

pūrvāśaiva sā vijñeyā sādhakena mahātmanā |
devīpaścāt pratīcī syāt prācī tu caṇḍikāpuraḥ || 131 ||

anyeṣāñcaiva devānāṃ vidhiścātra prakathyate |
pūjyapūjakayorantaḥ pūrvāśaiva nigadyate || 132 ||

likhitvā purato yantraṃ sthāpayedāsanopari |
sīsake kāṃśyapātre vā raṅge vā sādhakottamaḥ || 133 ||

phalakāyāṃ paṭe bhittau sthāpayenna kadācana |
sthāpitaṃ yadi lobhena mohenājñānato'pi vā || 134 ||

kulaṃ vittamapatyañca nirmūlaṃ yāti sarvathā |
puṣpaṃ tatra vinikṣipya gandhacandanasaṃyutam || 135 ||

aśūnyaṃ sthāpayeccakraṃ śūnye vighnasamo bhavet |
svakalpoktavidhānena devīṃ dhyātvā susādhakaḥ || 

ātmābhedena saṃbhāvya puṣpaṃ dadyāt svamūrddhani || 137 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye tṛtīyollāsaḥ || 3 ||



caturtha ullāsaḥ 


mantravidhānam


atha vakṣye ca mantrāṇāṃ vidhānaṃ yatra yadbhavet |


devatābhede vāmadakṣiṇabhedena pūjāvidhiḥ


bālāṃ vā tripurāṃ devīṃ savyāṃ vāpyatha bhairavīm || 1 ||

kāmeśvarīñca kāmākhyāṃ pūjayecca yathecchayā |
dākṣiṇyādvāmabhāvādvā sarvathā siddhimāpnuyāt || 2 ||

mahāmāyāṃ śāradāñca śailaputrīṃ tathāmbikām |
yathā tathā prakāreṇa vāmadakṣiṇato yajet || 3 ||

śmaśānabhairavīṃ kālīṃ ugratārāñca pañcamīm | 
ucchiṣṭabhairavīñcaiva tārāṃ tripurasundarīm || 4 ||

unmukhīñcaiva durgāñca mardinīṃ svapnabodhinīm | 
etāstu vāmabhāvena pūjyā dakṣiṇatāṃ vinā || 5 ||

siddhirna jāyate dakṣāllakṣajanma yajed yadi |
vāmāt sarvasamṛddhiḥ syāddevyāḥ priyataro bhavet || 6 ||

dakṣiṇena labhecchāpaṃ mṛte narakamāpnuyāt |
brahmāpi ca bhavedvāmo viṣṇuścaiva sadāśivaḥ || 7 ||

viṣṇośca vāmikā mūrttirnārasiṃhānvayo bhavet |
sā tu dakṣiṇavāmābhyāṃ pūjanīyā prayatnataḥ || 8 ||

tathaiva bālagopālamūrttiryā paramātmanaḥ |
matsyamāṃsāsavābhogī lolupaḥ strīṣu sarvadā || 9 ||

gaṇeśo'pi ca vetālaḥ kathito vāmanāyakaḥ |
bhairavo'pi bhavedvāmastathānye devatādayaḥ || 10 ||

etāstu vāmabhāvena pūjanīyā viśeṣataḥ |
anyāśca caṇḍikādevyā bālikāmūrtayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 11 ||

lakṣmyādyā vāmikāmūrtī raktā ca daśabhairavī |
vāgbhavī ca sarasvatyā bālikāmūrtirīritā || 12 ||

vāmabhāvena siddhiḥ syāddākṣiṇyena vinā kadā |
pūjako'pi bhavedvāmastathaiva satataṃ guruḥ || 13 ||

yajecca māṃsamatsyena maithunādyairviśeṣataḥ |
mahābhairavarūpeṇa tathā ca ratisaṅgame || 14 ||

nānyathā jāyate siddhirmantrāṇāñca suniścitam |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi pañcatattvavinirṇayam || 15 ||


pañcatattvavidhānam


atha vakṣye ca pañcānāṃ vidhānaṃ tantravartmanā |
pañcāmāttu paraṃ nāsti śāktānāṃ sukhamokṣayoḥ || 16 ||

kevalaiḥ pañcamaireva siddho bhavati sādhakaḥ |
kevalenādyayogena sādhako bhairavo bhavet || 17 ||

dvitīyena ca tattvena pūjako brahmarūpabhāk |
kevalena tṛtīyena mahābhairavatāṃ vrajet || 18 ||

caturthena tu tattvena bhuvi pūjakanāyakaḥ |
pareṇa paratāṃ yāti śivatulyaḥ sa sādhakaḥ || 19 ||

p. 71)


pañcamena bhavedbhogī sarvasiddhiparāyaṇaḥ |
yasya bhavedyadā nityaṃ pañcatattvasya sambhavaḥ || 20 ||

pañcatattve bhavedvīraḥ śivavandyaḥ sa sādhakaḥ |


pañcatattvavihīnā pūjā niṣphalā


pañcamena vinā devīṃ pañcamīṃ pūjayettu yaḥ || 21 ||

sa tu syāḍḍākinībhogyo nārakī brahmaghātakaḥ |
anyamūrtestu durgāyāḥ pañcamena vinā sadā || 22 ||

niścitaṃ naiva siddhiḥ syājjapahomāśca tarpaṇāt |
tadvihīnasya yā pūjā japaṃ vā tarpaṇaṃ punaḥ || 23 ||

rauravāya bhavatyeva tasya bhraṣṭasya niścitam |
caṇḍikāṃ pūjayedyastu vinā pañcamakārakaiḥ || 24 ||

catvāri tasya naśyanti āyurvidyā yaśodhanaṃ |
makārapañcakaṃ caiva devatāprītikārakam || 25 ||

pañcamena tu vīrendraḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | 
madyaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā matsyaṃ mudrā maithunameva ca || 26 ||

makārapañcakaṃ devi devatāprītidāyakam |
vinā pañcopacāraṃ hi devīpūjāṃ karoti yaḥ || 27 ||

yoginīnāṃ bhavedbhakṣyaḥ pāpañcaiva pade pade |

p. 72)

vinā māṃsena madyena nā se naṃ śca vi lī va pum || 28 ||

na mantrasiddhimāpnoti tasmād yatnaparo bhavet |
pramādādyadi lupyeta devatāśāpamāpnuyāt || 29 ||

yathāvidhi yajeddevīṃ makārapañcakaiḥ sadā |
tṛptyarthaṃ sarvadevānāṃ tattvajñānodbhavāya ca || 30 ||


madyādilakṣaṇavidhānam


teṣāñca lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye śodhanañca krameṇa tu |
madyasya lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca || 31 ||

satye kramāccaturvarṇaiḥ kṣīrājyamadhupiṣṭakaiḥ |
tretāyāṃ pūjitā devī ghṛtena sarvajātibhiḥ || 32 ||

madhubhiḥ sarvavarṇaiśca pūjitā dvāpare yuge |
pūjanīyā kalau devī kevalairāsavaiśca taiḥ || 33 ||

caturyugeṣu saṃpūjyā turīyairāsavaiḥ sadā |
tattu nānāvidhaṃ dravyaṃ kathyate tantravartmanā || 34 ||

vārkṣaṃ gauḍaṃ tathā pauṣpaṃ kṣaudraṃ phalasamudbhavam |

p. 73)

sarvotkṛṣṭantu vijñeyaṃ dravyamannena sambhavam || 35 ||

(athavā) 
patrapuṣpāṅkuraphalamūlavalkaladhānyajam |
rasaṃ vṛkṣalatājātamaikṣavaṃ daśavidhaṃ smṛtam || 36 ||

pānasaṃ drākṣamādhūkaṃ khārjūraṃ tālamaikṣavam |
madhūtthaṃ sīdhu mādhvīkaṃ maireyaṃ nārikelajam || 37 ||

madyānyekādaśaitāni bhuktimuktikarāṇi ca |
dvādaśantu surāmadyaṃ sarveṣāmuttamottamam || 38 ||

madhupuṣparasodbhūtamāsavaṃ taṇḍulodbhavam |
sarvasiddhikarī paiṣṭī gauḍī bhogapradāyiṇī || 39 ||

mādhvī muktikarī proktā khārjūrī ripunāśinī |
nārikelodbhavā śrīdā aikṣavaṃ sukhavarddhanam || 40 ||

anyatrāpi

gauḍī piṣṭakajātatālatarujā drākṣī ca mādhvī tathā 
khārjūrī navanārikela tarujā gauḍī ca bhogapradā |
paiṣṭī siddhikarī vipakṣadalanī tālī ca drākṣī śubhā 
anyā yā vihitā sadā śubhakarī mokṣapradāḥ saptikāḥ || 41 ||

p. 74)

apakvaiśca supakvaiśca dravyairamṛtasannibhaiḥ |
tarpaṇaṃ parameśānyāḥ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 42 ||

pañcaśuddhividhānam

ātmasthānamanudravyadehaśuddhiñca pañcamīm |
yāvanna kurute mantrī tāvaddevārcanaṃ kutaḥ || 43 ||

asaṃskṛtaṃ pibed dravyaṃ balātkāreṇa maithunam |
svapriyeṇa hataṃ māṃsaṃ rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet || 44 ||

saṃśodhanamanācarya strīṣu madyeṣu sādhakaḥ |
kṛte'pi siddhihāniḥ syāt kruddhā bhavati caṇḍikā || 45 ||


surāśodhanakathanam


tasmāt saṃśodhanaṃ vakṣye yathā tantrānusārataḥ |
vāmabhāge ca ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tanmadhye brahmarandhrakam || 46 ||

tadbāhye maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā caturasraṃ samālikhet |
sāmānyārghyajalenaiva prokṣayenmaṇḍalaṃ tataḥ || 47 ||

p. 75)

abhyarcyādhāraśaktiñca kumbhasyādhāramāstaret |
tatra saṃpūjayedvahnimaṇḍalaṃ tadanantaram || 48 ||


kumbhanirmāṇavidhiḥ


tatra saṃsthāpayet kumbhaṃ sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tathā |
tāmraṃ bhūmimayaṃvāpi yadvā lauhavivarjitam || 49 ||

sauvarṇaṃ bhogadaṃ proktaṃ rājataṃ mokṣadaṃ bhavet |
kāṃsyaṃ śāntikarañcaiva mṛṇmayaṃ puṣṭidaṃ tathā || 50 ||

pañcāśadaṅgulāyāma utsedhaḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaḥ |
kalasānāṃ pramāṇaṃ tu mukhamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ bhavet || 51 ||

eṣāmekatamaṃ kumbhaṃ astreṇa kṣālitaṃ tataḥ |
ādhāre sthāpayitvā tu pūjayet sūryamaṇḍalam || 52 ||

tataśca kāraṇairdivyaiḥ pūrayenmūlamuccaran |
bhūṣayedraktapuṣpeṇa raktamālyena sādhakaḥ || 53 ||

p. 76)

raktavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya lepayedraktacandanaiḥ |
somamaṇḍalamabhyarcya amṛtaṃ paribhāvya ca || 54 ||


caturasrādikā pañcamudrāḥ


vibhāvayettato dravyamiṣṭadevasvarūpakam |
praṇamedbhaktibhāvena mudrābhiḥ pañcasaṃjñakaiḥ || 55 ||

adhomukhau samau kṛtvā bhūmau pāṇitaladvayam |
sakalāṅgulibhiḥ samyaṅmudreyaṃ caturasrikā || 56 ||

adhomukhaṃ muṣṭiyugmaṃ saṃvṛttaṃ parikīrttitam |
anyonyābhimukhau hastau puṭākāreṇa kārayet || 57 ||

saṃpuṭākhyamahāmudrā yojitā natikarmaṇi |
etasyā eva mudrāyāḥ kaniṣṭhāmūladeśake || 58 ||

aṅguṣṭhau ca kṣipettatra saṃpuṭāñjalirīritā |
madhyame sarale kṛtvā tarjanyupari saṃsthite || 59 ||

anāmikāmadhyagate tathaiva hi kaniṣṭhike |
sarvā ekatra saṃyojya aṅguṣṭhaparipīḍitā || 60 ||

eṣā tu paramā mudrā yonimudrā prakīrtitā |


pañcamudrāmantrakathanam


hrāṃ namaścaturasraṃ syāt hrīṃ namaḥ saṃvṛtastathā || 61 ||

klāṃ namaḥ saṃpuṭaṃ jñeyaṃ bluṃ namaḥ saṃpuṭāñjali |
saḥ namo yonimudrā syāt pañcamudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 62 ||

bhaktyā vilokayeddravyaṃ mūlamantraṃ samuccaran |
praṇavaṃ varuṇaṃ sendu vāmadevañca ṅeyutam || 63 ||

vauṣaḍantaṃ samuccārya pūjayet yantramuttamam | 
viṣaṃ kūrcaṃ tataḥ paśupataye tadanantaram || 64 ||

astrāya varmabījañca phaṭkārāntaḥ paro manuḥ |
anenādau prapūjyaiva pathikasya baliṃ haret || 65 ||

trikoṇavṛttabhūgehaṃ likhet kumbhasamīpataḥ | 
sindūrakuṅkumābhyāñca sarvapathikadevatāḥ || 66 ||

tatra saṃpūjayitvā ca baliṃ dadyāttataḥ param |
sahetuṃ sāmiṣānnañca nidhāya manumuddharet || 67 ||

māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ samuddhṛtya sarvapathikadevatā |
bhyo hṛnmanuṃ samuccārya anenotsṛjya sādhakaḥ || 68 ||

uddhṛtya vāmahastena bhrāmayet kalaśopari |
tataḥ kṣiptvā bahiḥsthāne mūlamantraṃ samuccaran || 69 ||


vikārahāriṇīmantroddharaḥ.


dhūpayitvā tato dravyaṃ vikārānnāśayettataḥ |
māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ paśudvandvaṃ sendu dīrghadvayānvitam || 70 ||

churikā meti śobhini vikārānasya cāntaram |
dravyasya harayugmañca dviṭhāntaṃ prajapenmanum || 71 ||

vikārahāriṇīṃ japtvā caturdhā śodhanañcaret |
astreṇa tāḍanaṃ kuryādvarmaṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ tataḥ || 72 ||

mūlena vīkṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā prokṣayeddhṛdayena tu |
mūlena gandhamādāya bahannāsāpuṭe tridhā || 73 ||

dravyamadhye likhedyoniṃ tanmadhye ca hasaurlikhet |
hsauḥ sahauḥ namonatena pūjayet kumbhamadhyake || 74 ||


ānandeśvaragāyatrīvidhhanam


vāṅmāyā kamalā proktvā ānandeśvarāya vidmahe |
sudhādevyai dhīmahīti tanno'rdhanārīśvareti ca || 75 ||

pracodayāditi japed gāyatrīṃ daśadhā sudhīḥ |


brahmaśāpavimocanamantroddhāraḥ


māyāvahniyutaṃ kāmaṃ ratibindusamanvitam || 76 ||

paramasvāmini padaṃ paramākāśa śūnya ca |
vāhini candrataḥ prānte sūryāgnibhakṣiṇītyapi || 77 ||

pātraṃ viśa padānte tu viśa svāhā tataḥ param |
tridhā ca prajapenmantraṃ brahmaśāpaṃ vimocayet || 78 ||

vedādi vāruṇaṃ bījaṃ ṣaḍdīrghasvarabheditam |
brahmaśāpapadasyānte vimocitāñca ṅeyutām || 79 ||

sudhādevyai namo'ntañca daśadhā prajapenmanum |
māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ ka(ma)daṃ senduṃ ṣaḍdīrghasvaravahnimat || 80 ||


kṛṣṇaśāpavimocanamantroddhāraḥ


sudhākṛṣṇasvarūpānte śāpaṃ mocaya tat param |
amṛtaṃ srāvayadvandvaṃ dviṭhāntaṃ manumuttamam || 81 ||

daśadhā parisaṃjapya kṛṣṇaśāpavimuktaye |


śukraśāpavimocanamantroddhāraḥ


viṣaṃ binduyutaṃ vāntaṃ dīrghaṣaṭkasamanvitam || 82 ||

śukraśāpapadaṃ procya vimocitāṃ tu ṅeyutām | 
sudhādevyai hṛdantañca daśadhā sañjapenmanum || 83 ||

athavānyaprakāreṇa śukraśāpaṃ vimocayet |
māyāṃ kāmeśvarīṃ vācaṃ amṛte amṛtodbhave || 84 ||

amṛtavarṣiṇi proktvā kāmabījaṃ tato'mṛtam |
srāvayadvitayaṃ śaktibījaṃ bhārgavamuddharet || 85 ||

śāpañca mocaya proktvā jñānaṃ dehi tato vadet |
siddhisāmarthyamābhāṣya dahayugmaṃ mahāpadaṃ || 86 ||

khecarīpadato mudrāṃ tathā prakaṭayadvayam |
astrāya phaṭ dviṭhāntañca uddhṛtya daśadhā japet || 87 ||

p. 81)

athavānyat pravakṣyāmi śāpatrayavimocanam |
surāṇāmamṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ baladevena dhīmatā || 88 ||

samānītā prayatnena pānārthaṃ vāruṇī surā |
dattātrayeṇa muninā śukreṇa ca mahātmanā || 89 ||

dhīmatā balabhadreṇa purā pītotthitārṇavāt |
vāratrayantu saṃjapya paṭhedanyatamaṃ manum || 90 ||

śivaśaktiyutāṃ vāṇīṃ varuṇaṃ kṣitisaṃyutam |
ṣaḍdīrghasvarasambhinnaṃ nādabinduvibhūṣitam || 91 ||

amṛte amṛtodbhave amṛtavarṣiṇīti ca |
surāśukraśāpaṃ procya mocayadvitayaṃ tataḥ || 92 ||

amṛtaṃ srāvayadvandvaṃ svāhā śāpaharo manuḥ |
daśadhā parisañjapya śāpatrayaṃ vimocayet || 93 ||

triśāpamocanīṃ japtvā prakāśayuktāṃ tato japet |
māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ tato vācaṃ amṛte amṛtodbhave || 94 ||

amṛtavarṣiṇi procya mahāprakāśayukte'pi ca |
dviṭhāntañca samuccārya uccaret kubjikāntataḥ || 95 ||


kubjikāmantroddhāraḥ


pretabījaṃ samuccārya hasaklīṃ hasarīṃ tathā |
hasakhaphreñca hasarīṃ bhagavati tato vadet || 96 ||

anugraho hasaprānte kubjike'pi tataḥ param |
sahakhaphreṃ yugaṃ procya sanoreva tato vadet || 97 ||

ghorakhaprāñca khasaprīñca kiladvayasamanvitam |
vicco'pi hasakhānnñca hasakhableñca hasarīṃ || 98 ||

śivaśaktidvayānte ca māyāṃ hasreṃ tato vadet |
anena manunā mantrī amṛtaṃ paricintayet |
tiraskāriṇīṃ tato dhyāyet paśupāśa vināśinīm || 99 ||


tiraskariṇīvidyādhyānam


nīlaṃ hayaṃ samadhiruhya puraḥ prayāntīṃ nīlāṃśukābharaṇamālyavilepanāḍhyām |
nidrāpuṭena bhuvanāni tirodadhānāṃ 
khaḍgāyudhā bhagavatī paripātu bhaktān || 100 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā japedvidyādvayañca sādhakottama | 
māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ samuddhṛtya namo bhagavatīti ca || 101 ||

p.83)

māheśvari padasyānte sarvapaśujanoddharet |
manaścakṣustiraskariṇīṃ kurudvandvañca ṭhadvayam || 102 ||


tiraskariṇīvidyā nirūpaṇam


anyatiraskariṇīṃ vakṣye māyāṃ kāmeśvarīṃ tataḥ |
vāṇīṃ glaiṃ tiraskariṇi sakaleti janeti ca || 103 ||

vāgvādini padasyānte sakalapaśu ca bruyāt |
janānte ca manaścakṣuḥ śrotrajihvāpadantataḥ || 104 ||

ghraṇoktiñca tiraskānte riṇīṃ kurudvayaṃ tataḥ |
ṭhadvayañca sargayutaṃ dviṭhānto'yaṃ mahāmanuḥ || 105 ||

tridhā japtvā ca vīrendrastrikoṇe cintayettataḥ |
haṃsapīṭhe mantramaye svaguruṃ śivarūpiṇaṃ || 106 ||

akathāditripaṅktyā tu halakṣa madhyamaṇḍitam |
iti sañcintya tatpaścāddravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret |
athavānyaprakāreṇa dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 107 ||


haṃsavatī ṛk


oṃ haṃsaḥ śucisadvasurantarīkṣasaddhotā vedisadatithirduroṇasat |
nṛsadvarasadvyomasadabjā gojā ṛtajā adrijā ṛtaṃ bṛhat || 108 ||

aiṁ tṛptodbhavasiddhodbhava-sarvajñodbhava-sarvajanakodbhavāya amṛtāya amṛtanāthāya 

p. 84)

amṛtakulakumbhāya aiṁ phreṃ cala cala 
piva piva kulakuṇḍalini amṛtakuṇḍalini 
amṛtaṃ drava drava nirjhara nirjhara aiṁ hrīṁ 
kulakuṇḍalini amṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā || 109 ||

prathamaṃ praṇavaṃ haṃsaḥ śucīti tadanantaram | 
sadvasurantarīkṣeti saddhotā vedisatyatha || 110 ||

atithirduroṇasadityardhamasyā ṛco bhavet |
nṛsadvarasadityuktvā sadṛto'tithyanantaram |
sadvyomasadabjā gojā ṛtajā adrijā |
ṛtaṃ bṛhadityarddhañca asyā ṛca udāhṛtam || 111 ||

tridhā ca saṃjapenmantraṃ athavānyatamaṃ japet |
vāk ca tṛptodbhavasyānte siddhodbhava tataḥ param || 112 ||

sarvajñodbhava sarvānte janakodbhava ṅeyutam |
amṛtāyāmṛtaṃ proktvā nāthāya padato'mṛta || 113 ||

kulakumbhāya vāgbhavaṃ phreṁ tataśca calayugmakam |
pibadvandvaṃ kulaṃ proktvā kuṇḍalini tato vadet || 114 ||

amṛtapadataḥ kuṇḍalinīti amṛtaṃ drava |
dvayaṃ nirjharayugmañca vāgbhavaṃ bhuvaneśvarīm || 115 ||

kulakuṇḍalinīṃ procya amṛtaṃ kuruyugmakam |
dviṭhāntaṃ manumuccārya pāyayitvā ca kuṇḍalīm || 116 ||

śivaśaktisamāyogaṃ cintayitvā ca sādhakaḥ |
tasmāt sudhāṃ samutpādya pūrayet pātramuttamam || 117 ||

p. 85)

anenaiva vidhānena dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret |
kālikāyā viśeṣañca vakṣye'haṃ tantravartmanā || 118 ||

ekameva paraṃ brahma sthūlasūkṣmamayaṃ dhruvam |
kacodbhavāṃ brahmahatyāṃ tena te nāśayāmyaham || 119 ||

sūryamaṇḍalasambhūte varuṇālayasambhave |
amābījamaye devi śukraśāpādvimucyatām || 120 ||

vedānāṃ praṇavo bījaṃ brahmānandamayaṃ yadi |
tena satyena te devi brahmahatyāṃ vyapohatu || 121 ||

evaṃ mantratrayeṇaiva abhimantrya surāṃ śubhām |
pradadyāt kālikāyai ca tato naivedyabhugbhavet || 122 ||

iti śuddhitrayaṃ jñeyaṃ śāpānāṃ mocanaṃ param |
eṣāmekatamaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyedānandabhairavam || 123 ||

p. 86)

ānandabhairavadhyānam

sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ candrakoṭisuśītalam |
aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ devaṃ pañcavaktraṃ trilocanam || 124 ||

amṛtārṇavamadhyasthaṃ brahmapadmopari sthitam |
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 125 ||

ākalpakhaṭvāṅgadharaṃ ghaṇṭāḍamaruvādinam |
pāśāṅkaśadharaṃ devaṃ gadāmuṣaladhāriṇam || 126 ||

khaḍgakheṭakapaṭṭīśamudgaraśūladaṇḍadhṛk |
kheṭakamuṇḍahastañca varadābhayavigraham || 127 ||

lohitaṃ devadeveśaṃ dravyamadhye niyojayet |
tasyotsaṅge surāṃ devīṃ candrakoṭyayutaprabhām || 128 ||


ānandabhairavīdhyānam


himakundendudhavalāṃ pañcavaktrāṃ trilocanām |
bhujāṣṭadaśasaṃyuktāṃ sarvāyudhakarodyatām || 129 ||

p. 87)

prahasantīṃ viśālākṣīṃ devadevasya sammukhīm |
athavānyaprakāreṇa surādevīñca cintayet || 130 ||

candrāṃśusadṛśīṃ śvetāṃ vāruṇīṃ brahmarūpiṇīm |
śiraścandrādvigalantīṃ dhyāyettāṃ paradevatām || 131 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā tato vīraḥ pūjayenmantramuccaran |
ānandabhairavaṃ devamānandabhairavīṃ tathā || 132 ||

śivacandrādyavarṇantu kālabhūvaruṇānalam |
ṣaṣṭhasvarānvitaṃ vāyuṃ bindunādavibhūṣitam || 133 ||

ānandabhairavāyeti śikhāmantraṃ tato vadet |
tataḥ pūrvavaduddhṛtya viparītamukhaṃ dvayam || 134 ||

vāyubījaṃ vāmanetratyānandabhairavīṃ tataḥ |
ṅeyutāṃ netramantrañca kathito manusattamaḥ || 135 ||

pūjayitvā dravyamadhye varuṇaṃ vasudhā japet |
mūlañcopari saṃjapya amṛtaṃ paribhāvayet || 136 ||

p. 88)

āvāhanyādimudrāñca darśayettadanantaram |
āvāhanīṃ darśayitvā sthāpanīṃ tadanantaram || 137 ||

sannidhāpanamudrāñca tathā ca sannirodhanīm |
avaguṇṭhanamudrāñca vidhāya sādhakottamaḥ || 138 ||

aṅgaṣaṭkasya mudrāñca tālatrayapuraḥsaram |
digbandhanaṃ prakurvīta choṭikābhiranantaram || 139 ||

paramīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā amṛtīkaraṇañcaret |
dhenumudrāmṛtīkṛtya dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 140 ||

ālokājjighraṇāddhyānāt snānāddravyaṃ viśudhyati |
dravyasnānaṃ tataḥ kuryāddakṣe kṛtvā ca cullakam || 141 ||

dravyasya cullakaṃ dakṣakare kṛtvā tu sādhakaḥ |
vāmanāsāṅguṣṭhayogāt pūjādravyeṣu nikṣipet || 142 ||

binduṃ kṣiptvā svavaktre ca mārjayeddhastayugmakam |
hastābhyāṃ mārjayeddehaṃ mūlamantraṃ samuccaran || 143 ||


surāniruktiḥ


etattu kāraṇaṃ divyaṃ surasaṅghaniṣevitam |
ata-eva nāma tasya sureti bhuvanatraye || 144 ||


surādarśanaphalam


surā darśanamātreṇa sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
tadgandhāghrāṇamātreṇa śatakratuphalaṃ labhet || 145 ||

madyasparśanamātreṇa tīrthakoṭīphalaṃ labhet |
tathā tatpānataḥ sākṣāllabhet muktiṃ caturvidhām || 146 ||

icchāśaktiḥ surāmode jñānaśaktiśca tadrase |
tatsvāde ca kriyāśaktiḥ cetaḥ śodhanasādhanā || 147 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye caturthollāsaḥ || 4 ||



pañcama ullāsaḥ


tantravartmanā māṃsanirūpaṇam


athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi māṃsāni tantravartmanā |


māṃsabhedakathanam


māṃsañca trividhaṃ proktaṃ jalabhūcarakhecaram || 1 ||

gonarebhāśvamahiṣavarāhājamṛgodbhvam |
mahāmāṃsāṣṭakaṃ proktaṃ devatāprītikārakam || 2 ||

(yadvā) 
go'jebhāśvavarāhāṇāṃ māhiṣaṃ mṛgakhaḍgayoḥ |
etanmāṃsāṣṭakaṃ proktaṃ caṇḍikāprītikārakam || 3 ||

(yadvā) 
ajāśvāvapi gaṇḍā ca mahiṣaścamarī tathā |
varāhavyāghrabhallūkaśaśakāḥ kūrmakukkuṭau || 4 ||

kṛṣṇasāraśca hariṇastathā citramṛgā api |
manuṣyo gavayaścaiva śallakī ca viśeṣataḥ || 5 ||

haṃsaḥ pārāvataścaiva tathaiva grāmakukkuṭāḥ |
vihitā balayaḥ proktāścaṇḍikāprītikārakāḥ || 6 ||

p. 91)

eteṣāṃ māṃsadānena sarvasiddhiḥ prajāyate |
eṣāmekatamaṃ labdhvā tarpaṇārthaṃ prakalpayet || 7 ||

māṃsasandarśanenāpi surādarśanavat phalam |
pitṛdaivatayajñeṣu vede hiṃsā vidhīyate || 8 ||

ātmārthaṃ prāṇināṃ hiṃsā kadācinnoditā priye |
animittaṃ tṛṇaṃ vāpi chedayenna kadācana || 9 ||


devatārthaṃ paśuhanane na doṣaḥ


devatārthaṃ dvijaṃ gāñca hatvā pāpairna lipyate |
māṃsaṃ vināpi pūjāyāṃ pāpaṃ syācca pade pade || 10 ||

gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ pūjyamanyathā narakaṃ vrajet |
hanyānmantreṇa cānena tvabhimantrya paśuṃ tataḥ || 11 ||

śivotkṛttamidaṃ piṇḍamatastvaṃ śivatāṃ gataḥ |
tadbuddhasva paśo tvaṃ hi māśivastvaṃ śivo'si hi || 12 ||

brahmā syātpalale viṣṇurgandhe rudraśca tadrase |
paramātmā tadānande tasmāt sevyamidaṃ priye || 13 ||

p. 92)

iti hatvā paśuṃ paścāt cāru pakvaṃ samācaret |
pūjāsu āmamāṃsāni dadyādvai sādhakaḥ kvacit || 14 ||

ṛte tu lohitaṃ śīrṣamamṛtaṃ tattu jāyate |
saṃśodhayettu māṃsāni yathāśāstrapramāṇataḥ || 15 ||


māṃsaśodhanavidhānam


māṃsaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi śāktānāṃ siddhihetave |
astrāntamūlamantreṇa prokṣayitvā ca sādhakaḥ || 16 ||

caturdhā śodhanaṃ kuryāt pūrvoktena ca vartmanā |
śoṣayedvāyubījena vahnibījena sandahet || 17 ||

śivaśaktisamāyogādvāruṇenāmṛtaṃ kuru |
tataśca sañjapenmantraṃ māṃsānāṃ śodhanāya ca || 18 ||

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccāryaṃ pratadviṣṇustataḥ param |
stavate mṛgo na bhīmaḥ saṃprocya kucaro bhavet || 19 ||

p. 93)

gariṣṭheti ca yasyoruṣu triṣu proktastataḥ param |
vikramaṇeṣvadhikṣipanti bhuvanāni viśvāḥ || 20 ||

pūrvoktā darśayenmudrā āvāhanyādisaṃjñikā |
mūlamantraṃ japedvīraḥ saptavāraṃ tataḥ param || 21 ||

matsyānāṃ śodhanaṃ vakṣye yathātantrānusārataḥ |


matsyabhedakathanam

pāṭhīnañca vodālaśca tathā śakulaśālakau || 22 ||

mahāśakulacitrī ca khaḍgī ca jalavṛścikā |
anena vihitāḥ sarve'pīmā mīnāḥ śubhapradāḥ || 23 ||

matsyamāṃsaśodhanam

ādau kurvīta mīnañca pūrvavat prokṣaṇādikam |
japenmantraṃ tataḥ paścānmīnānāñca viśuddhaye || 24 ||

oṃ tryambakaṃ yajāmahe sugandhiṃ puṣṭivarddhanam |
urvārukamiva bandhanmṛtyormukṣīya mā'mṛtāt || 25 ||

śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācarya mudrāñca pariśodhayet |
vraiheyaṃ maṇḍalākāraṃ candravimbanibhaṃ śubham || 26 ||

cāru pakvamanohāri śarkarādyaiḥ prapūritam |

p. 94)

pūjākāle devatāyā mudraiṣā parikīrttitā || 27 ||

bhraṣṭadhānyādikaṃ yadyaccarvaṇīyaṃ prakalpayet |
tasyāḥ saṃjñā smṛtā mudrā mahāmodapravardhinī || 28 ||

mudaṃ rātīti mudrā syādyenaikā muṣṭibhedataḥ |
mudrāñca prokṣayedādau śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret || 29 ||

oṃ tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ | 
divīva cakṣurātatam || 30 ||

oṃ tadviprāso vipaṇyavo jāgṛvāṃsaḥ samindhate |
viṣṇoryat paramaṃ padam || 31 ||

iti saṃjapya vīrendraḥ śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret || 32 ||


kuṇḍagolodbhavāmṛtavidhānam


atha vakṣye ca kuṇḍotthaṃ devatāprītikārakam |
madyakumbhasahasraistu māṃsabhāraśatairapi || 33 ||

naiva tuṣṭirbhaveddevyāḥ kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ vinā |
bhagaliṅgātmikā devī bhagaliṅgāmṛtātmikā || 34 ||

devyāḥ śukrarajorūpatvam

śukrarūpā ca śukrasthā rajorūpā rajaḥsthitā |

p. 95)

vindurūpā ca vindusthā rajovindusvarūpiṇī || 35 ||

bhagaliṅgapriyā devo bhagaliṅgāmṛtapriyā |
bhagaliṅgāmṛtenaiva tasmāttāṃ paripūjayet || 36 ||

vīradravyasya pānena parastrīramaṇena ca |
bhagaliṅgāmṛtenaiva devīṃ yaśca prapūjayet || 37 ||

na duḥkhaṃ jāyate tasya siddhamantro bhavennaraḥ |
kulastrīkulapuṣpaiśca kuladravyaiḥ kulāmṛtaiḥ || 38 ||

saṃpūjya labhate kāmānaṣṭasiddhīṃśca sādhakaḥ |
vinā kulodbhavairdravyaiḥ pūjayedyaśca caṇḍikām || 39 ||

janmāntarasahasrasya sukṛtaṃ tasya naśyati | 
dravyaśodhanamācarya śaktiśodhanamācaret || 40 ||

śaktiśodhanam

yathā dravyaṃ tathā śaktiṃ samānīya yathākramam |
snāpitāṃ pramadāṃ divyāṃ hetuyuktāñca dīkṣitām || 41 ||

svakāntāṃ parakāntāṃ vā ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitām |
sālaṅkārāṃ suveśāñca sthāpayet tūlikopari || 42 ||

p. 96)

nyāsajālaṃ prakurvīta svakalpoktavidhānataḥ |
mātṛkānyāsamācarya kalānyāsaṃ samācaret || 43 ||

hrīṅkārantu lalāṭe ca klīṅkāraṃ vadane tathā | 
aiṅkāraṃ hṛdaye caiva nābhau bluṅkārameva ca || 44 ||

strīṅkāraṃ vinyasedūguhye pañcakāmān nyased budhaḥ |
ṛṣyādikaṃ svakalpoktaṃ pādyādibhistato yajet || 45 ||

drāviṇīṃ vinyasedyonau mūlavidyāṃ tataḥ param |
ādhāre hṛdaye caiva bhruvormadhye nyaset kramāt || 46 ||

punarmūlaṃ brahmarandhre nyaset sādhakapuṅgavaḥ | 
mūrddhādipādaparyantaṃ nyāsaṃ kuryāttataḥ param || 47 ||

maithunakathanam

maulau kuntalakarṣaṇaṃ nayanayorācumbanaṃ gaṇḍayo-
rdantenādharapīḍanaṃ hṛdi hatirmuṣṭyā ca nābhau śanaiḥ |
kukṣau kaṇṭhakapolamaṇḍalakucaśroṇīṣu deyā nakhāḥ 
sīmante likhanaṃ nakhairurasijaṃ gṛhṇīta gāḍhaṃ tataḥ || 48 ||

kurvītāvirataṃ manobhavagṛhe mātaṅgalīlā iva |
jaṅghāṅguṣṭhapadorugulphahananaṃ cānyo'nyataḥ kāminoḥ || 49 ||

p. 97)

pāśabījaṃ vāmanetraṃ kāmavaruṇabhūyutam | 
ekādaśasvareṇāḍhyaṃ candrabindusamanvitam || 50 ||

amukīṃ drāvaya svāhā vinyaset sādhakottamaḥ |
vāmā yā capalacitte reto muñcadvayaṃ paṭhet || 51 ||

varuṇaṃ bhūdakṣakarṇayutañca kāmabījakam |
somavadhūsamāyuktaṃ candramantyasvarānvitam || 52 ||

drāviṇībījaṃ kathitaṃ sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca | 
tasyā yonau nyasedvidyāṃ ret thu mā to ca mai na ta || 53 ||

ātmani cintayeddevīṃ śaktimādyāsvarūpiṇīm |
niṃ gha yed ṅge yo na rṣa li mūlamuccārya sādhakaḥ || 54 ||

dharmādharmahavirdīpta ātmāgnau manasā srucā | 
suṣumnāvartmanā nityamakṣavṛttīrjuhomyaham || 55 ||

svāhānto'yaṃ mahāmantra ārambhe parikīrttitaḥ |
tato japet striyaṃ gacchan mukhe jihvāmṛtaṃ pivet || 56 ||

kulamadyaṃ samānīya vāruṇena ca saṃskṛtam |

p. 98)

varuṇaṃ svarasaṃyukta pañcadīrghasamanvitam || 57 ||

vauṃ varuṇāya praṇavaṃ mantrarājena śodhitam |
muhurmuhuḥ pibet pānaṃ śaktijihvāviloḍitam || 58 ||

ātmani devīsaṃyogāddevībhūtāṃ vicintayet |
ātmani śaktisaṃyogādekībhūtāṃ vicintayet || 59 ||

sūkṣmarūpakamātmānaṃ śaktiṃ kuṇḍalirūpiṇīm |
jihvāṃ mūlamayīṃ cintya yoniñca kularūpiṇīm || 60 ||  

kulākulavibhāgena ātmānaṃ nīyate tvayi |
śuddhamantrauṣadhenaiva ret ma na neḥ ñca pra tha yo || 61 ||

madhyamāne punastasyā jāyate tattvamuttamam | 
tattvatyāge pīṭhenmūlaṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 62 || 

prakāśākāśahastābhyāmavalambyonmanīsrucā |
dharmādharmakalāsnehapūrṇa agnau juhomyaham || 63 ||

svāhānto'yaṃ mahāmantraṃ śukratyāge prakīrttitaḥ |
śivaśaktisamāyogo yatra yatra prajāyate || 64 ||

tatra tatraiva saṃyojyaṃ dvayamenaṃ yathākramāt |
gṛhṇīyāttatprayatnena dravyaṃ kuṇḍodbhavaṃ smṛtam || 65 ||

p. 99)

idaṃ kuṇḍodbhavaṃ dravyaṃ kathitaṃ suradurlabham | 
golodbhavaṃ pravakṣyāmi vīrāṇāṃ siddhihetave || 66 ||

kulīnāṃ dīkṣitāṃ mattāṃ patihīnāṃ vicakṣaṇām |
sālaṅkārāṃ hetuyuktāmanapatyāṃ manoramām || 67 ||

sundarīṃ śobhanāṃ kṣīṇāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām |
sādhakākāṅkṣahṛdayāṃ kalāśāstravicāriṇīm || 68 || 

nyāsādikaṃ prakurvīta svakalpoktavidhānataḥ |
pūrvoktakramayogena tattvamutpādayet sudhīḥ || 69 ||

tattvaṃ gṛhṇīyāt yatnena pūjārthaṃ sādhakottamaḥ |
idaṃ golodbhavaṃ dravyaṃ devatātṛptikārakam || 70 ||

tāmrapātre gṛhītvā tu tattvadravyaṃ kulocitam |
nānāgandhasamāyuktaṃ kaṭutrayasamanvitam || 71 ||

kuṇḍagolodbhavādīnāṃ śodhanañca pracakṣyate |
śodhayet kuṇḍagolādīn nayatvarcāsamāhitaḥ || 72 ||


śodhanamantrāḥ


oṃ viṣṇuryoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi piṃśatu |
āsiñcatu prajāpatirdhātā garbhaṃ dadhātu te || 73 ||

p. 100)

garbhaṃ dhehi sinīvāli garbhaṃ dhehi sarasvati | 
garbhaṃ te aśvinau devādhādhattāṃ puṣkarasrajau || 74 ||

pluṃ jluṃ mluṃ gluṃ tataḥ svāhā amṛteti tato vadet |
amṛte amṛtodbhave amṛtavarṣiṇīti ca || 75 ||

amṛtaṃ srāvaya svāhā dviruccāryetyapi smaret |
śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācarya athavānyatamañcaret || 76 ||

tāraṃ kūrcāstramuccārya śivāditrayavinduyuk |
vahnijāyā tāramāyā śrībījaṃ suratipriye || 77 ||

tāramāyāramāstrañca vahnijāyāñca vartulam |
tato'mṛtodbhavāyeti amṛtavarṣiṇī param || 78 ||

priye devi padaṃ śukraśāpaṃ mocayayugmakam |
vahnijāyāntamantreṇa dravyaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 79 ||

vīryametat samādāya viśeṣārghye niyojayet |
tenāmṛtena deveśīṃ tarpayet sādhakottamaḥ || 80 || 

sānnidhyaṃ jāyate devyāḥ sarvakāmamupālabhet |
acirānmṛtyumāpnoti dvandvaṃ kṛtvā narādhamaḥ || 81 ||


śaṅkāvihīnena kulācāraḥ kartavyaḥ


nirvikalpakacetāstu kuryādevaṃ samāhitaḥ |
śaṅkayā jāyate glāniḥ śaṅkayā duḥkhabhājanam || 82 ||

śaṅkayā sarvahāniḥ syācchaṅkayā dhanahānitā |
śaṅkayā siddhihāniḥ syāttasmācchaṅkāṃ parityajet || 83 ||


dravyāṇāṃśaktibhedakathanan


atha vakṣyāmi dravyāṇāṃ śaktibhedena yadbhavet |
svalpāṅgīṃ dīrghakeśīṃ ca sādhakaḥ śuddhabhāvataḥ || 84 ||

puṣpagandhā javāpuṣpairdevīṃ gandhaiśca pūjayet |
tasyāḥ puṣpaiḥ prayatnena sarvakāmamupālabhet || 85||

dīrghāṅgī dīrghanayanā śyāmā vilajjitā sadā |
sugandhā sā suratādyairbilvapatraiḥ sugandhitā || 86 ||

tasyāḥ puṣpaiḥ prayatnena devīṃ sampūjayet parām |
gaurāṅgī puṣṭajaṅghā yā puṣṭahastā madāturā || 87 ||

madagandhā ca khārjuraiḥ palāśaiḥ kusumairyajet |
puṣpagandhā jagaddhātrī sā sadā kṛṣṇarūpiṇī || 88 ||

p. 102)

kṛśāṅgī kṛśamadhyā ca madhu dattvā yajet parām |
kṣīṇāṅgī ca yadā kṛṣṇā dīrghakeśī madākulā || 89 ||

tīvragandhā ca mandārairnārikelodbhavaiḥ sadā |
puṣṭā kharvā'sitāṅgī yā sā bhavenmadanāśrayā || 90 ||

madhugandhā ca gauḍībhiḥ padmapuṣpairyajet parām |
kṛśāṅgī gauradīrghā yā sā parā śiśumālinā || 91 ||

sādhakaḥ pūjayeddevīṃ mallikābhirviśeṣataḥ |
puṣṭā kharvā sitāṅgī yā sendīvaravikārakaiḥ || 92 ||

asyā maithunasambhūtaiḥ ketakīgandhayojitaiḥ |
kṣīṇā dīrghā ca gaurāṅgī mālatīgandhayojitā || 93 ||

sā bhavedaṅkuśā nāmnī tasyā maithunabhāvukaiḥ |
bandhūkairyadi gaurāṅgī yānaṅgamekhalā śubhā || 94 ||

karpūragandhahāridradravyaistāṃ paripūjayet |
āsāṃ dravyaṃ gṛhītvā tu tenaiva pariśodhayet || 95 ||

tattatpuṣpaiḥ prapūjyaiva sarvasiddhimupālabhet |
jñātvā naivaṃ kramaṃ yo hi yadi devīṃ prapūjayet || 96 ||

bhaveddhi viphalaṃ sarvaṃ yajñeṣu ghṛtavarjitam |
yaduktā daśamī śaktiḥ saṅkīrṇavṛttisaṃsthitā || 97 ||

gandhamātraṃ tu gṛhṇīyādvarṇamātraṃ na bhedayet |
svayaṃ sraṣṭā svayaṃ viṣṇuḥ svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ || 98 ||

sādhakānandabhāvena yajeddevīṃ kulakrame |
gajāśvānāṃ vṛṣājānāṃ narāṇāṃ śoṇitāruṇaiḥ || 99 ||


pañcatattvapraśaṃsā


kuṇḍagolodbhavairmāṃsaiḥ svayambhūkusumaiḥ sadā |
matsyairmadyaiśca naivedyaiḥ pratyakṣīkriyate naraiḥ || 100 ||

ca yāṃ thu di ne rā mai ma jātibhedaṃ na cācaret |
nādharmo jāyate kiñcinmahāmantrasya sādhane || 101 ||

sarvasiddhirbhavatyeva pañcatattvanivedanāt |
dravyaśuddherabhāvena nityaṃ karmaṃ na lopayet || 102 ||

p. 104)

pramādādyadi lupyeta rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |
makārapañcakaṃ kṛtvā punarjanma na vidyate || 103 ||

ata-eva mahāprājñaḥ pañcatattvena pūjayet |
yenāvaśyaṃ vidhātavyaṃ madirādānapūjanam || 104 ||

naṣṭaiḥ paryuṣitocchiṣṭairdurgandhairgandhavarjitaiḥ |
hetubhiḥ parapātrasthaistarpaṇaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 105 ||

āmiṣāsavasaurabhyahīnaṃ yasya mukhaṃ bhavet |
prāyaścittī sa varjyaśca paśureva na saṃśayaḥ || 106 ||

yāvadāsavagandhaḥ syāttāvat paśupatiḥ svayam |
vinālimāṃsagandhena paśuḥ paśupatiḥ svayam || 107 ||

matsyamāṃsavihīnena dravyeṇāpi na tarpayet |
piśitaṃ tilamātrantu tilārdhamapi bindunā || 108 ||

sakṛttarpaṇamātreṇa yajñakoṭiphalaṃ labhet |
raktavastraiḥ samācchādya pañcatattvaṃ tu rakṣayet || 109 ||

p.105)

paśusannidhau vīrapūjā niṣidhā


makārapañcakairdevīṃ nārcayet paśusannidhau |
saṃsthāpayenna dakṣe ca dravyaśuddhiritīṃritā || 110 ||

etattu kathitaṃ dravyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
dvijānāmanukalpantu na sākṣācca vikalpinām || 111 ||


kuladravyapratinidhiḥ


athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dravyādiparikalpanam |
brāhmaṇastāmrapātre tu madhumadyaṃ prakalpayet || 112 ||

athavā tāmrapātreṣu gavyaṃ dadyādghṛtaṃ vinā |
nārikelodakaṃ kāṃsye guḍārdrakaṃ tathaiva ca || 113 ||

gauḍī deyā kṣatriyeṇa mādhvī vaiśyena tatra vai |
sarvaṃ śūdreṇa dātavyaṃ yatra vā tadrucirbhavet || 114 ||

(yadvā) 
matsyamāṃsādivijayā cāṣṭagandhaiḥ sumiśritā |
saṃmardya vaṭikāṃ kṛtvā saṃgṛhyātha vicakṣaṇaḥ || 115 ||

p. 106)

tadabhāve tu vijayā jale saṃloḍya tarpayet |
guḍamiśreṇa takreṇa tarpayenmadhuyojitam || 116 ||

sauvīreṇāthavā kuryādetatkarma na lopayet |
māṃsābhāve tu lasunamārdrakaṃ nāgarantu vā || 117 ||

śūraṇaṃ māṃsavaṭakaṃ mūlaṃ vānyatamañcaret |
māṃsānukalpaṃ kathitaṃ mīnasyātha vidhiṃ śṛṇu || 118 ||

māhiṣaṃ gavayaṃ kṣīraṃ ajākṣīraṃ tathaiva ca |
phalamūlañca yatkiñciddagdhañcedāmiṣaṃ bhavet || 119 ||


kuṇḍagolādipratinidhiḥ


mīnasya kathitaṃ kalpaṃ kuṇḍagolodbhavaṃ śṛṇu |
aparāpuṣpagarbhe tu kulasthānaṃ manoramam || 120 ||

sarvasukhaṃ bhavettattu mahākāmakalātmakam |
hayārikusume nityaṃ svayamasti sadāśivaḥ || 121 ||

tanmukhe dravyamādāya puṣpamadhyetu candanam |
raktakuṅkumarāgaṃ vā bhinnaṃ tattvaṃ śivātmakam || 122 ||

yojayecchivaśaktyostu aikyaṃ sambhāvayan dhiyā |
kṣaṇaṃ vicintya tatraiva saṃpūjya parameśvarīm || 123 ||

japtvā tadeva kuṇḍotthaṃ dravyaṃ paramadurlabham |
antarādadhikaṃ jñeyaṃ etaddravyaṃ sudurlabham || 124 ||

yadyacca kathitaṃ dravyamanukalpaṃ vikalpinām |
ādāya pūjayeddevīmanyathā niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 125 ||

akṛtvā pañcame pūjāṃ na labdhvā gurusammatim |
svahastena paśuṃ hatvā rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet || 126 ||



iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye pañcamollāsaḥ || 5 ||



ṣaṣṭha ullāsaḥ 

athedānīṃ pravakṣyāmi arghyasya vidhimuttamam |
gṛhītvājñāṃ mahādevyā viśeṣārghyaṃ samācaret || 1 ||

ātmadevīdvayormadhye bhūmau cakraṃ likhettataḥ |
trikoṇañcaiva ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛttaṃ bhūgṛhasaṃyutam || 2 ||

madhye māyāṃ samālikhya prokṣayettadanantaram |
astrāntamūlamantreṇa sāmānyārghyajalena ca || 3 ||

ādhāraśaktimabhyarcya pūjayet pīṭhadevatāḥ |
caturasre pūrṇaśailamuḍḍīyānaṃ jalandharam || 4 ||

pūjayet kāmarūpañca pūrvādikramayogataḥ |
aṅgaṣaṭkaṃ yajettatra ṣaṭkoṇeṣu ca mantravit || 5 ||

mūlamantraṃ trikoṇeṣu saṃpūjya sādhakottamaḥ |
ādhāraśaktiṃ madhye ca saṃpūjya tadanantaram || 6 ||


ādhārapātrādivarṇanam


astreṇa kṣālitādhāraṃ sthāpayettatra sādhakaḥ |
ādhāreṇa vinā bhraṃśo na ca tṛpyanti mātaraḥ || 7 ||

tasmādvidhivadādhāraṃ kalpayet kulanāyike |
ādhāraṃ tripadaṃ proktaṃ ṣaṭpadaṃ vā catuṣpadam || 8 ||

athavā vartulākāraṃ kuryādādhāramuttamam |
eṣāmanyatamaṃ sthāpya pūjayet kulavartmanā || 9 ||

pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā saṃpūjya tena vartmanā |
ādau senduṃ mahākālaṃ vahnimaṇḍalaṃ ṅeyutam || 10 ||

hṛdantaṃ mantramuccārya ādhāre pūjayettataḥ |
tatra sampūjayedvahneḥ kalādaśakaṃ saṃjñayā || 11 ||


vahneḥ kalādaśakam


dhūmrārciruṣmā jvalinī jvālinī visphuliṅginī |
suśrīḥ surūpā kapilā havyakavyavahe'pi ca || 12 ||

yādisendukṣakārāntā abjādyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
ṅeyutā vai namo'ntāśca kalā vahneritīritāḥ || 13 ||

asrāntamūlamantreṇa kṣālitaṃ pātramuttamam |
svarṇaraupyaśilākūrmakapālālābumṛṇmayam || 14 ||

nārikelaśaṅkhatāmramuktāśuktisamudbhavam |
puṇyavṛkṣasamudbhūtaṃ pātraṃ kuryānmanoramam || 15 ||

(yadvā) 
kāñcanaraupyakācatāmrajanitaṃ muktākapālodbhvaṃ 
viśvāmitramayañca kāmadamidaṃ haimaṃ priyaṃ sphāṭikam |
bhāvaprītimadaṣṭasiddhijanakaṃ śrīnārikelodbhvaṃ 
kāpālaṃ sphuṭamantrasiddhijanakaṃ muktipradaṃ mauktikam || 16 ||

(yadvā) 
sauvarṇaraupyatāmrāṇi sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca |
śāntike ca śilāpātraṃ stambhe caiva tu mṛṇmayam || 17 ||

nārikelaṃ sarvavaśye mokṣe caiva tu mauktikam |
jñānapradaṃ kapālaṃ syānmāraṇe lauhamuttamam || 18 ||

kuryāt svarṇamayaṃ pātraṃ viśvāmitraṃ kapālakam |
śaṅkhaṃ jñānapradaṃ muktāśuktividyāpradāyinī || 19 ||

kapālālāvūpātrāṇi yogasiddhikarāṇi ca |
puṇyavṛkṣajapātrāṇi sarvapāpaharāṇi ca || 20 ||

ukteṣveteṣu pātreṣu pātramekaṃ prakalpayet |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistāra ṣaṣṭhāṅgulasamucchrayam || 21 ||

athavāpi tadardhaṃ syāttadarddhaṃ vāpi kārayet |
ekāṅgulaṃ tathā sārdhaṃ ṣaṭkañca vai pramāṇataḥ || 22 ||

atisūkṣmamatisthūlaṃ sacchidraṃ parivarjjayet |
na yojayedbhagnapātraṃ svaprādeśaṃ praśasyate || 23 ||

eṣāmekatamaṃ pātraṃ sthāpayettripadopari |
maṇḍalaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā pūjayet pūrvavartmanā || 24 ||

sūryasya maṇḍalaṃ tatra cintayitvātha pūjayet |
svarādyaṃ bindusahitaṃ ṅeyutam arkamaṇḍalam || 25 ||

p. 112)

namo'ntaṃ mantramuccārya pūjayitvārghyapātrakam |
kalāḥ sampūjayettatra bhānordvādaśasaṃjñakāḥ || 26 ||


dvādaśasūryakalāḥ


tapinī tāpinī dhūmrā marīcirjvālinī ruciḥ |
