suṣumnā bhogadā viśvā bodhinī dhāriṇī kṣamā || 27 ||

kabhādyāśca ṭhaḍāntā vai binduyuktā yathākramāt |
bījādyāśca kalā jñeyāścaturthyādyā namo'nvitāḥ || 28 ||

imāḥ kalāḥ susampūjyāḥ pātrañca pūjayettataḥ |
raktacandanapuṣpaiśca sugandhaiḥ sumanoharaiḥ || 29 ||

tataśca pūrayet pātraṃ tribhāgaṃ kumbhahetutaḥ |
varuṇaṃ mūlamantrañca vilomamātṛkāṃ paṭhan || 30 ||

jalena bhāgamekaṃ tu pūrayet sādhakottamaḥ |
tatra dūrvākṣatāraktapuṣpacandanasaṃyutam || 31 ||

pūrvoddhṛtaṃ kuṇḍagolaṃ vilvapatraṃ kṣipettataḥ |
māṃsaṃ matsyaṃ vinikṣipya mudrāñcaiva viśeṣataḥ || 32 ||

nānāsugandhikañcaiva navaratnaṃ vinikṣipet |
elālavaṅgakakṣvelajātīphalasamanvitam || 33 ||

p. 113)

candracandanakastūrīrocanāgurukuṅkumam |
svayambhūkusumaṃ kuṇḍagolotthañcāṣṭagandhakam || 34 ||

tatra kṣiptvā ca vīrendraḥ pūjayeccandramaṇḍalam |
sabinduṃ vāmakarṇañca ṅeyutaṃ somamaṇḍalam || 35 ||

hṛdantaṃ manumuccārya pātramadhye prapūjayet | 
kalā somasya tatraiva dviraṣṭasaṃkhyakā yajet || 36 ||


ṣoḍaśasomakalā


amṛtā mānadā pūṣā tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭī ratirdhṛtiḥ |
śaśinī candrikā kāntirjyotsnā śrīḥ prītiraṅgadā || 37 ||

pūrṇā pūrṇāmṛtā kāmadāyinyaḥ svarajāḥ kalāḥ |
akārādyā binduyutā ṅeyutāśca namo'ntikāḥ || 38 ||

pūjyāḥ sarvāḥ prayatnena kalā jñeyā vidhorimāḥ |
pūrvaṃvattīrthamāvāhya pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ likhet || 39 ||

pūrvavavanmatramuccārya ānandabhairavaṃ yajet |
ānandabhairavīṃ devīṃ tatra saṃpūjya deśikaḥ || 40 ||

p. 114)

nijeṣṭadevatāṃ tatrāvāhayecca tataḥ param |
āvāhanyādimudrāśca sādhako darśayettataḥ || 41 ||

svakalpoktavidhānena mudrāḥ sandarśayettataḥ |
darśayitvā tato mudrāmiṣṭadevaṃ yajettataḥ || 42 ||

pūrvavacca ṣaḍaṅgāni trikoṇāni prapūjayet |
akathāditrirekhāyāṃ madhye māyāṃ samālikhet || 43 ||

hasau madhye ca saṃlikhya pūrvādicāturasrike |
sampūjayettataḥ paścāt pañcaratnaṃ susādhakaḥ || 44 ||


pañcaratnapūjāvidhānam


pañcāntakaṃ kṣitiyutaṃ dakṣakarṇasamanvitam |
gaganānte ca ratnebhyo namo'ntamanumuddharet || 45 ||

śītalaṃ śakrasaṃyuktamagrabinduvibhūṣitam |
svargapadaṃ samuccārya ratnebhyo nama ityapi ||  46 ||

pañcamaṃ devarājañca dakṣaśrotrasamanvitam |
pātālapada ratnebhyo namo'ntaścāparo manuḥ || 47 ||

p. 115)

mahākālaṃ kṣitiyutaṃ dakṣakarṇasamanvitam |
martyaratne tataḥ paścādbhyo namo'tra samuddharet || 48 ||

vāmapādaṃ kṣitiyutaṃ dakṣaśravaṇabhūṣitam |
nāgapadaṃ samuccārya ratnebhyo nama ityapi || 49 ||

iti madhye susampūjya trirekhāsu tataḥ param |
vāgbhavānte ca divyaughaṃ gurusaṃjñāñca pādukām || 50 ||

pūjayāmi tataḥ paścādevamvidhaṃ trayaṃ yajet |
siddhaughaṃ mānavaughañca pūjayitvā manuṃ japet || 51 ||


amṛteśvarīmantraḥ


akhaṇḍaikarasānandakare parasudhātmani |
svacchandasphuraṇāmatra nidhehyakūlarūpiṇi || 52 ||

akulasthāmṛtākāre śuddhijñānakare pare |
amṛtatvaṃ nidhehyasnin vastuni klinnarūpiṇi || 53 ||

tadrūpeṇaikarasyañca dattvā hyetatsvarūpiṇi |
bhūtvā parāmṛtākāraṃ mayi citsphuraṇaṃ kuru || 54 ||

iti mantratrayeṇaivamabhimantrya tataḥ param |
brahmāṇḍakhaṇḍasambhūtamaśeṣarasasambhavam || 55 ||

p. 116)

āpūritaṃ mahāpātraṃ pīyūṣarasamāvaha | 
vāgbhavaṃ pañcamaṃ kṣauṇīṃ vāmaśrotrasamanvitam || 56 ||

kṣauṃ jūṃ saṃ amṛte'mṛtodbhave'mṛteśvari |
amṛtavarṣiṇi procya amṛtaṃ srāvayadvayam || 57 ||

vahnijāyāntamuccāryaṃ coddharedaparantataḥ |
vāgbhavaṃ vadayugmañca vāgvādini tataḥ param || 58 ||

vāṅmanaḥ padamābhāṣya klinne kledini tatparam |
kledayadvitayaṃ mahāmokṣaṃ kurudvayaṃ vadet || 59 ||

kāmabījaṃ śaktibījaṃ mokṣaṃ kurudvayaṃ vadet |
pretabījaṃ samuccārya hasaurnamastataḥ param || 60 ||

anenaiva vidhānena abhimantrya tataḥ param |
ānandabhairavaṃ devamānandabhairavīmapi || 61 ||

trivāraṃ mūrdhni santarpya mudrāḥ sandarśayettataḥ |
pūrvoktāścāturasrādyāḥ śaṅkhamudrāṃ tataḥ param || 62 ||


śaṅkhamudrākathanam


vāmāṅgulyastathā śliṣṭāḥ saṃyuktāḥ suprasāritāḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃṅguṣṭha saṃsṛṣṭhā mudraiṣā śaṅkhasaṃjñikā || 63 ||


p. 117)

matsyamudrākathanam


adhomukhaṃ dakṣapāṇiṃ vāmañcaiva tathāvidham |
uparyupariyogena militāḥ saralāṅgulīḥ || 65 ||

aṅguṣṭhau cālayet kiñcinmudraiṣā matsyasaṃjñitā |
matsyamudrāñca sandarśya dhenumudrāñca darśayet || 66 ||

avaguṇṭhya tataḥ paścāt yonimudrāṃ ca darśayet |
tritālachoṭikābhiśca digbandhanaṃ tataścaret || 67 ||

aṣṭadhā mūlavidyāṃ ca japtvā tu cintayettataḥ |
iṣṭadevasvarūpañca vibhāvya sādhakottamaḥ || 68 ||

ārghyasya sthāpanaṃ proktaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu siddhidam |
tathāca siddhavidyānāmācāraḥ kathyate'dhunā || 69 ||

tataḥ śaṅkhaṃ vīrapātraṃ sthāpayenmadhyabhāgataḥ |
śrīvidyoktavidhānena tataḥ pūjāṃ samācaret || 70 ||


siddhavidyācāraḥ


visarjanāvadhi vīro noddharenna ca cālayet |
tasmādvinduṃ vinikṣipya sāmānyārghye tataḥ param || 71 ||

p. 118)


pātrasaṃkhyānirṇayaḥ


devyarghyakumbhayormadhye pātrāṇi sthāpayed yathā |
guroḥ pātraṃ ghaṭasyānte tadante bhogapātrakam || 72 ||

tadante śaktipātrañca yoginīnāṃ tadantike | 
tadante vīrapātrañca balipātraṃ tadantike || 73 ||

pādyamācamanīyañca pātrāṇi sthāpayedbudhaḥ |
uttamaṃ navapātrāṇi saptapātrāṇi madhyamam || 74 ||

kecidvadanti mantrajñāḥ pañcapātrāṇi madhyamam |
adhamaṃ tāni pātrāṇi ekapātraṃ na kārayet || 75 ||

ekapātraṃ na kurvīta yadi sākṣāt kuleśvaraḥ |
mantrāḥ parāṅmukhā yānti vighnāścaiva pade pade || 76 ||

iha loke ca dāridryaṃ mṛte ca paśutāṃ vrajet |
devīpātraṃ guroḥ pātraṃ bhogapātraṃ trayaṃ matam || 77 ||

pūjāpātraṃ baleḥ pātraṃ pañcapātreṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ |
śakteḥ pātraṃ vīrapātraṃ saptapātreṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 78 ||

p. 119)

pūjāpātrañca vijñeyaṃ yoginīnāñca pūjane |
pādyamācamanīyañca navapātreṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 79 ||


pātrasthāpanāvidhiḥ


pātrāṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ vakṣye yathātantravidhānataḥ |
kumbhasya dakṣiṇe kuryāttrikoṇaṃ vṛttabhūgṛham || 80 ||

pūrvavat prokṣaṇādiñca kṛtvādhāraṃ vinikṣipet |
tatra sampūjayedvahnimaṇḍalaṃ tadanantaram || 81 ||

prakṣālya pūrvavat pātraṃ ādhāre sthāpayettataḥ |
abhyarcya maṇḍalaṃ tatra sūryasya pūrayettataḥ || 82 ||

kumbhāmṛtena saṃpūjya somasya maṇḍalaṃ yajet |
kuṇḍagolaṃ kṣipettatra kusumaṃ gandhacandanam || 83 ||

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛttabhūpūraṃ likhitvā pūjayettataḥ |
aṅgaṣaṭkaṃ koṇaṣaṭkaṃ mudrāḥ pūrvavadācaret || 84 ||

digbandhanaṃ vidhāyātha tālatrayapuraḥsaram |

p. 120)

aṣṭadhā mūlamantrañca japtvā binduṃ kṣipettataḥ || 85 ||


tarpaṇavidhiḥ


viśeṣārghyasya vīrendro gurūṃśca paritarpayet |
svasvanāmayutān divyān siddhamānavasaṅghakān || 86 ||

tarpayitvā svīyamūrdhni mudrayā tattvasaṃjñakā |
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāñca vāmahastasya sarvadā || 87 ||

kathitā tattvamudreyaṃ yojitā tarpaṇe budhaih. |
guruṃ paraguruñcaiva parāparaguruṃ tathā || 88 ||

parameṣṭhiguruñcaiva tarpayettadanantaram |
viśeṣārghyāmṛtenaiva tarpayennijadevatām || 89 ||

pañcadhā saptadhā vāpi hṛtsaroje tu sādhakaḥ |


tattvaśuddhikathanam


gurūṇāṃ pātradakṣe ca bhogapātraṃ prakalpayet || 90 ||

pūrvavat pātraṃ saṃsthāpya tattvaśuddhiṃ samācaret |
bhogapātrāmṛtenaiva sammārjya karayugmakam || 91 ||

dakṣakare savṛttañca navayoniṃ samālikhet |
kalāyasadṛśaṃ tatra kṣipecchuddhicatuṣṭayam || 92 ||

p. 121)

dehaśuddhinirūpaṇam


aṅguṣṭhamadhyamānāmāyogairekañca dhārayet |
vāmahastasya viprendraḥ svīkuryāttadanena tu || 93 ||

māyāñca kamalābījaṃ śivaśaktisadāśivam |
ṅentaṃ vidyākalātmānaṃ sabinduṃ ṣoḍaśa svaram || 94 ||

uccārya vāgbhavaṃ mūlaṃ ātmatattvena tatparam |
sthūladehaṃ tataḥ paścāt śodhayāmi padāt dviṭhaḥ || 95 ||

anenādhaḥśuddhikhaṇḍaṃ svīkṛtya tadanantaram |
māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ tato māyākalāniyatiśabdataḥ || 96 ||

kalātmaśuddhavidyāśca rāgaśca puruṣātmane |
kādipañcakavargañca binduyuk kāmabījakam || 97 ||

mūlamantraṃ tato vidyātattvena sūkṣmadehakam |
śodhayāmi tataḥ svāhā svīkuryādaparantataḥ || 98 ||

lajjāṃ lakṣmīṃ prakṛtyahaṅkāra buddhimanaḥśrotratvak |
cakṣustatorasanāghrāṇavākpāṇipadaṃ tathā || 99 ||

pāyūpasthapadaṃ śabdasparśarūpaṃ tato rasaḥ |
gandhākāśapadaṃ vāyutejaḥ salilaśabdataḥ || 100 ||

bhūmyātmane samuccārya binduyugyādivarṇakam |
śaktibījaṃ svamantrañca śivatattvena tatparam || 101 ||

paraṃ dehaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā caiva tṛtīyakam |

p. 122)


svīkṛtya ca punarmāyāṃ lakṣmībījaṃ samuccaret || 102 ||

punaḥ śivādikaṃ procya māyādikaṃ tataḥ param |
prakṛtyādikamuccārya pañcāśanmātṛkāntataḥ || 103 ||

bālāñca mūlavidyāñca sarvatattvena tatparam |
tattvatrayāśritaṃ jīvaṃ śodhayāmi tataḥ svāhā || 104 ||

svīkuryādamunā yugmaṃ dakṣavāmakrameṇa tu || 105 ||

oṃ ārdraṃ jvalati jyotirahamasmi 
jyotirjvalati brahmāhamasmi |
so'hamasmi brahmāhamasmi ahamasmi 
ahamevāhaṃ māṃ juhomi svāhā || 106 ||

oṃ tāmeva pratyakṣaṃ brahma vadiṣyāmi 
ṛtaṃ vadiṣyāmi satyaṃ vadiṣyāmi |
tanmāmavatu tadvaktāramavatu avatu 
mām avatuvaktāraṃ svāhā || 107 ||

oṃ chandasāmṛṣayo gāvo yacchandohyamṛtā bhuvasāmandroḥ |
medhayā spṛṇotu bhuvi sruvaṃ meṇopāyatu svāhā || 108 ||

p. 123)

vāsasā karayugmañca spṛṣṭvā dehañca śodhayet |
pāṇibhyāṃ sarvadehañca proñchayediti śodhanam || 109 ||

kṣālayeddhastayugalamathavānyatamañcaret || 110 ||


tattva"uddhimantrāḥ


oṃ prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānā me śudhyantām |
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 111 ||

oṃ pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśāni me śudhyantām |
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 112 ||

oṃ prakṛtyahaṅkārabuddhimanaḥśrotrāṇi me śudhyantām |
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 113 ||

oṃ tvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇavacāṃsi me śudhyantām |
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 114 ||

oṃ pāṇipādapāyūpasthaśabdā me śudhyantām | 
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 115 ||

oṃ sparśarūpagandharasākāśāni me śudhyantām |
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 116 ||

oṃ vāyutejaḥsalilabhūmyātmāno me śudhyantām |
jyotirahaṃ virajā vipāpmā bhūyāsaṃ svāhā || 117 ||

p. 124)

saptamantramidañcaiva paṭhettattvaviśuddhaye || 118 ||

tattvaśuddhiṃ vidhāyātha sthāpayecchaktipātrakam |
yoginīnāñca saṃsthāpya vīrapātraṃ tataḥ param || 119 ||

balipātraṃ tataḥ paścāt pādyapātrañca sandiśet |
ācamanīyapātrañca pūrvavat sthāpayedbudhaḥ || 120 ||

yoginīpātratattvena tarpayedraśmivṛndakam |
balipātrāmṛtenaiva vaṭukādīṃśca tarpayet || 121 ||

tattadabhāvato vīraḥ sāmānyārghyeṇa tarpayet |
devīpātrāmṛtaṃ binduṃ pūjādravyopari kṣipet || 122 ||

ātmadehañca saṃprokṣya bhāvayeddevatāmayam |
bhogapātrāmṛtaṃ binduṃ svīkuryāttadanantaram || 123 ||


balipañcakanirūpaṇam

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi balipañcakamuttamam |
yāvanno vaṭuke dadyāt yoginyāṃ sarvabhūtake || 124 ||

kṣetrapāle tathā rājarājeśvare ca sādhakaḥ |
akṛtvā balidānaṃ hi kathaṃ pūjāṃ samācaret || 125 ||

p. 125)

adattvā kṣetrepālāya kathaṃ kṣetreṣu pūjayet |
adattvā vaṭukādīnāṃ yaḥ pūjayati caṇḍikām || 126 ||

pūjā ca viphalā tasya devīśāpaḥ prajāyate |
dakṣiṇe vaṭuke deyaḥ uttare yoginībaliḥ || 127 ||

sarvabhūte baliṃ dadyāt kṣetrapālasya paścime |
rājarājeśvare madhye baliṃ dadyāt susādhakaḥ || 128 || 

dakṣiṇādicaturdikṣu pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ likhet |
prokṣaṇaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā vaṭukaṃ tatra cintayet || 129 ||


baṭukadhyānam


karakalitakapālaḥ kuṇḍalī daṇḍapāṇiḥ 
taruṇatimiranīlo vyālayajñopavītī |
kramasamayasaparyāvighnavicchedahetu-
rjayati vaṭukanāthaḥ siddhidaḥ sādhakānām || 130 ||

iti dhyātvā tu saṃpūjya pūrvoktabalimācaret |
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāñca baṭukasya baliṃ haret || 131 ||

p. 126)

viṣaṃ māyā ramā devīputravaṭukanāthataḥ |
piṅgalapadato jaṭābhāsvara procya piṅgala || 132 ||

trinetra ca imaṃ baliṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇayugantataḥ |
vahnijāyāntamuccārya vaṭukasya mahātmanaḥ || 133 ||

anena balimutsṛjya yāginīścintayettataḥ || 134 ||


yoginīdhyānakathanam


yoginyaḥ kāmarūpā nikhilaguṇatāstaptakāntasvabhāvā 
mattāḥ kaṅkālamālākalitakucataṭī raktavastrottarīyāḥ | 
liṅgaṃ pāśaṃ kapālaṃ śṛṇimapi dadhataḥ susmitāḥ suprasannāḥ 
bhaktānāṃ sādhakānāmabhilaṣitaphalaṃ dīyamānāḥ suveśāḥ || 135 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā prapūjyaiva paścādapi baliṃ dadet |
aṅguṣṭhamadhyamānāmā yoginībhyo baliṃ haret || 136 ||

tāraṃ lajjāṃ ramāṃ sarvasiddhiñca yoginībhyaḥ |
sarvābhyo mātṛkābhyaśca ityutsṛjya baliṃ dadet || 137 ||

p. 127)

viṣaṃ māyāṃ tataḥ sarvabhūtebhyaśca tataḥ param |
sarvabhūtapatiṃ procya bhyo namo'ntaṃ samuccaret || 138 ||

sarvabhūtabaliṃ dattā kṣetrapālabaliṃ haret |
tataḥ sañcintayet kṣetranāyakaṃ siddhihetave || 139 ||


kṣetrapāladhyānam


nirvāṇaṃ nirvikalpaṃ nirupamasakalaṃ nirvikāraṃ kṣakāraṃ 
hūṅkāraṃ vajradaṃṣṭraṃ hutavahavadanaṃ raudramunmattabhāvam |
phaṭkāraṃ baddhanāgaṃ bhrukuṭitaṭamukhaṃ bhairavaṃ śūlapāṇiṃ 
khaṭvāṅgaṃ vyomanīlaṃ ḍamarukasahitaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ namāmi || 140 ||


kṣetrapālabalipradānam


iti dhyātvā tu sampūjya baliṃ paścānnivedayet |
viśaṃ māyāṃ ramābījaṃ kṣakāraṃ bindubhūṣitam || 141 ||

ṣaḍdīrghasvarasambhinnaṃ svasthāna kṣetrapāla ca |
imaṃ baliṃ padasyānte pūjāṃ gṛhṇayugaṃ tataḥ || 142 ||

svāhāntamantramuccārya kṣetrapālabaliṃ diśet |
kṣetrapālabaliṃ dattvā madhye dadyāttataḥ param || 143 ||

ityutsṛjya baliṃ dadyādrājarājeśvarāya ca |
balipātrāmṛtenaiva tarpayedvaṭukādikam || 144 ||

p. 128)

ekatra vā pañcabaliṃ dadyādvyāpakamaṇḍale |
vāmabhāge sthite caiva sarvavighnaharo bhavet || 145 ||

dakṣiṇe vaṭukaṃ dadyāduttare yoginībalim |
pūrve bhūḥ baliṃ dadyāt kṣetrapālasya paścime || 146 ||

rājarājeśvaraṃ madhye baliṃ dadyāt susādhakaḥ || 147 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣaṣṭhollāsaḥ || 6 ||



saptama ullāsaḥ 


pūjāvidhiḥ


atha vakṣye ca pūjāyā vidhānaṃ yatra yadbhavet |
pūjādau ca japādau ca japasyānte tathaiva ca || 1 ||

pūjānte cāgnikāryānte prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret |
prāṇāyāmaṣaḍaṅgañca kṛtvā sādhakasattamaḥ || 2 ||

pīṭhapūjāṃ prakurvīta svakalpoktavidhānataḥ |
svakalpoktavidhānena dhyātvā devīṃ samāhitaḥ || 3 ||

hṛtsarojāt samānīya nāsāpuṭapathā sudhīḥ |
tejomayīṃ mahādevīṃ dīpāddīpāntaraṃ yathā || 4 ||

puṣpāñjalau tataḥ paścānmantramadhye samānayet |
āvāhanyādimudrāśca darśayettadanantaram || 5 ||


āvāhanyādimudrānirūpaṇam

1. āvāhanīmudrā
2. sthāpanīmudrā


ūrdhvāñjalimadhaḥ kuryādiyamāvāhanī bhavet |
iyaṃ tu viparītā cettadā vai sthāpanī bhavet || 6 ||

p. 130)


3. sannidhāpanīmudrā
4. sannirodhanīmudrā

militaṃ muṣṭiyugalaṃ sannidhāpanarūpiṇī |
antaraṅguṣṭhamuṣṭibhyāṃ sannirodhanarūpiṇī || 7 ||

5. avaguṇṭhanīmudrā

etasyā eva mudrāyāstarjanyau sarale yadi |
avaguṇṭhanamudreyamabhito bhrāmitā satī || 8 ||

6. sakalākṛtimudrā
7. paramīkaraṇīmudrā
8. dhenumudrā

devatāṅge ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ nyāsaḥ syāt sakalīkṛtiḥ |
anyo'nyagrathitāṅguṣṭhaprasāritakarāṅguliḥ || 9 ||

mahāmudreti kathitā paramīkaraṇe budhaiḥ |
parivṛtya karau paścāt tarjanīmadhyamāyugam || 10 ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikāyugmaṃ parasparayutaṃ kuru |
dhenumudreyamākhyātā amṛtīkaraṇe budhaiḥ || 11 ||

svasvakalpoktavidhinā mudrāḥ sandarśayettataḥ |

p. 131)

darśayitvā tato mudrāṃ tālatrayapuraḥsaram || 12 ||

choṭikābhirdigbandhanaṃ kṛtvā jīvaṃ nyasettataḥ | 
lelihāmudrayā yantraṃ spṛṣṭvā pañcakuśena vā || 13 ||


lelihāmudrā


tarjanīmadhyamānāmāḥ samaṃ kṛtvā adhomukham |
anāmāyāṃ kṣipedvṛddhāṃ ṛjuṃ kṛtvā kaniṣṭhikām || 14 ||

leliheti ca vikhyātā jīvanyāse prakīrtitā |
mūlamantreṇa dīptātmā arghyadravyodakena ca || 15 ||

trivāraṃ prokṣayeddhīmān devaśuddhiritīritā |
tataḥ sampūjayeddevīṃ ṣoḍaśairupacārakaiḥ || 16 ||


ṣoḍaśopacārapūjanam


āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanasnānavasanābharaṇāni ca || 17 ||

gandhapuṣpe tathā dhūpadīpanaivedyavandanāḥ |
(athavā) 
pādyārghyācamanasnānavasanābharaṇāni yaca || 18 ||

gandhapuṣpe dhūpadīpanaivedyācamanaṃ tataḥ | 
tāmbūlamarcanāstotraṃ tarpaṇañca tataḥ param || 19 ||

p. 132)

arghyaṃ pādyaṃ nivedyātha tathaivācamanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanañca gandhaprasūnake tataḥ || 20 ||

dhūpadīpau ca naivedyaṃ daśopacārakaṃ smṛtam |
gandhādayo naivedyāntaḥ pūjā pañcopacārikā || 21 ||


uttamādibhedena trividhā pūjā


uttamā madhyamā caiva budhairuktā'dhamā kramāt |
abhāve puṣpatoyābhyāṃ patreṇa tadabhāvataḥ || 22 ||

tadabhāve sarṣapeṇa śvetenā'pi viśeṣataḥ |
tadabhāve'kṣatenaiva dūrvayā tadabhāvataḥ || 23 ||

abhāve vāriṇā vāpi tadabhāve ca mānasaiḥ |
mūlānte devatānāma ṅeyutañca samuddharet || 24 ||

deyadravyapadollekhaṃ kṛtvā ca tadanantaram |
hṛdayena tu pādyañca śirasārghyaṃ nivedayet || 25 ||

vāruṇenācamanaṃ proktaṃ tenaiva madhuparkakam |
śikhāmantreṇa gandhañca puṣpaṃ netreṇa dāpayet || 26 ||

nivedayāmi vadane dadyāt sarvaṃ susādhakaḥ |
pādyādikaṃ nivedyātha snānīyañca nivedayet || 27 ||

p. 133)

devīsnānanirūpaṇam


tataśca pāduke dattvā surakte snānamandire | 
nānāratnasamāyuktasvarṇasiṃhāsanānvite || 28 ||

tatrānīya mahādevīṃ sādhakaḥ śuddhabhāvataḥ |
ghṛtenodvartanaṃ kāryaṃ manasā tadanantaram || 29 ||

sugandhidivyatailena devakanyāsahasrakaiḥ | 
sahasraiḥ svarṇakumbhaiśca sugandhajalapūritaiḥ || 30 ||

āsiñced bhaktibhāvena bhavānīṃ bhaktavatsalām |
nānātīrthodakaiḥ śuddhairgandharājasahasrakaiḥ || 31 ||

nāgakanyāsahasraistu kinnarairapsarogaṇaiḥ |
pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyaiḥ snāpayettadanantaram || 32 ||

nārikelodakenaiva tathekṣuvāriṇā tataḥ |
saptamṛttikayā yukta śītalena jalena ca || 33 ||

śītaloṣṇodakenaiva tathā ratnodakena ca |
suvāsitajalenaiva devyāḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 34 ||

sahasraśīrṣāmantreṇa pāvamānyā tathaiva ca |
bhṛṅgārāṣṭakakumbhaistu devyāḥ snānaṃ vidhāya ca || 35 ||

p. 134)

upacārāḥ

tataḥ śuddhairdukūlaiśca gātramārjanamācaret |
kārpāsaṃ vālkalaṃ vārkṣaṃ koṣajaṃ vastramiṣyate || 36 ||

raktakauṣeyavastrañca mahādevyai praśasyate |
sapītāmbarakauṣaṃ vā svadevāya ca utsṛjet || 37 ||

vicitraṃ sarvadevebhyo divyāṃśukaṃ nivedayet |
nīlaraktaṃ tu yadvastraṃ tatsarvatra vivarjayet || 38 ||

vicitrarāgavipulaṃ lagnahīnaṃ vivarjayet |
vastraṃ dadyānmahādevyai nānyasmai tu kadācana || 39 ||

raktavastraṃ raktamālyaṃ raktālaṅkaraṇaṃ tathā |
dattvā'ṅgalepanaṃ kākiryaṃ gandhacandanasihlakaiḥ || 40 ||

punaśca pāduke dattvā manorame susādhakaḥ |
yantramadhye samānīya namaskuryāt samāhitaḥ || 41 ||

p. 135)

punaḥ pādyādikaṃ dattvā pūjayedvidhipūrvakam |
yathotpannaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dadyācca patrakam || 42 ||

puṣpāñjalipradānena na bhavenniyamastvayam |
nādhomukhaṃ phalaṃ dadyāditi śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ || 43 ||


jñānamudrā


jñānākhyamudrayā caiva pūjayet parameśvarīm |
aṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ tu jñānamudrā prakīrtitā || 44 ||

madhyamānāmikābhyāṃ tu aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa sādhakaḥ |
dadyācca vimalaṃ gandhaṃ mūlamantreṇa pūrvavat || 45 ||

yathā gandhaṃ tathā caiva dhūpaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
madhyamānāmikābhyāṃ tu madhyaparvaṇi deśikaḥ || 46 ||

aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa dhūpañca dhṛtvā ca vinivedayet |
uttolanaṃ tridhā kṛtvā gāyatryā mūlayogataḥ || 47 ||

anāmikāṅguṣṭhayoge naivedyaṃ tu nivedayet |
anyat sarvaṃ sa vīraḥ pradadyājjñānamudrayā || 48 ||

dadhimadhughṛtenaiva madhuparkaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
pāyasaṃ kṛśaraṃ dadyāt pālyannaṃ madhusaṃyutam || 49 ||

p. 136)

piṣṭakaṃ modakañcaiva dugdhaṃ dadhi tathā phalam |
tāmbūlañca tato dadyāt karpūrādisuvāsitam || 50 ||

tatra tatra jalaṃ dadyādupacārāntarāntarā |
punarmālyaṃ pradātavyaṃ gandhavandanapaṅkajam || 51 ||

darpaṇaṃ cāmaraṃ chatraṃ pādukāñca nivedayet |
vidhivat pūjanaṃ kṛtvā tarpayettattvamudrayā || 52 ||


tattvamudrā


aṅguṣṭhānāmikāyogādvāmahastasya sarvadā |
aṅguṣṭhe bhairavo devo'nāmāyāṃ caṇḍikā sadā || 53 ||


tarpaṇavidhiḥ


anāmāṅguṣṭhayogena tarpayet kulasantatim |
aṅguṣṭhamadhyamābhyāñca vaśyakarmaṇi tarpayet || 54 ||

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena stambhane tarpayettataḥ |
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayogena tarpayedabhicārake || 55 ||

evaṃ santarpya deveśīṃ kuladravyairyathāvidhi |

p. 137)

tarpaṇañcāṣṭadhā kāryaṃ sakṛdvāpi yathecchayā || 56 ||

nārcayedekahastena tarpayennaikapāṇinā |
tarpayitvāmahādevīṃ svīyakramamathācaret || 57 ||

devatāmantikaṃ kuryāt kusumairvihitaiḥ sadā |
pūjākāle devatāyā nopari bhrāmayet karam || 58 ||

devyanujñāṃ gṛhītvā tu pūjayedaṅgadevatāḥ |
dhyātvā gurukulaṃ dhīro raśmivṛndaṃ tathaiva ca || 59 ||


aṅgadevatāpūjāvidhiḥ


sva sva sthāne samāvāhya pūjayettadanantaram |
vinā yo hi guroḥ pūjāmiṣṭadevaṃ yajed yadi || 60 ||

niṣkṛtirnāsti tasyaiva narakārṇavakoṭiṣu |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena gurupūjāṃ samācaret || 61 ||


gurupūjā


divyaughā guravo hyeva siddhaughā guravastathā |
mānavaughāḥ samāsena pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 62 ||

p. 138)

athavā sarvaśāstreṣu guravaḥ pūrvasūcitāḥ |
yeṣu yeṣu ca mantreṣu ye ye ṛṣigaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 63 ||

te te pūjyāḥ saparyādau saṅkṣepād gaditaṃ mayā |
ajñātvā gurunāmāni gurutritayamarcayet || 64 ||

catuṣṭayaṃ vā saṅkoco na ca kāryaṃ tataḥ param |
guruḥ paraguruścaiva parāparagurustataḥ || 65 ||

parameṣṭhiguruścaiva kathitā gurava śubhāḥ |
na jānāmi gurornāma vaṃśaśuddhiḥ kadācana || 66 ||

tvatpādāmbujareṇūnāṃ karṇamādehi dāsatām |
iti stavena saṃstutya tasminnijaguruṃ yadi || 67 ||

tannāmnā pūjayedyastu tena pūjā gurorbhavet |
gurupūjāṃ vinā caiva yadi pūjāṃ samācaret || 68 ||

taddoṣaśamane caiva kulapūjāṃ samācaret |
pūrvavattarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā raśmivṛndaṃ prapūjayet || 69 ||

yatra yatra sthitiścaiva tatra taṃ nārcayedyadi |
tanmāṃsarudhireṇaiva pāraṇaṃ tasya jāyate || 70 ||

ekapīṭhe pṛthak pūjāṃ vinā yantraṃ karoti yaḥ | 
aṅgāṅgitvaṃ parityajya devatāśāpamāpnuyāt || 71 ||

p. 139)

āvāhya devatāmekāmarcayaṃścānyadevatām |
ubhābhyāṃ labhate śāpaṃ mantrī cañcalamānasaḥ || 72 ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena svasvasthāne yathākramāt |
sampūjya devatāḥ sarvāstarpayettadanantaram || 73 ||

brahmāṇyādyaṣṭaśaktīṃśca pūjayitvā ca tarpayet |
punaḥ sampūjayed devīmupacāraiśca vistaraiḥ || 74 ||

āsanaṃ prathamaṃ dadyāt raupyaṃ dārujameva vā |
vastraṃ vā cārmaṇaṃ koṣaṃ maṇḍapasyottare sṛjet || 75 ||

pādyaṃ dattvā mahādevyai cārghyam dadyāttataḥ param |
śyāmā dūrvā paṅkajañca viṣṇukrāntāsamanvitam || 76 ||

gandhapuṣpākṣatajavākuśāgratilasarṣapaiḥ |
sadūrvaiḥ sarvadevānāmetadarghyamudīritam || 77 ||

kevalaṃ toyamātreṇa tadācamanamiṣyate |
vāsitantu sugandhādyaiḥ karttavyaṃ yadi labhyate || 78 ||

p. 140)

gandhāṣṭakam


arghyādikaṃ pradadyāttu gandhāṣṭakaṃ nivedayet |
candanāgurukarpūraṃ corakuṅkumarocanāḥ || 79 ||

jaṭāmāṃsī kapijaṭā śaktergandhāṣṭakaṃ viduḥ |
nānāvidhāni puṣpāṇi vasanābharaṇāni ca || 80 ||

mālāṃ dadyāttato devyai gandhacandanamiśritam |
sarvāṅgalepanaṃ dadyād dhūpaṃ dadyāttataḥ param || 81 ||


dhūpanirmāṇanirūpaṇam


uśīraṃ candanaṃ kuṣṭhamaguruṃ gugguluṃ tathā |
madhunā ghṛtasaṃyukto dhūpo'yaṃ caṇḍikāpriyaḥ || 82 ||

(athavā) 
rogarogahararogadakeśāḥ suratarujatulaghupatraviśeṣāḥ | 
vakratārahitavārijamudrā dhūpavartiriha sundari bhadrā || 83 ||

haritakīmustamatho nakhī ca māṃsī jatuśailajadevadāruḥ |
kuṣṭhaṃ guḍaḥ sarjarasastathaiva daśāṅgadhūpaḥ kathito munīndraiḥ || 84 ||

p. 141)

(athavā) 
aguruśīragugguluśarkarāmadhucandanaiḥ |
dhūpayedājyasaṃmiśrairnocairdevasya deśikaḥ || 85 ||

kevalaṃ guggulaṃ dhūpamaguruṃ vāpi sandiśet |
eṣāmekatamaṃ kṛtvā nivedya śāstravartmanā || 86 ||

na bhūmau sthāpayeddhūpamāsane ca ghaṭe'pi vā |


kuladīpadānavidhiḥ


ghṛtapradīpaṃ prathamaṃ tilatailodbhavaṃ tataḥ || 87 ||

sārṣapaṃ phalaniryāsajātaṃ vā vārijodbhavam |
praṇavaṃ jayadhvanipadaṃ mātaḥ svāhā tataḥ param || 88 ||

anena pūjayed ghaṇṭāmanena parivādayet |
taijasaṃ dārujaṃ vāpi ārttikyaṃ dīpavṛkṣakam || 89 ||

atra dīpaṃ pradātavyaṃ bhūmau nahi kadācana |
evaṃ dīpaṃ prakurvīta netrarañjanakārakam || 90 ||

ghṛtadīpaṃ dakṣiṇe tu svavāme tailadīpakam |
abhito dīpayenmantrī dīpamālāṃ ghṛtalpatām || 91 ||

p. 142)

karpūravartikāyuktāṃ tenaiva vā pariplutām |
varttā karpūragandhinyā sarpiṣā tilajena vā || 92 ||

āropya darśayeddīpānuccaiḥ saurabhaśaśālinaḥ |
dīpābaliṃ tato dattvā kuladīpaṃ nivedayet || 93 ||

sauvarṇe rājate kāṃsye sthānake kuṅkumena tu | 
dalāṣṭakayutaṃ padmaṃ vilikhya tatra sādhakaḥ || 94 ||

yavagodhūmadugdhādyaiḥ kṛtvendurūpakaṃ carum |
aṣṭapatreṣu madhye ca sthāpayitvā tataḥ param || 95 ||

navadīpaṃ kṣipettatra mūlamantreṇa mantrayet |
saptadhā tatra uttolya navadhā bhrāmayettataḥ || 96 ||

nirmañcayenmahādevīṃ mūlamantraṃ samuccaran |
tataśca sādhakaśreṣṭhaścakramudrāṃ pradarśayet || 97 ||


cakramudrālakṣaṇam


dakṣiṇetarahastasya vṛddhāgarbhakaniṣṭhikā |
dakṣiṇe yojayitvā tu kaniṣṭhe garbhake budhaḥ || 98 ||

vāme ca yadakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaṃ sādhako viniyojayet |
anyonyayogataścaiva cakramudrā prakīrtitā || 99 ||

p. 143)

pradattvā kuladīpañca naivedyaṃ tu pradāpayet |
taijaseṣu ca pātreṣu prastare vā'bjapatrake || 100 ||

yajñadārumaye vāpi naivedyaṃ kalpayed budhaḥ |
sarvābhāve tu māheye svahastaghaṭite yadi || 101 ||

na dadyāt vālkale lauhe datte narakamāpnuyāt |
yadyogyaṃ sarvapātre tu tannidhāya nivedayet || 102 ||

annatoyairyathā sṛṣṭamarghyapātrasthitetaraiḥ |
na gṛhṇāti mahādevī dattaṃ vidhiśatairapi || 103 ||

naivedyaṃ ṣaḍrasopetaṃ nānāvidhaphalaṃ tathā |
nānāvidhāni dravyāṇi dattvā devyai prayatnataḥ || 104 ||

madyaṃ nivedya māṃsañca matsyaṃ mudrāṃ tataḥ param |
dattvā devyai prayatnena annaṃ bahuvidhaṃ tathā || 105 ||

vividhaṃ vyañjanaṃ ramyaṃ kaṣāyaṃ tiktasaṃyutam |
kaṭvamlamadhurañcaiva paramānnaṃ saśarkaram || 106 ||

p. 144)

śarkarādugdhakhaṇḍādibhāvitaṃ piṣṭakaṃ tathā |
dugdhaṃ dadhi tathā takraṃ navanītaṃ saśarkaram || 107 ||

śarkarāmodakaṃ dadyādanyaṃ nānāvidhaṃ tathā |
carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ lehyapeyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ nivedayet || 108 ||

aśaktau manasā dadyādetat karma na lopayet |
cullukañca tato dattvā prāṇamudrāṃ pradarśayet || 109 ||


prāṇādipañcamudrā


vṛddhānāmākaniṣṭhābhiḥ prāṇamudrā samīritā |
vṛddhāmadhyātarjanībhirapānasya prakīrtitā || 110 ||

vṛddhānāmāmadhyamābhirvyānamudrā pradarśitā |
kaniṣthābhinnasarvābhirudānasya samīritā || 111 ||

samānamudrā sarvābhiraṅgulībhiḥ pradarśitā |
prāṇāpānau tathā vyāna udānaśca samānakaḥ || 112 ||

caturthyanto'gnijāyāntastārādyo mudrikāmanuḥ |
vāmahaste prāsamudrā prāsavat parikīrtitā || 113 ||

p. 145)

punarācamanīyañca dattvā devyai susādhakaḥ |
svayambhūkusumaṃ kuṇḍagolotthaṃ rocanāgurum || 114 ||

kastūrīkuṅkumāraktacandanāni pradāpayet |
gandhapuṣpaṃ tathā mālyaṃ raktapuṣpādibhūṣaṇam || 115 ||

sindūraṃ kajjalaṃ dadyādalaktaṃ paṭṭagucchakam |
droṇāparājitāpuṣpaṃ karavīraṃ javāṃ tathā || 116 ||

prayatnena pradātavyaṃ caṇḍikāprītikārakam |
datte caiva javāpuṣpe paṭṭavastraphalaṃ labhet || 117 ||

aparāyāśca māhātmyaṃ vaktuṃ nahi kadācana |
tāmbūlañca tato dadyāt karpūrādisuvāsitam || 118 ||

punarmālyaṃ pradātavyaṃ gandhacandanasaṃyutam | 
darpaṇaṃ pādukāṃ chatraṃ cāmaraṃ vyajanādikam || 119 ||

ajādibalidānena caṇḍikāṃ toṣayettataḥ | 


svahastena paśuccheda niṣedhaḥ


yatnatastu baliṃ dadyānna hanyādātmanā punaḥ || 120 ||

p. 146)

svahastairna paśuṃ hanyāt svato hiṃsāṃ vivarjayet | 
yadi hanyāt pramādena rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet || 121 ||

chāgādīni baliṃ dattvā dhūpadīpaṃ nivedayet |
gṛhītvā śoṇitaṃ pātre svakīyaṃ hṛdayodbhavam || 122 ||

toyasaindhavasaṃyuktaṃ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam |
pradadyācca mahādevyai sarvasaubhāgyahetave || 123 ||

abhyukṣya rudhiraṃ dhīro vāgbhavena pradāpayet |
śaktibījena vā dadyānnābherūrdhvamadho nahi || 124 ||

puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ dattvā tarpayennijadevatām |
tataśca sādhakaśreṣṭho mantraśuddhiṃ mācaret || 125 ||

gṛhītvā mātṛkāvarṇaṃ mūlamantrākṣarāṇi ca |
kramāt kramād dvirāvṛttirmantraśuddhiritīritā || 126 ||


vācikādibhedena trividho japaḥ


ādau mantraviśuddhārthaṃ japaṃ kuryāttataḥ param |
japañca trividhaṃ proktaṃ vācikopāṃśumānasam || 127 ||

p. 147)

jihvauṣṭhādharasaṃsphūrtterjapo yaśca prajāyate |
tajjapaṃ vācikaṃ viddhi jihvāmātramupāṃśukam || 128 ||

manasā mantravarṇasya cintanaṃ mānasaṃ smṛtam | 
vācikasya śataṃ jñeyaṃ ekottaramupāṃśunā || 129 ||

tasmācchataguṇaṃ proktaṃ mānasaṃ japamuttamam | 
(athavā) 
nigadaśca tathā jñeyaḥ spaṣṭavācā nigadyate || 130 ||

avyaktastu sphuradvaktra upāṃśuḥ parikīrttitaḥ |
mānasaśca sa boddhavyaścintanākṣararūpavān || 131 ||

nigadena tu yajjaptaṃ lakṣamātraṃ susādhakaiḥ |
upāṃśusmaraṇenaiva tulyaṃ bhavati sarvathā || 132 ||

apāṃśulakṣamātraṃ tu yajjaptaṃ sādhakena ca |
mānasoccāraṇāttulyaṃ jāyate sādhakasya ca || 133 ||

prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṅgañca kṛtvā ṛṣyādikañcaret | 

p. 148)

svaguruṃ mūrdhni sañcintya hṛdi devīṃ tataḥ param || 134 ||

svayaṃ kāmakalārūpaṃ bhāvayan japamārabhet |
japasyādau śivāṃ dhyāyet dhyānasyānte punarjapet || 135 ||

japadhyānasamāyuktaḥ śīghraṃ sidhyati sādhakaḥ |
dhyānaṃ kṛtvā japenmantraṃ guruktakramato budhaḥ || 136 ||

sarve mantrāḥ siddhidāḥ syuḥ satyameva na saṃśayaḥ |
tataḥ sampūjayenmālāṃ toyairabhyukṣya yatnataḥ || 137 ||


mālāpūjā


oṃ hrīṁ akṣamālāyai hṛnmantreṇa prapūjayet |
oṃ māle māle mahāmāle sarvasiddhisvarūpiṇi || 138 ||

caturvargastvayi nyastastasmānme siddhidā bhava |


mālājapavidhiḥ


pūjayitvā tato mālāṃ gṛhītvā dakṣiṇe kare || 139 ||

hṛtsamīpe samānīya na tu vāmena saṃspṛśet |
vinā yantrañca mantrañca kavacaṃ mantraśūnyataḥ || 140 ||

mālāñca vāmahastena mṛtyucihnasya lakṣaṇam |
saṃkhyā kṛtā mṛttikā ca akṣatañca tathaiva ca || 141 ||

p. 149)

madhyamāyā madhyabhāge sthāpayitvā samāhitaḥ |
tarjanyā na spṛśenmālāmaṅguṣṭhāgreṇa cālayet || 142 ||

pārśvayoḥ setumādāya japakarma samācaret |
niḥsetuśca yathā toyaṃ kṣaṇānnimnaṃ prasarpati || 143 ||

mantrastathaiva praṇavo dvijānāṃ seturīritaḥ |
śūdrāṇāṃ candrabindustha aukāraḥ seturucyate || 144 ||

śūdrāṇāmādiseturvā dviseturvā yathecchayā |
dvisetavaḥ samākhyātāḥ sarvathaiva dvijātayaḥ || 145 ||

yathā mantraṃ tathā stotraṃ kavacañca tathāvidham |
setuṃ vinā bhavedvayarthaṃ tasmāt setuṃ samācaret || 146 ||

pratyekaṃ bījamādāya aṅgādūrdhvena mantravit |
pūrvabījaṃ japan yastu parabījantu saṃspṛśet || 147 ||

aṅguṣṭhena vinā yasya niṣphalaḥ sa japaḥ sadā |
evamuktavidhānena vilambaṃ tvaritaṃ tyajet || 148 ||

meruṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya prajapet sādhakottamaḥ |
gaṇanāvidhimullaṅghya yo japettajjapaṃ punaḥ || 149 ||

p. 150)

gṛhṇanti rākṣasāstena gaṇayet sarvadā budhaḥ |
athavā karamālāyāṃ mātṛkāyāṃ tathaiva ca || 150 ||

nityaṃ japaṃ kare kuryānna tu kāmyaṃ kadācana |
nityajape ca home ca yatra saṃkhyā na coditā || 151 ||


japasaṃkhyā


tatreyaṃ gaṇanā proktā aṣṭottarasahasrakam |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi triṃśadvāpi kadācana || 152 ||

triṃśannyūnaṃ na kuryācca nityajāpe ca sādhakaḥ |
yathāśakti japaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya ca || 153 ||


japasamarpaṇaṃ


gopayitvā tato mālāṃ saprasūnārghyavāriṇā |
tejomayaṃ japaphalaṃ devyā haste samarpayet || 154 ||


praṇāmavidhiḥ


guhyātīti samarpyātha stuvan ghaṇṭāñca vādayet |
trividhena natiṃ kuryādaṣṭāṅgakavidhānakaiḥ || 155 ||

p. 151)

cibukena mukhenātha nāsayā mastakena ca |
brahmarandhreṇa karṇābhyāṃ yadbhūmeḥ sparśanaṃ bhavet || 156 ||

(athavā) 
uraḥ śiroddaṣṭijānumanaścaraṇavākkaraiḥ | 
aṣṭāṅganamanaṃ jñeyaṃ nityahomaṃ tataḥ param || 157 ||

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ homaṃ sthaṇḍileṣu samācaret |
na kuryāt kāmyahomaṃ vai vinā kuṇḍena mantravit || 158 ||

svadakṣiṇe likheddhastapramāṇaṃ caturasrakam |
aṅgulotsedhasaṃyuktaṃ vālukābhiḥ samantataḥ || 159 ||

tatra madhye ca ṣaṭkoṇaṃ likhitvā prokṣya pūjayet |
ādhāraśaktimabhyarcya pūrvavacca ṣaḍaṅgakam || 160 ||

tato vahniṃ samujjvālya kravyādaṃśaṃ parityajet |
śabdāstramanunā mantrī nair-ṛtyāṃ jvaladagnikam || 161 ||

ātmano'bhimukhaṃ kṛtvā vahniṃ saṃsthāpayed budhaḥ |
agniṃ prajvālya mūlena jvālinīñca pradarśayet || 162 ||

p. 152)

jvālinīmudrā


maṇibandhau samau kṛtvā karau tu prasṛtāṅgulī |
madhyame milite kṛtvā tanmadhye'ṅguṣṭhakau kṣipet || 163 ||

iyaṃ syājjvālinī mudrā paramā homakarmaṇi |
agniṃ sampūjayitvā tu śivaśaktisamāgamāt || 164 ||

vahnimadhye samāvāhya iṣṭadevīṃ samāhitaḥ |
āvāhanyādikā mudrā darśayitvā tu rakṣayet || 165 ||

sampūjya ca mahādevīṃ havyaṃ saṃśodhayettataḥ |
saṃsthāpya havanīyañca pūrvavat prokṣaṇañcaret || 166 ||

caturdhā śodhanaṃ kṛtvā ājyasthālī tu sandiśet |
pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā pūjayet pūrvavartmanā || 167 ||

atra pātraṃ vinikṣipya havyaṃ tatra nidhāpayet |
daśadhā mūlamantreṇa abhimantrya hunettataḥ || 168 ||

praṇavaṃ bhūstataḥ svāhā idamagniñca ṅeyutam |
tāraṃ bhuvo vahnijāyā idañca vāyave padam || 169 ||

oṃ svaḥ svāhā idaṃ procya prajādhipataye tataḥ |
ityāhutitrayaṃ hutvā mahāvyāhṛtisāṅkam || 170 ||

havye vahnau tathā devyāṃ ātmanyaikyaṃ vibhāvayet |

p. 153)

ṣaḍaṅgamanunā ṣaṭkamāhutiṃ juhuyāttataḥ || 171 ||

mūlānte devatānāma ṅeyuktañca samuccaret |
tataḥ svāhā samuccārya aṣṭottarasahasrakam || 172 ||

haviṣā ca huneddhīra tilena palanena vā |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi tadarddhaṃ vāpi sādhakaḥ || 173 ||

athavāṣṭadaśaṃ kuryānnityahome tvayaṃ vidhiḥ |
māṃsena madhunā cājyairmatsyaiḥ puṣpaistilaistathā || 174 ||

yavairdhānyaiśca mudrābhiḥ kulapuṣpaistathā phalaiḥ |
apāmārgairbilvapatrairbhṛṅgarājaiḥ suśobhanaiḥ || 175 ||


puṣpanāmāni


karavīrairjavāpuṣpairaparādroṇakiṃśukaiḥ |
padmaiśca kumudaiḥ kundairnīlaraktotpalaistathā || 176 ||

saugandhikaiśca bandhūkaiḥ keśaraiścampakaistathā |
jātībhirmālatībhiśca mallikābhiḥ kadambakaiḥ || 177 ||

manoramaiḥ prasūnaiśca phalaiḥ patrairyathāvidhi |
anena vidhinā mantrī devyā homaṃ samāpya ca || 178 ||


homasamāptiḥ


pañcaprāṇāhutīrmūlaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ ṣaḍahutoḥ |
guruvṛndaṃ raśmivṛndaṃ samāvāhya hunettataḥ || 179 ||

p. 154)

kramādekāhutiṃ hutvā pratyekena susādhakaḥ |
āhutitritayaṃ hutvā punardevyai prayatnataḥ || 180 ||

punarācamanīyaṃ tu dattvā vahnau samānayet |
saṃhāramudrayā devīṃ oṃ agnaye tataḥ param || 181 ||

vahnijāyāntamantreṇa āhutitritayaṃ hunet |
visṛjyāgniñca devīñca śaṅkhaghaṇṭāñca vādayet || 182 ||

tarpayitvā tato devīṃ cakrasthāṃ parameśvarīm |
devyaṅgamūrdhni saṃhṛtvā śeṣaṃ karma samācaret || 183 ||


nivedita pañcatattvagrahaṇaphalakathanam


tataśca kaulikaiḥ sārddhaṃ pānaṃ kuryāt kulāmṛtam |
pañcatattvaṃ mahādevyai dattvā na svīkaroti yaḥ || 184 ||

naivedyādi tathā so'pi devīnāṃ śāpabhāgbhavet |
rogī duḥkhī daridraśca bhūtvā narakamāpnuyāt || 185 ||


pānasya traividhyam


ekākī nācaret pānaṃ kaulikairmāyayā saha |
pānañca trividhaṃ proktaṃ divyavīrapaśukramāt || 186 ||

p. 155)

divyaṃ devyagrataḥ pānaṃ vīramekāntavāsinām |
saṃskārādivihīnaṃ yattat pānaṃ pāśavaṃ smṛtam || 187 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye saptamollāsaḥ || 7 ||



aṣṭama ullāsaḥ 

annapūrṇāyāḥ pūjādhyānādi vidhānam


kramaṃ tasya pravakṣyāmi caturvargapradāyakam |
annamadhye tvaṣṭapatraṃ padmaṃ nirmāya tatra vai || 1 ||

kāraṇaiḥ pūrṇapātraṃ tu saṃsthāpya tatra sādhakaḥ |
ṣaḍdīrghamāyayā caiva ṣaḍaṅganyāsamācaret |
tato dhyāyedannapūrṇāṃ paramānandadāyinīm || 2 ||


annapūrṇādhyānakathanam


ādāya dakṣiṇakareṇa suvarṇadarvīn 
dugdhānna pūrṇāmitareṇa ca ratnapātram |
bhikṣānnadānaniratāṃ nava hemavarṇā 
mambāṃ bhaje sakalabhūṣaṇabhūṣitāṅgīm || 3 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā mahādevīṃ tatrāvāhya susādhakaḥ |
tattvamudrāṃ pradarśyātha idamannāmṛtaṃ vadet || 4 ||

pūrayadvandvamuccārya namaḥ svāhā tataḥ param |

p. 157)

tatra santarpayedbhaktyā annapūrṇā yajettataḥ || 5 ||

tārañca bhuvaneśānīṃ śrībījaṃ kāmabījakam | 
hṛdante bhagavatyante māheśvarīpadaṃ likhet || 6 ||

annapūrṇe agnijāyā tvannapūrṇāmanūttamaḥ |
yathāśakti japaṃ kṛtvā stutvā devīṃ samāhitaḥ || 7 ||

arcayitvā tu deveśīṃ kaulikānnārcayanti ye |
pāpiṣṭhāste caṇḍikāyāḥ prasādabhājanaṃ nahi || 8 ||

naivedyaṃ purato nyastaṃ darśanāt svīkṛtaṃ tvayā |
sādhubhaktasya jihvāyāmaśnāmi kamalekṣaṇe || 9 ||

vasanti tasya jihvāyāṃ śivaśaktyādidevatāḥ |
tasmāt kaulikavaktreṣu hotavyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ || 10 ||

caṇḍikāyāḥ priyākāṅkṣo devībhaktān yajet sadā |
kaulānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye yathātantrānusārataḥ || 11 ||


kaulalakṣaṇam


nivṛttaduḥkhasantuṣṭā vidyāgamavimatsarāḥ |

p. 158)

kulajñānaratāḥ śāntāḥ śivabhaktā dṛḍhavratāḥ || 12 ||

kīrtyamāne kule yeṣāṃ romāñcagadgadasvarāḥ |
ānandāśruyutā vīrāḥ kathitāḥ kaulikeśvarāḥ || 13 ||

sarvadharmo'dhiko loke kuladharmaḥ śivoditaḥ |
iti ye niścitadhiyaḥ proktāste kaulikottamāḥ || 14 ||


kulārcanapraśaṃsā


yo bhavet kulatattvajñaḥ kulaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
kulārcanaratapuṃsāṃ kaulikaḥ so'dhikaḥ sadā || 15 ||

kulabhaktān kulācārān kulajñānān kulavratān |
prīto bhavati yo dṛṣṭvā kaulikaḥ sa śivapriyaḥ || 16 ||

pañcatattvasvīkaraṇāt mūlamantrārthatattvavit |
devatāgurubhaktaścet kaulikaḥ syācca dīkṣayā || 17 ||

durlabhaṃ sarvalokeṣu kulācāryasya darśanam | 
yaḥ śaktīḥ svayamāhūya kulābhijñāṃstathaiva ca || 18 ||

abhyarcya devatābuddhyā gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ | 
mādibhiḥ pañcamudrābhiḥ sadbhaktyā paritoṣayet || 19 ||

p. 159)

teṣu tuṣṭeṣu santuṣṭāḥ śivādyāḥ sarvadevatāḥ |
kulajñānī vased yatra sa deśaḥ puṇyabhājanaḥ || 20 ||

darśanādarcanāttasya trisaptakulamuddharet |
kulajñāninamālokya svagṛhe'nyagṛhe sthitam || 21 ||

samāyānti mudā tatra yoginyo yogibhiḥ saha |
praviśya kulayogīśaṃ bhuñjate pitṛdevatāḥ || 22 ||

yat kṛtaṃ kulaniṣṭhānāṃ taddevānāṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet |
devāḥ kulapriyāḥ sarve tasmāt kaulikamarcayet || 23 ||

brahmendrācyutarudrādidevatā munirākṣasāḥ |
kuladharmaparā hyete mānuṣeṣu ca kā kathā || 24 ||

na tuṣṭā caṇḍikā'nyatra tathātaḥ syāt supūjitā |
kaulikendre'rcite samyag yathā tuṣṭā hi caṇḍikā || 25 ||

p. 160)

yat phalaṃ kaulikendrāṇāṃ pūjanāllabhate sudhīḥ |
tatphalaṃ nāpnuyāttirthaistapovratamakhaiḥ sadā || 26 ||


tannindāyāṃ doṣaḥ


pūjanājjapanāddhomāt sukṛtaṃ vividhaṃ tathā |
kaulikasya bhaved vyarthaṃ kaulikaṃ yo'vamānayet || 27 ||

śmaśānaṃ tadgṛhaṃ viddhi pāpiṣṭhaḥ śvapacādhamaḥ |
kulaniṣṭhaṃ parityajya yaccānyasmai pradīyate || 28 ||

taddānaṃ niṣphalaṃ vidyāddātā'pi narakaṃ vrajet |
pāpācārasamāyuktaṃ sarvalokabahiṣkṛtam || 29 ||

trāyate hi kuladravyaṃ kulayogīśvarārpitam |
kaulikendraḥ sakṛdbhuṅkte puṇyaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 30 ||


kuladharmapraśaṃsā


kiṃ punarbahubhirbhuktaistat puṇyaṃ naiva gaṇyate |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena sarvāvasthāsu sarvathā || 31 ||

kuladharmaparo bhūtvā kulajñāninamarcayet |
nandanti pitaraḥ sarve gāyanti ca pitāmahāḥ || 32 ||

p. 161)

prapitāmahāśca nṛtyanti kaulike gṛhamāgate |
yajñakoṭiphalaṃ tasya vājapeyaśatāni ca || 33 ||

dadāti kaulikebhyaśca bindumātrālisaṅgamāt |
pṛthivī hemapūrṇā ca kalpakoṭipradānataḥ || 34 ||

tatphalaṃ sparśanāj jñānāt kaulike yāgamandire |
kaulikeṣu prasanneṣu prasannā caṇḍikā bhavet || 35 ||

saṃsmṛtaḥ kīrtito dṛṣṭo vandito bhāṣito'pi vā |
punāti kuladharmiṣṭhaścaṇḍālo'pi yadṛcchayā || 36 ||

sarvajño vāpi mūrkho vā uttamo vā'dhamo'pi vā |
sarvathaiva śivaḥ so'pi tasmādyatnāt samarcayet || 37 ||

sa dhanyaḥ khalu lokeṣu puruṣaḥ kṣīṇakalmaṣaḥ |
yatsamīpaṃ samāyānti kulācāryāśca sādhakāḥ || 38 ||

atha kaulān samānīya śaktīścaiva viśeṣataḥ |
svaśaktiṃ vīraśaktiṃ vā dīkṣitāṃ gṛhiṇīmatha || 39 ||

p. 162)

pāyayitvā pibed dravyamiti śāstrasya nirṇayaḥ |
anivedya tu yaḥ śaktau kuladravyaṃ niṣevate || 40 ||

pūjā ca viphalā tasya devatā na prasīdati |
vinā yantreṇa yā pūjā vinā māṃsena tarpaṇam || 41 ||

vinā śaktyā tu yatpānaṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet |
āhūto yo nivarteta anāhūto'pi yo viśet || 42 ||

cakramadhye sa pāpī syāt sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ | 
tiraścāṃ yonimālambya narakān pratipadyate || 43 ||


cakravidhiḥ


pūjāsthāne prayatnena āsanāni pradāpayet | 
tataḥ kaulāḥ striyaḥ sarvā prakṣālya pāṇipādakam || 44 ||

praṇamya praviśeccakraṃ mudrābhiḥ pañcasañjñakaiḥ |
strīṇāmanyatamaṃ sthānaṃ puṃsāmanyatamaṃ pṛthak || 45 ||

athavā mithunaṃ kṛtvā kramāt samupaveśayet |
paṅktyākāreṇa vā samyak cakrākāreṇa vā'thavā || 46 ||

p. 163)

cakramadhye tu mūḍhātmā jātibhedaṃ karoti yaḥ |
taṃ bhakṣayanti yoginyastaṃ śapeccaṇḍikā sadā || 47 ||


cakramadhye jātibhedaniṣedhaḥ


jātibhedo na cakre'smin sarve śivasamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
pravṛtte bhairavīcakre sarve varṇā dvijātayaḥ || 48 ||

nivṛtte bhairavīcakre sarve varṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
rahasyamārganiratā ye ye varṇā bhavanti hi || 49 ||

te te viprāḥ sarvathaiva satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
svargādipuṇyalokeṣu devādanyo yathā na hi || 50 ||

tathaiva cakramadhye tu devāḥ sarve hi mānavāḥ |
vede'pi sthitamevaṃ hi sarvaṃ brahmeti cābravīt || 51 ||

bahunātra kimuktena cakramadhye tu sādhakāḥ | 
puruṣāḥ śivarūpāśca devīrūpāḥ striyaḥ sadā || 52 ||

śivaśaktidhiyā sarvāścakramadhye samarcayet |
saṃskārājjyeṣṭhakramato lalāṭe gandhacandanaiḥ || 53 ||

śaktīnāṃ sādhakānāñca tilakañca sahākṣatam |
kṛtvā vilepanaṃ mālyaṃ pradadyāt sādhakottamaḥ || 54 ||

p. 164)

alipānavidhiḥ


na sthūlaṃ na hi sūkṣmañca pātraṃ kṛtvā manoramam |
nayanāgnivāṇasaṃkhyātaiḥ karṣantu sādhakena ca || 55 ||

hetupānaṃ prakartavyamityuktaṃ kulasādhane | 
ityapyadhikapānaṃ tu na kartavyaṃ tu sādhakaiḥ || 56 ||

karābhyāṃ pātramuddhṛtya dvitīyañca yathākramāt |
tataḥ śrīgurupādāya sākṣāt paraśivāya ca || 57 ||

samāṃsaṃ devatābuddhyā samarpayedyathākramāt |
dattvāmṛtarasaṃ pūrṇaṃ gurave tadanantaram || 58 ||

yoṣidbhyastvavaśeṣaṃ tu vīrebhyaśca tataḥ param |
kulajāya kulīnāya kulabhaktāya dāpayet || 59 ||

gurubhyo gurupātrañca śaktibhyaḥ śaktipātrakam |
vīrebhyo vīrapātrañca bhogapātraṃ svayaṃ pibet || 60 ||

sākṣādyadi gururna syāttadā toye visarjayet |
sudhābuddhyā tataḥ sarve gṛhītvā pātramuttamam || 61 ||

p. 165)

dhyānaṃ kuryāt sudhādevyā yathāśāstrapramāṇataḥ |


sudhādevīdhyānam


samudre madhyamāne tu kṣīrābdhau sāgarottame || 62 ||

tatrotpannāṃ sudhāṃ devīṃ kanyakārūpadhāriṇīm |
gomūtrasadṛśākārāṃ phenāmṛtasamudbhavām || 63 ||

aṣṭādaśabhujairyuktāṃ nīrajāyatalocanām | 
ānandaśikhare jāta ānandaśca maheśvaraḥ || 64 ||

tayoryogād bhaved brahmā viṣṇuśca śiva eva ca |
tasmādimāṃ surādevīṃ pūrṇo'haṃ tāṃ bhajāmyaham || 65 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā mūlamantraṃ saptavāraṃ japettataḥ |
tasyopari tato vīraḥ pañca mudrāḥ pradarśayet || 66 ||

dhenuyoniṃ tataḥ paścāddarśayecca punaḥ punaḥ |
tālatrayachoṭikābhiḥ kurvīta dikṣu bandhanam || 67 ||

pūrvavattarpayet mūrdhni ānandabhairavaṃ tathā |
ānandabhairavīṃ devīṃ gurupaṅktiṃ prayatnataḥ || 68 ||

iṣṭadevaṃ raśmivṛndaṃ gaṇeśaṃ kṣetrapālakam |
vaṭukaṃ bhairavañcaiva yoginīṃ ḍākinīṃ tathā || 69 ||

mahābhūtān vighnadāṃśca tarpayecca yathākramāt |

p. 166)

purṇābhiṣekahīnānāṃ pāne nādhikāraḥ


ayaṣṭvā bhairavaṃ devamakṛtvā gurutarpaṇam || 70 ||

paśupānavidhau pītvā vīro'pi narakaṃ vrajet | 
gurupaṅktīrasantarpyaraśmivṛndaṃ tathaiva ca || 71 ||

yaḥ pibet kauliko dravyaṃ tasya pānaṃ tu pāśavam |
kaulajñānaṃ vinā yo'pi taddravyaṃ bhauktumicchati || 72 ||

sa mahāpātakī bhūyāt sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ | 
seveta madyamāṃsāni bhraṣṭaśca pātakī bhavet || 73 ||

mantrārthasmaraṇārthaṃ tu manasaḥ sthairyahetave |
bhedapāśavinirmukto madyapānaṃ samācaret || 74 ||

seveta yaḥ sukhārthāya madyādīni sa pātakī |
pāśayeddevatāprītyai tattvamāṃsāni sādhakaḥ || 75 ||

pañcamudrāṃ niṣeveta anyathā pātakī bhavet |
yāgakālaṃ vinā'nyatra dūṣaṇaṃ jāyate sadā || 76 ||

p. 167)

yaḥ śāstravidhimutsṛjya vartate kāmacārataḥ |
na sa siddhimavāpnoti paratra na parāṃ gatim || 77 ||

svecchayā vartamāno yo dīkṣāsaṃskāravarjitaḥ |
na tasya sadgatiḥ kā'pi tapasā sukṛtādibhiḥ || 78 ||

pūrṇābhiṣekayuktānāṃ pānamatyantamiṣyate |
pūrṇābhiṣekahīnānāṃ pānamatyantadurgatiḥ || 79 ||

akṛtvā kaulikācāramayaṣṭvā gurupādukām |
ye'smin cakre pravartante teṣāṃ hiṃsanti bhairavāḥ || 80 ||

adīkṣitairanācārairmudrāmantravivarjitaiḥ | 
japapūjāṃ vinā naiva na kāryaṃ dravyabhakṣaṇam || 81 ||

bhakṣaṇānnarakaṃ yāti niṣkṛtirnaiva jāyate |
mantrasaṃskāraśuddhātmā alipānaṃ samācaret || 82 ||

jāyate devatābhāvo bhavabandhavimocakaḥ |
vicārayettato vīraḥ sattarkeṇa viśeṣataḥ || 83 ||

p. 168)

dharmaśāstroktapānaniṣedhavicāraḥ


siddhānnaṃ bhuñjate viprāścūrṇaṃ śambūkasambhavam |
kāñjikaṃ kāsamardañca tathaiva ca śivāghṛtam || 84 ||

eteṣu yadi na doṣastadā sarvaṃ na doṣabhāk | 
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohādagnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet || 85 ||

iti manvādiśāstreṣu niṣedhaḥ pariniścitaḥ |
mohādajñānataḥ pītvā prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || 86 ||

yatpāne dūṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tadaiva hi |
devān pitṝn samabhyarcya vedaśāstroktavartmanā || 87 ||

jñānato bhairavīcakre jñānināṃ naiva dūṣaṇam |
guruṃ smaran pibanmadyaṃ khādanmāṃsaṃ na doṣabhāk || 88 ||

na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
yathā kratuṣu viprāṇāṃ somapānaṃ vidhīyate || 89 ||

madyapānaṃ tathā kāryaṃ samaye bhogamokṣadam |
phalārthināmahaṅkāravatāṃ dūṣaṇameva hi || 90 ||

p. 169)

nirahaṅkāriṇāñcaiva na niṣedho na vā vidhiḥ |
pānerbhrāntirbhaved yasya ghṛṇā syādraktaretasoḥ || 19 ||

śucau cā'śuddhatābhrāntiḥ pāpaśaṅkā ca maithune |
sa bhraṣṭaḥ pūjayeccaṇḍīṃ devīmantraṃ kathaṃ japet || 92 ||

rogī duḥkhī bhavet so'pi rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |
tasmādadvaitabhāvena sādhakaḥ pañcamaṃ bhajet || 93 ||


cakrānuṣṭhānam

pātraṃ brahmā surā viṣṇū rasādo rudra eva ca |
ānandaḥ śekharaḥ prokta ānandastu sadāśivaḥ || 94 ||

madyaṃ tu bhairavo devo madaḥ śaktiḥ samīritā |
aho bhuktañca madyaṃ hi mohayet tridaśānapi || 95 ||


surā śaktiḥ śivo māṃsaṃ

surā śaktiḥ śivo māṃsaṃ tadbhoktā bhairavaḥ svayam |
tayoraikyasamutpannamānandaṃ mokṣa ucyate || 96 ||

ānandaṃ brahmaṇo rūpaṃ tacca dehe pratiṣṭhitam |

p. 170)

tasyābhivyañjakaṃ madyaṃ yogibhistena pīyate || 97 ||

evaṃ vicintya matimān tattvaśodhanamācaret | 
pūrvavadvidhinā vīraḥ pātrasya vandanaṃ caret || 98 ||


prathamapātravandanamantraḥ

śrīmadbhairavaśekharapravilasaccandrāmṛtāplāvitaṃ kṣetrādhīśvarayoginīgaṇamahāsiddhaiḥ samāsevitam |
ānandārṇavakaṃ mahātmakamidaṃ sākṣāttrikhaṇḍāmṛtaṃ 
vande śrīprathamaṃ karāmbujagataṃ pātraṃ viśuddhipradam || 99 ||

vandayitvā tataḥ pātraṃ paṭhet bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
jānatā'jānatā vāpi yanmayā kriyate śive || 100 ||

tava kṛtyamidaṃ sarvamiti matvā kṣamasva me |
iti stutvā mahādevīmanyonya vandanañcaret || 101 ||

anujñāṃ purato labdhvā gṛhṇāmīti svayaṃ vadet |
juṣasvetyanujñāto guruṇā vā kulīnakaiḥ || 102 ||

p. 171)

cakrānuṣṭhānam

gṛhṇīyācca svayaṃ siddho baddhapadmāsanaḥ sudhīḥ |
savyenoddhṛtya pātrañca mudrāṃ kṛtvā'pasavyataḥ || 103 ||

vilāsāddhanabhogena na kuryāddravyabhakṣaṇam |
noddharedekahastena na pibedekapāṇinā || 104 ||

tataḥ sañcintayed devīṃ prakṛtiṃ paramāṃ kalām |
ātmamūlatrikoṇasthe koṭisūryasamaprabhe || 105 ||

kuṇḍalīśakticidvahnau huned dravyaṃ samantrakam |
saśabdaṃ na pibeddravyaṃ na binduṃ pātayedadhaḥ || 106 ||

saśabdaṃ yo mukhaṃ kuryāt kuladevyāstu tarpaṇe |
rauravaṃ narakaṃ yāti yāvadāhutasaṃplavam || 107 ||

vinā carvyeṇa yatpānaṃ kevalaṃ viṣavarddhanam |
tasmāt pracarvayeccarvyaṃ yathākramavidhānataḥ || 108 ||

ekāsane niviṣṭā ye bhuñjānāścaikabhājane |
ekapātre pibeddravyaṃ te yānti narakaṃ kila || 109 ||

ucchiṣṭaṃ na spṛśeccakre kuladravyāṇi sarvathā |

p. 172)

bahiḥ prakṣālya ca karau kuladravyāṇi dāpayet || 110 ||

naikahastena dātavyaṃ na mudrāvarjitaṃ sadā |
niṣṭhīvanaṃ malaṃ mūtramadhovāyuvisarjanam || 111 ||

cakramadhye tu yaḥ kuryāt sa bhavedyoginīpaśuḥ |
parīhāsaṃ pralāpañca vitaṇḍāṃ bahubhāṣaṇam || 112 ||

audāsīnyaṃ bhayaṃ krodhaṃ cakramadhye vivarjayet |
na pādābhyāṃ spṛśet pātramadhamāṅgena sarvathā || 113 ||

na pātraṃ cālayet sthānānna kuryāt pātrasaṅkaram |
saśabdaṃ noddharet pātraṃ tathaiva ca na pūrayet || 114 ||

nānyonyaṃ tāḍayet pātraṃ na pātramānayedadhaḥ |
sādhāraṃ noddharet pātramanādhāre na niḥkṣipet || 115 ||

riktapātraṃ na kurvīta na pātraṃ bhrāmayet kadā |
na pātraṃ laṅghayeddhīmān utkṣipya na ca pātayet || 116 ||

samīnañca punaḥ pātraṃ pradadyācca yathākramam |
vandayecca punaḥ pātraṃ imaṃ mantraṃ smaredbudhaḥ || 117 ||

p. 173)

dvitīyapātravandanamantrah

oṃ haimaṃ mīnarasāvahaṃ dayitayā dattañca peyādibhiḥ kiñciccañcalaraktapaṅkajadṛśā tasyai samāveditam | 
vāme svādu viśuddhiśuddhikaraṇaṃ pāṇau nidhāyātmake 
vande pātramahaṃ dvitīyamadhunā'nandaikasamvarddhanam || 118 ||

pūrvavattarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā tṛtīyañca samācaret |
mudrayā sahitaṃ dhṛtvā vandayet pātramuttamam || 119 ||


tṛtīyapātravandanamantraḥ

sarvāmnāyakalākalāpakalitaṃ kautūhaladyotakaṃ candropendramahendraśambhuvaruṇabrahmādibhiḥ sevitam |
dhyātaṃ devagaṇaiḥ paraṃ munigaṇairmokṣārthibhiḥ sarvadā vandepātramahaṃ tṛtīyamadhunā svātmāvabodhakṣamam || 120 ||

pūrvavattarpayitvā tu caturthaṃ vandayettataḥ || 121 ||


caturthapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ madyaṃ mīnarasāvahaṃ hariharabrahmādibhiḥ pūritaṃ mudrāmaithunadharmakarmanirataṃ kṣārāmlatiktāśrayam |
ācāryāṣṭakasiddhibhairavakalāmāṃsena saṃśodhitaṃ 
pāyāt pañcamakāratattvasahitaṃ pātraṃ caturthaṃ namaḥ || 122 ||

p. 174)

pūrvavattarpayitvā tu pañcamaṃ vandayettataḥ || 123 ||


pañcamapātravandanamantraḥ

ādhāre bhujagādhirājavalaye pātraṃ mahīmaṇḍalaṃ 
madyaṃ saptasamudravāripiśitaṃ cāṣṭau ca digdantinaḥ | 
so'haṃ bhairavamarcayan pratidinaṃ tārāgaṇai rakṣitai 
ādityapramukhaiḥ surāsuragaṇairājñākaraiḥ kiṅkaraiḥ || 124 ||

prapibet tu tataḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ pātraṃ sampūjya mantravit || 125 ||


ṣaṣṭhapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ rudraṃ cāmarabhadrapīṭhaparamānandoditaṃ dīpanaṃ 
vāmāṃ rājyamanoramāṃ śubhakarāṃ sāyujyasāmrājyakam |
nānāvyādhibhavāndhakūpaharaṇaṃ janmāntaraṃ nāśanaṃ 
śrīmat sundaritarpaṇaṃ harirasaṃ pātrañca ṣaṣṭhaṃ bhaje || 126 ||

p. 175)

pūrvavat tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā saptamañca samarcayet || 127 ||


saptamapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ jāgratsvapnasuṣuptitūryaparamaṃ caitanyaṃ sākṣipradaṃ vidyudbhāskarasannibhaṃ dyutidharaṃ jyotsnākalāvyāpitam |
iḍāpiṅgalamadhyagā trivalayā syātkuṇḍalī cordhvagā 
pātraṃ saptamapūraṇena paramānandādhikaṃ pātu mām || 128 ||

ebhiḥ prabandhya pātrāṇi svayaṃ sādhakasattamaḥ |
labheta sarvakarmāṇi dehānte bhairavo bhavet || 129 ||

pītvā saptamapātrañca śāntistotraṃ paṭhettataḥ || 130 ||


śāntistotram

yasyārcanena vidhinā kimapīha loke 
dharmaprasiddhamiti kāmaphalaṃ prasūte |
taṃ santataṃ sakalasādhakacittavṛttiṃ 
cintāmaṇiṃ kulagaṇādhipatiṃ namāmi || 131 ||


baṭukastuti

raktāmbaraṃ jvalanapiṅgajaṭākalāpaṃ 
bhālāvalīkuṭilacandradharaṃ pracaṇḍam |

p. 176)

bālārkadhātukanakācaladhātuvarṇaṃ 
devīsutaṃ vaṭukanāthamahaṃ namāmi || 132 ||

haratu kulagaṇeśo vighnasaṅghānaśeṣā-
nudayatu kulacaryā pūrṇatāṃ sādhakānām |
pibatu vaṭukanāthaḥ śoṇitaṃ nindakānāṃ 
diśatu sakalakāmān kaulikānāṃ gaṇeśaḥ || 133 ||

dharmo jayatvakhilalokasukhāvaho'yaṃ 
naśyatvadharmamakhilaṃ bahuduḥkhamūlam |
āśīrvacāṃsi samantvamṛtopamāni 
śāpāḥ patantu samayadviṣi yoginīnām || 134 ||

yāścakrakramabhūmikāvasatayo nāḍīṣu yāḥ saṃsthitāḥ 
yāḥ kāyodgataromakūpanilayā yāḥ saṃsthitā dhātuṣu |
ucchvāsormimaruttaraṅganilayā niśvāsavātāśca yā-
stā devyo ripupakṣabhakṣaṇaparāstṛpyantu kaulārcitāḥ || 135 ||

p. 177)

dehasthākhiladevatā gajamukhāḥ kṣetrādhipā bhairavā 
yoginyo vaṭukāśca yakṣapitaro vetālakāśceṭakāḥ |
anye bhūcarāḥ khecarā divicarā bhūtāḥ piśācā grahā-
stṛpyantāṃ kulaputrakasya pibataḥ pānaṃ sadīpaṃ carum || 136 ||

satyañcet guruvākyameva pitarau devāśca cet yogiṇo 
prītā cet paradevatā yadi bhaved vedā pramāṇaṃ hi cet | 
śākteyaṃ yadi darśanaṃ bhavati cet ājñā'pyamīdhā'pi cet 
satyañcāpi ca kauladharmaparamaṃ syānme jayaḥ sarvadā || 137 ||

śivādyavaniparyantaṃ brahmādistambasaṃyutam |
kālāgnyādiśivāntañca jagad yajñena tṛpyatu || 138 ||

sampūjakānāṃ paripālakānāṃ 
jitendriyāṇāñca tapodhanānām |
deśasya rāṣṭrasya kulasya rājñaḥ 
karotu śāntiṃ bhagavān gaṇeśaḥ || 139 ||

śivamastu sarvajagataḥ parahitaniratā bhavantu bhūtagaṇāḥ |
doṣāḥ prayāntu śāntiṃ sarvajanāḥ sukhībhavantu || 140 ||

p. 178)

nandantu kulayoginyo nandantu kulaputrakāḥ |
nandantu ca kulācāryā ye cānye kulapālakāḥ || 141 ||

nandantu sādhakāḥ sarve vibhīṣantu ca dūṣakāḥ |
avasthā śāmbhavī no'stu prasano'stu guruḥsadā || 142 ||

anekakoṭyaḥ kulayoginīnā-
mantarbahiḥ kaulikacakrasaṃsthāḥ |
nipīyamānena parāmṛtena 
prītāḥ prasannā varadā bhavantu || 143 ||

ye ye pāpadhiyaḥ sudūṣaṇaratā mannindakāḥ pūjane devācāravimardanaṣṭahṛdayā bhraṣṭāśca ye sādhakāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā ca kramapūrvamarcanavidhau ye kaulikā dūṣakā-
ste te yāntu vināśamatra samaye śrībhairavasyājñayā || 144 ||

sādhakānāñca dveṣṭāraḥ sadaivāmnāya dūṣakāḥ |
ḍākinīnāṃ mukhe yāntu tṛptāste piśitaiḥ sadā || 145 ||

iti stotraṃ paṭhan pānaṃ kuryāt sādhakasattamaḥ || 146 ||

p. 179)

aṣṭamapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ mūḍhājñānakadambakānanakaṭhorāgnisvarūpāṃ parāṃ jñānadhvāntasamastasaṃśayadhiyā pūrṇaṃ sudhādhārayā |
bhogaṃ mokṣakaraṃ sabhāvaśakaraṃ mūrdhni jvalantīṃ parāṃ 
devīṃ vakṣasi sañjapannanudinaṃ pātraṃ bhaje cāṣṭamam || 147 ||

pūrvavattarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā navamañca prapūjayet || 148 ||


navamapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ manye brahmamayaṃ samastajagatāṃ sāraṃ mahat śāśvataṃ 
durjñeyaṃ bhavabhogacañcaladhiyā sthūlākṛtiṃ dhyāyatām |
asmākaṃ dravarūpatāṃ karuṇayā prāptaṃ tadetaddrutaṃ 
tatpātraṃ navamaṃ pibecca niyataṃ bhuktiñca muktipradam || 149 ||

sahasrāre guruṃ dhyāyan hṛdi devīṃ tathaiva ca |
jihvāgre japasādhanaṃ śivo'hamiti cintayan |
yathāvidhisamabhyarcya juhuyāt kuṇḍalīmukhe || 150 ||


daśamapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ vāme candramukhī mukhe ca madhuraṃ pātraṃ karāmbhoruhe 

p. 180)

mūrdhni śrīgurucintanaṃ bhagavatīdhyānāspadaṃ mānasam |
jihvāyāṃ japasādhanaṃ pariṇataṃ kaulakramābhyāsanaṃ 
tatpātraṃ daśamaṃ pibecca paramaṃ bhuktiñca muktipradam || 151 ||

nāhaṃ kartā kārayitā na ca kāryaṃ 
nāhaṃ bhoktā bhojayitā na ca bhojyam |
nāhaṃ duḥkhī duḥkhayitā na ca duḥkhaṃ 
so'haṃ pratyakcitsvarūpo'hamātmā || 152 ||

evaṃ sañcintya vīrendraḥ punaḥ pānaṃ samācaret || 153 ||


ekādaśapātravandanamantraḥ

oṃ vāmāṃ vāmakare sudhāñca adhare mantraṃ japanmānase vīṇāveṇuravāvayantravidhivadgāyanti pañcopsarasaḥ |
krīḍākelikutūhalena kamalālāvaṇyalīlorasaḥ 
pānollāsavilāsapūrṇasamaye pātrañca ekādaśam || 154 ||

svapātrasthitahetuñca na dadyāt bhairavāya ca |
datte ca siddhihāniḥ syād devatāśāpamāpnuyāt || 155 ||

p. 181)

kvacityadṛcchayā prāptamanyamanyantu bhaktitaḥ | 
ādāya svīyapātrañca pibed dravyaṃ guruṃ smaran || 156 ||

guruśaktisūtānāñca gurujyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhayoḥ |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhakṣayet strīṇāṃ tābhyo nocchiṣṭamarpayet || 157 ||

anyathā cakramadhye tu patanaṃ sādhakasya ca |
śaktyucchiṣṭaṃ pibeddravyaṃ vīrocchiṣṭañca carvaṇam || 158 ||

śaktivīraprasādena kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale |
kaniṣṭhebhyaḥ svaśiṣyebhyaścocchiṣṭañca pradāpayet || 159 ||

dadyāt snehena yo'nyebhyaḥ sa bhavedāpadāṃ padam |
yāvanna calate dṛṣṭiryāvanna calate manaḥ || 160 ||

tāvat pānaṃ prakurvīta paśupānamataḥparam |
mantrārthasphuraṇārthāya brahmajñāna sthirāya ca || 161 ||

alipānaṃ prakartavyaṃ lolupo narakaṃ vrajet |
pītvā pītvā punaḥ pītvā patitvā ca mahītale || 162 ||

utthāya ca punaḥ pītvā punarjanma na vidyate |

p. 182)

alimīnāṅganāsaṅge yat sukhaṃ jāyate nṝṇām || 163 ||

tadeva mokṣo viduṣāmabudhānāñca pātakam |
cakramadhye ghaṭe bhagne pātre ca patite tathā || 164 ||

dravyasya patane bhūmau bhinne pātre tathaiva ca |
dīpanāśe ca tacchāntyai punaścakraṃ samācaret || 165 ||

pāyayitvā yathāyogyaṃ pītvā ca sādhakottamaḥ |
pūrvavat tarpayeddevīṃ parivārasamanvitām || 166 ||

punarmālyādikaṃ dattvā gandhacandanamiśritam |
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ tataḥ paścāddarpaṇaṃ cāmaraṃ tathā || 167 ||

naivedyaṃ śaṅkhaghaṇṭāñca vādayet sumanoharam |
yathāśakti stavaṃ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sādhakaḥ || 168 ||


ātmasamarpaṇam

sapuṣpārghyāmṛtaṃ nītvā ātmānañca samarpayet |
oṃ itaḥ pūrvaṃ tataḥ prāṇabuddhidehaṃ tato vadet || 169 ||

dharmādhikārato jāgratsvapnasuṣuptāvasthāsu ca |
karmaṇā manasā vācā hastābhyāñca tataḥ padbhyām || 170 ||

p. 183)

udareṇa ca śiśnā yat kṛtaṃ yat smṛtaṃ tataḥ |
yaduktaṃ procya tatsarvaṃ māṃ madīyañca saṃlikhet || 171 ||

sakalaṃ śrīmadiṣṭānte devatā ca pade tataḥ |
samarpaṇaṃ tataḥ paścādetenaiva samarpayet || 172 ||

devīpade tato dattvā kṣamasveti vadettataḥ |
punarācamanīyañca dadyāddevyai punaḥ punaḥ || 173 ||


arghyadānam

samastāvaraṇaṃ devyā aṅge līnaṃ vibhāvya ca |
arghyapātraṃ tataḥ paścāduttolya sādhakottamaḥ || 174 ||

devyupari tridhā bhrāmya nivedayettataḥ param |
tejomayīṃ mahādevīṃ saṃhāramudrayā hṛdi || 175 ||

nāsāpathāt samānīya nirmālyavāsinīṃ yajet |
nirmālyakusumairdevīṃ praṇamya gurupādukām || 176 ||


pūjāsamāptiḥ

bhaktibhāve tataḥ paścāt prāṇāyāmaṣaḍaṅgakau |
kṛtvā so'haṃ vibhāvyātha sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 177 ||

brahmarandhre guroḥ sthāne yantralepañca dhārayet |
nāstikebhyo na paśubhyo na mūrkhebhyo pradāpayet || 178 ||

p. 184)

kulīnāya ca dātavyamathavā jalamadhyataḥ |
naivedyaṃ caṇḍikādevyā vāñchanti vibudhāḥ sadā || 179 ||

tasmāddeyaṃ sadā puṣpaṃ brahmaṇe viṣṇave'pi ca |
mahyaṃ śukrāya sūryāya gaṇeśāya yamāya ca || 180 ||

agnaye varuṇāyāpi vāyave dhanadāya ca |
īśānāya mahādevīsādhakāya prakalpayet || 181 ||

athavā tarumūle ca garte vā śucideśake |
yatnataḥ sādhakaśreṣṭho nirmālyāni samarpayet || 182 ||

śaktibhyaḥ sādhakebhyaśca dattvā nirmmālyacandanam |
mālyaṃ gandhaṃ tato dattvā ātmadehaṃ tato'rpayet || 183 ||

athavā kārayet pānaṃ madhunā vīrasādhakaḥ |
śrīpātragrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā ādau cakraṃ karoti yaḥ || 184 ||

paśupānaṃ bhavettasya prāyaścittī sa sādhakaḥ |
arghyāmṛtaṃ tato vīraḥ sāmayikebhyo nivedayet || 185 ||

p. 185)

śeṣatattvagrahaṇavidhiḥ

tataśca sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ śeṣamātmani yojayet |
prabandhe pūrṇapātrañca pragṛhya sādhakottamaḥ || 186 ||

dehaṃ mantramayaṃ vidhāya purataḥ kṛtvā gurorvandanaṃ 
śrīpātraṃ kulavidyayā vilasitaṃ saṃśodhya divyāmṛtam | 
vidyāpīṭhacatuṣṭayaṃ śivaśive santarpya siddhikramā-
dāsvādyāpi pibedrasaṃ sukhakaraṃ duḥkhasya nirmūlanam || 187 ||

mahāpātakayukto vā bahupāpānvito'pi vā |
arghyasya bindumātreṇa mukto bhavati pātakāt || 188 ||

evaṃ pānaṃ samācarya svīkṛtya pūrṇapātrakam |
nyubjīkuryācca tadbhūmau tatra puṣpaṃ kṣiped budhaḥ || 189 ||

tadamṛtasnigdhabhūmau māyābījaṃ vilikhya ca |
kaniṣṭhāṅgulinā vāmahastasya tilakaṃ tathā || 190 ||

anena manunā mantrī mūlādyena samāhitaḥ |
yaṃ yaṃ spṛśāmi pādena yaṃ yaṃ paśyāmi cakṣuṣā || 191 ||

sa eva dāsatāṃ yātu yadi śakrasamo bhavet |
tataḥ prakṣālya vīrendro gopayet pātramuttamam || 192 ||

p. 186)

tato yathākrameṇaiva prasādaṃ pariveśya ca |
bhojayecca tato vidvān strībhiśca sādhakaiḥ saha || 193 ||

bhuktvā dadyāt punarmālyaṃ gandhacandanasaṃyutam |
tāmbūlābharaṇādīṃśca tathā talpaṃ susādhakaḥ || 194||

sevite ca kuladravye kulatattvārthadarśinaḥ | 
jāyate bhairavāveśaḥ sarvatra samadarśanaḥ || 195 ||

ntaṃ vā ga ta ccha raṃ pi mā kaulikaṃ na nivārayet |
vāraṇāt tripurā ruṣṭā tato hānistu jāyate || 196 ||

kaulikaḥ paśugāmī ca paraśaktiṃ ramed balāt |
goṣṭhīmadhye tu yatnena sparśaṃ tasya na kārayet || 197 ||

ryāṃ vā tāṃ gi bhā nīṃ su bha yo dadyāt kulayogine |
madhumattāya vīrāya puṇyaṃ tasya na gaṇyate || 198 ||

p. 187)

kulaṃ kulāya yo dadyāt so'pi yonau na jāyate |
bhagarūpā ca sā devī retaḥprītā ca sarvadā || 199 ||

retaḥsamarpaṇaṃ tasyai madyairmāṃsaiḥ samaṃ sadā |
dhyātvā kuṇḍalinīṃ śaktiṃ raman reto vimuñcayet || 200 ||

amantrā tu yadā nārī balād yatnādvā labhyate |
ātmadehasvarūpeṇa tatkarṇe mantramutsṛjet || 201 ||

tataḥ sā śaktirūpā syād bhuktimuktiphalapradā |
ratikāle ca dīkṣāyāmabhiṣekaṃ samācaret || 202 ||

surayā retasā vāpi jalena candanāmbunā |
sambhoge'bhiṣicennārīṃ raṇḍāñca mantravarjitām || 203 ||

ādau bālāṃ samuccārya tripurāyai samuccaret |
namaḥ śabdaṃ samuccārya imāṃ śiktaṃ tato vadet || 204 ||

pavitrīkuruśabdānte mama śaktiṃ kurudvayam |
vahnijāyāṃ samuccārya śuddhimantraḥ prakīrttitaḥ || 205 ||

anena manunā caiva abhiṣiktāḥ striyaḥ sadā | 
ramamāṇo bhavennityaṃ sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt || 206 ||

p. 188)

ihaloke paraṃbhogaṃ bhuktvā muktimavāpnuyāt |
rambhā śacyurvaśī mukhyā yā nārī gagane bhuvi || 207 ||

pātāle ca sthitā yā ca tasyā nāthastu kaulikaḥ |
tasya varjyā ca yā nārī kathyate tantravartmanā || 208 ||

tripuraiva guruḥ sākṣāttatpatnī tatsvarūpiṇī |
manasā karmaṇā vācā ramaṇaṃ tatra varjayet || 209 ||

tasyā eva pade bhakto muktimapyacirāllabhet |
guroḥ snuṣā ca kanyā ca svaputrī mantraputrikā || 210 ||

etasyā ramaṇaṃ varjyaṃ brahmavinmānasena ca |
kaulikasya ca patnī vai sākṣāt siddhīśvarī śivā || 211 ||

tasyā ramaṇamātreṇa kauliko nārakī bhavet |
mātā'pi gauravādvarjyā śivahīnā tathaiva ca || 212 ||

anyasthāne vicāre tu devīśāpaḥ prajāyate |

p. 189)

mantramātā ca putrī ca bhaginī ca kulāṅganā || 213 ||

na ramet kauliko nityametāḥ samyak samarcayet |
(yadvā) 
guruvīravadhūṃ tyaktvā ramyāḥ sarvāśca yoṣitaḥ || 214 ||


sandigdhānāṃ dvaitajñānināṃ pañcatattvagrahaṇe niṣedhaḥ

etā varjyāḥ prayatnena sandigdhānāñca sarvadā |
advaitānāñca kutrāpi niṣedho naiva vidyate || 215 ||

mātṛgarbhasthabījena śiśureva na saṃśayaḥ |
garbhaniḥsārakāle tu bālako hi digambaraḥ || 216 ||

ananījaṭharādyonirandhradvārādbahirbhavan |
jananīyonisambandhaḥsutaḥ śiśnā'dhigacchati || 217 ||

evaṃ vicāryamāṇe tu ko na syādgurutalpagaḥ |
nirvikāratayā nātra bhavet nahyanyathā tataḥ || 218 ||

ata-eva yadā yasya vāsanā kutsitā bhavet |
tadā doṣāya bhavati nānyathā dūṣaṇaṃ kvacit || 219 ||

nirvikalpamanā bhūtvā cinmayīṃ samupāsayet |
tadārūḍheṣu vīreṣu kāryākāryaṃ na vidyate || 220 ||

p. 190)

tasmāddvaitarūpeṇa sarvayoniṃ vicārayet |
niścayaṃ muktimāpnoti sahasrayonidarśanāt || 221 ||

anācāraḥ sadācāraḥ kukāryaṃ kāryamuttamam |
amedhyamapi medhyaṃ syāddvaitānāñca niścitam || 222 ||

apeyamapi peyaṃ syādabhojyaṃ bhojyameva ca |
agamyā'pi ca gamyā syāddvaitānāṃ suniścitam || 223 ||

na vidhirna niṣedhaśca na puṇyaṃ na ca pātakam |
na svargo naiva narakamadvaitānāñca niścitam || 224 ||

yatra yatra niṣedhaśca vidhirvā parikathyate |
tatra tatrāpi boddhavyaṃ sandigdhānāṃ suniścitam || 225 ||

sarvathaiva guroragre advaitaṃ naiva kārayet |
tasmācca yatnato vīro guruyoniṃ parityajet || 226 ||

advaitadvaitabhedena pūjanaṃ kathitaṃ mahat |
pañcatattvamasaṃyuktaṃ pañcamaṃ yastu kārayet || 227 ||

p. 191)

tasya pūjā ca sā naṣṭā prāyaścittī sa sādhakaḥ |
tattvahīnaṃ kṛtaṃ karma japakarma ca niṣphalam || 228 ||

śāmbhavī kupyate tebhyo brahmahatyā dine dine |
cakraṃ tyaktvā tu deveśīṃ pūjayettarpaṇaṃ vinā || 229 ||

catvāri tasya naśyanti āyurvidyāyaśobalam |
cakramadhye tu yaddṛṣṭaṃ śubhaṃ vā yadi vā'śubham || 230 ||

prabhāte tanna vaktavyaṃ sādhakena mahātmanā |
nityapūjā ca kathitā sādhakānāṃ susiddhaye || 231 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye aṣṭamollāsaḥ || 8 ||




navama ullāsaḥ 

iti guptataraṃ śreyaṃ gandharvasya kramaṃ śubham |
atyantagopanādyastu pañcatattvena pūjayet || 1 ||

sarvasiddhimavāpnoti mandabhāgyo'pi pūjanāt |
tasmāt pūjāṃ sadā kuryāt siddhārthī siddhisaṃgrahaḥ || 2 ||


pūjāphalakathanam

pūjāphalena daurbhāgyaṃ duḥkhadāridryanigraham |
sarvapraśamanaṃ yāti īśvaro jāyate bhuvi || 3 ||

arcanakṣaṇabhaṅgena vikalā yoginī bhavet |
pūjāhīno'śucirnityaṃ garhitaḥ sarvakarmasu || 4 ||

pūjāṃ vihāya yo mūḍho mantraṃ japati nityaśaḥ |
tajjapaṃ viphalaṃ vidyāt sa mantrī pātakī bhavet || 5 ||

p. 193)

vidyāṃ saṃgṛhya pūjāyāṃ śraddhā yasya na vidyate |
sa mahāpātakī bhūtvā paśuyonimavāpnuyāt || 6 ||

pūjāhīnasya māṃsena rudhireṇa viśeṣataḥ |
pāraṇaṃ yoginīnāñca bhairavāṇāṃ bhaveddhruvam || 7 ||

tasmāt pūjāṃ sadā kuryāt siddhyarthī mānase'thavā |
yadgehe pūjayed devīṃ sadaiva tatra pūjanam || 8 ||


divase pūjāniṣedhaḥ

atiguptena tatkāryaṃ divase naiva sarvathā |
divase pañcamaiḥ pūjā rāṣṭrahāniṃ karoti hi || 9 ||

sādhakasya mahākṣobhaṃ pradadāti suniścitam |
tasmāt pañcamakāreṇa divase naiva sarvadā || 10 ||


uttamādibhedena trividhā pūjā

uttamā nityapūjā syāt parvapūjā ca madhyamā |
māsapūjā'dhamā proktā māsādūrdhvaṃ paśurbhavet || 11 ||

p. 194)

paśorbhūyaḥ praveśecchā yadi syāddīkṣayet punaḥ |
vihitairmādibhirdravyairmāsādūrdhvaṃ samācaret || 12 ||


praśastakālanirṇayaḥ

kṛṣṇāṣṭamī caturdaśyamāvasyātha ca pūrṇimā |
saṃkrāntiḥ pañcaparvāṇi puṇyādi divaseṣu ca || 13 ||

sampattau ca yajellābhe tapodīkṣāvratotsave |
pīṭhopagamane vīrākulapīṭhasya darśane || 14 ||

deśikāgamane puṇyatīrthadaivatadarśane | 
evamādiṣu kāleṣu viśeṣadivaseṣu ca || 15 ||

gurujanmadine prāpte tadgurostadgurorapi |
mānavaughādipuṃsāñca svajanmadivase'pi ca || 16 ||

ekādaśyāṃ vyatīpāte karmalopaṃ na kārayet | 
aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ pūjā kāryā prayatnataḥ || 17 ||

na laṅghayedaṣṭamīñca laṅghanāt pātakī bhavet |
iti pūrvāpare prāpte pañcatattvaiśca sādhakaḥ || 18 ||

pūjāṃ kuryāt prayatnena anyathā siddhihā bhavet |

p. 195)

bahiḥpūjāvidhānena gandharvo vāpi kinnaraḥ || 19 ||

rājā'pi sādhako vāpi nānyaḥ ko'pi mahītale |
yatirvā bhūpatirvāpi nānyo'sti pūjakaḥ kvacit || 20 ||

na devaḥ parvatāgreṣu na deśe viṣṇusadmani |
devaścidānandamayo hṛdi bhāvena dṛśyate || 21 ||

yatra yatra dṛḍhā bhaktiryadā yasya mahātmanaḥ |
tatra tatra mahādevī prakāśamanugacchati || 22 ||


siddhakṣetrakathanam

atha vakṣye phalādhikyaṃ pūjāyāṃ yatra jāyate |
vārāṇasyāṃ mahāpūjā sampūrṇaphaladāyinī || 23 ||


sthānabhedena pūjāphalabhedaḥ

tatastu dviguṇā proktā puruṣottamasannidhau |
tato'pi dviguṇā proktā dvārakāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 24 ||

sarvakṣetreṣu tīrtheṣu pūjā dvārāvatīsamā |
vindhye śataguṇā proktā gaṅgāyāmapi tatsamā || 25 ||

p. 196)

āryāvartte madhyadeśe brahmāvartte tathaiva ca |
vindhyavat phaladā pūjā prayāge puṣkareṣu ca || 26 ||

tataścaturguṇā proktā karatoyānadījale |
tasmāccaturguṇaphalā nandikuṇḍe ca niścitam || 27 ||

tataścaturguṇā proktā jalpīśasya ca sannidhau |
tataḥ siddhīśvarīyonau tato'pi dviguṇā smṛtā || 28 ||

tataścaturguṇā proktā lauhityanadapāthasi |
tatsamā kāmarūpe ca sarvatraiva jale sthale || 29 ||

sarvaśreṣṭho yathā viṣṇurlakṣmīḥ sarvottamā yathā |
devīpūjā tathā śaktyāṃ kāmarūpe surālaye || 30 ||


kulārṇave

kāmarūpañca dvividhaṃ vyaktaṃ guptaṃ maheśvari |
karatoyānadīpūrvaṃ yāvaddikkaravāsinī || 31 ||

triṃśadyojanavistīrṇaṃ śatayojanamāyatam |
trikoṇaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇañca kāmarūpamudāhṛtam || 32 ||

p. 197)

dvividhaḥ kāmarūpaḥ

vyaktaṃ tatraiva vijñeyaṃ guptañcaiva gṛhe gṛhe |
vyaktād guptaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ labhyate sādhakottamaiḥ || 33 ||

devīkṣetraṃ kāmarūpaṃ vindyādanyanna tatsamam |
anyatra viralā devī kāmarūpe gṛhe gṛhe || 34 ||

tataḥ śataguṇā proktā nīlakūṭasya mastake |
tato'pi dviguṇā proktā sumerau śivaliṅgake || 35 ||

tato'pi dviguṇā proktā śailaputryāśca yoniṣu |
tataḥ śataguṇā proktā kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍale || 36 ||

tatsamāpi ca sā pūjāyoṣitāṃ yonimaṇḍale |
pīṭhānāṃ paramaṃ pīṭhaṃ kāmarūpaṃ mahākulam || 37 ||

p. 198)

tataḥ śataguṇaṃ proktaṃ kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍalam |
yatra koṭiguṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ māyā mahiṣamardinī || 38 ||

yatpīṭhaṃ brahmaṇo vaktraṃ guptaṃ sarvasukhāvaham |
yato devāśca devyaśca munayaścaiva bhāvajāḥ || 39 ||

sarve'pyāvirbhavantyatra tena guptaṃ mahākulam |
kāmākhyāyāṃ mahāyonau pūjāṃ yaḥ kṛtavān sakṛt || 40 ||

sa ceha labhate kāmān paratra śivatāṃ vrajet |
atra yatkriyate pūjā sakṛdvā sādhakena ca || 41 ||

vihāya sarvapīṭhāni tasya dehe vaset śivā |
atha vakṣyāmi sāmānya pūjāyāḥ kramamuttamam || 42 ||


sāmānyapūjākramaḥ

ādāvṛṣyādikanyāsaḥ karaśuddhistataḥ param |
aṅgulīvyāpakanyāsau hṛdādinyāsa eva ca || 43 ||

tālatritayadigbandhaḥ prāṇāyāmastataḥ param |
dhyānaṃ pūjā japaśceti sarvatantreṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 44 ||

japārthaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ vinyasettu lipiṃ vinā |

p. 199)

kṛtañca niṣphalaṃ vidyāt tasmādādau lipiṃ nyaset || 45 ||


śivābalikathanam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi śivāyā balimuttamam |
na dadāti baliṃ yastu śivāyāḥ śivatāptaye || 46 ||

sa pāpiṣṭhau nādhikārī kuladevyāstu pūjane |
paśurūpāṃ śivāṃ devīṃ yo nārcayati nirjane || 47 ||

śivārāveṇa tasyāstu sarvaṃ naśyati niścitam |
pūjā japavidhānāni yatkiñcit sukṛtāni ca || 48 ||

gṛhītvāśāpamāsādya śivā roditi nirjane | 
avaśyamannapānena niyataṃ toṣayecchivām || 49 ||

ekayā bhujyate yatra śivāyāścaiva niścitam |
tadaiva sarvaśaktīnāṃ prītiḥ paramadurlabhā || 50 ||

paśuśaktirnaraśaktiḥ pakṣiśaktistu sarvadā |
pūjitā viguṇaṃ karma saguṇaṃ sādhayed yataḥ || 51 ||

tena sarvaprayatnena kartavyaṃ pūjanaṃ mahat | 
rājādibhayamāpanne grahādibhayameva ca || 52 ||

śubhāśubhāni karmāṇi vicintya balimāharet |

p. 200)

bilvamūle prāntare vā śmaśāne vāpi sādhakaḥ || 53 ||

māṃsapradhānaṃ naivedyaṃ sandhyākāle nivedayet |
gṛhṇa devi mahābhāge śive kālāgnirūpiṇi || 54 ||

śubhāśubhaṃ phalaṃ vyaktaṃ brūhi gṛhṇa baliṃ tava |
evamuccārya dātavyo baliḥ kulajanapriyaḥ || 55 ||

kālīkālīti vaktavyastatromā śivārūpiṇī |
paśurūpā samāyāti parivāragaṇaiḥ saha || 56 ||

bhaktvā rauti yadaiśānyāṃ mukhamuttolya susvarā |
tadaiva maṅgalaṃ tasya nānyathā sādhakasya ca || 57 ||

yadi na bhujyate tatra tadā naiva śubhaṃ bhavet |
nitye naimittike kāmye baliṃ dadyāt prayatnataḥ || 58 ||

p. 201)

kulapūjāvidhānam

ati guptena kartavyaṃ balidānaṃ śubhāvaham |
kulapūjāṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāśāstravidhānataḥ || 59 ||


kulāgame

yathā kulavāre kulāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
kulatithau kularkṣe ca kulapūjāṃ samācaret || 60 ||

yoginīpūjanaṃ yatra pradhānaṃ kulapūjanam |
yathā viṣṇutithau viṣṇuḥ pūjito vāñchitapradaḥ || 61 ||

tathā kulatithau devī pūjitā varadāyinī |
nityaśrāddhaṃ tathā sandhyāvandanaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam || 62 ||

tathaiva kaulikānāñca nityatā kulapūjane |
ekarātraṃ dvirātraṃ vā nahi cet kulapūjanam || 63 ||

tadā taṃ sādhakaṃ tyaktvā layameṣyati caṇḍikā |
kulapūjāsamaṃ nāsti puṇyamanyajjagattraye || 64 ||

tasmād yaḥ pūjayed bhaktyā bhuktimuktyoḥ sa bhājanam |
anadhīto'pyaśāstrajño gurubhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ || 65 ||

kulapūjārato yastu sa me priyataro bhavet |
icchāsiddhiphalaṃ dadyāt pūjitā suvadhūriva || 66 ||

p. 202)

apūjitā yadā devī duḥkhadā kuvadhūriva |
kulapūjāntarāyaṃ tu yaḥ karoti hi durmatiḥ || 67 ||

sa yāti narake ghore ekaviṃśatibhiḥ kulaiḥ |
viguṇaṃ yatra yadyat syāt saguṇaṃ kulapūjanāt || 68 ||

kulāvalokanaṃ cet syāt kutaḥ prokṣaṇamārjanam |
kva snānaṃ kva ca vā śuddhiḥ kva ca nyāsaviśodhanam || 69 ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kulapūjāṃ samācaret |
labhate sarvasiddhiñca nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 70 ||


kulavāratithinakṣatrādividhānam

kathyate kulavārādinirṇayaḥ siddhihetave | 
dvitīyā daśamī ṣaṣṭhī kulākulamudāhṛtam || 71 ||

viṣamāścākulāḥ sarvāḥ śeṣāśca tithayaḥ kulāḥ |
raviḥ somo guruḥ śauriścatvāraścākulā matāḥ || 72 ||

bhaumaśukrau kulākhyau ca budhavāraḥ kulākulaḥ |

p. 203)

vāruṇārdrābhijinmūlaṃ kulākulamudāhṛtam || 73 ||

kulāni samadhiṣṭhāni śeṣāṇyakulabhāni ca |
tithivāre ca nakṣatre cākule sthāyino'jayaḥ || 74 ||

kularkṣe jayino nityaṃ sāmyañcaiva kulākule |
paurṇamāsyantamāsena tithikṛtyaṃ vidhīyate || 75 ||

mahāṣṭamyeva vijñeyā kulāṣṭamī susiddhidā |
kathitaḥ kulavārādinirṇayaḥ siddhihetave || 76 ||

snānaṃ pādyārghyaparyantaṃ svakalpoktaṃ vidhāya ca |
lākṣārasatadvadahe vīrastūlikāṃ racayettataḥ || 77 ||


kulakanyānirūpaṇam

naṭī kāpālikī veśyā rajakī nāpitāṅganā |
brāhmaṇī bhadrakanyā ca tathā gopālakanyakā || 78 ||

mālākārasya kanyā ca navakanyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
(yadvā) 
caṇḍālī carmakārī ca māgadhīpukkasī tathā || 79 ||

śvapacī khaṇḍikā caiva kaivarttī viṭayoṣitaḥ |
kulāṣṭakaṃ tu kathitamakulāṣṭakamucyate || 80 ||

p. 204)

akulāṣṭaka kanyākathanam

kalmaṣī caiva śauṇḍī ca śastrajīvī ca rākṣasī |
gomukhī rajakī śilpī venakī ca tathāṣṭamī || 81 ||

(yadvā) 
gaṇikā śauṇḍikī caiva kaivarttī rajakī tathā |
tantukārī carmakārī mātaṅgī pukkasī tathā || 82 ||

(yadvā) 
naṭī kāpālikī veśyā pukkasī yoginī tathā |
rajakī rajjakī caiva sairindhrī ca vilāsinī || 83 ||

khaṭikā ghaṭikā caiva tathā gopālakanyakā |
viśeṣavaidagdhyayutāḥ sarvā eva kulāṅganāḥ || 84 ||

uktajātyaṅganābhāve bhairavīṃ tadgatāṃ yajet |


śaktipūjāvidhiḥ

mantratantrasamāyuktāḥ samayācārapālikāḥ || 85 ||

avīrāśca vrataparā yogamudrādharāstathā |
gurubhaktā devabhaktā ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitāḥ || 86 ||

saṅgopanaratāḥ prāyastaruṇyaḥ siddhidāyikāḥ |
dīkṣitā manunā yena kena mūlena vā punaḥ || 87 ||

p. 205)

varjyā nārīkathanam

tatra varjyā ca yā nārī kathyate tantramārgataḥ |
vikṛtāṃ vidhavāṃ vyaṅgīṃ vṛddhāñcaiva tapasvinīm || 88 ||

hīnāṅgīṃ hīnavasanāṃ kuṭilāṃ kalahapriyām |
lokavedaviruddhāñca tathā niṣṭhurabhāṣiṇīm || 89 ||

mithyāpralāpaniratāṃ mithyācāramadotsukām |
īrṣyādidoṣasaṃyuktāṃ pramattāñca vivarjayet || 90 ||

vikṛtāsyāmadīkṣitāṃ savikalpakamānasām |
varṣīyasīṃ pāparatāṃ krūrāmatyantalolupām || 91 ||

atarkamānasāṃ dīnāṃ varjayet sādhakottamaḥ |
tataḥ samānayed vīraḥ pūrvoktāṣṭakulodbhavām || 92 ||

akrūrāñca surūpāñca sālaṅkārāṃ sudīkṣitām |
ghṛṇāhrīṃvarjitāṃ śāntāṃ śivavākyadṛḍhavratām || 93 ||

gurudevapade bhaktāṃ yuvatīṃ sādhikāṃ parām |
svakāntāṃ parakāntāṃ vā vidagdhāṃ cāruhāsinīm || 94 ||

p. 206)

tasyai pādyādikaṃ dattvā sthāpayettūlikopari |
sādhayet siddhimatulāṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 95 ||

pūjākāle śubhāḥ proktāḥ śaktayaḥ sahajā budhaiḥ |
tasyā dehe svakalpoktanyāsajālaṃ pravinyaset || 96 ||

devī parā bhagavatī jagadādhārarūpiṇī |
brahmaviṣṇuśivānāñca yonirutpattikāriṇī || 97 ||

sā yoniḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sṛṣṭisthitilayātmikā |
guṇatrayasamāpannā vākyatrayavibhāvinī || 98 ||

śṛṅgārarūpamāpannā trikoṇākāratāṃ gatā |
bhagarūpeṇa sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ dehamadhyagā || 99 ||

śivasannidhimāsthāya ratyarthaṃ liṅgarūpiṇī |
sarveṣāṃ jananī sā tu ratyarthaṃ ramaṇī parā || 100 ||


yonipraśaṃsā

puṃso yoniḥ striyo yonirbhagadā bhagarūpiṇī |
yasyāṃ tu jāyate jantustasyāṃ cānte pralīyate || 101 ||

p. 207)

triśaktisahitā yonirbrahmaviṣṇuśivātmikā |
devī bhagavatī khyātā bhagavāṃśca sadāśivaḥ || 102 ||

ekaiva paramā devī bhagaliṅgasvarūpiṇī |
sarveṣāṃ yonirūpeṇa sā devī bhagarūpiṇī || 103 ||

yonisthā yonimadhyasthā yonirūpasvarūpiṇī |
trikoṇā sā triguṇā sā tridaivatamayī parā || 104 ||

saṃyoge cāturī yāyā pūrṇatā pañcamī smṛtā |
tasmādyonau yajed devīṃ nityamānandarūpiṇīm || 105 ||

prakṣālya gandhatoyena dhūpayitvā'mbudhūpakaiḥ |
sindūrarajasā caiva yonau cakraṃ samālikhet || 106 ||

svakalpoktavidhānena tatra devīṃ prapūjayet |
sarvayoṣinmaye devi sarvakāmārthadāyini || 107 ||

kāmeśvari kāmabhūte kāmuke sannidhiṃ kuru |
anenā'vāhayed devīṃ svīyakramamathācaret || 108 ||

p. 208)

pañcamīpūjane caiva varjyaṃ svakulamantrakam |
yāvat puṃsāṃ prasaṅgo'tra tāvat svairaṃ vivarjayet || 109 ||

yadi śaṅkāspadaṃ tatra tadā devīṃ visarjayet |
pramādādyadi pāpātmā tatsaṅgamanugacchati || 110 ||

tadā kuṣṭhībhavet sadyo ninditāṃ siddhimāpnuyāt |
madyaṃ māṃsaṃ vinā yastu kulapūjāṃ samācaret || 111 ||

janmāntarasahasrasya sukṛtaṃ tasya naśyati |
vidhivat pūjanaṃ kṛtvā japtvā stutvā praṇamya ca || 112 ||

tasyāṃ yantraṃ samālikhya punastasyāṃ nivedayet |
yāvat sā vihvalā na syāttāvattāṃ na visarjayet || 113 ||

visarjanaṃ vidhāyaiva śaktīcchā yadi vidyate |
tasyā ājñāṃ gṛhītvā tu ret mai naṃ rva ca van thu pū || 114 ||

tato japet striyaṃ gacchan śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret |
madyaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā cānyaṃ yatkiñcit kulasādhanam || 115 ||

tasyai dattvā tataḥ śeṣaṃ gurave vinivedya ca |
tadanujñāṃ mūrdhni kṛtvā śeṣamātmani yojayet || 116 ||

śaktaye dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā so'haṃ saccintayettataḥ |

p. 209)

kulapūjanametattu kathitaṃ siddhihetave || 117 ||


devīyajanavidhānam

saṅkṣepayajanaṃ vakṣye yathā tantrānusārataḥ |
āliṅganaṃ bhavennyāsaścumbanaṃ dhyānamīritam || 118 ||

śītkāraṃ yajanaṃ proktaṃ naivedyaṃ nakhalekhanam |
pānaṃ jihvāmṛtaṃ jñeyaṃ ramaṇañca japo bhavet || 119 ||

ānandastavanaṃ viddhi retaḥpāto visarjanam |
advaitajñānamāśritya sadā devīṃ samarcayet || 120 ||

aśvamedhaśatenāpi brahmahatyāśatena vā |
puṇyapāpairna lipyante yeṣāṃ brahma hṛdi sthitam || 121 ||

pṛthivyāṃ yāni karmāṇi jihvopasthanimittakam |
jihvopasthaparityāgī karmaṇā kiṃ kariṣyati || 122 ||

tāvat karmāṇi kurvīta yāvajjñānaṃ na jāyate |
pūjākoṭisamaṃ stotraṃ stotrakoṭisamo japaḥ || 123 ||

japakoṭisamaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyānakoṭisamo layaḥ |

p. 210)

nahi dhyānāt paro mantro na devastvātmanaḥ paraḥ || 124 ||

nānusandhāt parā pūjā na hi tṛpteḥ paraṃ phalam |
mantrodakairvinā sandhyāṃ japahomairvinā tapaḥ || 125 ||

upacārairvinā pūjāṃ yogī nityaṃ samācaret |
deho devālayo devi jīvo devaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 126 ||

tyajedajñānanirmālyaṃ so'hambhāvena pūjayet |
jīvaḥ śivaḥ śivo jīvaḥ sa jīvaḥ kevalaḥ śivaḥ || 127 ||


pāśabaddhaḥ paśuḥ pāśamuktaḥ śivaḥ

pāśabaddhaḥ paśurdevi pāśamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
tuṣeṇa baddho brīhiḥ syāt tuṣābhāve tu taṇḍulam || 128 ||

karmabaddhaḥ sadā jīvaḥ karmamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
dhyāyatāṃ kṣaṇamātraṃ vā śraddhayā paramaṃ tviha || 129 ||

yadbhavettanmahāpuṇyaṃ tasyānto naiva gamyate | 
kṣaṇaṃ brahmāhamasmīti yaḥ kuryādātmacintanam ||
tatsarvaṃ pātakaṃ hanyāttamaḥ sūryodaye yathā || 130 ||

p. 211)

yogīndralakṣaṇam

snātaṃ tena samastatīrthasalilaiḥ dattā ca pṛthvī dvije 
nūnaṃ tena sutarpitāśca pitaro devāśca sampūjitāḥ |
yajñānāñca sahasramevamakhilaṃ tenaiva sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ 
yasya brahma vicāraṇe kṣaṇamapi prāptañca dhairyaṃ manaḥ || 131 ||

vratakratu tapastīrthadānadevārcanādiṣu |
yatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ tadevāpnoti tattvavit || 132 ||

uttamā sahajāvasthā madhyamā dhyānadhāraṇā |
japastutiḥ syādadhamā homapūjā'dhamādhamā || 133 ||

uttamā tattvacintā syājjapacintā tu madhyamā |
śāstracintā'dhamā jñeyā lokacintā'dhamādhamā || 134 ||

yo nindāstutiśīleṣu sukhaduḥkhāribandhuṣu |
samaścet sa tu yogīndro harṣāharṣavivarjitaḥ || 135 ||

p. 212)

nispṛho nityasantuṣṭaḥ samadarśī jitendriyaḥ |
āste dehe pravāsīva sa yogī paratattvavit || 136 ||

niḥsaṅkalpo nirvikalpo nirjitopādhivāsanaḥ |
nijasvarūpanirmagnaḥ sa yogī paratattvavit || 137 ||

yathā paṅgvandhabadhiraklīvonmattajaḍādayaḥ |
nivasanti ca sarvatra sa yogī paramo mataḥ || 138 ||

pañcamudrāsamutpannaḥ paramānandanirbharaḥ |
ya āste sa tu yogīndraḥ paśyatyātmānamātmani || 139 ||

yena yuktena manasā prasannā caṇḍikā bhavet |
tena santuṣṭahṛdayaiḥ sevyā devī na saṃśayaḥ || 140 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye navamollāsaḥ || 9 ||


daśama ullāsaḥ 

kaulācāravidhānam

athācāraṃ pravakṣyāmi yathātantrānusārataḥ |
sarvabhūtahite yuktaḥ samayācāratatparaḥ || 1 ||


samayācārakathanam

anityakarmasantyāgī nityānuṣṭhānatatparaḥ |
mantradhāraṇamātreṇa śivabhāvena tatparaḥ || 2 ||

parasyāṃ devatāyāṃ tu sarvakarmanivedakaḥ |
yeṣāñca niścalābhaktirgurau deve manāvapi || 3 ||

teṣāṃ siddhirbhavedāśu nānyathā kalpakoṭiṣu |


gurumāhātmyam

gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ || 4 ||

gururmanurgururjāpo gurureva parantapaḥ |
teṣāṃ siddhirbhavedāśu nābhaktasya kadācana || 5 ||

p. 214)

prātareva guruṃ dhyāyet brahmarandhre ca sādhakaḥ |
sampūjya sakalaṃ karma kuryāttasyā'jñayā sadā || 6 ||

viṃśatiḥ puruṣān vāpi sapta pañcatrayo'pi vā |
ajñātvā guruvaṃśānāṃ śiṣyaścennaṣṭasantatiḥ || 7 ||


guruvaṃśagauravam

dhyātvā gurukulaṃ naiva naṣṭamārgo bhaviṣyati |
naṣṭamārgān mantravidye na tādṛksiddhigocare || 8 ||

gurūṇāṃ śiṣyabhūtānāṃ nāsti cet santatikramaḥ |
tantramantrāśca vidyāśca niṣphalā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 9 ||

svavaṃśādadhiko jñeyo guruvaṃśaḥ śubhāvahaḥ |
utpādakabrahmadātrorgarīyān mantradaḥ pitā || 10 ||

asmān manyeta satataṃ piturapyadhikaṃ gurum |
gamanaṃ pūjanaṃ svapnaṃ bhojanaṃ ramaṇaṃ tathā || 11 ||

gṛhītvā'jñāṃ guroḥ kuryāttasya siddhirvinā japāt |
gurau manuṣyabuddhiñca mantre cākṣarabhāvanām || 12 ||

p. 215)

pratimāsu śilābuddhiṃ kurvāṇo narakaṃ vrajet |
gurau mānuṣabuddhyā na paśyet śiṣyaḥ kadācana || 13 ||

kadācinna labhet siddhiṃ mantrairvā devatārcanaiḥ |
śrīguruṃ prākṛtaiḥ sārddhaṃ ye smaranti vadanti ca || 14 ||

teṣāṃ hi sukṛtaṃ sarvaṃ pātakaṃ bhavati kṣaṇāt |
cāpalyaṃ parihāsañca guroḥ sākṣādvivarjayet || 15 ||

vṛthā tu pūjanaṃ mudrā kriyate mandabuddhibhiḥ |
śrīgurau saṃsthite sākṣāt kiṃ vṛthā dhyānayogataḥ || 16 ||

gurau sannihite yastu pūjayedanyadevatām |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ sā pūjā viphalā bhavet || 17 ||

yataḥ sarvatra mantrāṇāṃ gurupūjā garīyasī |
tadagre mantratantrāṇāṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ naiva kārayet || 18 ||

pūjite gurupāde vai sarvadevaḥ sukhī bhavet |
sarveṣāṃ mantratantrāṇāṃ pitā'sau yaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 19 ||

p. 216)

anyadevasaparyā vā anyadevasya kīrtanam |
gurudevaṃ vinā caiva tadagre narakaṃ vrajet || 20 ||

guroḥ prītikaraṃ karma yaḥ kuryāt sādhakottamaḥ |
tasyā'śu siddhayaḥ sarvāḥ santi pādatale sadā || 21 ||

kāyena manasā vācā yo bhaktaścaraṇe guroḥ |
tasya siddhirbhavedāśu pūjāhomajapādṛte || 22 ||

gurubhaktivihīnaścet hānistasya pade pade |
pratyakṣe vā parokṣe vā pratyahaṃ praṇamed gurum || 23 ||

guroragre pṛthak pūjāmauddhatyañca vivarjayet |
dīkṣāṃ vyākhyāṃ prabhutvañca guroragre na kārayet || 24 ||

sarvathā tuṣṭimutpādya guroḥ prasādamālabhet |
ṛṇadānaṃ tathā'dānaṃ vastūnāṃ krayavikrayam || 25 ||

na kuryād guruṇā sārddhaṃ śiṣyo'pi ca kadācana |
ājñābhaṅgo'rthaharaṇaṃ gurorapriyavartanam || 26 ||

p. 217)

gurudrohamidaṃ prāhuḥ yaḥ kuryāt sa ca pātakī |
snehadravyaniyogañca nā'nivedya gurau caret || 27 ||

anivedya tu yaḥ kuryāt sa bhaved brahmaghātakaḥ |
gurunāma na bhāṣeta japakālādṛte kvacit || 28 ||

śrīnātha deva svāmītyabhivāde sādhane vadet |
guruṃ tuṅkṛtya huṅkṛtya vācā nirbhartsya yaḥ kadā || 29 ||

devaṃ guruṃ gurusthānaṃ kṣetraṃ kṣetrādhidevatāḥ |
siddhiṃ siddhādhivāsāṃśca śrīpūrvaṃ samudīrayet || 30 ||

vikalpya kulaśāstrāṇi bhavanti kularākṣasāḥ |
ekākṣarapradātāraṃ yo guruñcāvamānayet || 31 ||

śuno yoniśataṃ gatvā caṇḍālatvamavāpnuyāt |
gurvagre na tapaḥ kuryānnopavāsavratādikam || 32 ||

tīrthayātrāñca yaḥ kuryānna kuryādātmaśuddhaye |

p. 218)

na niyogaṃ gurordadyād duṣṭatāṃ na vibhāvayet || 33 ||

yasmin dravye gurorasti spṛhā nānubhavettataḥ |
avaśyaṃ yadi vāñchā syādanubhūyāttadājñayā || 34 ||

yastilārdhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā gurusvamupajīvati |
mohāllobhāt patedyastu narake ca trisaptake || 35 ||

bahvalpaṃ hi gurordravyaṃ adattaṃ svīkaroti yaḥ |
tiraścāṃ yonimālambya kravyādairbhakṣyate sadā || 36 ||

gurudravyābhilāṣī ca gurustrīgamanotsukaḥ |
patitasyāpi tasyāpi prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 37 ||

tatpāpaṃ samavāpnoti gurvagre'nṛtabhāṣaṇāt |
gobrāhmaṇavadhaṃ kṛtvā yatpāpaṃ samavāpnuyāt || 38 ||

śakticchāyāṃ gurucchāyāṃ devacchāyāṃ na laṅghayet |
yadyatra guruṇā sārddhaṃ svapityupaviśettu yaḥ || 39 ||

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ yāvadindrāścaturdaśa |

p. 219)

guruvākyahataṃ kṛtvā ātmavākyaṃ tu ropayet || 40 ||

guruṃ jetuṃ mano yasya pacyate narakārṇave |
anekadhā paśorannaṃ bhuñjate ye ca kaulikāḥ || 41 ||

tebhyaḥ prakupyate devī tatsaṅgaṃ naiva kārayet |
lakṣatrayañjapellopāṃ lakṣaṃ vā'pyajapāṃ japet || 42 ||

homayeddhaviṣā'jyena niṣpāpaḥ syāttadā dhruvam |
guruṇā'lokitaḥ śiṣya uttiṣṭhedāsanaṃ tyajet || 43 ||

guruṇā sadasadvāpi yaduktaṃ tanna laṅghayet |
rabhasaṃ maithunaṃ mithyā yo vaded gurumandire || 44 ||

sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ śambhunā paribhāṣitam |
na viśedāsane dhīro devatāgurusannidhau || 45 ||

guroḥ siṃhāsanaṃ deyaṃ jyeṣṭhānāmuttamāsanam |
deśyāsanaṃ kaniṣṭhānāmitareṣāṃ samāsanam || 46 ||

p. 220)
