riktahasto na paśyettu rājānaṃ daivataṃ gurum |
phalapuṣpādikaṃ nītvā yathā śaktyā samarpayet || 47 ||

evaṃ yo nācarenmantrī brahmarākṣasatāṃ vrajet |
āliṅganapradānena gurau tuṣṭe sakṛdyadi || 48 ||

tadā kimvā na labhate sādhakā gurusevakāḥ |


gurutyāgāt narakagāmitvam

gurutyāge dhruvo mṛtyurmantratyāgāddaridratā || 49 ||

gurumantraparityāgādrauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |
gurutyāgakaraḥ śiṣyaḥ prāyaścittī bhaveddhruvam || 50 ||

lakṣaṃ śrīpādukāmantraṃ japtvā pāpāt pramucyate |
mantratyāgakaraḥ śiṣyastatsaṅgaṃ naiva kārayet || 51 ||

lakṣamekaṃ japenmantraṃ homatarpaṇataḥ śuciḥ |
brahmāparāśaravyāsaviśvāmitrādayaḥ punaḥ || 52 ||

guruṣuśrūṣaṇāt siddhiṃ prāptāste bhuvanatraye |
śivo guruprasādena sarvavitsarvagaḥ prabhuḥ || 53 ||

p. 221)

sādhakaḥ śiva eva syād gurau tuṣṭe sakṛd yadi |
tadā kimvā na labhante sādhakā gurusevakāḥ || 54 ||

tasmādeva prayatnena gurusevāṃ samācaret |
sarvadā gopayedenaṃ guruñca manumeva ca || 55 ||

guruśayyāsanaṃ yānaṃ pādukopānahau tathā |
snānodakaṃ tathā chāyāṃ laṅghayenna kadācana || 56 ||

vṛthā kālaṃ na gamayed dyūtakrīḍādinā sudhīḥ |
gamayed devatāpūjājapayāgastavādinā || 57 ||

guroḥ kṛpālāpakathāstotrāgamāvalokanaiḥ |
gamayedaniśaṃ kālaṃ na vadet paradūṣaṇam || 58 ||

rāgalobhamadakrodhapaiśunyādi vivarjayet |
kulācāraṃ guruṃ devaṃ manasā'pi na nindayet || 59 ||

pūjāṃ dhyānaṃ japaṃ homaṃ yathā karmacatuṣṭayam |
pratyahaṃ sādhakaḥ kuryāt svayañcet siddhimicchati || 60 ||

p. 222)

paradārādisaṅgatiḥ varjanīyā

kanyāyoniṃ paśukrīḍāṃ digvastrāṃ prakaṭastanīm |
nālokayet paradravyaṃ paradārāśca varjayet || 61 ||

na ninded vratinaṃ vipraṃ vedāṅgasaṃhitāstathā |
purāṇāgamaśāstrāṇi kalpāṃścāpi na dūṣayet || 62 ||

anyamantrārcane śraddhāmanyamantraprapūjanam |
ekasyāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā nānyasādhanamācaret || 63 ||

anyasya smaraṇād duḥkhaṃ yoginīśāpamālabhet | 
apamṛtyubhavettasya mṛte ca narakaṃ vrajet || 64 ||

kimadhikañcāparaiḥ sādhyaṃ tasyāḥ sarvaṃ yato labhet |
kuladarśanaśāstrāṇi kuladravyāṇi kaulikān || 65 ||

sevakān prerakān vāpi vācakān darśakāṃstathā |
śikṣakān mokṣakān vāpi yoiginīsiddhirūpakān || 66 ||


strīnindā na kartavyā

kanyāṃ kumārikāṃ nagnāmunmattāmapi yoṣitam |
na nindenna jugupseta na hasennāvamānayet || 67 ||

p. 223)

nāpriyaṃ nānṛtaṃ brūyāt kasyāpi kulayoginaḥ |
kurūpeti nikṛṣṭeti na vadet kulayoṣitam || 68 ||

parīkṣayenna bhaktāṃśca vīrāṇāñca kṛtākṛtam |
yā kācidaṅganā loke sā mātṛkulasambhavā || 69 ||

kupyanti kulayoginyo vanitānāmatikramāt |
strīṇāṃ śatāparādhena puṣpeṇāpi na tāḍayet || 70 ||

kulastrīvīranindāñca tattvadravyāpahāraṇam |
kularodhaprahārañca varjayenmatimān sadā || 71 ||

strīmayañca jagat sarvaṃ svayaṃ tāvattathā bhavet |
na hiṃsedakhilān jantūnna nindedvāmalocanām || 72 ||

na nindeddakṣiṇaṃ vāmaṃ devavākyaṃ tathaiva ca |
na nindet śaktisiddhāntaṃ śaktipūjanatatparaḥ || 73 ||

p. 224)

na ninded vṛddhavākyaṃ tu na ninded vedavādinam |
na ninded bahumānyañca na nindecca pativratām || 74 ||

na ninded devalokādīnna nindet samayasthitān |
na ninded bahulaṃ māṃsaṃ nindāṃ sarvatra varjayet || 75 ||

pramattāmantyajāṃ kanyāṃ puṣpitāṃ patitastanīm |
virūpāṃ muktakeśīñca kāmārttāñca na nindayet || 76 ||


strīḥ śaktirūpiṇī

śaktimayaṃ jagat sarvaṃ svayaṃ tāvattathā bhavet |
śaktirūpā yato devī sṛṣṭimadhye vyavasthitā || 77 ||

śakteḥ sṛṣṭiḥ samutpannā śaktau ca līyate punaḥ |
tasmāt śaktiḥ pradhānā ca pūjanīyā prayatnataḥ || 78 ||

bhakṣayedyoṣitā sārddhaṃ juhuyādagnisammukhe |
āraktavasanāṃ nārīṃ yatnād bhaktyā samānayet || 79 ||

śaktipūjāṃ sadā kuryāt prasannā yena jāyate |
sukhaṃ śaktiprasanne tu duḥkhaṃ tadvarjanād bhavet || 80 ||

p. 225)

śāktavyavahāraḥ

bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ tathā peyaṃ gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā punaḥ |
śaktau nivedya prathamaṃ tathā cā'bharaṇādikam || 81 ||

tatpaścādātmasāt kuryāt siddhyarthī sādhakaśca yaḥ |
śaktau darpaṃ ghṛṇāṃ lajjāṃ varjayet krodhadarśanam || 82 ||

kadāciddarparūpeṇa puṣpeṇāpi na tāḍayet |
śaktau na vismayaṃ kāryaṃ yasmāt kruddhā ca caṇḍikā || 83 ||

vismite bahulaṃ nāśaṃ mṛtyulābhamavāpnuyāt |
mṛte'pi narakaṃ yāti śriyamāpnoti pūjanāt || 84 ||

carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ lehyapeyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ gṛhaṃ sukham |
sarvañca yuvatīrūpaṃ bhāvayet yatamānasaḥ || 85 ||

kulajāṃ yuvatīṃ vīkṣya namaskuryāt samāhitaḥ |
yadi bhāgyavaśenaiva kuladṛṣṭistu jāyate || 86 ||

tadaiva mānasīṃ pūjāṃ tatra tāsāṃ prakalpayet |
bālāṃ vā yauvanonmattāṃ vṛddhāṃ vā sundarīṃ tathā || 87 ||

kutsitāṃ vā mahāduṣṭāṃ namaskṛtya visarjayet |

p. 226)

tāsāṃ prahāraṃ nindāñca kauṭilyamapi cāpriyam || 88 ||

sarvathā na ca karttavyamanyathā siddhirodhakṛt |
striyo devyaḥ striyaḥ prāṇāḥ striya eva vibhūṣaṇam || 89 ||

strīsaṅginā sadā bhāvyamanyathā svastriyā api | 
na paśyet patitāṃ nagnāmunmattāṃ prakaṭastanīm || 90 ||

divase na ramennārīṃ tadyoniṃ na nirīkṣayet |
viparītaratā sā tu bhavitā hṛdayopari || 91 ||

strīdveṣo naiva karttavyo viśeṣāt pūjanaṃ striyaḥ |
japasthāne mahāśaṅkhaṃ niveśyordhvaṃ japañcaret || 92 ||


jape kālaśucyādi niyamo nāsti

striyaṃ gacchan spṛśan paśyan viśeṣāt kulajāṃ śubhām |
māṃsamatsyadadhi kṣaudrabhakṣyadravye yathāruci || 93 ||

bhojyānnāsanabhaktāni bhuktvā bhakṣyaṃ carejjapam |
vinā pītvā surāṃ bhuktvā māṃsaṃ gatvā rajasvalām || 94 ||

yo japeccaṇḍikāṃ devīṃ duḥkhaṃ tasya pade pade |

p. 227)

kaulikamāhātmyam

antaḥśāktā bahiḥśaivāḥ sabhāyāṃ vaiṣṇavā matāḥ || 95 ||

nānāveśadharāḥ kaulā vicaranti mahītale |
kulapūjādiliṅgaistu rahito viṣṇutatparaḥ || 96 ||

gacchan svapan bhraman vāpi skhalan viṣṇuparāyaṇaḥ |
harināmnā jātabhāvo bhāvākhilaviceṣṭitaḥ || 97 ||

jaya viṣṇo hare brahman nānārthaparavistaraiḥ |
smarttavyā ca mahādevī kulīnairvipine gṛhe || 98 ||


rātrau japaḥ praśastaḥ

rātrau paryaṭanañcaiva rātrau ca śaktipūjanam |
na karoti kathañcaiva sādhakaḥ kauliko bhavet || 99 ||

badhirād viharedekaḥ sadā saṅgavilaṅghitaḥ |
dikkālaniyamo nātra sthityādiniyamo na ca || 100 ||

na jape kālaniyamo nārcādiṣu baliṣvapi |
svecchāniyama ukto'tra śaktimantrasya sādhane || 101 ||

p. 228)

snānādimānasaṃ śaucaṃ mānasaḥ pravaro japaḥ |
pūjanaṃ mānasaṃ divyaṃ mānasaṃ tarpaṇādikam || 102 ||

sarva eva śubhaḥ kālo nāśubho vidyate kvacit |
na viśeṣo divārātrau na sandhyāyāṃ mahāniśi || 103 ||

vastrāsanasthānageha dehasparśādidhāriṇaḥ | 
śuddhiṃ na cācarettatra nirvikalpaṃ manaścaret || 104 ||

nātra śuddherapekṣāsti na cāmedhyādidūṣaṇam |
ya evaṃ cintayenmantrī sarvakāmārthasiddhibhāk || 105 ||

gadyapadyamayī vāṇī sabhāyāṃ tasya jāyate |
tasya darśanamātreṇa vādino niṣprabhāṃ gatāḥ || 106 ||

rājāno'pi ca dāsatvaṃ bhajante kiṃ pare janāḥ |
sarvadevamayaṃ dehaṃ sarvadevamayīṃ tanūm || 107 ||

ātmānaṃ devatārūpaṃ advaitaṃ paricintayet |
ātmā ca paramātmā ca jagadvyāpī svayaṃ vibhuḥ || 108 ||

p. 229)

ātmānaṃ brahmarūpaṃ ca sarvadā paricintayet |
śatravaścāpi mitranti sākṣāddāsanti bhūmipāḥ || 109 ||

vācayanti yaśaḥ sarve sādhakānāṃ suniścitam |
durmukhāḥ sumukhāḥ sarve garvitā praṇamanti ca || 110 ||

bādhakāḥ sādhakā yānti sādhakasya viśeṣataḥ |
sarvadā pūjayed devīmasnātaḥ kṛtabhojanaḥ || 111 ||

mahāniśāśucau deśe baliṃ mantreṇa dāpayet |
atyantarogayukto vā śucirvā'pyathavā'śuciḥ || 112 ||

akāle'pyathavā kāle adhikārī sadaiva hi |
kṛtārthaṃ manyamānastu santuṣṭanijamānasaḥ || 113 ||

nādharmo vidyate kiñcit kiñciddharmo mahān bhavet |
kulānnañcaiva bhuñjīta kulīnena saha svapet || 114 ||

kulaprasaṅgaḥ karttavyaḥ kulīnairna paśoḥ kvacit |
yasminmantre ya ācārastasya dharmastu tādṛśaḥ || 115 ||

p. 230)

taddhastāvacitaṃ bhojyaṃ taddhastāvacitaṃ jalam |
taddhastāvacitaṃ puṣpaṃ devatābhyo nivedayet || 116 ||

ṣaḍaṅganyāsamācarya dhyātvā rātrau japet sudhīḥ |
sadākālaṃ japenmantraṃ sarvasiddhiparāyaṇaḥ || 117 ||

na doṣaḥ sarvadā jāpe sarvadeśe'pi sarvadā |
japaniṣṭho japedyastu sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet || 118 ||

sarveṣāmeva yajñānāṃ japayajñaḥ praśasyate |
rātrau japaikamātreṇa siddhidā caṇḍikā bhavet || 119 ||

iḍāyāñca gate rātrau śaktimantraṃ japed yadi |
piṅgalāyāṃ gate jīve puṃmantraṃ prajapet sadā || 120 ||

na cejjanmasahasraistu naiva siddhiḥ prajāyate |
kṛtārthastena jāyeta svargo vā mokṣa eva vā || 121 ||

p. 231)

bhrāntiratra na karttavyā siddhihāniḥ prajāyate |
vismitā vilayaṃ yānti paśavaḥ śāstramohitāḥ || 122 ||

viśuddhacitto'tra bhavet siddhiḥ syādapavargadā |
etaistu tatkṣaṇāt siddhirvismayo nāsti cāparaḥ || 123 ||


kulabhraṣṭasya niṣkṛtirnāsti

sarvācāraparibhraṣṭaḥ kulācāraṃ samācaret |
kulācāraparibhraṣṭo rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet || 124 ||

śāstreṣu niṣkṛtirdraṣṭā mahāpātakināmapi |
kulabhraṣṭasya kutrāpi na draṣṭā niṣkṛtiḥ kvacit || 125 ||

āpadaṃ duritaṃ rogo dāridryaṃ kalaho bhayam |
yoginīnāṃ prakopaśca skhalitāni pade pade || 126 ||


kulācārī yoginīpriyaḥ

bhraṃśamānaḥ praṇaṣṭaśca tejohīnaśca durmatiḥ |
ninditaḥ sarvavidviṣṭo vihvalaḥ saṅgavarjitaḥ || 127 ||

deśāddeśāntaraṃ yāti kāryahāniśca sarvadā |
tatrāpi kulamārgasthāḥ śākinyaḥ kulapālikāḥ || 128 ||

bhakṣayanti purā tāsāṃ varo datto mayaiva tu |

p. 232)

tasmādācāravān vīro yoginīnāṃ priyo bhavet || 129 ||

kulācāreṇa devatvaṃ yoginīvīramelanam |
tiryagyoniṃ samāpnoti kaulikastadviparyayāt || 130 ||

ājñāsiddhirbhavettasya ācārapālanāt sadā |
saṃskārasya vihīnatvād guruvākyasya laṅghanāt || 131 ||

ācāravarjanāccaiva kaulikaḥ patito bhavet |
bhogo mokṣāyate tasya pātakaṃ sukṛtāyate || 132 ||

mokṣāyate ca saṃsāraḥ sādhakaśuddhabhāvataḥ |
pañcatattvena karttavyaṃ sadaiva pūjanaṃ mahat || 133 ||

atiguptena karttavyaṃ sarvathaiva suniścitam | 
ityācāraparaḥ śrīmān japapūjāparāyaṇaḥ || 134 ||

pālakaḥ kulatattvānāṃ paratattve pralīyate |
aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhīnāmāśrayaḥ sādhako bhavet || 135 ||

p. 233)

prāyaścittaṃ bhṛgoḥ pātaṃ sannyāsaṃ vratadhāraṇam |
kāyakleśañca nāstikyaṃ tīrthayātrāmupoṣaṇam || 136 ||

varjayecca prayatnena yadīcchet siddhimātmanaḥ |

pañcamahāpātakāni

vīrahatyā vṛthāpānaṃ vīradravyāpahāritā || 137 ||

vīrastrīgamanañcāpi tatsaṃsargācca pañcamam |
mahāpātakamityuktaṃ sarvathā parivarjayet || 138 ||

kusumbhaṃ nālikāñcaiva ciñcāmeva na khādayet |
bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt siddhavidyāprapūjakaḥ || 139 ||

ikṣukhaṇḍaṃ na bhuñjīta śivā kālīti no vadet |
na chedayet kadambañca tripurāpūjakaḥ sadā || 140 ||

pramādādyadi kurvīta sundarīśāpamāpnuyāt |
kuladharmaṃ samāśritya ācāraṃ yo na pālayet || 141 ||

yathecchācāriṇastasya mahāpātakinaḥ sadā |
niṣkṛtirnāsti tasyaiva mahārauravasaṅkule || 142 ||

sa sadā paśurityukto devatāśāpamāpnuyāt |

p. 234)

guptaprakaṭasambhūtaṃ jñānājñānakṛtaṃ tathā || 143 ||

niṣiddhācaraṇaṃ yatra kutrāpi yadi jāyate |
prāyaścittaṃ tatra tatra karttavyaṃ sādhakena ca || 144 ||

niṣiddhācaraṇe samyak pāpasya gurulāghavam |
deśaṃ kālaṃ vayo vittaṃ samyak jñātvā yathāvidhi || 145 ||

prāyaścittaṃ guroḥ kuryāt sarvapāpaviśuddhaye |
śiṣyo'pi khalu tatproktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ samācaret || 146 ||

athavā sarvapāpānāṃ gurunāmajapaḥ smṛtaḥ |
jāmbūnadasya kaluṣaṃ pariśuddhaṃ yathā'gninā || 147 ||

anācārasya mālinyaṃ prāyaścittā'gninā dahet |
lakṣaṃ nāmno gurorjāpe sarvapāpādviśuddhyati || 148 ||

athavā'pyajapālakṣaṃ lopālakṣatrayaṃ tathā |
japānmucyeta pāpebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ sādhakaḥ sadā || 149 ||


lopāmudrāmantroddhāraḥ

viyacchūnyayutaḥ śaktiḥ savisargo'japāmanuḥ |

p. 235)

viyacchaktistataḥ kāmaḥ kṣauṇī ca parameśvarī || 150 ||

ākāśaṃ bhṛgukāmau ca śivaḥ śakraśca pārvatī |
candro'naṅgo dharādevī lopāmudrā prakāśitā || 151 ||

bahunā'tra kimuktena rahasyaṃ kathyate'dhunā |
varṇāśramāṇāṃ sarveṣāmācāraḥ sadgatipradaḥ || 152 ||


guruḥ śiṣyapāpam āpnoti

prajādoṣaśca rājānaṃ jāyādoṣaṃ patiṃ yathā |
tathā prāpnotyasandehaḥ śiṣyapāpaṃ guruḥ sadā || 153 ||

gurustrivāramācāraṃ kathayecca svaśiṣyake |
na gṛhṇāti hi śiṣyaścet tadā pāpaṃ gurorna hi || 154 ||


kulaśāstraṃ paśubhyaḥ goptavyam

kuladharmaprasaṅgantu paśūnāṃ puratastyajet |
kadācinnaiva kuryāddhi śūdrāgre vedapāṭhavat || 155 ||

p. 236)

pīṭhakṣetrāgamāmnāyaṃ tadvidyācārakaulikān |
kuladravyādikañcāpi na vadet paśusannidhau || 156 ||

yathā rakṣati caurebhyo dhanadhānyāmbarādikam |
kuladharmaṃ tathā caiva paśubhyaḥ parirakṣayet || 157 ||

suguptakaulikācāramanugṛhṇanti devatāḥ |
vāñchadāsiddhimavāpnoti nāśayanti prakāśakān || 158 ||


yā devī bhuvaneśvarī tribhuvanalokoddhṛtā hyagrato 
vidyāsṛṣṭikṛtā svakīyavapuṣo varṇāśritā devatā |
sā nityā sakalā gṛhasthanilayā kāntādṛtā komalā 
nityaṃ pañcaguṇairbhajasva tapasā bhogena tāṃ sādhaka ! || 159 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye daśamollāsaḥ || 10 ||



ekādaśa ullāsaḥ 

bhāvanirūpaṇam

paśvadibhedena bhāvasya traividhyam

atha bhāvaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā tantrānusārataḥ |
bhāvastu trividhaḥ prokto divyavīrapaśukramāt || 1 ||


tadbhedena gurumantradevatānāṃ traividhyam

guruśca trividhaścaiva tathaiva mantradevatā |
ādyabhāvo mahān śreyān sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ || 2 ||

dvitīyo madhyamaścaiva tṛtīyo viśvaninditaḥ |
bahujāpāttathā homāt kāyakleśādi vistaraiḥ || 3 ||

na bhāvena vinā caiva yantramantrāḥ phalapradāḥ |
kiṃ vīrasādhanairlakṣaiḥ kimvā'kṛṣṭakulākulaiḥ || 4 ||

kiṃ pīṭhapūjanenaiva kiṃ kanyābhojanādibhiḥ |
svakule prītidānena kiṃ pareṣāntathaiva ca || 5 ||

kiṃ jitendriyabhāvena kiṃ kulācārakarmaṇā |
yadi bhāvaviśuddhātmā na syāt kulaparāyaṇaḥ || 6 ||

p. 238)

bhāvena labhate muktiṃ bhāvena kulavardhanam |
bhāvena gotravṛddhiḥ syād bhāvena kāyaśodhanam || 7 ||

kiṃ nyāsavistareṇaiva kiṃ bhūtaśuddhivistaraiḥ |
kiṃ vṛthā pūjanenaiva yadi bhāvo na jāyate || 8 ||

kena vā pūjyate vidyā na vā kena prajāyate |
phalābhāvaśca niyataṃ bhāvābhāvāt prajāyate || 9 ||


divyabhāvanirūpaṇam

prathamaṃ divyabhāvastu kathyate tantravartmanā |
yadvarṇaṃ devatā yatra tattejaḥpuñjapūritam || 10 ||

tejomayaṃ jagat sarvaṃ vibhāvya mūrttikalpanam |
tattanmūrttimayairmantraiḥ svena svenaiva vā punaḥ || 11 ||

ātmānaṃ tanmayaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvaṃ bhāvaṃ tathaiva ca |
tatsarvaṃ yoṣiti dhyātvā cintayedyatamānasaḥ || 12 ||

p. 239)

aṣṭābdāt ṣoḍaśābdāntaṃ yuvatīti pracakṣyate |
tatra bhāvaprakāśaścet sa bhāvaḥ paramo mataḥ || 13 ||

caraṇānmūlaparyantaṃ pītvā pītvā punaḥ pibet |
tasyā nakhamaṇijyotsnākhaṇḍitāghatamo bhavet || 14 ||

snigdhāntaḥkaraṇo vīro nirvikāraḥ sadā bhavet |
suvṛttajānunā cārujaṅghorujaghanaśriyā || 15 ||

nābhiromāvalī vakṣaḥsthalaiḥ pīnapayodharaiḥ |
grīvānayanaparyantaṃ keśāgraiḥ prakarasmitaiḥ || 16 ||

yāvamadhumadāpūrṇadivyabhāvavilokitaiḥ |
snigdhendriyaśca santuṣṭaścaturvargapadāśrayaḥ || 17 ||


kāmakalāpūjā

tataḥ kāmakalāṃ dhyāyet sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |
nivṛttikāle punastasyā bhāvapūrṇāmṛtairnijam || 18 ||

mukhaṃ binduvadākāraṃ tadadhaḥ kucayugmakam |
tadadhaḥ saparārdhañca cintayettadadhomukham || 19 ||

sarvavidyāmṛtāpūrṇaṃ sarvavāgvibhavapradam |
sarvārthasādhakañcaiva sarvarañjanakārakam || 20 ||

p. 240)

sarvadevādibhūtañca sarvadevanāmaskṛtam |
sarvāhlādanasampūrṇaṃ sarvamohanakāraṇam || 21 ||

sarvārthasādhanañcaiva sarvavaśyapravarttakam |
evaṃ kāmakalādhyānaṃ sugopyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ || 22 ||

etadrūpaṃ tu cātmānaṃ cintayet sādhakaḥ sadā |
meḍhrasthāne śivākāramādhāre kanakaprabham || 23 ||

nābhisthaṃ sūryavimbābhaṃ taruṇāditya varcasam |
hṛdi vahniśikhākāraṃ tadūrdhve bhāskaradyutim || 24 ||

kaṇṭhe dīpaśikhākāraṃ ghaṇṭā vaidūryasannibham |
lambike candravimbābhaṃ bhrūmadhye ratnavadrucim || 25 ||

nayane viśvatejaśca cintayedeṣu sādhakaḥ |
dhṛtvā spṛṣṭavā'thavā dṛṣṭvā dhyātvā kāmakalātanūm || 26 ||

kuleṣu tatkalārūpamakule paricintya ca |
kṣaṇaṃ tadvadvimarśena jāyate bhāvasañcayaḥ || 27 ||

p. 241)

kulīno jāyate yasmāttat sarvaṃ kathitaṃ śubham |
kule sākṣād yatastattvaṃ devī tiṣṭhati tattvataḥ || 28 ||

tena tatkulaśāstrāṅge pūjanīyā prayatnataḥ |
kuladevī cādyā devī niścalā yasya vidyate || 29 ||

sa dhanyaḥ puruṣo loke niścalo yata eva saḥ |
iti kāmakalādhyānaṃ kathitaṃ sarvasiddhidam || 30 ||

parayoṣādhikā jñeyā nijastrī prītivarddhinī |
āgate svāgataṃ kuryājllīlāyogāt svalīlayā || 31 ||

talpāccotthāpya hastāgre dhṛtvā samveśayettaḥ |
vāmabhāgasamāsīnāṃ suveśīṃ kāmarūpiṇīm || 32 ||

dhyātvā kāmakalāṃ tatra vindvādipūrvavigrahām | 
gandhamālyaṃ tato dattvā kuladravyāṇi yojayet || 33 ||

hetuyuktaṃ satāmbūlaṃ dattvā bhuktvā ca sādhakaḥ |
pṛcchet kulakathāṃ sarvāṃ laukikālaukikādikām || 34 ||

p. 242)

tasyāṃ nindābhayakrodha kaṭūktiyojanaṃ yadi |
karoti kulaśāstrajñaḥ siddho'pi naśyati dhruvam || 35 ||

hitvā doṣādikaṃ tatra snehavṛṣṭīva śikṣayā |
bhāvayenna kaṭūktyā vai kṛte tu niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 36 ||

kulaśāstre rahasyaṃ tu kulamūlaṃ tato jagat |
varaṃ kulamanostyāgo varaṃ kulagurorapi || 37 ||

na tyāgayogyaṃ svakulaṃ dṛṣṭadoṣeṇa sarvathā |
svakule kulabāhulyaṃ yadi syād bhāgyayogataḥ || 38 ||

samarūpaṃ vidhātavyaṃ vaiparītyaṃ parityajet |
pṛthak sthānaṃ pṛthagdhyānaṃ pṛthakpūjā pṛthak stutiḥ || 39 ||

na karttavyā prayatnena kimebhirbahujalpitaiḥ |
vīrāpatyakule caiva vīrapatnīkule'pi ca || 40 ||

sadā tiṣṭhati deveśi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
tāsāṃ niśvāsayogena taddeśaścaiva naśyati || 41 ||

p. 243)

pramodādamṛtasnānaṃ devyāḥ syānnātrasaṃśayaḥ |
kulajā sā mahāyuddhe vīrāsphālananādinī || 42 ||

na doṣamanugacchantī pālayantī yato jagat |
mātsaryaṃ patividveṣaḥ kaṭūktiḥ kalahādikam || 43 ||

nīcābhigamanāccaiva na sā devī harapriyā |
evaṃ kulaṃ parityajya sādhayet siddhimātmanaḥ || 44 ||


phalapākavidhānam

phalapākavidhānaṃ kiṃ kathyate tantravartmanā |
vīrapatnī ca paramā svayameva maheśvarī || 45 ||

tatsutā kulavidyānāmādyā raudrī sanātanī |
tadvadhū bhuvaneśānī sarvamantraprabodhinī || 46 ||

nyāyato'nyāyato vāpi vīrapatnī gururyadi |
dīkṣaṇādeva sarvārthasādhakaḥ sādhako bhavet || 47 ||

yadi bhāgyavaśāddevi vīrapatnī tu labhyate |
phalapākaṃ tatra kṛtvā kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale || 48 ||

p. 244)

phalapākavidhānaṃ tu sarvadevanamaskṛtam |
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭāphalañcaiva devagandharvakinnarāḥ || 49 ||

āyānti phalapākārthaṃ tasmādyatnaviśeṣataḥ |
visarjanaṃ vidhātavyaṃ gopanaṃ bhāvaśaktikam || 50 ||


pātivratyapraśaṃsā

tattatkalpavidhānena kulaṃ kuryāt suśikṣitam |
yataḥ pativratādharmāt dharmo nāsti jagattraye || 51 ||

patiḥ prāṇaprado nityaṃ patireva parā gatiḥ |
patireva paraṃ brahma patiḥ prāṇādhikaḥ prabhuḥ || 52 ||

sarvatīrthamayaḥ svāmī sarvadevamayaḥ patiḥ |
sarvadevīmayaścaiva patirevamahāguruḥ || 53 ||

sarvadevamayaṃ hitvā yā nārī paragocarā |
śūkarī gardabhī kāko śṛgālī jāyate dhruvam || 54 ||

ahaṃ tu putravaddevi nātra bhītirna ca trapā |

p. 245)

tava liṅge yo hi yāti patirūpadharaḥ kvacit || 55 ||

mūlamantrajaptatoyaṃ tadgātre deyamīśvari |
tadā yadi bahirna syāttadā svapatireva hi || 56 ||

ityādiśikṣayā kāryāḥ śikṣitāḥ kulayoṣitaḥ |
āgatyopaviśet pārśve mukhaṃ vīkṣya sthirā bhavet || 57 ||

patibhāveṅgite dakṣā hṛṣṭā syāt patidarśane |
uttare nottaraṃ dadyāt vyāharecchubhakarmaṇi || 58 ||

rakṣāṃ kṛtvā vidhānena sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ | 
yoginīnāṃ mahāpūjā baṭukānāṃ tathaiva ca || 59 ||

kāryā vighnavināśārthaṃ kulavārarkṣayogataḥ |
tāsāṃ mūlatarormūle mūlavidyāñca kālikām || 60 ||

visarjanavidhau kuryāt sādhakaḥ kulapātanam |
yadrūpe prītiretasya kulasya kulavittamaḥ || 61 ||

tadrūpaṃ vigrahaṃ kṛtvā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |
tatra bījārpaṇaṃ kṛtvā kurvanti kularakṣaṇam || 62 ||

p. 246)

liṅgairvākyaiḥ padaiścaiva jñātvā kulasamudbhavam |
devajātaṃ narairjātaṃ yathārthakulaśāstrataḥ || 63 ||

jñātvā kulavibhāgaṃ tu pañcamāt parato budhaḥ |
saptamāṣṭamamāsādvā vṛkṣamadhye sarovare || 64 ||

hastaṃ datvā pādukākhyāṃ vidyāṃ saṃhārayogataḥ |
japedaṣṭasahasraṃ vai svayaṃ vṛkṣāt patiṣyati || 65 ||

pādukāṃ pūjayāmīti kathitaḥ pādukāmanuḥ |
evaṃ kṛte yat patitaṃ tatphalaṃ devanirmitam || 66 ||

svasvāmikañca patati vaiparītyāgamāgamāt |
etalliṅgaphalaṃ jñātvā ānīya kṣālayettataḥ || 67 ||

gandhadravyaṃ kaṭudravyaṃ dattvā ca sādhakottamaḥ |
dhenumudrāmṛtīkṛtya homaṃ kuryādalakṣitaḥ || 68 ||

rātrāvevaṃ vidhāyaiva yajñaśeṣaṃ bhujettataḥ |
bhuktamātre tu tasmin vai cirajīvī kṣitau bhavet || 69 ||

p. 247)

mukhyā madhumatī tasya karasthā bhogadāyinī |
nityā madhumatī tasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 70 ||

madhumatyāḥ phalañcaiva kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva me |
sudhādhārāsamā vāṇī vaktrāttasya prajāyate || 71 ||

sarvajñaḥ sarvaśāstrāṇāmadhīśaḥ kulanāyakaḥ |
candrasūryāvadhi śrīmān bhunakti bahulaṃ sukham || 72 ||

devakanyāpsarobhiśca divyagandharvacāraṇaiḥ |
nānāvilāsasampannaiḥ kalākuśalakovidaiḥ || 73 ||

nṛtyagītaiśca bahulairnānopāyarasāyanaiḥ |
amṛtādyaiḥ prītikarairdevabhogyaiḥ sudurlabhaiḥ || 74 ||

devakanyāhastagatairathamāruhyasotsukā |
āyāti sādhakākāṅkṣahṛdayā parameśvarī || 75 ||

yatra kāmeśvaro devaścintārasamupāgataḥ |
tatraivā'yānti satataṃ devakanyāḥ sahasraśaḥ || 76 ||

indrayogyāṃ purīṃ kṛtvā vasanti sādhakaiḥ saha |
tatropagatapīṭhasthā bhuktvā tu bahulaṃ sukham || 77 ||

p. 248)

cirakālaṃ bhuvi sthitvā cānte muktimavāpnuyāt |
sādhakānāṃ kulajñānāṃ na doṣaḥ phalapātane || 78 ||

etatphalanipātārthaṃ kulaprāptyā pade pade |
vighnaṃ karoti sarvatra nijasaṅkocakāraṇāt || 79 ||

phalaṃ vṛkṣopari tathā vighnamācarita dhruvam |
phalavṛkṣasya hāniścet kadāciddaivayogataḥ || 80 ||

sādhako'pi bhavedatra tadā ca savikalpakaḥ |
tāvanna kulapūjā syādyāvattu niṣkṛtirbhavet || 81 ||

tāvannijakulālāpā varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
etat kāryavidhāne tu yogyo nāstīha bhūtale || 82 ||

atidūrasthite deśe nāmavarṇavivarjite |
karoti yadi tatra syādvighnaṃ vā phalapātanam || 83 ||

na doṣaistatra bādhyante sādhakāḥ kulasevakāḥ |
vighnapradhānametattu kadācit phalasādhanam || 84 ||

p. 249)

saṅketenaiva karttavyaṃ sarvatra phalasādhanam |
svaphalaṃ samanuprāpya kriyate yadi pāmaraiḥ || 85 ||

phalaṃ bhavati tenaiva pāpena badhyate dhruvam |
divyabhāvaḥ sādhakendro marttyo śakrasamo bhavet || 86 ||

eṣa te kathito divyo bhāvaḥ parasukhāvahaḥ | 
na deyo yasya kasyāpi paśoḥ gopyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 87 ||


vīrabhāvavarṇanam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi vīrabhāvañca madhyamam |
nirdvandvamānaso bhūtvā hṛdi kāmakalātanuḥ || 88 ||

niśi pūjā prakarttavyā hetuyuktaḥ sadā bhavet |
nijaṃ kulaṃ samādāya svayaṃ bhairavarūpadhṛk || 89 ||

kulañca bhairavīrūpaṃ tadgātre nyāsavistaram |
vinyaset sakalaṃ nyāsaṃ navayonyātmakaṃ tathā || 90 ||

prasūnatūlikāmadhye puṣpaprakarasaṅkule | 
nānāgandhasamākīrṇaṃ kuladravyeṇa yantrakam || 91 ||

p. 250)

likhitvā pūjayecchaktau ghaṭasthāpanapūrvakam |
pūrvavanmaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktaikatamaṃ ghaṭam || 92 ||

saṃsthāpya hetunā'pūrya brāhmaṇādivibhedataḥ |
tatra mantraṃ vilikhyādau yadyatkulasamudbhavam || 93 ||

dhyātveṣṭadevatāṃ tatra japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
dhenumudrāṃ pradarśyā'tha amṛtaṃ tadvicintayet || 94 ||

dṛṣṭā'rghyasya tu pātraṃ vai nṛtyanti yoginīgaṇāḥ |
indrādayaḥ surāḥ sarve nṛtyanti madhulolupāḥ || 95 ||

brahmaviṣṇumaheśādyā nṛtyanti harṣatatparāḥ |
arghyapātraṃ tridhā kṛtvā gurave caikabhāgakam || 96 ||

ekaṃ kulāya dattvā vai ekena devatarpaṇam |
pītvā kularasaṃ pūrṇa nānālaṅkārabhūṣitaḥ || 97 ||

ānandarūpavān bhūtvā pūjayet parameśvarīm |
tattatkalpoktavidhinā tattanmantraṃ prapūjya ca || 98 ||

visarjanaṃ vidhāyātha svakule yojayettataḥ |
bhaktisvarasapānenadevī bhunakti cāmṛtam || 99 ||

p. 251)

tattatkularasāsvādairdevyāḥ prītiḥ prajāyate |
tatphalagrahaṇādeva sumeruśṛṅgarohaṇam || 100 ||

latāliṅganamātreṇa sudhādhautakalevaraḥ |
mūlayoge kṛte tatra japedaṣṭasahasrakam || 101 ||

japasyāntaṃ havirdravyaṃ gṛhītvā tena tarpayet |
vidhāya tarpaṇaṃ caiva pradakṣiṇamanuvrajan || 102 ||

kalpoktena praṇamyā'tha stotreṇa toṣayettataḥ |
idaṃ vīrakulaṃ caiva sundaraṃ sumanoharam || 103 ||

yaddeśe vidyate vīrastatkulaṃ caiva tatsutaḥ |
na ca mārībhayaṃ tatra na ca rājabhayādikam || 104 ||

sumaṅgalaṃ sadā tatra dhanaputravivardhanam |
lakṣmīrvāṇī sadā tatra susthirā bhavati dhruvam || 105 ||

mantrapuṣpaṃ prabodhinyāmavaśyaṃ brahmamūlataḥ |
yojanīyaṃ prayatnena na ca vighnaṃ pravartate || 106 ||

p. 252)

nānyavīreṇa tadyogaṃ grahaṇe daivatairapi |
yoginībhirna luptaṃ tanna ca pāpena yujyate || 107 ||

yatra tatra kuje vāre śmaśāne gamane kṛte |
pūjāphalaṃ bhavettatra saptavāsarasammitam || 108 ||

caturdaśyāṃ gate tatra pakṣapūjāphalaṃ labhet |
na gate nārccite sthāne paśureva na saṃśayaḥ || 109 ||

nānyasmādadhiko deva iti cintāparāyaṇaḥ |
sādhake kṣobhamāpanne devyāḥ kṣobhaḥ prajāyate || 110 ||

tasmādyatnād bhogayukto bhavedvīravaraḥ sudhīḥ |
bhogena mokṣamāpnoti bhogena kulasādhanam || 111 ||

yadyadvadati nidrāti yatkaroti yadarcati |
tatsarvaṃ kularūpaṃ tu dhyātvā ca viharet sudhīḥ || 112 ||

p. 253)

ekākī nirjane deśe śmaśāne nirjane vane |
śūnyāgāre nadītīre niḥsaṅgo viharenmudā || 113 ||

vīrāṇāṃ japakālastu sarvadaiva praśasyate |
sarvapīṭhe sarvadeśe kartavyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 114 ||

vīrasādhanakāryañca karttavyaṃ vīrapuruṣaiḥ |
divyairapi ca kartavyaṃ paśubhirna ca pāmaraiḥ || 115 ||

varaṃ pāmarakāryañca na paśoriti niścayaḥ |
yacca devyāḥ purā proktaṃ śāśvataṃ mantrasādhanam || 116 ||

añjanaṃ guṭikādīṃśca kuryādvīro mahābalaḥ |
divyavīre na bhedo'sti yo bhedaḥ sa tu kathyate || 117 ||


divyavīrayorbhedakathanam

śānto vinīto madhuraḥ kalālāvaṇyasaṃyutaḥ |
divyaśca devavat prāyo vīraścoddhatamānasaḥ || 118 ||

p. 254)

vibhūtibhūṣaṇo vāpi candanenāpi lepitaḥ |
ākāragopano vāpi vyakto vā divyasādhakaḥ || 119 ||

raktacandanadigdhāṅgo vaiṣṇavo vāpyavaiṣṇavaḥ |
asaṃskṛtaḥ saṃskṛto vā nityasiktaḥ kuleśvaraḥ || 120 ||

apamāne ca pūjāyāṃ hṛṣṭapuṣṭaḥ sadā bhavet |
devanindāparo vāpi tatpūjādiparo'pi vā || 121 ||

pūjāratastadrahitaḥ kulākulamate sthitaḥ |
nijabhāvasamāyukto devavat viharet kṣitau || 122 ||

vedahīne dvije caiva yathā na śrutisaṃskriyā |
viṣṇubhaktiṃ vinā caiva bhaktirna prabhavedyathā || 123 ||

śaktijñānaṃ vinā muktiryathā hāsyāya kalpate |
guruṃ vinā yathā tantre nādhikāraḥ kathañcana || 124 ||

patihīnā yathā nārī sarvakarmavivarjitā |
kulaṃ vinā yathā vīro devyā vā mama sādhakaḥ || 125 ||

p. 255)

nādhikārīti kauleyastasmād bhāvaparo bhavet |
vinā kulena divyānāṃ vīrāṇāñca tathaiva ca || 126 ||

mantrasiddhirna jāyeta janmakoṭiśatairapi |
avinītaṃ kulaṃ yasya ca kathaṃ mama pūjakaḥ || 127 ||

tasmādyatnāt sadā kāryaṃ yathā syādvinayānvitam |
bhāvābhāvāt kule śāstre nādhikāraḥ kathañcana || 128 ||

tena bhāvaviśuddhastu sādhakaḥ kauliko bhavet |
idaṃ vīrakulañcaiva divyādapi manoramam || 129 ||


paśubhāvanirūpaṇam

paśubhāvaṃ pravakṣyāmi tṛtīyaṃ sarvaninditam |
yathāvidhi paśorvidyāṃ gṛhītvā bhāvatatparaḥ || 130 ||

prathamaṃ pūrvamevārthaṃ yatnataḥ śuddhimācaret |
na matsyabhojanaṃ kuryānna striyaṃ manasā smaret || 131 ||

paradravye na lobhaḥ syānna bhogo mānaso bhavet |
sindhutīre parvate vā kānane vā surālaye || 132 ||

bilvamūle vivakte tu puṇyakṣetre suśobhane |
na śūdradarśanaṃ kuryāt kauṭilyaṃ dūratastyajet || 133 ||

p. 256)

devatā śubhravarṇā tu dhyātavyā susamāhitaiḥ |
trisandhyaṃ devapūjā syāt trisandhyaṃ japamācaret || 134 ||

rātrau mālāñca yantrañca spṛśan naiva kadācana |
na mantramuccared bhuktvā maunī syāt sarvakarmasu || 135 ||

parvakāle striyaṃ naiva gacchedvai sādhakottamaḥ |
puṣpaṃ gandhaṃ jalaṃ caiva svayamānīya pūjayet || 136 ||

maithunaṃ tatkathālāpaṃ tadgoṣṭhīṃ parivarjayet |
ṛtukālaṃ vinā gacchenna ca svastriyamādarāt || 137 ||

purāṇaśravaṇe śraddhā vedavedāṅgatatparaḥ |
na rātrau bhojayedvidvāṃstāmbūlañca tathaiva ca || 138 ||

guruṇā yadyadādiṣṭaṃ tatsarvaṃ yatnataścaret |
svajātakusumaṃ caiva hetudravyaṃ tathaiva ca || 139 ||

etatspṛṣṭvā trirātrañca pañcagavyena śudhyati |
raktavastraṃ na gṛhṇīyāt devībhaktiparāyaṇaḥ || 140 ||

p. 257)

viṣṇutantroktakalpādi tadanuṣṭhānameva ca |
kāryaṃ vīrakathālāpaṃ na kuryādvīravanditam || 141 ||

nityaśrāddhaṃ gavāṃ grāsaṃ sandhyāvandanameva ca |
tīrthasnānaṃ pīṭhadeśe gamanaṃ dharmatatparaḥ || 142 ||


dīkṣitādīkṣitabhedena paśordvaividhyam

paśustu dvividhaścaivā'dīkṣito dīkṣitastathā |
dīkṣitasyā'dhikāro'sti pūjāyāṃ caiva niścitam || 143 ||

gurutve tasya kutrāpi nādhikāraśca sarvadā |
pūrvasya nādhikāro'sti pūjādeśagatāgatau || 144 ||

pūrvastu kaulikaiḥ sarvairbahiḥ kāryāḥ svadeśataḥ |
pūrvadarśanamātreṇa kuladarśanamācaret || 145 ||

brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ varaṃ sahyaṃ kathañcana |
pūrvasya darśanaṃ naiva satyaṃ satyaṃ suniścitam || 146 ||

p. 258)

devyātmā so'pi niyataṃ vibhutvādyadyapi dhruvam |
tathāpi jāyate śaṅkā tadā devyāḥ pade pade || 147 ||

varaṃ kulaparityāgaṃ varaṃ svakulahiṃsanam |
varaṃ svakulakutsā syādvaraṃ svakulanindanam || 148 ||

varaṃ svakulahānistu varaṃ svakulakuṭṭanam |
kāyena manasā vācā varaṃ svakulapātanam || 149 ||

na ca kuryāt pūrvasaṅgaṃ devyaṅgakṣayakāraṇāt |
kulaśāstre dvayaṃ pāpaṃ gariṣṭo bhavati dhruvam || 150 ||

svakule mahatī nindā parayoge tathaiva ca |
tasmādapi mahat pāpaṃ paśusaṅgāt prajāyate || 151 ||

golokena sahā'lāpāt paśoḥ sambhāṣasaṃskṛtāt |
na sidhyanti mahāmantrāḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ suniścitam || 152 ||

brahmahatyā surāpānamagamyāgamanaṃ tathā |
steyaṃ saṃsarga evaite mahāpātakajātayaḥ || 153 ||

p. 259)

dīkṣādeva naśyanti japānnaśyanti cāpare |
vīrahatyā vṛthāpānaṃ vīrajāyāśca saṅgamaḥ || 154 ||

etadgranthaikadeśasya gurorarthāvalopanam |
jñātvā vīravarañcāpi tannindā tadakīrttanam || 155 ||

na ca tasmai dhanāropasteyamityabhidhīyate |


paśusaṅgaḥvarjanīyaḥ

paśunā yaḥ samālāpaḥ samā śayyā samāsanam || 156 ||

ekatra bhojanaṃ caiva saṃsargaśca tataḥ param |
saṃsargapātakaṃ proktaṃ kulīnasya mahātmanaḥ || 157 ||

pātakaṃ na ca teṣāṃ vai saṅkṣaye puṇyasañcayam |
prabhavanti na tīrthāni na gaṅgā na ca kāśikā || 158 ||

mahāvidyājapādeva catvāri pātakāni ca |
naśyanti na ca saṃsargaḥ kṣayaṃ yāti kadācana || 159 ||

ajñānāt paśusaṃsargo yadi daivāt prajāyate |
tadā dvādaśavarṣākhyaṃ vratārthaṃ yatnamācaret || 160 ||

kulīnāyāḥ samīpasthaḥ kulasevāparāyaṇaḥ |
ucchiṣṭabhojī tannāmaprajāpī tatpaterapi || 161 ||

p. 260)

tadante tāṃ samabhyarcya yatnaiśca paritoṣya ca |
śucirbhūtvā punarvidyāṃ gṛhītvā śuddhimāpnuyāt || 162 ||

vratāśakto yadi bhavet suvarṇaiḥ paritoṣakṛt |
dadyāt kulāya pāpānāṃ kṣayārthaṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 163 ||

jñānāt saṃsargamāsādya maraṇannaiva śudhyati |
mṛte ca bhāsakākādi yonimālambya sarvataḥ || 164 ||

nānākleśasamāyukto narakān pratipadyate |
na cainaṃ dīkṣayennāma na cāsya darśanañcaret || 165 ||

devīśāstrakathāñcātra prayatnena vivarjayet |
karmaṇā manasā vācā paśuśāstrādipūjanam || 166 ||

prakurvanti mahāpāpāstyajyāste kulasādhakaiḥ |
niṣedhe kulaśāstrāṇi sādhakaḥ sarvadā caret || 167 ||

paśuśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi varjayet paradāravat |
śvacarmasthaṃ yathā kṣīraṃ na deyaṃ syāddvijeṣvapi || 168 ||

tathā paśumukhāddharmo na śrotavyaśca kaulikaiḥ |
aśraddadhānā ye cātra kuladharmeṣu kaulikāḥ || 169 ||

p. 261)

na pātakānnivartante yāvadāhūtasaṃplavam |
kaulāḥ paśuvratāścaiva pakṣadvayaviḍambakāḥ || 170 ||

keśasaṃkhyā smṛtāya yāvattāvattiṣṭhanti raurave |
dīkṣaṇāt pūjanāddhomāttathā dṛṣṭyavalokanāt || 171 ||

yatkiñcij jñānamātreṇa paśunā nirjito yadi |
tasya sarvaṃ hareddevī śāpaṃ dattvā suniścitam || 172 ||

paśorvidyāṃ samādāya yadi pūjāparo bhavet |
tasya vaktraṃ samālokya kulavaktraṃ vilokayet || 173 ||

evaṃ na cet kulīnasya vidyāhānistu jāyate |
paśūpadiṣṭaṃ yatkiñcit kriyate kulasādhakaiḥ || 174 ||

tattatkarma sadā teṣāmabhicārāya kalpate |
yā yāḥ sarvā mahāvidyāścaṇḍikāyāḥ suniścitam || 175 ||

paśudṛṣṭinipātena sādhakaṃ nāśayed dhruvam |
yadi daivāt paśorvidyāṃ labhate kulajairnaraiḥ || 176 ||

dvijasya kālikāṃ prārthya punarvidyāmupālabhet |

p. 262)

ajñānād yat kṛtaṃ mātarnā'locya kulakaulikam || 177 ||

kṣamasva mātastatpāpaṃ hara devi kṛpāṃ kuru |
evaṃ prārthya mahāyatnai ratnaiḥ svarṇādivistaraiḥ || 178 ||

sampūjya kuladāmpatyamaṣṭamyāṃ yatnato nayet |
bhraṣṭasya cā'ṣṭamī ceti tithireva na cānyathā || 179 ||

dīkṣākālaḥ sa eva syānnānyaḥ kālaḥ praśasyate |
bhraṣṭā bahuvidhāḥ santi yadyatra kulasādhakāḥ || 180 ||

paśuśiṣyasamo nāsti satyaṃ satyaṃ na cānyathā |
kulācāraṃ paśau guptaṃ tattu teṣāṃ paraṃ viduḥ || 181 ||

etacchāstraprasaṅgo vā etat pustakadarśanam |
paśoragre na karttavyaṃ prāṇānte'pi kadācana || 182 ||

kṛtvā sūryamukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā smarttavyaḥ kulanāyakaḥ |
nirjīve kāṣṭhaloṣṭhe vā śarkarāyāṃ tṛṇe'pi vā || 183 ||

sarvatra cintitā devī na paśormantravigrahe |
cetanādhiṣṭhitaṃ sarvaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ prakalpitam || 184 ||

tatraiva cetanābhāvānniyamo nāsti tādṛśaḥ |
tasmād devyāśca sarvasvaṃ devīpūjāparāyaṇaiḥ || 185 ||

p. 263)

goptavyaṃ sarvathā caiva paśoḥ kalyāṇahetave |
sarvathā tatra bhāvau dvau na prakāśyau kadācana || 186 ||

sabhāvaśca vibhāvaśca paśurdvedhā vyavasthitaḥ |
sabhāvaḥ paśubhāvena janmatrayavibhāvanāt || 187 ||

vīrabhāvo bhavedeva tato devaḥ prajāyate |
daivācca jāyate vīro vīrāddevaḥ prajāyate || 188 ||

daive vīre na sandehaḥ sāmyamityabhidhīyate |


bhāvanirṇayo'nirvacanīyaḥ

bhāvastu manaso dharmaḥ sa hi śābdaḥ kathaṃ bhavet || 189 ||

tasmādbhāvo na vaktavyo diṅmātraṃ samudāhṛtam |
yathekṣuguḍamādhuryaṃ jihvayā jñāyate sadā || 190 ||

tasmādbhāvo vibhāvastu manasā paribhāvyate |
eka eva mahābhāvo nānātvaṃ bhajate yataḥ || 191 ||

p. 264)

upādhibhedabhāvena bhāvabhedo layiṣyati |
ānandaghanasandohaḥ prabhuḥ prakṛtirūpadhṛk || 192 ||

rasarūpī sa evā'tmā sa prabhuḥ paramo mahān |
śrotavyaḥ sa ca mantavyo nidhyātavyaḥ sa eva hi || 193 ||

sākṣāt kāryastato vīrairāgamairvividhaistathā |
śrotavyaḥ śrutivākyebhyo mantavyo mananādibhiḥ || 194 ||

sopapattibhirevāyaṃ dhyātavyo gurudeśitaiḥ |
tadā sa eva sarvātmāpratyakṣībhavati dhruvam || 195 ||

tasmin dehe tu bhagavān pratyakṣaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
bhāvairbahuvidhaiścaiva bhāvastatrāpi līyate || 196 ||

bhuktvā nānāvidhaṃ grāsaṃ gavi caiko yathā rasaḥ |
dugdhādyabhyāsayogena nānātvaṃ bhajate yataḥ || 197 ||

tṛṇena jāyate caiva rasastasmāt paro rasaḥ |
tasmāddadhi tato havyaṃ tasmādapi rasodayaḥ || 198 ||

p. 265)

sa eva kāraṇaṃ tasya tat kāryaṃ na ca kathyate |
dṛśyate ca sadā tatra na kāryaṃ nāpi kāraṇam || 199 ||

tathaivāyaṃ sa evā'tmā nānāvigrahayoniṣu |
jāyejjaniṣyate jātaḥ kāryabhedādi bhāvyate || 200 ||

sa jātaḥ sa mṛto baddhaḥ sa muktaḥ sa sukhī pumān |
sa strīrnapuṃsakaḥ so'pi sa evā'naṅga eva saḥ || 201 ||

nānādhyānasamāyogānnānātvaṃ bhajate yathā |
eka eva sa evātmā rasarūpī sanātanaḥ || 202 ||

avyaktaḥ sa ca suvyaktaḥ prakṛtyā kriyate dhruvam |
tasmāt prakṛtiyogena jñāyate nānyayā kvacit || 203 ||

vinā ghaṭatvayogena na pratyakṣo yathā ghaṭaḥ |
itarād bhidyamāno'pi na bhedamupagacchati || 204 ||

na bhedaṃ puruṣo yāti vinā śaktiṃ kathañcana |
na prayogairna ca jñānairna śrutyā na gurukramāt || 205 ||

p. 266)

prakṛtyaiva brahmajñānasya varṇanam

prakṛtyā jñāyate brahma prakṛtyā līyate punaḥ |
prakṛtyā'dhiṣṭhitaṃ sarvaṃ prakṛtyā vāñchitaṃ jagat || 206 ||

prakṛtyā bhedamāpnoti prakṛtyā līyate'khilam |
na vastu prakṛtirnaiva na pumān parameśvaraḥ || 207 ||

tayoḥ samarasaṃ jñānaṃ vastutattvaṃ paraṃ padam |
ubhayoḥ samavāyastu vastutattvaṃ suniścitam || 208 ||

jñāyate'smin svayaṃ prājñaścitsvarūpaḥ pumānasau |
nirvikāraḥ sarvadaiva pramāṇāgocaraḥ pumān || 209 ||

vyakto bhavati sarvatra svayamātmā svayambhūvaḥ |
upāyāḥ santi bahavo jñātuṃ brahma sanātanam || 210 ||

tathāpi prakṛteryogāt kṣipraṃ pratyakṣatāṃ vrajet |
sāṃyātrikāḥ kule deśe yathā nāvaṃ pragṛhya ca || 211 ||

nānādeśaṃ samuttīrya samudraṃ viśate cirāt |
tattaṭe nāvamāsthāya kṣaṇād yānti yathā punaḥ || 212 ||

vaijātyaṃ nāsti cettatra kathaṃ tattaddhi kāraṇam |
tasmāt paramparāyātāste hi kāraṇatāṃ gatāḥ || 213 ||

p. 267)

na kāraṇāni te caiva sākṣāt prakṛtikāraṇam |
strībhāvaḥ prakṛtirjñeyaḥ pumbhāva puruṣo mataḥ || 214 ||

jñaptijñeyavibhāgena jñāyatāṃ sādhakottamaiḥ |
ghaṭapratyakṣatāyāṃ tu āloko vyañjako yathā || 215 ||

cakṣurādi tataḥ kiñcidadhikaṃ nirvikalpanam |
ghaṭatvaṃ punaretasya bhedakāraṇameva hi || 216 ||

tathā na vyaktiviṣaye tvanye vyañjakatāṃ gatāḥ |
sākṣātkāraṇamevaitat prakṛtiścaiva niścitam || 217 ||

divyabhāvo vīrabhāvo yasya dehe vyavasthitaḥ |
ekena janmanā tasya paraṃ pratyakṣamāpnuyāt || 218 ||

jīvanmuktaḥ sa evā'tmā bhogārthamaṭate mahīm |
devīputraḥ sa evā'tmā bhairavaḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 219 ||


divyavīrapaśubhiḥ svasvamārgeṇa pūjā kartavyā

bhāvatrayāṇāṃ madhye tu dvau bhāvau supratiṣṭhitau | 

p. 268)

na vaktavyau muktimārgau kulasārau kulottamau || 220 ||

yo bhāvo yasya vai proktastairbhāvairnārcayedyadi |
daśāhakramayogena bhraṣṭo bhavati sādhakaḥ || 221 ||

nopadiśettatra bhāvaṃ na pūjāṃ tatra sandiśet |
kulānmantraṃ gṛhītvā tu bhāvaśuddhiḥ prajāyate || 222 ||

tasmād bhāvaparo bhūtvā devīṃ sampūjayet sudhīḥ || 223 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye ekādaśaḥ ullāsaḥ || 11 ||



dvādaśa ullāsaḥ 


mālāvidhānam

atha vakṣye ca mālānāṃ vidhānaṃ tantravartmanā |


japavidhiḥ

ārabhyā'nāmikāmadhyāt prādakṣiṇyena vai kramāt || 1 ||

tarjanīmūlaparyantaṃ japeddaśasu parvasu |
tarjanyagre tathā madhye yo japet sa tu pāmaraḥ || 2 ||

catvāri tasya naśyanti āyurvidyā yaśodhanam |
śaktimālā samākhyātā sarvamantrapradīpanī || 3 ||

(athavā) 
anāmikātrayaṃ parva kaniṣṭhā ca triparvikā |
madhyamāyāstrayaṃ parva tarjanīmūlaparvikā || 4 ||

aṅgulīrna viyuñjīta kiñcit saṅkocayettalam |
aṅgulīnāṃ viyoge tu chidreṣu kṣarate japaḥ || 5 ||

p. 270)

aṅgulyagreṣu yajjaptaṃ yajjaptaṃ merulaṅghane |
parvasandhiṣu yajjaptaṃ tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 6 ||


mālābhedakathanam

nityajāpe mahādevi karamālā śubhapradā |
akṣamālā tathā'nyā ca sarvathā siddhaye punaḥ || 7 ||

(athavā) 
muktāphalamayī mālā rājyamokṣapradāyinī |
sarvasiddhipradā nityaṃ sarvarājavaśaṅkarī || 8 ||

yathā muktāphalamayī tathā sphāṭikanirmitā |
rudrākṣamālikā mokṣe bhavet sarvasusiddhidā || 9 ||

pravālāmālikā vaśye lakṣmīvidyāpradā matā |
tathā māṇikyamālā ca sāmrājyaphaladāyinī || 10 ||

putrajīvakamālā tu vidyālakṣmīpradāyinī |
padmākṣamālayā lakṣmīrjāyate ca mahaddhanam || 11 ||

raktacandanamālā tu vaśyasaubhāgyadāyikā |

p. 271)

rudrākṣamālikā sūte japena svamanorathān || 92 ||

padmākṣaiḥ śatruhā mālā kuśagranthyā tu pāpahā |
putrajīvabhavā mālā putraṃ vitanute'cirāt || 13 ||

hiraṇmayī kāmadā syāt pravālaiḥ puṣkalaṃ dhanam |
saubhāgyaṃ sphāṭikī mālā rudrākṣe'kṣayakāmadā || 14 ||

śāktānāṃ sphāṭikīmālā raktacandanasambhavā |
putrajīvairdaśaguṇaṃ śataṃ śaṅkhaiḥ sahasrakam || 15 ||

pravālaiḥ padmarāgaiśca tathā'yutaguṇaṃ bhavet |
ayutaṃ sphāṭikaiḥ proktaṃ mauktikairlakṣamucyate || 16 ||

padmākṣairdaśalakṣaṃ syādrājataiḥ koṭirucyate |
sauvarṇairdaśakoṭiḥ syādanantaṃ raktacandanaiḥ || 17 ||

indrārkṣaiśca mahāsiddhiṃ tanute mālikā sadā |
raktacandanamālābhiḥ śivatvaṃ labhate dhruvam || 18 ||

p. 272)

kuśagranthena rudrākṣairanantaphaladā bhavet |
guñjāyāḥ sarvasiddhiḥ syādvilvakāṣṭhena siddhidā || 19 ||

śaṅkhena racitā mālā sarvasaubhāgyadāyikā | 
sāmānya kathitā mālā viśeṣāt kathyate'dhunā || 20 ||


devatāviśeṣasya mālāvarṇanam

raktena candanenāpi bālāmālāṃ prakalpayet |
dantena kālikāyāstu rājadantena meruṇā || 21 ||

ugratārājape śastā mahāśaṅkhasya mālikā |
unmukhyāśca tathā jñeyā mokṣadā mālikā bhavet || 22 ||

vaiṣṇavāṇāṃ padmabījairmālikā tulasī matā |
gajadantairgaṇeśe tu śaive rudrākṣa ucyate || 23 ||

gardabhānāṃ varāhāṇāṃ dantairapyabhicārake |


mālāyāṃ bījasaṃkhyākathanam

aṣṭottaraśatā mālā catuḥpañcāśikā'pi vā || 24 ||

p. 273)

ṣaṭtriṃśadracitā mālā uttamādhamamadhyamāḥ |
athavā mātṛkāsaṃkhyā mālā kāryā prayatnataḥ || 25 ||

saptaviṃśatibhiḥ kāryā sarvasādhāraṇe jape |
pañcaviṃśatibhirmokṣārthī dhanārthī triṃśatā japet | 26 ||

puṣṭyarthī saptaviṃśatyā pañcadaśyabhicārake |
aṣṭottaraśataiḥ sarvā siddhiruktā manīṣibhiḥ || 27 ||

pañcāśadbhiḥ sarvakāryaṃ karttavyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
suvṛttaṃ sundaraṃ bījaṃbadarībījamālikam || 28 ||


mālāgrathanavidhānam

śilpine dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā gṛhītvā sādhakottamaḥ |
saṃskārañca tataḥ kuryāt yathāśāstrapramāṇataḥ || 29 ||

apratiṣṭhitamālābhirmantraṃ japati yo naraḥ |
sarva tadviphalaṃ vindyāt kruddhā bhavati caṇḍikā || 30 ||

p. 274)

puṇyastrīracitaṃ sūtraṃ kārpāsaṃ vā'tha paṭṭakam |
paṅkajodbhavasūtrañca tathā ca śaṇasūtrakam || 31 ||

koṣakārasya sūtreṇa grathayedakṣamālikām |
svarṇādisambhavaṃ sūtraṃ triguṇaistriguṇīkṛtam || 32 ||

śuklaṃ raktaṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ śāntivaśyābhicārake |
rakte muktimavāpnoti śvetairyogādisādhanam | 33 ||

pītaiḥ kāmaṃ yaśasyañca kṛṣṇe rogādisambhavaḥ |
nitye naimittike śastaṃ śuklaṃ raktaṃ tathā śubham || 34 ||

bījāni ca tathā sūtraṃ pañcagavye dvayaṃ kṣipet |
prakṣālya mūlamantreṇa grathayedatha sūtrakam || 35 ||

guruśvaśurajāmātṛputraiḥ svayamananyadhīḥ |
taireva grathitāṃ mālāṃ kārayennā'nyataḥ kvacit || 36 ||

kāntayā racitā mālā drutasiddhikarī matā |

p. 275)

ekaikamaṇimādāya brahmagranthiṃ vinirdiśet || 37 ||

granthihīnaṃ na karttavyaṃ spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭena dūṣyati |
śaive ca vaiṣṇave saure gaṇeśe śrīkare'pi ca || 38 ||

bījasparśe punaḥ kuryātsaṃskāraṃ yatnataḥ sudhīḥ |
dūṣaṇaṃ yatra nāstyeva granthihīno'pi nityaśaḥ || 39 ||

kālikātvaritayostu vajrādyāḥ ṣaṭkabhedake |
totalāvanavāsinyorvārāhyādyāviśeṣataḥ || 40 ||

anyāyāścaṇḍikādevyā granthihīnā vidhīyate |
ekaikamaṇimādāya sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 41 ||

brahmagranthiṃ tato dadyāt hṛdaye tāramāsmaran |
svayameva japenmantramanyaḥ praṇavamuccaret || 42 ||

gopucchasadṛśī kāryā athavā sarparūpiṇī |
merumekaṃ vidhāyātha mālānte sādhakottamaḥ || 43 ||

arcayacca guruṃ dhyāyan nyāsamantrañca dakṣiṇe | 
vāmato'pi bhaveddevaṃ dhyānataḥ siddhimāpnuyāt || 44 ||

p. 276)

yo meruḥ taṃ guruṃ vindyānmantrabījantu dakṣiṇe |
evaṃ niṣpādya mālāñca pratiṣṭhāñca samācaret || 45 ||


mālāsaṃskārakramavivecanam

anulomavilomābhyāṃ mātṛkāntaritāṃ nyaset |
pañcagavye kṣipenmālāṃ śivamantreṇa mantritām || 46 ||

sāntaṃ śakrasvarārūḍhaṃ nādabinduvibhūṣitam |
kathitaṃ śivamantrañca sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca || 47 ||

pañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcagavyaiḥ snāpayenmūlamuccaran |
ghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ tathā nīraṃ śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam || 48 ||

pañcāmṛtamidaṃ khyātaṃ pratyekantu palaṃ palam |
palārdhaṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ prasthaṃ dadhi tathā samam || 49 ||

gomūtraṃ prasthamānaṃ syādājyañcaiva tadardhakam |
mūlena mantritaṃ kṛtvā yojayet pañcagavyakam || 50 ||

aśītalajalenaiva snāpayettadanantaram |
kṣālayet pañcagavyena sadyojātena mārjayet || 51 ||

p. 277)

mālāsaṃkāramantraḥ

oṃ sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmi sadyojātāya vai namaḥ |
bhave'bhave'nādibhave bhajasva māṃ bhavodbhavāya namaḥ || 52 ||

candanāgurugandhādyairvāmadaivena gharṣayet || 53 ||

oṃ vāmadevāya namo jyeṣṭhāya namaḥ śreṣṭhāya namo rudrāya namaḥ 
kālāya namaḥ kalavikaraṇāya namaḥ |
balāya namo balavikaraṇāya namo balapramathanāya namaḥ 
sarva bhūtadamanāya namo nama unmanāya namaḥ || 54 ||

sudhūpairdhūpayettāñca aghoramanunā budhaḥ |
oṃ aghorebhyo'tha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ 
sarvataḥ sarvasarvebhyo namaste'stu rudrarūpebhyaḥ || 55 ||

gandhacandanakuṅkumairlepayetpuruṣeṇa ca || 56 ||

oṃ tatpuruṣāya vidmahe mahādevāya dhīmahi | 
tanno rudraḥ pracodayāt || 57 ||

mantrayecca tato dhīraḥ pratyekañca śataṃ śatam |
īśānamanunā meruṃ śataṃ caiva tataḥ param || 58 ||

oṃ īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāmīśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānām 

p. 278)

brahmādhipatiḥ brahmaṇo'dhipatiḥ śivo me'stu sadāśivom || 59 ||


mālāprāṇapratiṣṭhāvidhānam

tataḥ saṃsthāpayenmālāṃ svarṇādipīṭhake budhaḥ |
padmākāreṣu cāśvatthapatreṣu sthāpayecca vā || 60 ||

pīṭhe vāpi ca saṃsthāpya pūjayet svīyadevatām |
sāṅgāvaraṇasaṃyuktāmupacāraiḥ suvistaraiḥ || 61 ||

ekaikakramayogena bījānāṃ sādhakottamaḥ |
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kurvīta aṣṭottaraśataṃ tataḥ || 62 ||


mālāprāṇapratiṣṭhā mantraḥ

prāṇaśaktisamārūḍhaṃ aukārabindurūpadhṛk |
praṇavaṃ akṣamālādhipataye tadanantaram || 63 ||

hṛdantaṃ mūlamuccārya pūjayenmālikāṃ kramāt |
tilena ghṛtayuktena śaktyā homaṃ prakalpayet || 64 ||

homakarmaṇyaśaktaśced dviguṇaṃ japamācaret |
tāro'kṣamālādhipataye susiddhādiṃ vadettataḥ || 65 ||

p. 279)

sarvamantrārthasādhinītisādhayadvitayaṃ punaḥ |
siddhiṃprakalpaya svāhā abhimantritamālayā || 66 ||

mūlamantraṃ japettatra aṣṭottarasahasrakam |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi japtvā mālāṃ samarpayet || 67 ||

śiṣyahaste tataḥ so'pi japedaṣṭasahasrakam |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi japtvā mālāñca gopayet || 68 ||

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā yathāśaktyā tu sādhakaḥ |
gurorabhāve mālāyāḥ saṃskāraṃ svayamācaret || 69 ||


mālāpradarśanaṃ varjanīyaṃ

guruṃ prakāśayedvidvān na tu mantraṃ kadācana |
akṣamālāñca mudrāñca gurorapi na darśayet || 70 ||

bhūtarākṣasavetālāḥ siddhagandharvacāraṇāḥ |
haranti prakaṭādyasmāttasmādyatnena gopayet || 71 ||

japānyasamaye mālāṃ pūjayitvā tu gopayet |
aśucirna spṛśenmālāṃ śucau na prakalpayet || 72 ||

p. 280)

hastāt patati cenmālā na japtavyā tryahaṃ budhaiḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ vidhātavyaṃ japenmantraṃ sahasrakam || 73 ||

(athavā) 
karabhraṣṭe śabdakṛte japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
jīrṇe sūtre punaḥ sūtraṃ prathayitvā śataṃ japet || 74 ||

chinnā bhavati cenmālā pūjāṃ kṛtvā tato'dhikām |
gurūṇāṃ śilpināñcaiva pūjāṃ kṛtvā tu sādhakaḥ || 75 ||

pratiṣṭhāṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā punarjāpaṃ samācaret |
evaṃvidhā sarvamālā sarvadeve pratiṣṭhitā || 76 ||


akṣamālāpūjākathanam

athānyāṃ sampravakṣyāmi mālāṃ kulamayīṃ parām |
kuṇḍagolodbhavairdravyaiḥ svayambhūkusumena ca || 77 ||

rocanākuṅkumenaiva akṣamālāṃ samarcayet |
karpūrāgurukastūrī tathā mṛgamadena ca || 78 ||

akṣamālāmupaskṛtya pratiṣṭhāṃ vidhivaccaret |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mālāṃ sarvottamottamām || 79 ||

p. 281)

yasya vijñānamātreṇa mantrāḥ sidhyanti tatkṣaṇāt |
anulomavilomena mantramāturvibhedataḥ || 80 ||

mantreṇā'ntaritaṃ varṇaṃ varṇenā'ntaritaṃ manum |
kuryādvarṇamayīṃ mālāṃ sarvamantrapradīpinīm || 81 ||

caramārṇaṃ merurūpaṃ laṅghayenna kadācana |
etadrahasyaṃ paramaṃ nā'khyeyaṃ yasya kasyacit || 82 ||

anulomavilomasthairbinduyuṅmātṛkākṣaraiḥ |
kṣamerukaiḥ sāṣṭavargaiḥ kḷptayā varṇamālāyā || 83 ||

pratyekaṃ varṇayuṅmantrā japtāḥ syuḥ kṣiprasiddhidāḥ |
sabinduṃ varṇamuccārya paścānmantraṃ japet sudhīḥ || 84 ||

kṣaṃ meruṃ kalpayitvā tu japettannā'bhilaṅghayet |
atha yantrapratiṣṭhānaṃ kathayāmi prasaṅgataḥ || 85 ||

p. 282)

yantrapratiṣṭhāvidhānam

suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ madhyamathā'dhamam |
tāmre lakṣaguṇaṃ proktaṃ raupye koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 86 ||

suvarṇe'nantaphaladaṃ sphāṭike'pi tathā samam |
ekatolaṃ dvitolaṃ vā tritolaṃ pañcatolakam || 87 ||

rasatolaṃ catustolaṃ saptatolaṃ palantu vā |
sādhakasya manojñaṃ vā kṛtvā pīṭhe susādhakaḥ || 88 ||

pūrvoktadhātudravyaiśca sundaraṃ sumanoharam |
surekhaṃ sumukhaṃ yantraṃ lekhayitvā vidhānataḥ || 89 ||

athavā pratimāṃ kṛtvā nijadevasvarūpiṇīm |
pratiṣṭhāñca tataḥ kuryād yathāśāstrapramāṇataḥ || 90 ||

snātvā saṅkalpayenmantrī tato nyāsādikañcaret |
pañcagavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śivamantreṇa śodhitam || 91 ||

tatra yantraṃ kṣipenmantrī praṇavena samākulam |
tasmāduddhṛtya taccakraṃ sthāpayet svarṇapaṭṭake || 92 ||

pañcāmṛtena susnāpya śītalena jalena ca |
vāsasā jalamuttolya sthāpayeddhemapīṭhake || 93 ||

p. 283)

pīṭhapūjāṃ vidhāyātha kuśairyantrañca saṃspṛśan |
praṇavaṃ yantrarājāya vidmahe tadanantaram || 94 ||

mahāyantrāya dhīmahi tanno yantraḥ pracodayāt |
gāyatrīmiti sañjapya śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tataḥ || 95 ||

āvāhya pūjayed devīṃ jīvanyāsapuraḥsaram |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirnānāvidharasāyanaiḥ || 96 ||

nānāvidhaphalairdravyairnānāṅgarāgavistaraiḥ |
svakalpoktavidhānena sampūjya parameśvarīm || 97 ||

tato jāpyaṃ sahasraṃ tu karyādīpsitasiddhaye | 
balidānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā praṇameccakramuttamam || 98 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ homaṃ ghṛtena kārayet sudhīḥ |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāttathā ca śilpine tataḥ || 99 ||


devatāpratiṣṭhā

devatāyāḥ pratiṣṭhānamevaṃ tu kārayed budhaḥ |
nyāsādikaṃ svakalpoktaṃ gāyatraṃ tatra sandiśet || 100 ||

p. 284)

devatāyāḥ pratiṣṭhāne viśeṣeḥ kathito mayā |
atha vakṣyāmi mantrāṇāṃ puraścaraṇamuttamam || 101 ||


puraścaraṇakathanam

bhāgyahīno'pi mūrkho'pi yadbodhādamaro bhavet |
sādhayet siddhisakalān sākṣāt siddhīśvaro bhavet || 102 ||

ādau puraskriyāṃ kuryānniyamena yathāvidhi |
tataḥ siddhamanurmantrī prayogārho na cānyathā || 103 ||

puraścaraṇasampanno mantro hi phaladāyakaḥ |
kiṃ homaiḥ kiṃ japaiścaiva kiṃ mantranyāsavistaraiḥ || 104 ||

vaśyāya sarvamantrāṇāṃ yadi na syāt puraskriyā |
jīvahīno yathā dehī sarvakarmasu na kṣamaḥ || 105 ||

puraścaraṇahīno'pi tathā mantraḥ prakīrttitaḥ |
puraskriyā hi mantrāṇāṃ pradhānaṃ jīva ucyate || 106 ||

tasmādādau puraścaryāṃ kṛtvā sādhakasattamaḥ |
prayogañca tataḥ kuryāt sādhakaḥ siddhihetave || 107 ||

p. 285)

sthānabhede phalabhedakathanam

grāme krośamitaṃ sthānaṃ nadyādau svecchayā matam |
nagarāt parataḥ krośaṃ krośayugmamathā'pi vā || 108 ||

āhārādivihārārthaṃ tāvatīṃ bhūmimākramet |
gṛhe japaḥ samaḥ prokto nadyāṃ śataguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 109 ||

puṇyāraṇye tathā'rāme sahasraguṇa ucyate |
ayutaṃ parvate puṇyaṃ lakṣaṃ bhāgīrathījale || 110 ||

koṭirdevālaye prāhuranantaṃ śivasannidhau |
yatra vā kutracid deśe liṅge vai paścimāmukhe || 111 ||

svayambhūrbāṇaliṅge vā itare vā tadantike |
prayāge suranadyāṃ vā vārāṇasyāṃ varāhake || 112 ||

ujjaṭe parvate vāpi śmaśāne gahanāntare |
ekaliṅge śūnyagehe catuṣpathe śivāntike || 113 ||

ekavṛkṣagirau vāpi vaṭamūle śivālaye |
mahāniśipuraścaryāṃ kuryādeṣu sthaleṣu ca || 114 ||


vighnanivāraṇārthaṃ pūjāvidhānam

kṣīravṛkṣasya kāṣṭhāni daśāstramantritāni vai |

p. 286)

pūrvādikramato vīraḥ sthāpayet kīlakāni ca || 115 ||

indramagniṃ yamaṃ caiva nir-ṛtiṃ varuṇaṃ tathā |
vāyuṃ kuberamīśānaṃ brahmānantau tataḥ param || 116 ||

svasvadikṣu prapūjyaiva baliṃ paścānnivedayet |
īśānaśakrayormadhyamūrdhvaṃ jñeyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ || 117 ||

varuṇāsurayormadhyamadhaśca parikathyate |
kṣetre ca kīlite mantrī na vighnaiḥ paribhūyate || 118 ||

kṣetrapālaṃ gaṇeśañca vidhivattatra pūjayet |
dīpasthāne ca vīrendro yathāvidhi puraḥsaram || 119 ||

maṇḍalaṃ tatra kurvīta caturdvārātmakaṃ tataḥ |
śuciḥ pūrvadine bhūtvā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 120 ||

prātaḥ snātvā ca gāyatrīṃ sahasraṃ prayato japet |
jñātājñātasya pāpasya kṣayārthaṃ prathamaṃ punaḥ || 121 ||

viprān santarpayet samyagbhojanāt sādarādibhiḥ |
bahubhirvastrabhūṣābhiḥ sampūjya gurumātmanaḥ || 122 ||

ārabhecca japaṃ paścāt tadanujñāpuraḥsaram |
ārambhakāle niyataṃ parayoṣāṃ prapūjya ca || 123 ||

dīkṣitāṃ vastrapuṣpādyaiḥ svayaṃ saṃyatamānasaḥ |

p. 287)

pāyasaṃ piṣṭakaṃ caiva nānārasasamanvitam || 124 ||

dugdhaṃ dadhi tathā takraṃ navanītaṃ saśarkaram |
aikṣavaṃ modakaṃ caiva nānāvidharasāyanam || 125 ||

nānāvidhaphalaṃ caiva nānāgandhavilepanam |
candanaṃ mṛganābhiñca śrīkhaṇḍaṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā || 126 ||

āsanaṃ pādukāṃ vastraṃ tathā cā'bharaṇādikam |
dhūpadīpañca kauṣeyaṃ puṣpamālāṃ tathā param || 127 ||

pañcatattvaṃ kulāṣṭañca śūnyagehe samānayet |
dvāradeśe tato vīraḥ pūjayed dvāradevatām || 128 ||

gaṇeśaṃ vaṭukaṃ caiva kṣetrapālañca yoginīm |
caturdvāreṣu sampūjya pūrvādikramato budhaḥ || 129 ||

tebhyo baliṃ pradadyāttu sādhakaśca tataḥ param |
bhūtāpasaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dīpasthānaṃ samācaret || 130 ||

nānālaṅkāraracito vihitāsanamāśritaḥ |
svakalpoktaṃ vidhāyā'tha prokṣayettadanantaram || 131 ||

arghyodakena sambhāramātmānañca tataḥ param |

p. 288)

nānālaṅkārasaṃyuktamarghyodakaviśodhitam || 132 ||

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā śaktīraṣṭau sukhaṃ nayet |
aṣṭakanyārūpabhedaṃ vilomayā'marśaceṣṭitam || 133 ||

brāhmaṇī kṣatriyā vaiśyā śūdrā ca kulabhūṣaṇā |
veśyā nāpitakanyā ca rajakī yoginī tathā || 134 ||

vidagdhāḥ sarvajātīnāṃ praśastāḥ sarvasiddhidāḥ |
brahmāṇyādyaṣṭaśaktīnāṃ nāmabhiḥ kṛtasañjñakāḥ || 135 ||

āsanaṃ prayamaṃ dattvā svāgatañca punaḥ punaḥ |
arghyaṃ pādyañca pānīyaṃ madhuparkaṃ jalaṃtataḥ || 136 ||

snāpayed gandhapuṣpādbhiḥ keśasaṃskāramācaret |
dhūpayitvā tataḥ keśān kauṣeyañca nivedayet || 137 ||

tataḥ sthānāntare pīṭhe āstīrya pādukāyugam |
dattvā tatra samāsīnāṃ nānālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ || 138 ||

bhūṣayitvā'nulepañca gandhaṃ mālyaṃ nivedayet |
tāṃ tāṃ śaktiṃ tato dhyāyed yathākramavidhānataḥ || 139 ||

p. 289)

brahmāṇyādyaṣṭaśaktidhyānam

haṃsārūḍhāṃ suvarṇābhāṃ caturvaktrāṃ trilocanām |
brahmakūrcābjadaṇḍākṣasūtrāṇi karapaṅkajaiḥ || 140 ||

dadhatīṃ cārurūpāñca jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitām |
stryalaṅkārasamāyuktāṃ brahmāṇīṃ saṃsmaret sudhīḥ || 141 ||

māheśvarīṃ vṛṣārūḍhāṃ śuklāṃ netratrayānvitām |
kapālaṃ ḍamaruṃ bāṇaṃ śūlaṃ ṭaṅkamabhītidam || 142 ||

karābjairdadhatīṃ candracūḍāratnavibhūṣitām |
trinetrāṃ kuṅkumanibhāṃ kaumārīṃ śikhivāhanām || 143 ||

śaktipāśāṅkuśābhītikarāṃ cāruvibhūṣitām |
vaiṣṇavīṃ garuḍārūḍhāṃ śyāmāṃ madhumadākulām || 144 ||

ghaṇṭāṃ śaṅkhaṃ kapālañca cakrañca karapaṅkajaiḥ |
dadhānāṃ pītavasanāṃ muktāmaṇivibhūṣitām || 145 ||

p. 290)

varāhavāhinīṃ dhūmrāṃ varāhasadṛśānanām |
phalakāṃ mūṣalaṃ caiva varaṃ khaḍga tathā param || 146 ||

pāṇipaṅkajavibhrāṇāṃ cārubhūṣaṇabhūṣitām |
indranīlaprabhāmaindrīmairāvataniṣeduṣīm || 147 ||

candravaktrāñca dadhatīṃ vajraśaṅkhadhanuḥ śarān |
nānālaṅkāraracitāṃ sadā bhaktānurāgiṇīm || 148 ||

bhīmavaktrāñca cāmuṇḍāṃ muṇḍamālāvalīyutām |
sāṭṭahāsāṃ śavāsīnāṃ maṇimuktāvirājitām || 149 ||

kapālaṃ kheṭakaṃ śūlaṃ khaḍgañca karapaṅkajaiḥ |
vibhrāṇāṃ nīlameghābhāṃ bhāvayed bhaktavatsalām || 150 ||

siṃhāsanāṃ mahālakṣmīṃ pītābhāṃ ratnabhūṣitām |
mātuluṅgaphalābhītimālāpadmavidhāriṇīm || 151 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā kramādvīra āvāhanyādimudrayā |
tāṃ tāṃ śakti samāvāhya mūrdhni tāsāṃ prapūjayet || 152 ||

p. 291)

punaḥ pādyādikaṃ dattvā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |
bhojyamaṇḍalamadhye tu svarṇādisthānake tataḥ || 153 ||

carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ lehyapeyaṃ bhojyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ nivedayet |
adīkṣitāścettatraiva tato māyāṃ nivedayet || 154 ||

tāsāṃ savyeṣu karṇeṣu tataḥ stotraṃ paṭhennaraḥ |


brahmāṇyādyaṣṭaśaktistotram

mātardevi namaste'stu brahmarūpadhare'naghe |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 155 ||

māheśi varade devi paramānandarūpiṇi |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 156 ||

kaumāri sarvavighneśi kumārakrīḍane vara |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 157 ||

viṣṇurūpadhare devi vinatāsutavāhini |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 158 ||

vārāhi varade devi daṃṣṭoddhṛtavasundhare |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 159 ||

p. 292)

śakrarūpadhare devi śakrādisurapūjite |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 160 ||

cāmuṇḍe muṇḍamālāsṛkcarcite bhayanāśini |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 161 ||

mahālakṣmi mahotsāhe kṣobhasantāpanāśini |
kṛpayā hara vighnaṃ me mantrasiddhiṃ prayaccha me || 162 ||

mitimātṛmaye devi mitimātṛbahiṣkṛte |
eke bahuvidhe devi viśvarūpe namo'stu te || 163 ||

etat stotraṃ paṭhedyastu karmārambheṣu saṃyataḥ |
vidagdhāñca samālokya tasya vighnaṃ na jāyate || 164 ||

kulīnasya dvāradevyaḥ kathitāstava putraka |
dīkṣākāle nityapūjāsamaye nā'rcayed yadi || 165 ||

tasya pūjāphalaṃ sarvaṃ līyate yakṣarākṣasaiḥ |
yadi vrīḍāparā sā tu bhojayettadgṛhād bahiḥ || 166 ||

sthitvā stotraṃ paṭhettāvadyāvattṛptiḥ prajāyate |
ācamya mukhavāsāditāmbūlañca nivedayet || 167 ||

p. 293)

tato dadyāt punarmālyaṃ gandhacandana paṃkilam |
binduyuktasya dīrghasya ekaikānte niyojayet || 168 ||

svasvanāma caturthyantaṃ hṛnmanuṃ tadanantaram |
mantrāṇi kathitānyeva tāsāṃ vīrastato japet || 169 ||

yathāśakti kramājjaptvā samarpyā'tha stutiṃ paṭhet |
visṛjya ca namaskṛtya varaṃ prārthya sukhī bhavet || 170 ||

anyā yadi nā'gacchanti nijakanyā nijānujā |
agrajā vā mātulānī mātā vā tatsapatnikā || 171 ||

vayasā jātito vāpi hīnā'pi paramā kalā |
pūjyāḥ kularasaiḥ sarvā nijāhaṅkāravarjitaiḥ || 172 ||

sarvābhāve ekatarā pūjanīyā prayatnataḥ |
saṃskṛtā'saṃskṛtā vāpi jananī vāpi niṣpatiḥ || 173 ||

p. 294)

pūrvābhāve'parā pūjyā devyaṃśā yoṣito yataḥ |
ekaścet kulaśāstrajñaḥ pūjārhastatra niścitam || 174 ||

sarvatraiva surāḥ pūjyāḥ brahmāviṣṇuśivādayaḥ |
ekā cedyuvatī tatra pūjitā cā'valokitā || 175 ||

sarvā eva parā devyaḥ pūjitāḥ sādhakottamaiḥ |
ādāvante ca madhye ca lakṣapūrttau viśeṣataḥ || 176 ||

na pūjayati cet kāntāṃ tadā vighnairvilipyate |
pūrvārjitaphalaṃ nāsti kā kathā parajanmani || 177 ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena yadīcchedātmano hitam |
devīnāṃ krodhasantāpaśamanaṃ vighnanāśanam || 178 ||

sarvathā ca prayatnena pūjayet kulayoṣitam |
śaktipūjāṃ samāpyā'tha prakṛtañca samācaret || 179 ||

svakalpoktavidhānena devīṃ sampūjayettataḥ |

p. 295)

prātareva tataḥ paścājjapenmadhyandināvadhi || 180 ||

anyūnaṃ nātiriktañca na dinañcā'tilaṅghayet |
ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ vātha sādhakaḥ || 181 ||

snānaṃ kuryācchuddhatoyairhaviṣyānnaṃ subhojayet |
ekagrāme sthito nityaṃ gatvā vandettato gurum || 182 ||

praṇamettaddiśo'nyatra devatāvandanaṃ sadā |
prātarutthāya pūjāyāṃ snānakāle'thavā punaḥ || 183 ||

saṃskṛtā'saṃskṛtā vāpi hīnajātā'pi vā tathā |
namasyā sarvajātīnāṃ kulīnānāṃ kulārcane || 184 ||

varjayed gītavādyādiśravaṇaṃ nṛtyadarśanam |
abhyaṅgagandhalepañca puṣpadhāraṇameva ca || 185 ||

asnātāṃśca dvijān śūdrān striyaṃ naiva spṛśet kvacit |
tyajeduṣṇodakasnānamaniveditabhojanam || 186 ||

nānācāraḥ prakarttavyo na cāraṇamitastataḥ |

p. 296)

bhūtahiṃsā na karttavyā paśuhiṃsā viśeṣataḥ || 187 ||

balidānaṃ vinā devyā hiṃsāṃ sarvatra varjayet |
anyamantrapuraskāraṃ nindāṃ caiva vivarjayet || 188 ||

uṣṇīśī kañcukī nagno muktakeśo'pyanāvṛtaḥ |
apavitrakaro'śuddhaḥ pralapanna japet kvacit || 189 ||

anāvṛtakaro bhūtvā śirasā cāvṛto'pi vā |
cintāvyākulacitto vā kruddho vā na japet kadā || 190 ||

vīrāsanaśayāno vā gacchannakhila eva vā |
rathyāyāmaśivasthāne na japettimirālaye || 191 ||

patitānāmantyajānāṃ darśane bhāṣaṇe śrute |
kṣute'dhovāyugamane jṛmbhaṇe japamutsṛjet || 192 ||

utthāyā'camanaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaṣaḍaṅgakam |
kṛtvā samyagjapeccheṣamathavā sūryadarśanam || 193 ||

p. 297)

striyaṃ śūdraṃ paśuñcaiva tathā ca bhraṣṭasādhakam |
sambhāṣayet kadācinna brahmacaryaṃ samācaret || 194 ||

japakāle ca satataṃ bhāryāṃ yatnena varjayet |
śayītaśuddhaśayyāyāṃ śucirvastradharaḥ sadā || 195 ||

pratyahaṃ kṣālayecchayyāmekākī nirbhayaḥ svapet |
nā'lokayedanyaśāstraṃ vedāgamaṃ vinā sudhīḥ || 196 ||

naktaṃ bhojī haviṣyānnaṃ japedvidyāṃ divā śuciḥ |
divase sarvathā vīro brahmacārī bhavet sadā || 197 ||

devatādhyānasaṃyukto gamayedaniśaṃ budhaḥ |
rātrau sampūjayed devīṃ kulācārakrameṇa tu || 198 ||

pañcatattvaiḥ prapūjyaiva yathāśakti japañcaret |
puraścaraṇakāle tu yadi syāt pīṭhadarśanam || 199 ||

tadā tatra pīṭhapūjā manasāpi na hīyate |
prasaṅgāt kathyate pīṭhapūjā tantravidhānataḥ || 200 ||

p. 298)


catuḥpīṭhe pūjyadevīnāmāni

devīkūṭe mahābhāge uḍḍīyāne tathā punaḥ |
yoganidrā kāmarūpe mahiṣāsuramardinī || 201 ||

kātyāyanī kāmabhūmau kāmākhyā kāmadāyinī |
jālandhare ca pūrṇeśī pūrṇaṃ śaile ca caṇḍikā || 202 ||

kāmarūpe tu vehāre pūjyā dikkaravāsinī |
athavā kāmarūpasya darśanaṃ yadi bhāgyataḥ || 203 ||

tadā bhagādidevīnāṃ pūjā tatra vidhīyate |
vakṣyamāṇavidhānena tāsāṃ jñātvā svanāmakam || 204 ||

kulanāthaṃ punardhyātvā svayamacyutamānasaḥ |
pūjāṃ samāpayedvīraḥ tadanusmṛtipūrvakam || 205 ||

pūjākāle hīnajātā svayoṣidvā vidhānataḥ |
pūjanīyā prayatnena dvedhaṃ tatra vivarjayet || 206 ||


kulācārasya mantraguptikathanam

yathā viṣṇupuraṃ gopyaṃ yathā vā śambhurīśvaraḥ |
yathā kamalajanmāpi ye vā vyāsamukhā dvijāḥ || 207 ||

indrādyālokapālāśca sarvagandharvakinnarāḥ |

p. 299)

yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācādyā guhyacāraṇavandinaḥ || 208 ||

tairyathā gopitaṃ guptaṃ taduktaṃ śāstrasambhavam |
tathā śaivena goptavyaḥ kulācāraḥ sudurlabhaḥ || 209 ||


yugabhede japasaṃkhyābhedaḥ

anena vidhinā mantrī puraścaryāṃ samācaret |
yeṣāṃ jape ca home ca saṃkhyā noktā manīṣibhiḥ || 210 ||

teṣāmaṣṭasahasrāṇi saṃkhyoktā japahomayoḥ |
yasmiṃśca nigade caiva mantre saṃkhyā vidhīyate || 211 ||

tatra sarvatra mantrāṇāṃ saṃkhyāvṛddhiryugakramāt |
kalpokte yat kṛtāsaṃkhyā tretāyāṃ dviguṇā bhavet || 212 ||

dvāpare triguṇā proktā kalau saṃkhyā caturguṇā |
na divyavīrayoreva yugasevāpariśramaḥ || 213 ||

paśūnāṃ vidyate cātra saṃkhyāvṛddhiryugakramāt |
teṣāṃ pūjā trikālaṃ hi rātrau naiva kadācana || 214 ||

p. 300)

kathitaṃ sarvaśāstreṣu vidhānaṃ tantravartmanā |
caturguṇaṃ tu tārāyā vinā śaṅkhākṣamālayā || 215 ||

evaṃ kṛtvā haviṣyāśī japellakṣacatuṣṭayam |
narāsthimālāmādāya japedvā lakṣamekakam || 216 ||

bhāvanārahitānāñca kṣudrāṇāṃ kṣudracetasām |
caturguṇajapaḥ proktaḥ siddhaye nānyathā bhavet || 217 ||


unmukhīkālikāvidhānam

siddhavidyā yathā nātra yugasevāpariśramaḥ |
viśeṣataḥ kaliyuge tārā kālī tathonmukhī || 218 ||

āsāmeva kadā nāsti yugasevāvidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
unmukhyāḥ kālikāyāśca viśeṣaḥ kathyate'dhunā || 219 ||

divase brahmacaryeṇa svīyasaṃkhyaṃ japaṃ paṭhet |
rātrau māṃsāsavairmatsyairmudrābhirmaithunodbhavaiḥ || 220 ||

sampūjya ca mahādevīṃ gandhapuṣpādibhistathā |
tato nagnāṃ striyaṃ nagno ramaṇakleśato'pi vā || 221 ||

p. 301) 

japellakṣaṃ prayatnena sādhako mantrasiddhaye |
dvijanmanāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ dvidhā vidhirihocyate || 222 ||

śūdrāṇāṃ tu tathā proktaṃ rātrāviṣṭaṃ mahāphalam |
tataḥ sarve svakalpoktajapasaṃkhyāṃ samāpya ca |
tatastu taddaśāṃśena homayeddhaviṣā'nale || 223 ||



iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye dvādaśollāsaḥ || 12 ||



trayodaśa ullāsaḥ 


homavidhiḥ

atha homavidhiṃ vakṣye yathātantrānusārataḥ |
ādau bhūmiṃ parīkṣeta vāstujñānaviśāradaḥ || 1 ||


kuṇḍavidhānam

śalyādīn śodhayedyatnādathavā puruṣaṃ khanet |
navahastapramāṇaṃ vā saptahastamathāpi vā || 2 ||

pañcahastapramāṇaṃ vā caturasrasamanvitam |
vicchidraṃ tu caturdvāraṃ divyadṛṣṭivibhūṣitam || 3 ||

divyamālāparivṛtaṃ tantunā pariveṣṭitam |
ādau kuṇḍaṃ prakurvīta sādhakaḥ siddhihetave || 4 ||


vividhākārāṇikuṇḍāni

caturasraṃ yonimardhacandraṃ tryasraṃ suvarttulam |
ṣaḍasraṃ paṅkajākāraṃ aṣṭāsraṃ tāni nāmataḥ || 5 ||

p. 303)

(1) caturasra kuṇḍamānam

hastamātramitāṃ bhūmiṃ pūrvavat parisūtrayet |
samantāt kuṇḍametat syāccaturasraṃ śubhāvaham || 6 ||

caturviṃśatyaṃgulāḍhyaṃ hastaṃ tantravido viduḥ |
kartturdakṣiṇahastasya madhyamāṅguliparvaṇaḥ || 7 ||

madhyasya dairghyamānena mānāṅgulamudīritam |
yavānāmaṣṭabhiḥ kḷptaṃ mānāṅgulamitīritam || 8 ||


(2) yonikuṇḍam

caturasrīkṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ pañcadhā vibhajet sudhīḥ |
nyaset purastādekāṃśaṃ koṇārdhārdhapramāṇataḥ || 9 ||

bhrāmayettena mānena tathānyadapi mantravit |
sūtrayugmaṃ tato dadyāt kuṇḍaṃ yoninibhaṃ bhavet || 10 ||


(3) ardhacandrakuṇḍam

caturasrīkṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ daśadhā vibhajet sudhīḥ |

p. 304)

ekamekaṃ tyajedaṃśamadha ūrdhvañca mantravit || 11 ||

jyāsūtraṃ pātayedagre tanmānāt bhrāmayet punaḥ |
ardhacandranibhaṃ kuṇḍaṃ ramaṇīyamidaṃ bhavet || 12 ||


(4) tryasrakuṇḍam

caturdhā bhedite kṣetre vinyaset pārśvayordvayoḥ |
ekaikamaṃśaṃ tanmānādagrato lāñchayet punaḥ || 13 ||

sūtrayugmaṃ budhaḥ kuryāt tryasraṃ kuṇḍamudāhṛtam |


(5) vṛttakuṇḍam

aṣṭādaśāṃśake kṣetre nyasedekaṃ bahirbudhaḥ || 14 ||

bhrāmayettena mānena vṛttaṃ kuṇḍamanuttamam |


(6) ṣaḍasrakuṇḍam

aṣṭadhā vibhajet kṣetraṃ madhyasūtrasya pārśvayoḥ || 15 ||

bhāgaṃ nyasedekamekaṃ mānenā'nena madhyataḥ | 
kuryāt pārśvadvaye matsyacatuṣkaṃ tantravittamaḥ || 16 ||

sūtraṣaṭkaṃ tato dadyāt ṣaḍasraṃ kuṇḍamuttamam |

p. 305)

(7) padmakuṇḍam

caturasrīkṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ vibhajyā'ṣṭādaśāṃśataḥ || 17 ||

ekaṃ bhāgaṃ bahirnyasya bhrāmayettena varttulam |
vṛttāni karṇikādīnāṃ bahistrīṇi prakalpayet || 18 ||

padmakuṇḍamidaṃ proktaṃ vilocanamanoharam |


(8) aṣṭāsrakuṇḍam

pūrvoktaṃ vibhajet kṣetraṃ caturviṃśatibhāgataḥ || 19 ||

ekaṃ bhāgaṃ bahirnyasya caturasraṃ prakalpayet |
antaḥsthacaturasrasya koṇārdhārdhapramāṇataḥ || 20 ||

bāhyasya caturasrasya koṇābhyāṃ parilāñchayet |
diśaṃ prati yathānyāyamaṣṭasūtrāṇi pātayet || 21 ||

aṣṭāsraṃ kuṇḍametaddhi tantravidbhirudāhṛtam |


khātamānam

yāvān kuṇḍasya vistāraḥ khananaṃ tāvadīritam || 22 ||


mekhalālakṣaṇaṃ tanmānañca

kuṇḍānāṃ yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ mekhalānāñca tādṛśam |
kuṇḍāderdvyaṅgulaṃ tyaktvā karttavyā mekhalāḥ śubhāḥ || 23 ||

p. 306)

kuṇḍānāṃ mekhalāstisro muṣṭimātreṇa tāḥ kramāt |
utsedhāyāmato jñeyā dvyekārdhāṅgulisammitāḥ || 24 ||

aratnimātrakuṇḍasya tāstridvikāṅgulātmikāḥ |
aṣṭādaśāṅgulaṃ jñeyam aratnimānaṃ dhīmatā || 25 ||

dvādaśāṅgulibhiścaiva hastārdhaṃ parikīrttitam |


nemilakṣaṇam

ekahastamite kuṇḍe vedāgninayanāṅgulāḥ || 26 ||

mekhalānāṃ bhavedantaḥ parito nemiraṅgulāt |
eka hastasya kuṇḍasya vardhayet tatkramāt sudhīḥ || 27 ||

kuṇḍe dvihaste tā jñeyā rasavedaguṇāṅgulāḥ |
caturhasteṣu kuṇḍeṣu vasutarkayugāṅgulāḥ || 28 ||

kuṇḍe rasakare tāḥ syurdaśāṣṭarasasammitāḥ |
vasuhastamite kuṇḍe bhānupaṅktyāṣṭakāṅgulāḥ || 29 ||

daśahastamite kuṇḍe manubhānudaśāṅgulāḥ |
vistārotsedhato jñeyā mekhalāḥ sarvato budhaiḥ || 30 ||

p. 307)

yonilakṣaṇam

hoturagre yonirāsāmuparyaśvatthapatravat |
muṣṭyaratrekahastānāṃ kuṇḍānāṃ yonirīritā |
ṣaṭcaturdvyaṅgulāyāmavistāronnatiśalinī || 31 ||


nālalakṣaṇam tanmānañca

yonyāḥ paścimato nālaṃ vistāraṃ caturaṅgulam |
tridvyekāṅgulamānena kramānmūlāgramiṣyate || 32 ||

nālamekhalayormadhye paridhisthāpanāya ca |
sthalādārabhya nālaṃ syāt yonyā madhye sarandhrakam || 33 ||

nārpayet kuṇḍakoṇeṣu yoniṃ tāṃ tantravittamaḥ |
muṣṭyaratnyekahastānāṃ kuṇḍānāṃ yonirīritā || 34 ||

ekaikāṅgulato yoniṃ kuṇḍeṣvanyeṣu vardhayet |
yavadvayakrameṇaiva yonyagramapi vardhayet || 35 ||

p. 308)

nābhipramāṇam

kuṇḍānāṃ kalpayedantarnābhimambujasannibhām |
tattat kuṇḍānurūpaṃ vā mānamasya nigadyate || 36 ||

muṣṭyaratnyekahastānāṃ nābhirutsedhatārataḥ |
netravedāṅgulopetā kuṇḍeṣvanyeṣu vardhayet || 37 ||

yavadvayakrameṇaiva nābhiṃ pṛthagudāradhīḥ |
yonikuṇḍe yonimabjakuṇḍe nābhiṃ vivarjayet || 38 ||

nābhikṣetraṃ tridhā bhitvā madhye kurvīta karṇikām |
bahiraṃśadvayenāṣṭau patrāṇi parikalpayet || 39 ||

muṣṭimātramitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ śatārdhaṃ sampracakṣate |
śatahome'ratnimātraṃ hastamātraṃ sahasrake || 40 ||

koṇasūtrapramāṇena kuryāddhastakuṇḍakam |
caturhastādike'pyevaṃ karttavyaṃ sādhakena ca || 41 ||

dvihastamayute lakṣe caturhastamudīritam |
daśalakṣeṣu ṣaṭkaraṃ koṭyāmaṣṭakaraṃ smṛtam || 42 ||

(yadvā) 
ekahastamitaṃ kuṇḍamekalakṣe vidhīyate |

p. 309)

lakṣāṇāṃ daśakaṃ yāvat tāvaddhastena vardhayet || 43 ||

daśahastamitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ koṭihome ca dṛśyate |


kuṇḍānāṃ phalakathanam

sarvasiddhikaraṃ kuṇḍaṃ caturasramudāhṛtam || 44 ||

putrapradaṃ yonikuṇḍaṃ ardhacandraṃ śubhapradam |
śatrukṣayakaraṃ tryasraṃ vartulaṃ śāntikarmaṇi || 45 ||

cheda māraṇayoḥ kuṇḍaṃ ṣaḍasraṃ padmasannibham |
vṛṣṭidaṃ rogaśamanaṃ kuṇḍamaṣṭāsramīritam || 46 ||

(yadvā) 
yonikuṇḍaṃ vākpradaṃ syādabjamākṛṣṭikaraṇam |
lakṣmīpradaṃ varttulañcacandrārdhaṃ tu bhayaṃ haret || 47 ||

vṛttaṃ trikoṇakuṇḍaṃ hi khecarīsiddhidāyakam |
caturasraṃ śāntilakṣmīpuṣṭi-ṛddhipradāyakam || 48 ||

kāraṇaṃ sarvasampatterdhanasaubhāgyavarddhanam |
ṣaḍasraṃ sarvasampattikāraṇaṃ pauruṣapradam || 49 ||

p. 310) 

aṣṭāsraṃ tu tathā jñeyaṃ samīhitaphalapradam |
guṇāni sarvakāryāṇi caturasre bhavanti hi || 50 ||

viprāṇāṃ caturasraṃ syād rājñāṃ vartulamiṣyate |
vaiśyānāmardhacandrābhaṃ śūdrāṇāṃ tryasramīritam || 51 ||

caturasraṃ tu sarveṣāṃ kiñcittāntrikasammatiḥ |
kuṇḍasya rūpaṃ jānīyāt paramaṃ prakṛtervapuḥ || 52 ||


kuṇḍākṛtibhedenaphalabhedaḥ

prācyāṃ śiraḥ samākhyātaṃ bāhū dakṣiṇasaumyayoḥ |
udaraṃ kuṇḍamityuktaṃ yoniḥ pādau tu paścime || 53 ||

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ sthaṇḍile vā samācaret |
hastamātraṃ tu tat kuryādbālukābhiḥ suśobhanam || 54 ||

aṅgulotsedhasaṃyuktaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ |
eṣāmekatamaṃ kuṇḍaṃ niṣpādya sruksruvau tataḥ || 55 ||

p. 311)

prakalpayet srucaṃ yāge vakṣyamāṇena vartmanā |
śrīphalaśiṃśapākṣīraśākhiṣvekatamaṃ budhaḥ || 56 ||

gṛhītvā vibhajeddhastamātraṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatā punaḥ |
viṃśatyaṃśairbhaveddaṇḍo vedī tairaṣṭabhirbhavet || 57 ||

ekāṃśena mitaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ saptabhāgamitaṃ mukham |
vedītryaṃśena vistāraḥ kaṇṭhasya parikīrttitaḥ || 58 ||

agraṃ kaṇṭhasamānaṃ syāt mukhe mārgaṃ prakalpayet |
kaniṣṭhāṅgulimānena sarpiṣo nirgamāya ca || 59 ||


uddiṣṭavedhā racanāprakāram

vedīmadhye vidhātavyā bhāgenaikena karṇikā |
vidadhīta bahistasyā ekāṃśenā'bhito'vaṭam || 60 ||

tasya khātaṃ tribhirbhāgairvṛttamardhāṃśato bhavet |
aṃśenaikena parito dalāni parikalpayet || 61 ||

mekhalā mukhavedyoḥ syāt parito'rdhāṃśamānataḥ |
daṇḍamūlāgrayorgaṇḍī guṇavedāṅgulaiḥ kramāt || 62 ||

p. 312)

gaṇḍīyugmaṃ yamāṃśaiḥ syāddaṇḍasyā'nāha īritaḥ |
ṣaḍbhiraṃśaiḥ pṛṣṭabhāgo vedyāḥ kūrmākṛtirbhavet || 63 ||

haṃsasya vā hastino vā potriṇo vā mukhaṃ likhet |
mukhasya pṛṣṭhabhāgasya samproktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ srucaḥ || 64 ||

srucaścaturviṃśatibhirbhāgairvā racayet sruvam |
dvāviṃśatyā daṇḍamānamaṃśairetasya kīrttitam |
caturbhiraṃśairānāhaḥ karṣājyagrāhi tacchiraḥ || 65 ||

aṃśadvayena nikhanet paṅke mṛgapadākṛti |
daṇḍamūlāgrayorgaṇḍī bhavet kaṅkaṇabhūṣitā |
mūlāt ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ tyaktvā agrecaina dvisaptakam || 66 ||

sruvantu dhārayenmantrī mūlameva na darśayet |
sruvasya vidhirākhyāto yathātantravidhānataḥ || 67 ||


kuṇḍasya aṣṭādaśa saṃskārāḥ

saṃskārāṇi ca vā kuryāt kuṇḍasya sādhakottamaḥ |
aṣṭādaśāḥ syuḥsaṃskārāḥ kuṇḍānāṃ tantracoditāḥ || 68 ||

mūlena vīkṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā astreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ tataḥ |

p. 313)

tenaiva tāḍanaṃ darbhairvarmaṇā'myukṣaṇaṃ matam || 69 ||

astreṇa khanoddhārau hanmantreṇa prapūraṇam |
samīkaraṇamastreṇa varmaṇā secanaṃ matam || 70 ||

kuṭṭanaṃ hetimantreṇa kavacena tu mārjanam |
vilepanaṃ kalārūpakalpanaṃ tadanantaram || 71 ||

trisūtrīkaraṇaṃ paścāt hṛdayenā'rcanaṃ tathā |
śareṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ hṛdayena kuśaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 72 ||

catuṣpathaṃ tanutreṇa tanatayādakṣapāṭanam |
yāge kuṇḍāni saṃskuryātsaṃskārairebhirīritaiḥ || 73 ||


prakārāntarasaṃskārakathanam

athavā tāni saṃskuryāccaturbhivīkṣaṇādibhiḥ |
tisrastisro likhed rekhā hṛdā prāgudagapragāḥ || 74 ||

p. 314)

prāgaprāṇāṃ smṛtā devā mukundeśapurandarāḥ |
rekhāṇāmudagaprāṇāṃ brahmavaivaśvendavaḥ || 75 ||

ṣaṭkoṇāntargatāṃ yoniṃ maṇḍalaṃ cāṣṭapatrakam |
vṛttaṃ kṣitigṛhaṃ caiva kuṇḍamadhye likhettataḥ || 76 ||

tāreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā pūjayet pīṭhadevatāḥ |
maṇḍale kālavahniñca rudrameva tataḥ param || 77 ||

ādhāraśaktiṃ kūrmañca pṛthivīṃ ratnadvīpakam |
maṇigehaṃ prapūjyātha kalpavṛkṣaṃ tataḥ sudhīḥ || 78 ||

suvarṇavedikāmadhye āgneyādiṣu tatparam |
dharmaṃ jñānañca vairāgyaṃ aiśvaryañca yathākramāt || 79 ||

pūrvādyetāṃśca madhye ca punarmadhye tato yajet |
paramānandakandañca samvinnālaṃ tataḥ param || 80 ||

sarvatattvārthakābjañca prakṛtipatrakaṃ tataḥ |
vikāramayakeśaraṃ mātṛkāmayakarṇikām || 81 ||

sūryasya maṇḍalaṃ somamaṇḍalaṃ vahnimaṇḍalam |
sattvarajastamaścaiva ātmānamantarātmanām || 82 ||

paramātmānamabhyarcya jñānātmānaṃ tataḥ param |
nāmādyakṣarabījādyān caturthyantānnamo'nvitān || 83 ||

praṇavādyān yajedetān varttule bhuvaneśvarīm |

p. 315)

vāgīśvarīñca vāgīśaṃ yajedyogasvarūpakam || 84 ||

pīṭhāya nama ityuktvā karṇikāyāṃ prapūjayet |


vāgīśvarīdhyānam

vāgīśvarīmṛtusnātāṃ nīlendīvarasannibhām || 85 ||

vāgīśvareṇa saṃyuktāmupacāraiḥ prapūjayet |
māyābījaṃ samuccārya vāgīśvarīñca ṅeyutām || 86 ||

namo'nto'yaṃ mahāmantro vāgīśvaryāḥ prakīrttitaḥ |
yogīśvaraṃ svamantreṇa tato vahniṃ samānayet || 87 ||

sūryakāntādisambhūtaṃ yadvā śrotriyagehajam |
tāmrapātre puṭīkṛtya kravyādāṃśaṃ parityajet || 88 ||

vahnibījena cā'streṇa nair-ṛtyāṃ diśi sādhakaḥ |
saṃskuryāttaṃ yathānyāyaṃ caturbhirvīkṣaṇādibhiḥ || 89 ||

audaryavaidavāgnibhyāṃ bhaumasyaikyaṃ smaran vasoḥ |
yojayedvahnibījena caitanyaṃ pāvake tataḥ || 90 ||

p. 316)

tāreṇa mantritaṃ mantrī dhenumudrāmṛtīkṛtam |
astreṇa rakṣitaṃ paścāt kavacenā'vaguṇṭhitam || 91 ||

arcitaṃ triḥ paribhrāmya kuṇḍasyopari deśikaḥ |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tadā tāramantroccāraṇapūrvakam || 92 ||

ātmano'bhimukhaṃ vahniṃ jānuspṛṣṭamahītalaḥ |
śivabījadhiyā devyā yonāveva viniḥkṣipet || 93 ||

paścād devasya devyāśca dadyādācamanādikam |
jvālinīṃ darśayitvā ca jvālayenmanunā'munā || 94 ||


vahnimantrakathanam

citpiṅgala hana daha pacayugmānyudīrya ca |
sarvajñā'jñāpaya svāhā matro'yaṃ samudīritaḥ || 95 ||

agniṃ prajvalitaṃ vande jātavedaṃ hutāśanam |
suvarṇavarṇamamalaṃ samiddhaṃ viśvatomukham || 96 ||

upatiṣṭheta vidhivat manunā'nena pāvakam |
vinyasedātmano dehe mantrī jihvā havirbhujaḥ || 97 ||

liṅgapāyuśirovaktraghrāṇanetreṣu sarvataḥ |
vahnīrāghīṃśasaṃyuktāḥ sādiyāntāḥ savindavaḥ || 98 ||

vahnermantrāḥ samuddiṣṭāḥ jihvānāṃ sapta deśikaiḥ |
jihvāstāstrividhāḥ proktā guṇabhedeṣu karmasu || 99 ||

p. 317)


sattvarajastamobhedena vahnijihvākathanam

hiraṇyā gaganā raktā kṛṣṇā'nyā suprabhā matā |
bahurūpā'tiriktā ca sātvikyo yāgakarmasu || 100 ||

padmarāgā suvarṇā'nyā tṛtīyā bhadralohitā |
lohitā'nantaraṃ śvetā dhūminī ca karālikā || 101 ||

rājasyo rasanā vahnervihitāḥ kāmyakarmasu |
viśvamūrttisphuliṅginyau dhūmravarṇā manojavā || 102 ||

lohitā'nyā karālākhyā kālī tāmasya īritāḥ |
etāḥ sapta prayujyante krūrakarmasu mantribhiḥ || 103 ||

svasvanāmasamābhāḥ syurjihvāḥ kalyāṇaretasaḥ |
amartyapitṛgandharvayakṣanāgapiśācakāḥ || 104 ||

rākṣasāḥ saptajihvānāmīritāstvadhidevatāḥ |
vahṇeraṅgamanūn nyasyettanāvuktena vartmanā || 105 ||

p. 318)

sahasrārciḥ svastipūrṇa uttiṣṭhapuruṣaḥ punaḥ |
dhūmavyāpī saptajihvo dhanurdhara itīritaḥ || 106 ||

ṣaḍaṅgamanavaḥ proktā jātibhiḥ saha saṃyutāḥ |
mūrttīraṣṭau tanau nyasyed deśiko jātavedasaḥ || 107 ||

mūrddhāṃśapārśvakaṭyandhu kaṭī pārśvāṃśukeṣu ca |
pradakṣiṇavaśānnyasyeducyante tā yathākramam || 108 ||


vahnisaṃjñā

jātavedāḥ saptajihvo havyavāhanasañjñakaḥ |
aśvodarajasañjño'nyaḥ punarvaiśvānarāhvayaḥ || 109 ||

kaumāratejāḥ syādviśvamukho devamukhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
tārāgnaye padādyāḥ syurnatyantā vahnimūrttayaḥ || 110 ||

āsanaṃ kalpayitvā'gnermūrttiṃ tasya cintiyet || 111 ||


vahnidhyānam

iṣṭaṃ śaktiṃ svastikābhītimuccai-
rdīrghairdorbhiḥ dhārayantaṃ javābham |
hemākalpaṃ padmasaṃsthaṃ trinetraṃ 
dhyāyedvahniṃ baddhamauliṃ jaṭābhiḥ || 112 ||

pariṣiñcettatastoyairviśuddhairmekhalopari |
darbhairagarbhamadhyasthamekhalāyāṃ paristaret || 113 ||

p. 319)

niḥkṣipeddikṣu paridhīn prācīvarjañca mantravit |
prādakṣiṇyena sampūjyāste brahmādyaṣṭamūrttayaḥ || 114 ||

dhyāyan vahniṃ yajenmadhye gandhādyairmanunā'munā |
vaiśvānara jātavedapadaṃ paścādihā'vaha || 115 ||

lohitākṣa padasyānte sarvakarmāṇi sādhaya |
vahnijāyāvadhiḥ prokto manuḥ pāvakavallabhaḥ || 116 ||

madhye ṣaṭsvapi koṇeṣu jihvā vahnestato yajet |
keśareṣūktamārgeṇa pūjayedaṅgadevatāḥ || 117 ||

daleṣu pūjayenmūrttīḥ śaktisvastikadhāriṇīḥ |
dalāgre mātaraḥ pūjyāḥ sāsitāṅgādibhairavāḥ || 118 ||

lokapālāṃstato dikṣu pūjayeduktalakṣaṇān |
paścādādāya pāṇibhyāṃ sruksruvau tāvadhomukhau || 119 ||

p. 320)

triḥ sampratāpayedvahnau darbhānādāya deśikaḥ |
tadagramadhyamūlāni śodhayettau yathākramāt || 120 ||

gṛhītvā vāmahastena prokṣayeddakṣiṇena tau |
punaḥ pratāpya tau mantrī darbhānagnau viniḥkṣipet || 121 ||

ātmano dakṣiṇe bhāge sthāpayettau kuśāstare |


vīkṣaṇādiṣaṭsaṃskārāḥ

ājyasthālīmathā'dāya prokṣayedarghavāriṇā || 122 ||

tasyāmājyaṃ viniḥkṣipya saṃskuryādvīkṣaṇādibhiḥ |
niruhya vāyavyeṅgārān hṛdā teṣu niveśayet || 123 ||

idaṃ tāpanamuddiṣṭaṃ deśikaistantravedibhiḥ |
dīptadarbhayugmamājye kṣiptvā'nale kṣipettataḥ || 124 ||

gururhṛdayamantreṇa pavitrīkaraṇantvidam |

p. 321)

dīptena darbhayugmena nīrājyājyaṃ savarmaṇā || 125 ||

agnau visarjayeddarbhamabhidyotanamīritam |
ghṛte prajvalitān darbhān pradarśyā'strāṇunā guruḥ || 126 ||

jātavedasi tān nyasyedudyotanamīritam |
gṛhītvā ghṛtamaṅgārān saṃyojyā'gnau jalaṃ spṛśet || 127 ||

aṅguṣṭhopakaniṣṭhābhyāṃ darbhau prādeśasammitau |
dhṛtvotpunīyādastreṇa ghṛtamutpavanantvidam || 128 ||

tadvaddhṛdayamantreṇa kuśābhyāmātmano mukham |
ghṛtena plāvanaṃ kuryāt saṃskārāḥ ṣaḍudīritāḥ || 129 ||

prādeśamātraṃ sagranthi darbhayugmaṃ ghṛtāntare |
niḥkṣipya bhāgau dvau kṛtvā pakṣau śukletarau smaret || 130 ||

vāme nāḍīmiḍāṃ bhāge dakṣiṇe piṅgalāṃ punaḥ |
suṣumṇāṃ madhyato dhyātvā kuryāddhomaṃ yathāvidhi || 131 ||

sruveṇa dakṣiṇabhāgādādāyā'jyaṃ hṛdā sudhīḥ |
juhuyādagnaye svāhā agnerdakṣiṇalocane || 132 ||

p. 322)

vāmatastadvadādāya vāme vahnivilocane |
juhuyādatha somāya svāheti hṛdayāṇunā || 133 ||

hṛnmantreṇa sruveṇā'jyaṃ bhāgādādāya dakṣiṇāt |
juhuyādagnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti tanmukhe || 134 ||

iti sampātayed bhāgeṣvājyasyā'thā'hutiṃ kramāt |
ityagninetravaktrāṇāṃ kuryādudghāṭanaṃ sudhīḥ || 135 ||


vahneḥ garbhādhānādisaṃskārāḥ

satārābhirvyāhṛtibhirājyena juhuyāt punaḥ |
juhuyādagnimantreṇa trivārān sādhakottamaḥ || 136 ||

garbhādhānādikāṃ vahneḥ kriyāṃ nirvartayet kramāt |
aṣṭābhirājyāhutibhiḥ praṇavena pṛthak pṛthak || 137 ||

garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ punaḥ |
anantaraṃ jātakarma syānnāmakaraṇaṃ tathā || 138 ||

upaniṣkramaṇaṃ paścādannaprāśanamīritam |
cūḍopanayane bhūyo mahānāmnā mahāvratam || 139 ||

tathopaniṣadaṃ paścāt godānodvāhakau mṛtiḥ |
śubheṣu syurvivāhāntāḥ kriyāstāḥ krūrakarmasu || 140 ||

p. 323)

maraṇāntāḥ samuddiṣṭā vahnerāgamavedibhiḥ |
tataśca pitarau tasya sampūjyā'tmani yojayet || 141 ||

samidhaḥ pañca juhuyādādāya ghṛtasaṃplutāḥ |
mantrairjihvāṅgamūrttīnāṃ kramādvahneryathāvidhi || 142 ||

pratyekaṃ juhuyādekāmāhutiṃ mantravittamaḥ |
avadāya sruveṇā'jyaṃ catuḥ sruci pidhāya tām || 143 ||

sruveṇa tiṣṭhannevāgnau deśiko yatamānasaḥ |
juhuyādvahnimantreṇa vauṣaḍantena sampade || 144 ||

vighneśvarasya mantreṇa juhuyādāhutīrdaśa |


vighnarājamantroddhāraḥ

tāraṃ ramā tathā māyāṃ klīṃ glauṃ tadanantaram || 145 ||

gaṃ gaṇapataye varavaradānte sarvapadaṃ janam |
me vaśamānaya procya vahnijāyā tataḥ param || 146 ||

kathitaṃ vighnarājasya mantrarājaṃ sudurlabham |
sāmānyaṃ sarvadevānāmetadagnimukhaṃ matam || 147 ||

p. 324)

ekīkaraṇam

tataḥ pīṭhaṃ samabhyarcya devatāyā hutāśane |
arcayedvahnirūpāṃ tāṃ devatāmiṣṭadāyinīm || 148 ||

tanmukhe juhuyānmantrī pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
ājyena mūlamantreṇa vahnyaikīkaraṇaṃ tvidam || 149 ||

vahnidaivatayoraikyamātmanā saha bhāvayan | 


nāḍīsandhānam

mūlamantreṇa juhuyādājyairekādaśā'hutīḥ || 150 ||

nāḍīsandhānamuddiṣṭametadāgamavedibhiḥ |
juhuyādaṅgamukhyānāmāvṛtīnāmanukramāt || 151 ||

ekaikāmāhutiṃ samyak sarpiṣā deśikottamaḥ |
trimadhvaktaistilairmantrī hunejjapadaśāṃśataḥ || 152 ||

mudrā jñeyā ca dhīreṇa pāṇihomeṣu sarvadā |
śūkarī karasaṅkocī haṃsī muktakaniṣṭhikā || 153 ||

kaniṣṭhā tarjanī muktā mṛgīti kathitā tridhā |
abhicāre ca puṣṭau ca śāntyādau ca yathākramāt || 154 ||

p. 325)

riktahastena havane phalābhavaḥ

riktahastena yaddānaṃ hūyate ca hutāśane |
dātuḥ puṇyaphalaṃ nāsti hotā ca narakaṃ vrajet || 155 ||

evaṃ jñātvā viśeṣajño homaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ |
ājyena madhunā caiva śarkarā ca madhutrayam || 156 ||

dravyaiḥ pūrvoditairvāpi homayet saṃskṛte'nale |
svakalpavihitaidravyaiḥ sādhako yatamānasaḥ || 157 ||

japasaṃkhyādaśāṃśena homayedvidhipūrvakam |
anukte tu havirdravye tilājyaṃ havirucyate || 158 ||

chinnāyāḥ kālikāyāśca viśeṣastanyate'dhunā |
divā japadaśāṃśena madhuratrayasaṃyutam || 159 ||

hutvā saṃkhyāṃ samāpyātha rātrikalpaṃ samācaret |
yonikuṇḍe sthite sarpirmāṃsamatsyayutaṃ bhṛśam || 160 ||

p. 326)

kāmyakarmavarṇanam

atha kāmyavidhiṃ vakṣye sarvaśākteṣu siddhidam |
mallikājātipunnāgairhomād bhāgyodayo bhavet || 161 ||

phalairvilvasamudbhūtaistatpatrairvā hutād bhavet |
rājaputtrasya rājyāptiḥ paṅkajaiḥ śriyamāpnuyāt || 162 ||

utpalairvaśayedviśvaṃ lakṣmīṃ puṣpaistathā naraḥ |
bandhūkapuṣpairvakulairjavotthaiḥ kiṃśukodbhavaiḥ || 163 ||

vaśyāya juhuyānmantrī madhunā saha siddhaye |
lavaṇairmadhuropetairhutvā karṣati sundarīm || 164 ||

vañjulasya samiddhomāt vṛṣṭiṃ vitanute'cirāt |
kṣīrāktairamṛtākhaṇḍairhomo nāśayati jvaram || 165 ||

dūrvābhirāyurāpnoti kadambairvaśyamāpnuyāt |
rājīlavaṇa homena śatrūna mārayate'cirāt || 166 ||

antena madhuyuktena nirdhano dhanamāpnuyāt |
sarvaṃ trimadhuropetaṃ homadravyamudāhṛtam || 167 ||

p. 327)

nandyāvarttabhavaiḥ puṣpairhomādvāgmī bhavennaraḥ |
bilvaprasūnairjuhuyādīpsitāṃ śriyamāpnuyāt || 168 ||

palāśakusumairhomāttejasvī jāyate naraḥ |
candanāgurukarpūrarocanākuṅkamādibhiḥ || 169 ||

vaśyāya juhuyānmantrī vaśayedakhilaṃ jagat |
nirguṇḍīmūlahomena nigaḍānmucyate naraḥ || 170 ||

nimbatailānvitairloṇairhomaḥ śatruvināśanaḥ |
haridrācūrṇasaṃmiśrairlavaṇaiḥ stambhayet parān || 171 ||

rasavadbhiḥ phalaiḥ pakvaiḥ puṣpaiḥ parimalānvitaiḥ |
hutvā samyagavāpnoti sādhakaḥ sarvamīpsitam || 172 ||

mālatīpuṣpahomena vāgmī drutakavirbhavet |
vaśayedvacahomena narānnarapatīnapi || 173 ||

padmairhutvā jayet śatrūn dūrvābhiḥ śāntimāpnuyāt |
palāśakusumaiḥ puṣṭiṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ śriyaṃ labhet || 174 ||

p. 328)

śaśakasya tu māṃsena madhunā loḍhitena ca |
hutvā hemamavāpnoti vittavidyāvarastriyaḥ || 175 ||

kākapakṣaiḥ kṛte home vidveṣaṃ tanute nṛṇām |
marīcahomairmaraṇaṃ ripurāpnoti niścitam || 176 ||

ayutaṃ vaṭavṛkṣotthaiḥ saṃśuddhirarcite'nale |
homaṃ samidvaraiḥ kuryānnāśayatyāpadāṃ kulam || 177 ||

apāmārgasamidbhirvā tilairvā kānanodbhavaiḥ |
vrīhibhirjuhuyāddhīro vatsarāt vrīhimān bhavet || 178 ||

dūrvāhomena dīrghāyurmadhunā vatsarād bhavet |
āragvadhaiḥsamṛddhiḥ syādājyena labhate dhanam || 179 ||

godugdhena gavāṃ vṛddhimāpnuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
kāmyahomaṃ sadā dhīraḥ kuryādayutasaṃkhyayā || 180 ||

latāpuṣpānvitaṃ kṛtvā parṇānāṃ śatakaṃ sudhīḥ |
tāni saṃmantrya vidhivadasakṛt sādhakottamaḥ || 181 ||

tato vai homayettāni saṃskṛte'gnau yathāvidhi |
yugānāmayutaṃ tena pūjanaṃ jāyate tathā || 182 ||

p. 329)

anena kramayogena yaścared bhuvi sādhakaḥ |
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcittriṣu lokeṣu vidyate || 183 ||

vīro bhavati vāgmīkaḥ sarvasiddhimupālabhet |
hunedājyena takreṇa māṃsena rudhireṇa ca || 184 ||

raktapuṣpeṇa sājyena saraktena viśeṣataḥ |
āmiṣādibhirapyevaṃ śmaśāne juhuyāt sudhīḥ || 185 ||

māṃsaṃ raktaṃ tilaṃ keśaṃ nakhaṃ bhaktañca pāyasam |
ājyaṃ caiva viśeṣeṇa juhuyāt sarvasiddhaye || 186 ||

evaṃ kṛtena sarvatra labhate siddhimuttamām |
yat yat kāmayate kāmaṃ tattadāpnoti niścitam || 187 ||

devavanmānuṣo bhutvā bhunakti bahulaṃ sukham |
yonikuṇḍe hunedvidvān yathāvat sādhite'nale || 188 ||

haviṣā caiva bhaktena māṃsena rudhireṇa ca |
raktacandanapuṣpeṇa kṛṣṇena ca viśeṣataḥ || 189 ||
