śmaśāne juhuyānmantrī śuddhāmikṣābhirapyatha |
dhane dhaneśvaro bhūyādājñayā ca śacīpatiḥ || 190 ||

balena pavano bhūtvā devavadvicared bhuvi |

p. 330)

atha dhyātvā careddhomaṃ dadhisiddhānnataṇḍulaiḥ || 191 ||

sahasraikavidhānena samastaphalasiddhaye |
vidyākāmena hotavyaṃ śarkarāyutapāyasaiḥ || 192 ||

malanāśyaṃ bhavatyeva sadyo vidyācaturdaśa |
rajasvalāyā vastreṇa madhunā saha sarpiṣā || 193 ||

ayutaikaṃ vidhānena siddhayo'ṣṭau bhavanti hi |
sadyomārjāramāṃsena ghṛtena madhunā saha || 194 ||

cāṇḍālakeśayuktena sarvasiddhirbhaved dhruvam |
bandhūkakusumairhomaṃ kṛtvā raktānvitairdhruvam || 195 ||

durbhagāyā bhaved bhāgyaṃ sarvasiddhirna saṃśayaḥ |
rambhāpuṣpaṃ śālamatsyaṃ juhuyānmantravittamaḥ || 196 ||

sarvasiddhimanuprāpya ante mokṣamavāpnuyāt |
sākṣataṃ vilvapatrañca juhuyācca sahasrakam || 197 ||

labhate mañjulāṃ vāṇīṃ mahāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
evaṃ jñātvā prakurvīta dravyamānamathocyate || 198 ||

p. 331)

homadravyādimānam

karṣamānaṃ ghṛtaṃ home śuktimātraṃ payaḥ smṛtam |
pañcagavyañca tatsaṃkhyaṃ bhavenmadhu tathā samam || 199 ||

dugdhānnamakṣasadṛśaṃ dadhi prasṛtimānakam |
lājā muṣṭimitā jñeyā pṛthukaṃ śuktimeva ca || 200 ||

guḍaṃ palārdhamānaṃ syāt śarkarā'pi tathā samā |
matsyaṃ māṃsaṃ tathā jñeyaṃ rudhiraṃ śuktimātrakam || 201 ||

grāsārdhaṃ carumānaṃ syādikṣu parvāvadhi smṛtam |
ekaikapatrapuṣpāṇi tathāpūpāni kalpayet || 202 ||

kadalīphalanāraṅgaphalānyekaikaśo viduḥ |
mātuluṅgaṃ catuḥkhaṇḍaṃ panasaṃ daśadhā bhavet || 203 ||

aṣṭadhā nārikelañca mālūrañca tridhā bhavet |
kapitthañca tridhā jñeyaṃ ūrvārukaṃ tridhā bhavet || 204 ||

p. 332)

phalānyanyānyakhaṇḍāni samidhaḥ syurdaśāṅgulāḥ |
dūrvātrayaṃsamuddiṣṭaṃ guḍuco caturaṅgulā || 205 ||

vrīhayo muṣṭimānāḥ syurmudgamāṣayavā api |
taṇḍulāḥ syustadardhañca athavā muṣṭisammitāḥ || 206 ||

godhūmā raktakalabhā muṣṭimānāstilāstathā |
muṣṭimānāḥ sarṣapāśca lavaṇaṃ śuktimānakam || 207 ||

marīcāni ca viṃśatiḥ puraṃ badarasaṃmitam |
rāmaṭhaṃ tatsamaṃ jñeyaṃ rocanā tatsamā bhavet || 208 ||

candanāgurukarpūrakastūrīkuṅkumāni ca |
tintiḍībījamānāni samuddiṣṭāni deśikaiḥ || 209 ||

vaiśvānaraṃ sthitaṃ dhyāyet samiddhomeṣu deśikaḥ |
śayānamājyahomeṣu niṣaṇṇaḥ śeṣavastuṣu || 210 ||

p. 333)

āsyāntarjuhuyādvahnervipaścit sarvakarmasu |
jvalat śikhā mukhaṃ vahnerboddhavyaṃ sādhakottamaiḥ || 211 ||


vahnivarṇādibhedataḥ śubhāśubhakathanam

śubhāśubhaṃ tathā jñeyaṃ vahnervarṇādibhedataḥ |
svarṇasindūravālārkakuṅkamakṣaudrasannibhaḥ || 212 ||

suvarṇaretaso varṇaḥ śobhanaḥ parikīrttitaḥ |
bherīvāridahastīndradhvanirvahneḥ śubhāvahaḥ || 213 ||

nāgacampakapunnāgapāṭalāyuthikānibhaḥ | 
padmendīvarakalhārasarpirguggulasannibhaḥ || 214 ||

pāvakasya śubho gandha ityuktastantravedibhiḥ |
pradakṣiṇāstyaktakampāḥsuprabhāḥ śikhinaḥ śikhāḥ || 215 ||

śubhadāḥ sādhakānāṃ tu anyathā'niṣṭadā bhavet |
kundendudhavalo dhūmo vahneḥ proktaḥ śubhāvahaḥ || 216 ||

kṛṣṇavarṇo bhaved dhūmo mantriṇaṃ nāśayed dhruvam |
śveto rāṣṭraṃ dahatyāśu vāyasasvarasannibhaḥ || 217 ||

kharasvarasamo vahnerdhvaniḥ sarvavināśakṛt |

p. 334)

pūtigandho hutabhujo hoturduḥkhaprado bhavet || 218 ||

chinnāvarttā śikhā kuryānmṛtyuṃ dhanaparikṣayam |
śukapakṣinibho dhūmaḥ pārāvatasamaprabhaḥ || 219 ||

hāniṃ turagajātīnāṃ gavāñca kurute'cirāt |
evaṃvidheṣu doṣeṣu prāyaścittāya sādhakaḥ || 220 ||


prāyaścitta nirūpaṇam

mūlenā'jyena juhuyāt pañcaviṃśatimāhutīḥ |
svīyasaṃkhyāvidhānena hutvāśeṣaṃ samācaret || 221 ||

srucā pūrṇāhutiṃ kṛtvā mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ |
udvāsya devatāṃ kumbhe sāṅgāvaraṇapūrvikām || 222 ||

punarvyāhṛtibhirhutvā jihvādīnāṃ vibhāvasoḥ |
ekaikāmāhutiṃ hutvā pariṣicyādbhirātmani || 223 ||

pāvakaṃ yojayitvā sve paridhīn saparistarān |
naimittike kāmyahome dahettānna tu nityake || 224 ||

p. 335)

kāmyatarpaṇavidhānam

homasya vidhirākhyātastarpaṇasyāpi kathyate |
tataḥ santarpayenmantrī homasyāpi daśāṃśataḥ || 225 ||

tīrthatoyena dugdhena sarpiṣā surayā'pi vā |
gandhodakena vā kuryāt sarvatra sādhakottamaḥ || 226 ||

homānte devatāyāśca pīṭhaṃ toye vicintayet |
athavā homapātrādau yantraṃ kṛtvā tataḥ param || 227 ||

pūjayitvā tato devīṃ parivāragaṇānvitām |
ādau santarpayedyantre gurvādīṃstu yathākramāt || 228 ||

ekaikamañjaliṃ kṛtvā santarpya raśmivṛdakam |
pūrvadravyairdevamukhe tarpayedvidhinā tataḥ || 229 ||

homasyāpi daśāṃśena tarpaṇañca samācaret |
prasaṅgāt kathyate kāmyatarpaṇañca viśeṣataḥ || 230 ||

iṣṭadevīṃ tataḥ paścāt yathoktasaṃkhyayā budhaḥ |
tarpayitvā mahādevīṃ kāmyatarpaṇamācaret || 231 ||

tarpayecca payobhiśca raktadhārāyutaistathā |
majjābhiśca yathā tattat svakīyena kacena ca || 232 ||

p. 336)

ākarṣitāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ kulaprakṣālanena ca |
meṣamāhiṣaraktena nararaktena caiva hi || 233 ||

mūṣāmārjāraraktena tarpayet paradevatām |
evaṃ tarpaṇamātreṇa sākṣāt siddhīśvaro bhavet || 234 ||

kavitā jāyate tasya drākṣārasaparamparā |
bṛhaspatisamo bhūtvā devīvadbhuvi modate || 235 ||

na tasya pāpapuṇyāni jīvanmukto bhaved dhruvam |
kālāgurudravopetairvaśayejjagatīmimām || 236 ||

sacandanena toyena saubhāgyaṃ labhate naraḥ |
toyaiḥ kuṅkamamiśraiśca stambhayedakhilaṃ jagat || 237 ||

sitāmiśritatoyena bṛhaspatisamo bhavet |
karpūrāktajalenaiva karṣayedasurānapi || 238 ||

rocanāyutatoyena mucyate sarvadurgrahāt |
sarvaśāstreṣu kathitaṃ tarpaṇaṃ śubhadāyakam || 239 ||

p. 337)

unmukhīkālikāvidhānam

unmukhyāḥ kālikāyāśca rātrikalpasya sādhakaḥ |
tarpayenmadirābhiśca matsyamāṃsaiḥ samaṃ budhaḥ || 240 ||

tataḥ sampūrṇasaṃkhyāyāṃ svakalpoktapramāṇataḥ |
sthāpayedātmahṛtpadme parivāragaṇaiḥ saha || 241 ||

devatāñca tataḥ paścāttarpaṇasya daśāṃśataḥ |
abhiṣekaṃ prakurvīta gandhatoyaiḥ susādhakaḥ || 242 ||

gṛhītvā gandhatoyañca mudrayā kumbhasañjñayā |
mūlamantraṃ samuccārya tataścā'mukadevatām || 243 ||

abhiṣiñcāmi tatpaścāt hṛnmanustadanantaram |
ityuccārya svamūrdhni taṃ cintayitvā samantrakam || 244 ||

tataḥ sañcintayed devīṃ sāṅgāvaraṇadevatām |
kṣipettoyaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ gaṇān siñcettataḥ param || 245 ||

abhiṣekaṃ svīyasaṃkhyaṃ vidhāya tadanantaram |
tattanmantrayutān viprān bhojayed devatādhiyā || 246 ||

p. 338)

vidhivat pūjayitvā ca bhojayitvā ca sādhakaḥ |
tattaddevapriyairdravyairabhiṣekadaśāṃśataḥ || 247 ||


chinnākālikāyāḥ divākalpaḥ

chinnāyāḥ kālikāyāśca divākalpasya sādhakaḥ |
taddaśāṃśān haviṣyānnaiḥ bhojayed bhaktibhāvataḥ || 248 ||

tattanmanvavido viprān rātrikalpaṃ samācaret |
hetuṃ māṃsañca matsyañca carvaṇañca pradāpayet || 249 ||


viprabhojanādikathanam

brāhmaṇān devatābuddhyā bhojayitvā ca sādhakaḥ |
siddhamantrī bhavet so'pi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 250 ||

japahomau tarpaṇañcābhiṣeko viprabhojanam |
pañcāṅgeyañca kathitā puraskriyā susiddhaye || 251 ||

(athavā) 
japahomatarpaṇañcā'bhiṣeko'pyaghamarṣaṇam |
sūryārghyaṃ jalapānañca praṇāmaṃ caiva pūjanam || 252 ||

brāhmaṇānāṃ bhojanañca daśāṅgeyaṃ puraskriyā |
tattatkarmadaśāṃśena kuryāddhomādikaṃ budhaḥ || 253 ||

p. 339)

aghamarṣaṇaṃ dineśārghyaṃ kṛtvā pūrvavidhānataḥ |
ravyarghyasya daśāṃśena jalapānaṃ samācaret || 254 ||

ātmānaṃ devatārūpaṃ vibhāvya yatamānasaḥ |
dakṣahaste jalaṃ nītvā mūlānte'mukadevatām || 255 ||

pāyayāmi namaḥ paścāt svavaktraistat pibettataḥ |
tatsaṃkhyāñca samāpyā'tha mūlānte svīyadevatām || 256 ||

namāmīti namaskuryājjalapānadaśāṃśataḥ |
pūjanañca tataḥ kuryāt yathāvidhividhānataḥ || 257 ||

viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ kuryānnamaskāradaśāṃśataḥ |
viprārādhanamātreṇa vyaṅgaṃ sāṅgaṃ bhaved yataḥ || 258 ||

athavā taddine kuryāttattajjapadaśāṃśataḥ |
dvijādīnāñca varṇānāṃ vidhiḥ proktaḥ śubhāvahaḥ || 259 ||

p. 340)

homakarmādyaśaktānāṃ dviguṇo japa īritaḥ |
itareṣāñca varṇānāṃ triguṇādisamīritaḥ || 260 ||

kutrāpi yadi hīnaṃ syāddaśakasyā'ṅgakarmaṇi |
tattaddaśaiva kāryāṇi daśaśūnyaṃ na kārayet || 261 ||

tataḥ sampūjayed bhaktyā sambhārairvividhairgurum |
dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadyāt mantrāṇāñca viśuddhaye || 262 ||

gurave guruputrāya tatpatnyai vā pradāpayet |
tattadabhāvato mantrī kulīnāya kulāya vā || 263 ||

dattvā ca sādhakaśreṣṭho mahāpūjāṃ samāpayet |
tattatkalpasamācārānmantrasiddhiḥ prajāyate || 264 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye trayodaśollāsaḥ || 13 ||



caturdaśa ullāsaḥ 


puraścaraṇanirūpaṇam

athavā'nyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |
śmaśāne tu puraścaryā kathitā bhuvi durlabhā || 1 ||


sarvamantrasādhakānāṃ śmaśānasādhane'dhikāritā

vaiṣṇave gāṇapatye ca śaive caivā'nyamantrake |
śākte caiva viśeṣeṇa sādhayet sādhakottamaḥ || 2 ||


śmaśānasādhanavidhānam

bhaumavāre tamisrāyāṃ yāme yāte ca sādhakaḥ |
aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi || 3 ||

kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣeṇa sādhayedanayā hitam |
akṣālitacitāyāṃ tu sādhako nirbhayaḥ sudhīḥ || 4 ||

sāmiṣānnaṃ guḍaṃ chāgaṃ surāpiṣṭakapāyasam |
nānāphalañca naivedyaṃ svakalpoktena sādhitam || 5 ||

puṣpādikaṃ samānīya suhṛdbhiḥ śastrapāṇibhiḥ |
samānaguṇasampannaiḥ sādhako vītabhīḥ svayam || 6 ||

p. 342)

bhītaścet sādhakastatra caturdikṣu ca sādhakāḥ |
no cet svayaṃ kevalo'sau bhairavaḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 7 ||

vastrālaṅkārabhūṣādyairbhūṣitaḥ pūrvasammukhaḥ |
citāyāḥ paścime bhāge upaviśya sthiraḥ śuciḥ || 8 ||

astrāntamūlamantreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ yāgabhūmiṣu |
gurupādarajo dhyātvā vāmapādapuraḥsaraḥ || 9 ||

gaṇeśaṃ vaṭukaṃ caiva yoginīṃ mātṛkāstathā |
vidhivat pūjayitvā tu puṭāñjaliḥ paṭhedimam || 10 ||


ātmarakṣāmantraḥ

ye cātra saṃsthitā devā rākṣasāśca bhayānakāḥ |
piśācayakṣasiddhāśca gandharvāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ || 11 ||

yoginyo mātaro bhūtā sarvāśca khecarāḥ striyaḥ |
siddhidāstā bhavantvatra tathā ca mama rakṣakāḥ || 12 ||

praṇamyeti citāmadhye puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ kṣipet |
śmaśānādhipatiṃ paścād bhairavaṃ kālabhairavam || 13 ||

mahākālaṃ yajedyatnāt pūrvādidikcatuṣṭaye |
pādyādibhiśca mantrajño baliṃ paścānnivedayet || 14 ||

p. 343)

balipradānam

praṇavaṃ kūrcabījaṃ tu śmaśānādhipa tatparam |
imamante sāmiṣānnaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇadvayaṃ vadet || 15 ||

gahṇāpaya dvayaṃ vighnanivāraṇaṃ tataḥ kuru |
siddhiṃ me ca prayacchā'nte svāhānte'pi baliṃ diśet || 16 ||

dugdhenāpyutsṛjedvāpi pañcagavyena vā tathā |
bhairavabhayānakāt pūrvaṃ māyābījaṃ paṭhet sudhīḥ || 17 ||

pūrvavadbalimuddhṛtya dakṣiṇe balimāharet |
kūrcānte kālaśabdānte bhairaveti tataḥ param || 18 ||

śmaśānādhipataye pūrvapaścime balimāharet |
hūmante ca mahākāla pūrvavaduttare haret || 19 ||

citāmadhye tato dattvā balitrayamanuttamam |
kālarātri mahākāli kālike ghoraniḥsvane || 20 ||

gṛhāṇemaṃ baliṃ mātardehi siddhimanuttamām |
kālakāyai baliṃ dattvā bhūtanāthāya dāpayet || 21 ||

hūmante bhūtanāthānte śmaśānādhipa ityapi |

p. 344)

praṇavādyena manunā dāpayedbalimuttamam || 22 ||

kūrcaṃ sarvagaṇanāthādhipataye tato vadet |
śmaśānamastakecaiva baliṃ pūrvavadāharet || 23 ||

tārādyena baliṃ dattvā pañcagavyena caiva hi |
asthisamprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā tatra pīṭhe manuṃ nyaset || 24 ||

bhūrje vā vaṭapatre vā likhitvā cakramuttamam |
likhitvā svīyamantrañca sthāpayedāsanopari || 25 ||


vighnanivāraṇārthaṃ vīrārdanamantrakathanam

pīṭhamāstīrya tasmin vai baddhapadmāsanaḥ sudhīḥ |
kūrcabījadvayaṃ paścāt māyāyugmaṃ samuddharet || 26 ||

kālike bajradaṃṣṭre ca pracaṇḍe caṇḍanāyike |
dānavān dārayetyuktvā haneti dvitayaṃ tataḥ || 27 ||

śavaśarīre mahāvighnaṃ chedaya dvitayaṃ tataḥ |
svāhānte varmacā'strānto vīrārdanamanurmataḥ || 28 ||

anena mantritaṃ loṣṭraṃ daśa dikṣu viniḥkṣipet |
tanmadhye bhairavo devo na vighnaiḥparibhūyate || 29 ||

yadi pramādavaśenaiva sādhako bhayavihvalaḥ |

p. 345)

arkendusitavāṭyālamūlairnirmitavarttikām || 30 ||

pradīpaṃ gartte saṃsthāpya astramantreṇa sādhakaḥ |
astraṃ prapūjayettatra dīpaṃ saṃrakṣayettataḥ || 31 ||

hate tasmin mahādīpe vighnaiśca paribhūyate |
svasvakalpoktavidhinā bhūtaśuddhyādikañcaret || 32 ||


ṣoḍhānyāsakathanam

ṣoḍhāṃ vā tārakaṃ vāpi vinyasya pūjayettataḥ |
nānāvidyāprabhedena ṣoḍhā ca bahudhābhavet || 33 ||

tārakaḥ sarvaśāstreṣu kathyate tantravartmanā |
vinyasenmātṛkāsthāne mātṛkāṃ tārasampuṭām || 34 ||

mātṛkāpuṭitaṃ tāraṃ tārakaḥ parikīrttitaḥ |
vidhivat pūjayitvā ca nijadevīṃ susādhakaḥ || 35 ||

mantradhyānaparo bhūtvā japenmantramananyadhīḥ |


mantrākṣarasaṃkhyābhedena japasaṃkhyā

ekākṣaraṃ yadi bhaved diksahasraṃ tato japet || 36 ||

dvyakṣare cā'ṣṭasāhasraṃ tryakṣare tvayutārdhakam |
ataḥ parantu mantrajño gajāntaka sahasrakam || 37 ||

p. 346)

niśāyāṃ vā samārabhya udayāntaṃ samācaret |
japādau tu baliṃ dadyāt paścādapi baliṃ haret || 38 ||

japānte japamadhye vā dehi dehīti bhāṣite |
tadāpi ca baliṃdadyāt mahiṣaṃ chāgameva vā || 39 ||


balidānavidhānam

balidānavidhānaṃ hi vakṣye'haṃ siddhihetave |
taruṇaṃ sundaraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ kṣatādidoṣavarjitam || 40 ||

svayaṃ pūrvamukho bhūtvā balimuttaravaktrakam |
samānīya svavāme ca prokṣayettadanantaram || 41 ||

arghodakena vīrendrastārādimanunā'munā |
narmadā yamunā gaṅgā karatoyā sarasvatī || 42 ||

kāverī candrabhāgā ca sindhubhairavasāgarāḥ |
ajasnāne maheśāni sānnidhyamiha kalpaya || 43 ||

oṃ paśupāśavināśāya hemakūṭasthitāya ca |
parāparāya parameṣṭhine hūṅkārāya ca mūrttaye || 44 ||

samprokṣaṇaṃ vidhāyā'tha baliṃ sampūjayettataḥ |

p. 347)

brahmarandhre ca brahmāṇaṃ mastake medinīṃ tathā || 45 ||

karṇayośca tathā'kāśaṃ grīvāyāṃ sarvatomukham |
jyotīṃṣi netrayorviṣṇuṃ vadane paripūjya ca || 46 ||

lalāṭe pūjayeccandraṃ śakraṃ dakṣiṇagaṇḍataḥ |
vāmagaṇḍe tathā vahniṃ grīvāyāṃ samavarttinam || 47 ||

keśāgre nair-ṛtiṃ madhye bhravoścāpi pracetasam |
nāsāmūle ca śvasanaṃ skandhe caiva dhaneśvaram || 48 ||

hṛdaye sarparājendraṃ pūjayitvā paṭhedimam |
ito'nyathā pāpayutaṃ malamūtravasāyutam || 49 ||

taṃ baliṃ nahi gṛhṇāti devatā ca kadācana |
anyeṣāṃ mahiṣādīnāṃ balīnāmatha pūjanāt || 50 ||

kāyomedhyatvamāyāti raktaṃ gṛhṇāti vai śivā |
bhūṣayedraktamālyena sindūreṇa viśeṣataḥ || 51 ||


paśupūjāmantraḥ

taṃ nirīkṣya paṭheddhīro nijadevasvarūpakam |
chāga tvaṃ balarūpeṇa mama bhāgyādupasthitaḥ || 52 ||

praṇamāmi tataḥ sarvarūpiṇaṃ balirūpiṇam |
caṇḍikāprītidānena dāturāpadvināśine || 53 ||

cāmuṇḍābalirūpāya bale tubhyaṃ namo'stute |

p. 348)

yajñārthe balayaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayamevasvayambhuvā || 54 ||

atastvāṃ ghātayāmyadya tasmādyajñe vadho'vadhaḥ |
tato vāgbhavo māyā ca kamalā tadanantaram || 55 ||

ityuccārya balemūrdhniṃ puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ kṣipet |


paśucchedamantraḥ

śivabuddhyā susañcintya taṃ pūjya ca tataḥ param || 56 ||

tataḥ sampūjayet khaḍgaṃ gṛhītvā taṃ paṭhed budhaḥ |
kṛṣṇaṃ pinākapāṇiñca kālarātrisvarūpiṇam || 57 ||

ugraṃ raktāsyanayanaṃ raktamālyānulepanam |
raktāmbaradharaṃ caiva pāśahastaṃ kuṭumbinam || 58 ||

pivamānañca rudhiraṃ bhuñjānaṃ kravyasaṃhatim |
iti dhyātvā susampūjya namaskuryāt prayatnataḥ || 59 ||

āśīviṣasamaḥ khaḍgastīkṣṇadhāro durāsadaḥ |
śrīgarbho vijayaścaiva dharmapāla namo'stu te || 60 ||

puṭāñjaliṃ vidhāyātha paṭhedamuṃ manuṃ budhaḥ |
rasanā tvaṃ caṇḍikāyāḥ suralokaprasādhakaḥ || 61 ||

māyāyugmaṃ tataḥ procya kālikakāli tato vadet |
vajreśvari padasyānte lauhadaṇḍaśca ṅeyutaḥ || 62 ||

hṛdayañca paṭhitvā ca aṅgāya namo yajet |
viṣaṃ māyāyugaṃ kāliyugmaṃ vikaṭaśabdataḥ || 63 ||

daṃṣṭre ca spheṃdvayaṃ paścāt phetkāriṇi ca khādaya |

p. 349)

dvayaṃ chedayayugmañca sarvaduṣṭāṃśca māraya || 64 ||

dvayamajañca khaḍgena chindhidvayaṃ kilidvayam |
cikidvandvaṃ pibadvandvaṃ rudhiraṃ sphroṃ (sphoṃ) yugantataḥ || 65 ||

kilidvayaṃ kāliyugmaṃ hṛnmanuṃ tadanantaram |
anena mantritaṃ khaḍgamuttolya sādhakottamaḥ || 66 ||

dettā pūrvamukho bhūtvā balimuttaravaktrakam |
āṃ hūṃ phaḍiti mantreṇa chedayitvā tataḥ param || 67 ||

rudhiraṃ jalasaindhavaśarkarāmadhusaṃyutam |
gandhapuṣpānvitaṃ kṛtvā utsṛjenmantramuccaran || 68 ||


āpyāyanamantroddhāraḥ

praṇavaṃ vāgbhavaṃ māyāṃ lakṣmīṃ kauśikiśabdataḥ |
rudhireṇa tataḥ paścādāpyāyatāṃ tataḥ param || 69 ||

nivedya rudhiraṃ vīraḥ śiro dadyāt sadīpakam |
na dikṣu vīkṣaṇaṃ kuryāt na ca bandhusamāgamam || 70 ||

p. 350)

jalādidurgasarpāṇāṃ gajānāṃ daṃṣṭriṇāṃ tathā |
pakṣikīṭapiśācānāṃ yadvā manasi saṃsthitam || 71 ||

tatsarvaṃ svapnavaddhyātvā bhayaṃ sarvatra varjayet |
sādhanañca samāpyātha dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadet || 72 ||

pūjādravyaṃ jale caiva gartte vā niḥkṣipettataḥ |
samyak siddhaikamantrasya nā'sādhyaṃ vidyate kvacit || 73 ||

bahumantravataḥ puṃsaḥ kā kathā śiva eva saḥ |
svakulānte puraścaryā rātrau kāryā na cānyathā || 74 ||

sarvasiddhipradaṃ sākṣāt sarvadevanamaskṛtam |
sarvapāpaharaṃ caiva sarvarogavināśanam || 75 ||

nānyat siddhipradaṃ caiva vīrasādhanavarjitam |
mahābalo mahābuddhirmahāsāhasikaḥ śuciḥ || 76 ||

mahāsvaccho dayāvāṃśca sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |
teṣāṃ kṛte pravakṣyāmi vīrasādhanamuttamam || 77 ||

p. 351)

vīrasādhanam

nāsmāt parataraṃ kiñcit satvaraṃ siddhidāyakam |
sarvasiddhirbhavatyeva ahorātre kalau yuge || 78 ||

dvāpare tacca māsena tretāyāṃ hāyanena tu |
kṛte tu daśabhirvarṣaḥ satyaṃ siddhirna saṃśayaḥ || 79 ||

kaṭībandhena vastrañca mūlena paridhāpayet |
dbāhye ca punarvastraṃ mūlena candanādikam || 80 ||

kṛtoṣṇīśaśca mūlena sindūreṇā'rdhapuṇḍrakam |
iṣṭadevaṃ guruṃ natvā rātrau praharamadhyataḥ || 81 ||

kāryā ca sādhakaiḥ sārdhaṃ kulamantraṃ parāmṛśan |
akṣubdho bhuktabhojyastu yadi syādvīrasādhakaḥ || 82 ||

divyo baliḥ paśustatra bhuktvā sādhanamācaret |


śavasādhanam

pūrvoktadivase vīro nānopacārasaṃyutaḥ || 83 ||

matsyamāṃsayutaṃ bhaktaṃ guḍaṃ chāgañca piṣṭakam |
pāyasānnaṃ surāṃ caiva māṣabhaktabaliṃ tathā || 84 ||

tilaṃ kuśaṃ sarṣapañca dīpaṃ caiva sudhūpakam |

p. 352)

elālavaṅgakarpūrajātikhadiramārdrakam || 85 ||

tāmbūlaṃ paṭṭasūtrañca elājinañca kambalam |
caṣakaṃ yajñakāṣṭhañca svaprādeśapramāṇakam || 86 ||

pañcagavyaṃ svakalpoktaṃ pūjādravyaṃ pragṛhya ca |
yaṣṭividdhaṃ śūlaviddhaṃ khaḍgaviddhaṃ payomṛtam || 87 ||

vajraviddhaṃ sarpadaṣṭaṃ cāṇḍālaṃ vā'tibhūtakam |
taruṇaṃ sundaraṃ śūraṃ raṇe naṣṭaṃ samujjvalam || 88 ||

palāyanaviśūnyañca sammukhe raṇavarttinam |
na svayaṃ ghātayedvidvān mantrāṇi tatra varjayet || 89 ||

svecchāmṛtaṃ dvivarṣaṃ ca vṛddhaṃ striyaṃ dvijaṃ tathā |
anāturamṛtaṃ kuṣṭhi saptarātrordhvagaṃ tathā || 90 ||

aṅgahīnañca sanyajya pūrvoktānyatamaṃ param |
gṛhītvā mūlamantreṇa pūjāsthāne samānayet || 91 ||

śūnyāgāre nadītīre parvate nirjane tathā |
bilvamūle śmaśāne vā tatsamīpe vanasthale || 92 ||

p. 353)

dīpasthānaṃ samāśritya bhūmau vīrārdanaṃ likhet |
vīrārdanāṅkite bhūmau māyā moho na vidyate || 93 ||


gurupūjādikam

gurupūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvoktamatamācaret |
ye cātretyādimantreṇa bhūmau puṣpāñjaliṃ kṣipet || 94 ||


aghorāstrādimantrāḥ

aghorāstreṇa rakṣāṃ vai sudarśanena vā punaḥ |
māyāsphuradvayaṃ bhūyaḥ prasphuradvitayaṃ punaḥ || 95 ||

ghoraghoratare'tyante tattvarūpe padaṃtataḥ |
caṭayugmaṃ tadante ca pracaṭadvitayaṃ punaḥ || 96 ||

kahadvayaṃ vamadvandvaṃ tato dahayugaṃtataḥ |
ghātayadvitayaṃ varmaphaḍanto'ghorako manuḥ || 97 ||

hālāhalaṃ samuddhṛtya sahasrārasvarūpakam |
varmāstrānto mahāmantraḥ sudarśanasya kīrttitaḥ || 98 ||

praṇavādyāstramantreṇa prokṣitaṃ pūjayettataḥ |
praṇavaṃ kūrcavajaṃ tu mṛtakāya namaśca phaṭ || 99 ||

puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ dattvā praṇamet sparśapūrvakam |

p. 354)

atīva paramānanda śivānanda kuleśvara || 100 ||

ānandabhairavākāra devīparyaṅka śaṅkara |
vīro'haṃ tvāṃ prapadyāmi santiṣṭha devikārcane || 101 ||

praṇamyānena manunā kṣālayettadanantaram |


śavaśodhanam

praṇavaṃ śabdabījañca mṛtakāya namastataḥ || 102 ||

ityuccāryaṃ gandhatoyaiḥ prakṣālya śavamuttamam |
raktayukto yadi bhavet bhakṣayet kulasādhakam || 103 ||

sudhūpairdhūpayettañca lepayed gandhacandanaiḥ |
upacāraṃ svadakṣe tu sthāpayitvā susādhakaḥ || 104 ||

adhikāriviśeṣeṇa surayā tatra pūjayet |
gṛhītvā tatra dātavyaṃ sarvaṃ naiva ca saṃspṛśet || 105 ||

rakṣāṃ dikṣu prakalpyātha vīrārdanamanuṃ smaran |
mṛdvāsane tato vīro vīrāsana parigrahaḥ || 106 ||

bhūtaśuddhyādikaṃ kṛtvā nyāsajālaṃ pravinyaset |

p. 355)

ātmarakṣāṃ vidhāyātha aghoreṇa samāhitaḥ || 107 ||

oṃ durge durge rakṣaṇīti vahnijāyā tataḥ param |
anena manunā vīraḥ sarṣapān dikṣu niḥkṣipet || 108 ||

oṃ tilo'si somadaivatyo sarveṣāṃ tṛptikārakaḥ |
pitṛṇāṃ svargadātā tvaṃ martyānāṃ mama rakṣakaḥ || 109 ||

bhūtapretapiśācānāṃ vighneṣu śāntikārakaḥ |
iti kṣiptvā tilāṃstatra caturbhāgaṃ śavāditaḥ || 110 ||

iti saṃrakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā śavamātmapuro nayet |
kuśaśayyāṃ paristīrya tatra saṃsthāpayecchavam || 111 ||

elādisahitaṃ vīrastāmbūlaṃ tasya vaktrake |
niḥkṣipya ca tataḥ kuryācchavaṃ paścādadhomukham || 112 ||

sthāpayitvā ca tatpṛṣṭhaṃ candanena vilepayet |
bāhumūlādikaṭyantaṃ caturasraṃ vibhāvya ca || 113 ||

madhye padmaṃ caturdvāraṃ dalāṣṭakasamanvitam |
tatra caileyamajinaṃ kambalāntaritaṃ nyaset || 114 ||

śmaśānādhipatīnāñca baliṃ dadyācca pūrvavat |
niḥkṣipeddaśa kāṣṭhāni pūrvādidaśadikṣu ca || 115 ||

kṣiptvā sampūjayettatra indrādidaśadevatāḥ |
viṣamindrapadaṃ likhya surādhipataye tataḥ || 116 ||

imaṃ baliṃ gṛhṇayugmaṃ gṛhṇāpayayugaṃ tataḥ |
vighnanivāraṇaṃ kṛtvā siddhiṃ prayaccha ṭhadvayam || 117 ||

anena manunā pūrve baliṃ dadyācca sāmiṣam |
svasvanāmādikaṃ kṛtvā pūrvavadbalimuddharet || 118 ||

p. 356)

śavādhiṣṭhātṛbhūtebhyo baliñca surayā saha |
catuḥṣaṣṭiyoginībhyo ḍākinībhyo'pi sandiśet || 119 ||

dvāradeśe tato vīraḥ kuryāduttarasādhakam |
samānaguṇasampannaṃ māntrikaṃ vijitendriyam || 120 ||

abhiṣekavidhijñaṃ ca daivavīraviśāradam |
saṃsthāpyāsanamabhyarpya svamantrānte parāntataḥ || 121 ||

phaḍityanena mantreṇa tataścā'vāhanaṃ viśet |
kuśān pādatale dattvā śavakeśān pragṛhya ca || 122 ||

dṛḍhaṃ nibadhya juṭikāṃ kṛtasaṅkalpasādhakaḥ |
tatra devīṃ susampūjya upacāraiḥ suvistaraiḥ || 123 ||


śavaṃ prati prārthanā

utthāya sammukhe sthitvā paṭhed bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
oṃ vaśo me bhava deveśa mama vīrapadantataḥ || 124 ||

siddhiṃ dehi mahābhāga kṛtāśrayaparāyaṇa |

p. 357)

anena manunā mantrī abhilapya kṛtāñjaliḥ || 125 ||

oṃ bhīma bhīrubhayābhāva bhavyalocana bhāvuka |
trāhi māṃ devadeveśa śavānāmadhipādhipa || 126 ||

iti pādatale tasya trikoṇaṃ cakramuddharet |
tadotthātuṃ na śaknoti śavastu niścalo bhavet || 127 ||

athavā mūlamantrañca saṃsmaran sādhakottamaḥ | 
paṭṭasūtreṇa badhnīyāt śavapādadvayaṃ tataḥ || 128 ||

upaviśya punastasya bāhū niḥsārya pārśvayoḥ |
hastayoḥ kuśamāstīrya pādau tatra nidhāpayet || 129 ||

oṣṭhau tu sampuṭau kṛtvā sthiracittaḥ sthirendriyaḥ |
sadā devīṃ hṛdi dhyātvā maunī japanamācaret || 130 ||

tato'rdharātriparyantaṃ yadi kiñcinna labhyate |
jayadurgāmanunā'rghyaṃ tenaiva sarṣapān kṣipet || 131 ||

pūrvavattilamutkṣipya gatvā saptapadānatha |
devīṃ tatrāpi sampūjya prajapenmanumuttamam || 132 ||

nirbhayaḥ prajaped devi siddhiṃ vā labhate naraḥ |
pūrvoktakramayogena akṣamālāṃ vidhānataḥ || 133 ||

calāsanādbhayaṃ nāsti bhaye jāte vadettataḥ |

p. 358)

yat prārthayasi deveśi dātavyaṃ kuñjarādikam || 134 ||

dināntare ca dāsyāmi svanāma kathayasva me | 
ityuktā saṃskṛtenaiva nirbhayastu punarjapet || 135 ||

punaścenmadhuraṃ vakti vaktavyaṃ madhuraṃ tataḥ |
tataḥ satyaṃ kārayitvā varantu prārthayennaraḥ || 136 ||

yadi satyaṃ na karoti varaṃ tu na prayacchati |
tadā punarjapeddhīmān ekāgramānasaṃ yathā || 137 ||

nararūpaṃ vinā tatra devo'pi na hi sarpati |
yatnatastena boddhavyaṃ naro vā devayonijaḥ || 138 ||

mātā mātṛsvasā vāpi mātulānī tathaiva ca |
āgatya vighnaṃ kurute māyayā'cchādya vigraham || 139 ||

uttiṣṭha vatsa te kāryaṃ sarvaṃ jātaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
prabhātasamayo jātastvatpitā krośate gṛhe || 140 ||

p. 359)

prāyo vimatsarā lokā rājāno daṇḍadhāriṇaḥ |
kadācitkena vā dṛṣṭastadā'niṣṭaṃ bhaviṣyati || 141 ||

ityādivividhairvākyairna ca jāpaṃ parityajet | 
mṛtāḥ pitṛgaṇāstatra dūradeśanivāsinaḥ || 142 ||

bāndhavāstatra cāyānti devarūpadharāstataḥ |
strīputrasevakādīṃśca gṛhītvā nīyate paraiḥ || 143 ||

rudanti putrakāḥ sarve bhrātaro'nujasevakāḥ |
nijakāntāṅgasaṃsparśavastrādyābharaṇādikam || 144 ||

gṛhītvā nīyate pattipālakaistadbhayaṃ tyajet |
divase yatra vā śaṅkā saiva tatra prajāyate || 145 ||

yadi na kṣubhyate tatra tadā kiṃ vā na labhyate |
strīrūpadhāriṇī devī dvijarūpadharaḥ pumān || 146 ||

varaṃ gṛhṇeti śabde vai trirātraṃ te varaṃ labhet |
sādhunā'sādhunā vāpi yoṣiccedvaradāyinī || 147 ||

tadā vīrapatestasya kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale |
niṣpāpapuruṣecaiva satkulanaiśca saṃskṛtā || 148 ||

asaṃskṛtavarā devī pāpayuktena saṃśayaḥ |
sammukhe'sammukhe vāpi saṃskṛtaṃ vakti cāparam || 149 ||

p. 360)

saiva devī na sandehaḥ sa devo bhairavaḥ svayam |
na cedevaṃ bhaveccaiva māyāracitavigrahaḥ || 150 ||

na varayedvaraṃ tatra na kiñcit pravadet kvacit |
sā cet saṃskṛtamākhyānaṃ vakti vā vyaktamīdṛśam || 151 ||

no cet svayaṃ kaulikoktyā varaṃ grāhyaṃ nirākulam |
athavā utkaṭaṃ kiñcit jyotirvā nīlalohitam || 152 ||

śabdo vā jāyate samyagamṛtaṃ vāpi labhyate |
vicārya tadgṛhītavyamevaṃ dik parikīrtitam || 153 ||

devakṛtyāstu bahudhā narāstvakṛtabuddhayaḥ |
avaśyaṃ tatra bhetavyaṃ na tatra pratyayaḥ kvacit || 154 ||

bhairavā baṭukāścaiva kulaśāstraparāyaṇāḥ |
etacchāstraprasaṅgena kṛtvā kuṭilavigrahāḥ || 155 ||

putro bhūtvā haredvidyāṃ nārī bhūtvā vimohayet |
tasmāttattu bhavanno vā vicāre yatnamācaret || 156 ||

p. 361)

satye kṛte labdhvā santyajecca japādikam |
phalaṃ jātamiti jñātvā juṭikāṃ mocayettataḥ || 157 ||

atha tairyācitānaśvanarakuñjaraśūkarān |
dattvā piṣṭamayānante karttavyaṃ samupoṣaṇam || 158 ||

pare'hni nityamācarya pañcagavyaṃ pibettataḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayettatra pañcaviṃśati saṃkhyakān || 159 ||

pañca pañca vihīnān vā kramāccaiva daśāvadhi |
tatraḥ snātvā ca bhuktvā ca nivaseduttame sthale || 160 ||

yadi na syādviprabhojyaṃ tadā nirdhanatāṃ vrajet |
tena cennirdhanatvaṃ syāttadā devaḥ prakupyati || 161 ||

trirātraṃ ṣaḍrātraṃvā navarātrañca gopayet |
śayyāyāṃ yadi gacchedvai tadā vyādhiṃ vinirdiśet || 162 ||

gītaṃ śrutvā ca badhiro niścakṣarnṛtyadarśanāt |
yadi vakti dine vākyaṃ tadā sa mūkatāṃ vrajet || 163 ||

pañcadaśadināntaṃ hi dehe devasya saṃsthitiḥ | 

p. 362)

gobrāhmaṇānāṃ nindāñca na kuryācca kadācana || 164 ||

devagobrāhmaṇādīṃśca pratyahaṃ saṃspṛśecchuciḥ |
prātarnityakriyānte tu bilvapatrodakaṃ pibet || 165 ||

tataḥ snātvā ca gaṅgāyāṃ prāptaṣoḍaśavāsare |
tataḥ śatatrayādūrdhvaṃ devānāṃ tarpaṇaṃ jalaiḥ || 166 ||

snānatarpaṇaśūnyasya na syāddevasya tarpaṇam |
ityanena vidhānena siddhiṃ prāpnoti sādhakaḥ || 167 ||

iha bhuktvā varān bhogānante yāti hareḥ padam |
asāṅga sāṅgamevaṃ vā niṣphalaṃ saphalañca vā || 168 ||

kṛtvā sādhanamevaitat śakteḥ priyataro bhavet |
śavābhāve śmaśāne vā kāryā vai vīrasādhanā || 169 ||


prakārāntareṇa puraścaraṇakathanam

nṝṇāṃ śīghraphalāvāptyai prakārāntaramucyate |
catuṣpathe caturdikṣu puruṣaṃ hṛdayaṃ khanet || 170 ||

jīvitaṃ brahmarandhre vai dīpaṃ prajvālayet sudhīḥ |

p. 363)

madhye tathā khanedekaṃ tatra mṛdvāsanaṃ bhavet || 171 ||

pūrvoktakramamārgeṇa tatra saṃskāramācaret |
mahākālādidevebhyo baliṃ pūrvavadāharet || 172 ||

kalpoktapūjāṃ sampūjya japet prayatamānasaḥ |
digvāsāśca japenmantramayutaṃ sarvadaivatam || 173 ||

japānte ca baliṃ dattvā dakṣiṇāṃ vibhavāvadhi |
sarvasiddhirbhavedvidvān sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ || 174 ||

pūrvavadvīraveśena yātrāṃ kṛtvā susādhakaḥ |
sādhakaiḥ saha sāmagrīṃ pūjāyāṃ vividhāṃ tathā || 175 ||

sthāne pūrvodite vīraḥ śavaṃ caiva samānayet |
varmāstramanunā hastapramāṇaṃ caturasrakam || 176 ||

likhitvā maṇḍalaṃ tatra tenaiva sthāpayecchavam |
tato nāthayugantrāhi yugalaṃ tadanantaram || 177 ||

mama kāryaṃ kuru svāhā astrāya phaṭ tataḥ param |
ityātmānaṃ śavaṃ caiva veṣṭayitvā ca sādhakaḥ || 178 ||

p. 364)

rekhātrayaṃ prakurvīta baliṃ paścānnivedayet |
ḍākinīhākinībhyaśca śākinībhyo'pi sandiśet || 179 ||

samāṃsamadhusaṃyuktaṃ pūrvapaścimayogataḥ |
vāmadevāya dattvā vai raktadantāya dāpayet || 180 ||

tejase ca balitrayaṃ ghṛtamiraṃ saśarkaram |
dakṣiṇe rudrakālāya vajranābhāya sandiśet || 181 ||

sapiṣṭakaṃ ghṛtānnañca yavapiṣṭañca dāpayet |
uttarasyāñca vahnaye dattvā ca balirūpiṇe | 182 ||

guḍaudanaṃ pāyasañca baliṃ nivedya sādhakaḥ |
tataḥ sampūjayed devīṃ svakalpoktavidhānataḥ || 183 ||

astramantreṇa saṃsthāpya śavaṃ tailaharidrayā |
udvarttanaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā snāpayitvā tu sādhakaḥ || 184 ||

sammārjya vāsasā tatra mahāpretāsanaṃ yajet |
jīvanyāsaṃ vidhāyātha pādyādibhiḥ prapūjayet || 185 ||

mūlena juṭikāṃ baddhvā padmaṃ pūrvavadālikhet |
āsanaṃ pūrvavat kṣiptvā mūlamuccārya sādhakaḥ || 186 ||

aśvārohaṇarūpeṇa sthitvā mantraṃ japet sudhīḥ |
ananyamānaso bhūtvā japāt siddhiḥ prajāyate || 187 ||

p. 365)

bhītaścet sādhakastatra gurupādayugaṃ smaret |
gurupādaprasādena mā hiṃsanti ca hiṃsakāḥ || 188 ||

iti cittaṃ sthirīkṛtya japenmantramananyadhīḥ |
devatā yadi vāme vai āgacchati kadācana || 189 ||

sambhāṣaṇā ca kāryā vai na dātavyaṃ priyaṃ vacaḥ |
hūṅkāriṇaiva tāṃ kuryāt svadakṣe sādhakottamaḥ || 190 ||

gatvā ca dakṣiṇe sāpi dehi dehīti bhāṣite |
tathāpi ca baliṃ dattvā aśaktau manasā'thavā || 191 ||

labdhvābhīṣṭaṃ nijaṃ vīro gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadet |
śavādikañjale kṣiptvā gṛhaṃ gacchet yathāsukham || 192 ||

baṭamūle śavaṃ nītvā tatra devīṃ prapūjya ca |
snātvā tatra manuṃ japtvā ayutaṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 193 ||

sarvasiddhīśvaro vīro bhavennātra vikalpanam |
bilvamūle nijakroḍe śavamāropya yatnataḥ || 194 ||

nṛsiṃhamudrayā vīkṣya japenmātṛkayā sudhīḥ |
sahasraṃ tatra japtvā vai sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet || 195 ||

p. 366)

jānumadhye karau kṛtvā cibukoṣṭhau samāvṛtau |
hastau ca bhūmisaṃlagnau kampamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 196 ||

mukhaṃ vivṛtakaṃ kuryāt lelihānañca jihvayā |
nārasiṃhī bhavenmudrā sarvasiddhipradāyikā || 197 ||

sahasraṃ tatra japtvā vai sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet |
kuṅkumāgurukastūrīrocanāghanacandanam || 198 ||

karpūraṃ padmakāṣṭhañca keśaraṃ haricandanam |
ekatra sādhitaṃ kṛtvā pratyekaṃ sādhayennaraḥ || 199 ||

vidyudagniyutaṃ caiva netrāntaṃ candrabinduyuk |
bījaṃ pratyekaṃ dravyāṇāṃ militānāñca sādhakaḥ || 200 ||

mūlamantraṃ ca mantrajño japet sāṣṭasahasrakam |
asya tilakamātreṇa rājānaṃ vaśamānayet || 201 ||

jihvāgre rudhiraṃ vīra ākāśe svayamāharet |
tenaiva guṭikāṃ kṛtvā tatra kālīṃ prapūjayet || 202 ||

p. 367)

nīlāṃ nīlapatākāñca lolajihvāṃ kapālinīm |
jihvāgre rudhiraṃ gṛhṇa cāmuṇḍe ghoraniḥsvane || 203 ||

baliṃ gṛhṇa varaṃ dehi rudhiraṃ gagane'male |
kāli kāli pracaṇḍogre tato'straṃ kavacaṃ tataḥ || 204 ||

kāliketi samākhyātā vīrāṇāṃ hitakāmyayā |
kūrcayugmaṃ tu nīlāyāḥ kathitaṃ mantramuttamam || 205 ||

viyadbhṛguramāṃ caiva balamindraravī ratiḥ |
candrakhaṇḍasamīyuktaṃ tato nīlapadaṃ tataḥ || 206 ||

patāke huṃ phaḍantaḥ syāt pūrvakūṭamanurmataḥ |
santapteyaṃ mahāvidyā kathitā suradurlabhā || 207 ||

jaya śrīdharaṇīdevī patāke varaṇasthale |
tena nīlapatākeyaṃ saṃyojyā nīlasādhane || 208 ||

śrīśaktismarabījāni tadante hūmiti smaret |
bhṛguṃ sargānvitaṃ paścāddīrghaṃ kavacamuddharet || 209 ||

ugracaṇḍe padaṃ paścānmāmante rakṣayugmakam |
mama śatrupadasyānte tato bhakṣapadaṃ vadet || 210 ||

hrīṃ klīṃ saḥ ityante śrīśaktismarabījāni tadante |
rājyaṃ me dehi sambhāṣya bhṛguṃ sargānvitaṃ vadet || 211 ||

klīṃkārañca tato brūyāt kāmaṃ māyāṃ ramāntataḥ |
ekacatvāriṃśadarṇo manuḥ prokto dhruvādikaḥ || 212 ||

p. 368)

ugracaṇḍā mahāvidyā lolajihveti kīrttitā |
yā sā vidyā mahātārā sā karāleti kīrttitā || 213 ||


mahātārāmantroddhāraḥ

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya tāre tu tāre ca tathā |
namaḥ svāheti mantro'yaṃ daśākṣara udīritaḥ || 214 ||

kathitā ca mahātārā sarvasiddhipradāyikā |
ebhirāmantritaṃ caiva mūlenaiva sahasrakam || 215 ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ kṛtvā sādhako vītabhīḥ svayam |
mahāṣṭamīnavamyostu saṃyoge purataḥ sthitaḥ || 216 ||

chāgamahiṣameṣāṇāṃ caturdikṣa śavān kṣipet |
kabandhaṃ muṇḍapuñjañca dīpādibhiralaṃkṛtam || 217 ||

madhye kabandhamāstrīrya tatra gandharvarūpadhṛk |
tāmbūlapūravaktrañca añjanāñcitalocanam || 218 ||

kṛtvā tāraṃ manuṃ japtvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet |

p. 369)

atha pūrvodite sthāne catuṣpathe viśeṣataḥ || 219 ||

ūrdhvaṃ dvivarṣādyadi vā pañca vā taruṇaṃ yadi |
saptamāṣṭamamāsīyaṃ garbhajaṃ yadi vā śavam || 220 ||

cāṇḍālañcābhibhūtaṃ tu śīghrasiddhiphalapradam |
ānīya sthāpayedādau nyāsajālaṃ samācaret || 221 ||

pretamantraṃ samālikhya gandhapuṣpādibhistathā |
abhyarcya cā'sanaṃ dattvā rakṣāmātmani kārayet || 222 ||


śavasādhanena sarvasiddhiḥ

tataḥ śavāsye vidhivaddevatāpyāyanaṃ tataḥ |
tato japtvā sarvamantra sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet || 223 ||

tāraṃ śabdaṃ mṛtakāya namo'ntaṃ mantramuccaran |
śavasthāpanamantro'yaṃ śavāsanamanuṃ tataḥ || 224 ||

mūlānte bhuvaneśī syāt phaḍanto mantra īritaḥ |
karakāñcīṃ samādāya muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitaḥ || 225 ||

tenaiva tilakaṃ kṛtvā tattadbhasmavibhūsitaḥ |
śmaśāne ca sakṛjjaptvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet || 226 ||

matsyamānīya vīrendro niḥkṣipet pitṛkānane |

p. 370)

tatra sakṛjjapitvā tu devatāmelanaṃ bhavet || 227 ||

tatra natvā mahādevaṃ mahādevīñca sādhakaḥ |
tadbhasmatilakaṃ kṛtvā svayaṃ vīreśvaro bhavet || 228 ||

niśāyāṃ mṛtahaṭṭe ca unmattānandabhairavaḥ |
digvāsā vimalo bhasmabhūṣaṇo muktakeśakaḥ || 229 ||

kapālī khaḍgahastaśca japenmātṛkayā sudhīḥ |
tadā tasya mahāsiddhirjāyate śatajāpataḥ || 230 ||

ḍākinīṃ yoginīṃ vāpi anyāṃ vā bhūtalāṅganām |
tatrā'pyānīya sampūjya gajāntakasahasrakam || 231 ||

japtvā ca sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet |
mahāśavāḥ praśastāḥ syuḥ pradhānaśavasādhane || 232 ||

kṣudrāḥ prayogakartṝṇāṃ praśastāḥ sarvasiddhidāḥ |
sarveṣāṃ jīvahīnānāṃ jantūnāṃ nīlasādhane || 233 ||

brāhmaṇaṃ gomayaṃ tyaktvā sādhayedvīrasādhanam |
evaṃ nīlakramaṃ caiva kathitañca śubhāvaham || 234 ||

p. 371)

na kasmaicitpravaktavyaṃ mantrasiddheśca kāraṇam |
kuje vā śanivāre vā naramuṇḍaṃ samāhitaḥ || 235 ||

pañcagavyena militaṃ candanādyairvilepitam |
niḥkṣipya bhūmau hastārdhamānataḥ kānanāntare || 236 ||

tatra taddivase rātrau sahasraṃ yadi mānavaḥ |
ekākī prajapenmantraṃ sa bhavet kalpapādapaḥ || 237 ||

śavamānīya taddvāri tatraiva parikhanya ca |
taddināttaddinaṃ yāvattāvadaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 238 ||

sa bhavet sarvasiddhīśo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
athavā vijane'raṇye'sthiśayyāsano naraḥ || 239 ||

udayāstaṃ divā japtvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet |
aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi || 240 ||

sūryodayāt samārabhya yāvat sūryodayāntaram |
tāvajjaptvā nirātaṅkaḥ sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet || 241 ||


candrasūryagrahaṇe puraścaraṇavidhānam

grahaṇe candrasūryasya śuciḥpūrvamupoṣitaḥ |
nadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ nābhimāvasthite'mbhasi || 242 ||

p. 372)

yadvā śuddhodake snātvā śucau deśe samāhitaḥ |
grāsādvimuktiparyantaṃ japaṃ kuryādananyadhīḥ || 243 ||

anantaraṃ daśāṃśena kramāddhomādikañcaret |
tadante mahatīṃ pūjāṃ kuryād brāhmaṇabhojanam || 244 ||

tataśca mantrasiddhaṃ tu guruṃ sampūjya toṣayet |
sūryagrahaṇakālāddhi nānyaḥ kālaḥ praśasyate || 245 ||

tatra yadyat kṛtaṃ karma anantaphaladaṃ bhavet |
śaratkāle caturthyādi navamyantaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 246 ||

bhaktitaḥ pūjayitvā ca rātrau tāvat sahasrakam |
japedekākī vijane kevalaṃ timirālaye || 247 ||

aṣṭamyādi navamyantamupavāsaparo bhavet |
sa bhavet sarvasiddhīśo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 248 ||

aṣṭamīsandhivelāyāmaṣṭottaralatāgṛham |
praviśya mantrī vidhivattāḥ samabhyarcya yatnataḥ || 249 ||

svakalpoktavidhānena pūjayitvā ca sādhakaḥ |
kevalaṃ kāmabhāvena japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 250 ||

tāsāñca patramūlena ulkāṃ saṃgṛhya karṇake |

p. 373)

mahāsiddhirbhavet sadyo latādarśanapūjanāt || 251 ||

kulāṣṭakaṃ latāgāre likhitvā yantrameva ca |
prapūjya tatra saṃskāraṃ kṛtvā tasyai nivedayet || 252 ||

kiñcijjaptvā manuṃ nītvā devatābhāvanāparaḥ |
tāṃ visṛjya namaskṛtya svayaṃ japtvā susaṃyataḥ || 253 ||

prītaḥ strībhyo baliṃ dattvā mantrasiddhirna saṃśayaḥ |
athavā gurumānīya devabhāvena pūjayet || 254 ||

vastrālaṅkārahemādyaiḥ santoṣya gurumeva ca |
tatsutaṃ tatsutāṃ caiva patnīṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ || 255 ||

pūjayitvā manuṃ japtvā svayaṃ siddhīśvaro bhavet |
sahasrāre guroḥ pādapadmaṃ dhyātvā prapūjya ca || 256 ||

kevalaṃ devatābuddhyā japtvā siddhīśvaro bhavet |
gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyādyathāvibhavavistaraiḥ || 257 ||

guroranujñāmātreṇa duṣṭamantro'pi sidhyati |

p. 374)

guruṃ vilaṅghya śāstre'sminnā'dhikārī suro'pi ca || 258 ||

eṣāñca mantrahīnānāṃ prayogaḥ kriyate yadi |
guruvaktraṃ vinā caiva siddhi hāniḥ prajāyate || 259 ||

etattantrañca mantrañca putrebhyo'pi na darśayet |
anyathā pretarājasya bhavanaṃ yāti niścitam || 260 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye caturdaśollāsaḥ || 14 ||



pañcadaśa ullāsaḥ 


kumārīpūjananirūpaṇam

atha vakṣye kumārīṇāṃ pūjāṃ tantravidhānataḥ |
homādikaṃ hi viphalaṃ kumārīpūjanaṃ vinā || 1 ||


tadvihīnaṃ homādikaṃ niṣphalam

paripūrṇaphalaṃ naiva jīvahīnaṃ yathā vapuḥ |
yathā yathā devatāyā pūjanaṃ vā yathāguroḥ || 2 ||

tathaiva hi kulīnānāṃ kumāryāḥ pūjanaṃ bhavet | 
yathāśaktyā hi vitaret kumāryai yadyadīpsitam || 3 ||

aśakyaṃ śakyamevaṃ hi dānābhāve phalātyayaḥ |
puṣpaṃ kumāryai yaddattaṃ tanmerusadṛśaṃ matam || 4 ||

dattamanyacca yatkiñcid bhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ tathā |
alpamapyathavā mantrahīnaṃ bahuguṇaṃ bhavet || 5 ||

p. 376)

kumārīpūjāphalam

kumārīpūjanāccaiva kumārībhojanādapi |
kumārīvandanādeva kumārīsadṛśo bhavet || 6 ||


kumāryaṅge sarvadevatāsthitiḥ

kumārikā bhavet devī śivo'pi ca kumārikā |
kumārī yoginī sākṣāt kumārī sarvadevatā || 7 ||

tasyāḥ pūjanamātreṇa trailokyapūjanaṃ bhavet |
sukumārī mahādevī tridhā mūrttirvyavasthitā || 8 ||

parā caivā'parā caiva tṛtīyā ca parāparā |
yatra kāle na kiñcit syād devāsuramahoragāḥ || 9 ||

trailokyaṃ layamānañca tadā jātā kumārikā |
ādyasṛṣṭeśca yā śaktiḥ pratyakṣā sā tu tattridhā || 10 ||

tilatilā tu sā jātā sṛṣṭirūpā bhavārṇave |
bhūrbhuvomūrttirūpā sā ekā pūjyā kumārikā || 11 ||

ekīkṛtya tribhiścaiva saptāṣṭanavadhā punaḥ |
nityakrameṇa deveśi pūjayedvidhipūrvakam || 12 ||

p. 377)

aṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ vāpi ekāṃ kanyāṃ prapūjayet |
pūjitāḥ pratigṛhyante nirgacchantyavamānitāḥ || 13 ||


kumārīpūjnena sarvadeva santuṣṭiḥ

asurāśca tathā nāgā ye ca duṣṭagrahā api |
bhūtavetālagandharvā ḍākinīyakṣarākṣasāḥ || 14 ||

krūrāśca devatāḥ sarve bhūrbhuvaścaiva bhairavāḥ |
pṛthivyādīni sarvāṇi brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaram || 15 ||

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
te tuṣṭāḥ sarvatuṣṭāśca yastu kanyāṃ prapūjayet || 16 ||

kumārī pūjanāccaiva koṭipūjāphalaṃ labhet |
ghnanti vighnān pūjitāstāstathā śatruṃ mahotkaṭam || 17 ||

vyādhayaḥ sarvariṣṭāni palāyante na saṃśayaḥ |
grahā yakṣāḥ kṣayaṃ yānti bhūtavetālapannagāḥ || 18 ||

asurā guhyakāḥ pretā yoginī guhyaḍākinī |
mahābhayāni durbhikṣamutpātāni sahasraśaḥ || 19 ||

duḥsvapnamapamṛtyuśca anye ye ye samudbhavāḥ |
kumārī pūjanādeva na tasya prabhavanti ca || 20 ||

p. 378)

aṇimādīni siddhāni pātālaguṭikāñjanāḥ |
catuṣkaṃ divyavetālaṃ bhavet kumārikārcanāt || 21 ||


kumārīlakṣaṇam

lakṣaṇaṃ yajanaṃ tāsāṃ vakṣye'haṃ tantravartmanā |
dvitīyavatsarādūrdhvaṃ yāvat syādaṣṭamābdikam || 22 ||

tāvat kumārī vijñeyā mantratantraphalapradā |
athavā ṣoḍaśābdāntaṃ yāvat puṣpaṃ na jāyate || 23 ||

tāmyaḥ puṣpaphalaṃ vāpi gandhacandanapāyasam |
bālapriyañca naivedyaṃ balābhāvaviceṣṭitam || 24 ||

tābhiḥ priyakarālāpaistoṣayet sādhakottamaḥ |


vayobhedena kumārīṇāṃ nāmabhedaḥ

ekavarṣā bhavet sandhyā dvivarṣā ca sarasvatī || 25 ||

trivarṣā ca tridhāmūrttiścaturvarṣā ca kālikā |
subhagā pañcavarṣā ca ṣaḍvarṣā ca umā bhavet || 26 ||

saptabhirmālinī sākṣādaṣṭavarṣā ca kubjikā |
navabhiḥ kālasaṅkarṣā daśabhiścāparājitā || 27 ||

ekādaśe tu rudrāṇī dvādaśābdā tu bhairavī |
trayodaśe mahālakṣmīrdvisaptā pīṭhanāyikā || 28 ||

kṣatrajñā pañcadaśabhiḥ ṣoḍaśe vācikā matā |
evaṃ krameṇa pūjyā tu yāvat puṣpaṃ na vidyate || 29 ||

p. 379)


praśastadivasakathanam

pratipadādidarśāntaṃ vṛddhibhedena pūjayet |
mahāparvasu sarveṣu viśeṣaśca pavitrake || 30 ||

mahānavamyāṃ vīrendraḥ kumārīḥ samprapūjayet |
piṅgalāṃ pūjayedyastu ṣoḍaśaiścaiva bhaktimān || 31 ||

pūjayitvā bhaktibhāve sarvasampattimālabhet |
pūjayet kulavidyāñca caikāṃ bālakabhairavīm || 32 ||


kumārīpūjanamantra kathanam

evaṃ praṇavayogena caitanyatanumarcayet |
vāṇī māyā tathā lakṣmīrmāyā kūrcadvayantataḥ || 33 ||

ete ca praṇavā jñeyāḥ kumāryāḥ paripūjane |
ekādaśasvareṇā'ḍhyo bhṛguvindvinda saṃyutaḥ || 34 ||

caitanyabījaṃ kathitaṃ sādhakānāṃ samṛddhidam |

p. 380)

evaṃ dvābhyāṃ tribhiścaiva saptadhā navadhā punaḥ || 35 ||

nityakrameṇa niyataṃ pūjayedvidhipūrvakam |
vāgbhavena jalaṃ deyaṃ māyayā pādaśaucakam || 36 ||

lakṣmyā cārghaṃ pradadyāttu kūrcabījena candanam |
śaktibījena puṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ ṣaṣṭhena dāpayet || 37 ||

vāgbhavena purakṣemo māyayā ca guṇāṣṭakam |
śrīṃbījena śriyo lābho hrīṃbījenā'risaṅkṣayaḥ || 38 ||

bhairaveṇa tu bījena khaṅgatvamanugacchati |
nyāsādikaṃ prakurvīta ādau svīyakrameṇa tu || 39 ||


kumāryaṅge viśeṣanyāsaḥ

kumāryaṅge tataḥ paścādviśeṣanyāsamuttamam |


viśeṣanyāsasya mantrāḥ

aiṃ klīṃ śrīṃ klauṃ hsauḥ kulakumārike hṛdayāya namaḥ || 40 ||

aiṃ haiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klāṃ eṃ svāhā śirase svāhā |
aiṃ klīṃ saiṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ || 41 ||

aiṃ kulavāgīśvaravāgīśvari kavacāya huṃ |
klīṃ astrāya phaṭ |
aiṃ siddhi jayāya pūrvavaktrāya namaḥ || 42 ||

aiṃ jayāya uttaravaktrāya namaḥ |
aiṃ klīṃ śrīṃ kubjike paścimavaktrāya namaḥ |
kraṃ kālike dakṣiṇavaktrāya namaḥ || 43 ||

varṇanyāsaṃ ṣaḍaṅgañca kumāryaṅge pravinyaset |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi ekāṃ kanyāṃ prapūjayet || 44 ||

p. 381)

pādyamarghyaṃ punardhūpaṃ kuṅkumaṃ candanādikam |
bhaktibhāvena sampūjya tasyai sarvaṃ nivedayet || 45 ||

taruṇollāsavān bhūtvā svayamacyutamānasaḥ |
bālāmalaṅkṛtāṃ paśyan cintayet sveṣṭadevatām || 46 ||

yathāśakti japet paścādaṣṭottaraśatādikam |
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kuryādādimadhye śubhaṃ bhavet || 47 ||

tatastāṃ devatābuddhyā namaskṛtya visarjayet |
paścācca dakṣiṇā deyā rajatasvarṇamauktikaiḥ || 48 ||


kumārīpūjanaphalam

kumārī pūjanāccaiva koṭipūjāphalaṃ labhet |
kumārīpūjane caiva jātimātraṃ na cintayet || 49 ||

p. 382)

aśeṣakulasampannā nājātisamudbhavāḥ |
nānādoṣodbhavā vāpi guṇalāvaṇyasaṃyutāḥ || 50 ||

sarvathaiva kulīnānāṃ pūjyāstāḥ śubhadāyikāḥ 
yathāyathā tatpriyakṛt sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet || 51 ||

mahābhaye samutpanne kanyakāñca prapūjayet |
tatkṣaṇāllabhate mokṣaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 52 ||

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
te tuṣṭāḥ sarvatuṣṭāśca yastu kanyāṃ prapūjayet || 53 ||

vivāhayet svayaṃ kanyāṃ brahmahatyādi naśyati |
gohatyāṃ strīvadhaṃ caiva sarvapāpaṃ praṇaśyati || 54 ||

mātaraḥ pitaraścaiva bhrātaraścaiva sarvataḥ |
ye tu ye tu puna sarve kanyādānaṃ prakalpayet || 55 ||

bhuktimuktiphalaṃ teṣāṃ saubhāgyaṃ sarvasampadaḥ |
rudraloke vasennityaṃ trinetro bhagavān hariḥ || 56 ||

p. 383)

ṣaṣṭikoṭisahasrāṇāṃ cāśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet |
kumārīkalpamākhyātaṃ athavā'nyatamañcaret || 57 ||


baṭukapūjanam

sandhyādinavakanyāñca samānīya prapūjya ca |
pūrvavadvidhivadvīro nava bālakamānayet || 58 ||


praśastakālanirṇayaḥ

baṭukaṃ pañcavarṣañca navavarṣaṃ gaṇeśvaram |
gandhapuṣpākṣatākalpairyathāvibhavavistaraiḥ || 59 ||

abhyarcya devatā buddhyā padārthaiḥ paritoṣayet |
svakāryaphalasiddhyarthaṃ vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ || 60 ||

navarātraṃ yedekottaravṛddhyā krameṇa tu |
navarātrakṛtāṃ pūjāṃ mahādevyai samarpayet || 61 ||

tāmbūlaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā kumārīstān visarjayet |
evaṃ navakumārīṇāmarcanaṃ prativatsaram || 62 ||

yaḥ karoti hi puṇyātmā devatāprītimāpnuyāt |
mano'bhilaṣitaṃ prāpya nivasettavasannidhau || 63 ||

p. 384)

śaktipūjā

athavā yauvanārūḍhāḥ pramadā nava śobhanāḥ |
pūjayedvidhivadbhaktyā nava rātriṣu mantravit || 64 ||

hṛllekhāṃ gaganāṃ raktāṃ mahotsukāṃ karālikām |
icchāṃ jñānāṃ kriyāṃ durgāṃ vaṭukañca gaṇeśvaram || 65 ||

pūrvavat pūjanādyaiśca padārthaiḥ paritoṣayet |
prauḍhollāsena saṃyuktāḥ santuṣṭā yadi cet tadā || 66 ||

sādhakastīrthamāsādya nivasecchivasannidhau |
evaṃ yaḥ pūjayed devīṃ prativarṣaṃ yativrataḥ || 67 ||

ṣaṇmāse vā trimāse vā māse māse'thavā budhaḥ | 
tisro vā pañca vā sapta pūjayed devatādhiyā || 68 ||

sarvaiśvaryasamṛddhyātmā sa bhavecchivayoḥ priyaḥ |
athavā'nyaprakāreṇa kathyate mithunārcanam || 69 ||


mithunārcanavidhānam

tulāyāṃ vāpi meṣe vā sarvasaṃkrāntiṣu bhṛśam |
gaurīśivo ramāviṣṇū vāṇīsarasijāsanau || 70 ||

śacīndrau rohiṇīcandrau svāhāgnī ca prabhāravī |
bhadrakālīvīrabhadrau bhairavībhairavāvapi || 71 ||

samānīya prayatnena pūjayet pūrvavartmanā |
tritārādinamo'ntena tattannāmnā vidhānataḥ || 72 ||

vāgbījaṃ śaktibījañca śrībījañca tritārakam | 

p. 385)

gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjya tattvādyaiḥ paritoṣayet || 73 ||

prauḍhollāsena yuktāni kurvīta mithunāni ca |
evaṃ kṛte na sandehastuṣṭā mithunadevatāḥ || 74 ||

anugṛhṇanti taṃ devāḥ prayacchanti manoratham |
prativarṣaṃ tu yaḥ kuryād bhaktyā ca mithunārcanam || 75 ||

śivalokeṣu nivaset sarvaiśvaryasamanvitaḥ |
bhṛguvāre yajedyatnāt kāntāmārūḍhayauvanām || 76 ||

sarvalakṣaṇasampannāmanukūlāṃ manoramām |
kulākulāṣṭamāṃ caivā āmantrayā'hūyapuṣpiṇīm || 77 ||

abhyaṅgasnānaśuddhāṅgīṃ svāsane copaveśayet |
ātmānaṃ gandhapuṣpādyairalaṃkuryāt susādhakaḥ || 78 ||

āvāhya devatāṃ tasyāṃ yajennyāsakrameṇa hi |
kṛtvā kramārcanaṃ dhūpadīpañca kuladīpakam || 79 ||

pradarśya devatābuddhyā padārthaiḥ ṣaḍrasānvitaiḥ |
māṃsādibhakṣyabhojyādyaistoṣayed bhaktibhāvataḥ || 80 ||

prauḍhollāsena sahitāṃ paśyaṃśca prajapenmanum |
yauvanollāsasahitaḥ svayaṃ taddhyānatatparaḥ || 81 ||

p. 386)

nirvikāreṇa cittena aṣṭottarasahasrakam |
japādikaṃ samāpyātha tayā saha niśāṃ nayet || 82 ||

trisaptapañcanavasu bhṛguvāreṣu yaḥ sadā |
pūjayedvidhinā caiva tasya puṇyaṃ na gaṇyate || 83 ||

catuḥpīṭhārcanaphalaṃ sa prāpnoti suniścitam |
athānyaṃ sampravakṣyāmi samayācāramuttamam || 84 ||


samayācārakathanam

yena hīnā na sidhyanti mantrāḥ koṭisahasraśaḥ |
mānavaḥ kulaśāstrāṇāṃ kulacaryānusāriṇām || 85 ||

udāracittaḥ sarvatra vaiṣṇavācāratatparaḥ |
paranindāsahiṣṇuḥ syādupakārarataḥ sadā || 86 ||

parvate vipine caiva nirjane śūnyamaṇḍale |
catuṣpathe kalāśūnye yadi daivādgatirbhavet || 87 ||

kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā manuṃ japtvānatvā gacchedyathāsukham |
gṛdhraṃ vīkṣya mahākālīṃ namaskuryādalakṣitam || 88 ||

p. 387)

gṛdhrādinamaskāramantrāḥ

kṣemaṅkarīṃ tathā vīkṣya jambūkīṃ yamadūtikām |
kuraraṃ droṇakākaṃ tu kṛṣṇamārjārameva ca || 89 ||

kṛṣṇasāraṃ tathā vyāghraṃ khaḍginaṃ siṃhameva ca |
kṛśodari mahācaṇḍe muktakeśi balipriye || 90 ||

kulācāraprasannāsye namaste śaṅkarapriye |
śmaśāne ca śivāṃ dṛṣṭvā pradakṣiṇamanuvrajan || 91 ||

praṇamyānena manunā mantrī sukhamavāpnuyāt |
ghoradaṃṣṭre kaṭhorākṣi kiṭiśabdaninādini || 92 ||

ghoraghoraravāsphāle namaste citivāsini |
raktavastrāṃ raktapuṣpāṃ vilokya tripurāmbikām || 93 ||

praṇamya daṇḍavadbhūmāvimaṃ mantramudīrayet |
bandhūkapuṣpasaṅkāśe tripure bhayanāśini || 94 ||

bhāgyodaye samutpanne namaste varavarṇini |
kṛṣṇavastraṃ tathā puṣpaṃ rājānaṃ rājaputrakam || 95 ||

hastyaśvarathaśastrāṇi phalakān vīrapuruṣān |
mahiṣaṃ kuladevañca dṛṣṭvā mahiṣamardinīm || 96 ||

praṇamya jayadurgāṃvā sa ca vighnairna lipyate |
jaya devi jagaddhātri tripurādye tridaivate || 97 ||

bhaktebhyo varade devi mahiṣaghni namo'stu te |

p. 388)

madyabhāṇḍaṃ samālokya matsyaṃ māṃsaṃ varastriyam || 98 ||

dṛṣṭvā ca bhairavīṃ devīṃ praṇamyavimṛśenmanum |
ghoravighnavināśāya kulācārasamṛddhaye || 99 ||

namāmi varade devi muṇḍamālāvibhūṣite |
raktadhārāsamākīrṇavadane tvāṃ namāmyaham || 100 ||

sarvavighnahare devi namaste haravallabhe |
eteṣāṃ darśane caiva yadi naivaṃ prakurvate || 101 ||

śaktimantraṃ puraskṛtya teṣāṃ siddhirna jāyate |
eteṣāṃ māraṇoccāṭahiṃsanaṃ vā bhayādibhiḥ || 102 ||

kriyate yadi pāpātmā devībhaktaḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
pradhānāṃśasamudbhūtā ete kulajanāḥ priye || 103 ||

ḍākinyaśca tathā sarvā devyaṃśā yoṣito yataḥ |
labdhasiddhisamāyogāt ḍākinīhiṃsanaṃ yadi || 104 ||

athavā mānavānāñca madbhaktānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
baṭukānāṃ bhairavāṇāṃ tasya siddhirna jāyate || 105 ||


kāmukīṃ prativīrasādhakakartavyam

grāme vā nagare vāpi haṭṭe vā catvare'pi ca |
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā yuvatī nārī kalākuśalasaṃyutā || 106 ||

bhāvaikabhinnahṛdayā vakraṃ dṛṣṭyā'valokate |
utkṣipya bhujamūlasya vāsasaṃ kṣipyate yadi || 107 ||

celāñcalaparīvartadarśitā jaghanāñcalā |
kaṇḍūyabhāvavyājena śithilīkṛtavāsasā || 108 ||

darśitastanaparyantabhūbhāgā punarāvṛtā |
skhalat pādayugāt pādaṃ patitā punarutthitā || 109 ||

sakhībhirvyājamāsādya karṇākarṇī manoharam |
ityādibhāvabhāvitāṃ dṛṣṭvā sādhakasattamaḥ || 110 ||

tasyā nijamanohāri saviśeṣaṃ nidhāya ca |
tatra sthitvā punaḥ kṣobhaṃ kuryāt kāma vāparaḥ || 111 ||

vṛddhāṃ vā yuvatīṃ vāpi bālāṃ vā sundarīṃ tathā |
vidagdhāṃ vā mahābhraṣṭāṃ kutsitāṃ vāpi hīnajām || 112 ||

bhāvaikabhinnahṛdayāṃ jñātvā vīravarottamaḥ |
sambhogena vinopekṣāṃ na kuryācca kadācana || 113 ||

ālasyena bhayenāpi aśaktyā vā yadi tyajet |
kruddhā bhagavatī tasya śāpaṃ dadyāt sudāruṇam || 114 ||

mṛte ca narakaṃ gatvā nānākleśayuto bhavet |
niṣkṛtirnāsti tasyaiva yāvadācandratārakam || 115 ||

p. 390)

phalaṃ puṣpañca tāmbūlaṃ annapānādikañca yat |
mahādevyai vinā dattvā na bhoktavyaṃ kadācana || 116 ||

pativratāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ tathaiva ṛtusaṅgame |
namaskuryāt prayatnena caṇḍikāṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 117 ||

śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ vāpi tatheṣṭāpūrttakarmaṇi |
yadā kuryāttadā natvā devīṃ yātrāṃ samācaret || 118 ||

tauryatrikaṃ yadā paśyeddevānāṃ gītameva vā |
tatra devyai nivedyaiva karttavyañca prapūjanam || 119 ||

vyāyāme yadi vā devasabhāyāṃ vāpi parvate |
vane vā durgamevāpi yadi gacchati sādhakaḥ || 120 ||

tatra sthitvā mahādevīṃ praṇamed bhaktibhāvataḥ |
tarpaṇādau prakurvīta tṛpyatāṃ brahmabhairava || 121 ||

āvāhane svapitṝn vai bhairavāniti kīrttayet |
tṛpyatāṃ bhairavī mātā pitā bhairava tṛpyatām || 122 ||

ādau ca tripurāpūrvāṃ tarpaṇe bhairavīṃ yajet |
jyotiṣṭomāśvamedhādau yatra yaṃ yaṃ prapūjayet || 123 ||

p. 391)

praṇamya śirasā dhīro bhairavīṃ cintayeddhiyā |
striyo dṛṣṭvā tathaikatra yuvatyatimanoharāḥ || 124 ||

tābhyastripurabhairavyāḥ prītaye candanādikam |
dadyāttattatpriyaṃ dravyaṃ manasā cintya caṇḍikām || 125 ||


tripurārghyadānamantraḥ

bhairavī pratigṛhṇāti bhairavo'haṃ pratigrahe |
kanyāyā bhāṣaṇe śrīmattripurāyāḥ prapūjanam || 126 ||

bhairavāya dadāmyarghyaṃ devīṃ tripurabhairavīm |
evaṃ vadet pradāne tu kanyakāyāḥ prapūjane || 127 ||

kathitaḥ samayācāraḥ sādhakasya susiddhaye |
atrā'śakto bhavedyastu tasyārthaṃ likhyate stavaḥ || 128 ||


tripurāstotram tasyāḥ nava nāmāni

tripurā tripureśī ca sundarī puravāsinī |
śrīrmālinī ca siddhā'mbā mahātripurasundarī || 129 ||


guptaguptatarādibhedena yoginībhedaḥ

prakaṭāśca tathāguptāstathā guptatarāḥ parāḥ |
sampradāyāḥ kulaṃ kaulā rahasyātirahasyagāḥ || 130 ||

parāpararahasyā ca tathā kāmeśvarī śubhā |

p. 392)

bhagamālādi mahāvidyā nāmakathanam

bhagamālā nityaklinnā bheruṇḍā vahnivāsinī || 131 ||

mahāvrajeśvarī dūtī tvaritā kulasundarī |
nityā nīlapatākā ca vijayā sarvamaṅgalā || 132 ||

jvālāṃśumālinī citrā vaśinī śubhagā tathā |
pūrṇākhyā ca tathā jñeyā kāmeśī modinī tathā || 133 ||

vimalā cā'ruṇā devī jayinī kulabhairavī |
sarveśvarī tathā kaulī vāgīśī sarvakāminī || 134 ||

siddheśvarī tathā cogrā durgā mahiṣamardinī |
svapnāvatī śūlinī ca mātaṅgī surasundarī || 135 ||

mahākālī mahogrā ca citrarūpā mahodarī |
prāṇavidyā tathaikākṣī caikapādā mahāṅkuśā || 136 ||

vāmā śiva tathā jyeṣṭhā surūpā cārubhāṣiṇī |
trikhaṇḍā triśirā śaurī gaurī vindhyanivāsinī || 137 ||

vibhurjāyā madhumatī tathā nīlasarasvatī |
kālī kuṇḍamayī kuṇḍarūpiṇī bandhurūpiṇī || 138 ||

bālā pūrṇā mahādevī nīlalakṣmīti kathyate |
kṣobhiṇī nādinī bhadrā lalitā bahurūpikā || 139 ||

sarvasampatkarī tārā bhavānī viśvavāsinī |
troṭeśvarī mahāvidyāḥ kathitāstava bhairava || 140 ||

p. 393)


tripuropāsakanāmastotram

upāsakān mahādeva śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ svayam |
manuścandraḥ kuberaśca manmathastadanantaram || 141 ||

lopāmudrā munirnandī śakraḥ sundaḥ śivastathā |
krodhabhaṭṭārakaścaiva pañcamī ca prakīrttitā || 142 ||

durvāsā vyāsasūryau ca vaśiṣṭhaśca parāśaraḥ |
aurvo vahniryamaścaiva nair-ṛto varuṇastathā || 143 ||

vāyurviṣṇuḥ svayambhūśca bhairavo gaṇakastathā |
aniroddho bharadvājo dakṣiṇāmūrttireva ca || 144 ||

gaṇapaḥ kulapaścaiva vāṇī gaṅgā sarasvatī |
dhātrī śeṣaḥ pramattaśca unmattaḥ kulabhairavaḥ || 145 ||

kṣetrapālo hanūmāṃśca dakṣo garuḍa eva ca |
prahlādaḥ śukadevaśca rāmo rāvaṇa eva ca || 146 ||

kāśyapaḥ kumbhakarṇaśca jamadagnirbhṛgustathā |
bṛhaspatiryaduśreṣṭho dattātreyo yudhiṣṭhiraḥ || 147 ||

arjuno bhīmasenaśca droṇācāryo vṛṣākapiḥ | 
duryodhanastathā kuntī sītā ca rukmiṇī tathā || 148 ||

satyabhāmā draupadī ca urvaśī ca tilottamā |
puṣpadanto mahābuddho bāṇaḥ kālaśca mandaraḥ || 149 ||

kailāsaḥ kṣīrasindhuśca udadhirhimavāṃstathā |
nārado bhīṣmakarṇau ca meruścāruṇa eva ca || 150 ||

janakaśca tathā kautsaḥ kathitā brahmasādhakāḥ |
mahāvidyāprasādena svasvakarma samanvitāḥ || 151 ||

p. 394)

eteṣāṃ śubhanāmāni nityāvidyopasevinām |
prātaḥkāle śucirbhūtvā yaḥ paṭhet prayatātmanā || 152 ||

pūjayed vā śucirbhūtvā prasīdāmi kulapriye |
aśucirvā nirālambo sālambo vā kulāntike || 153 ||

nityapūjāphalaṃ tasya dadāmi varamīpsitam |
cakrasaṅketakaṃ caiva gurusaṅketakaṃ tathā || 154 ||

mantrasaṅketakaṃ caiva nāmasaṅketakaṃ tathā |
samayācārasaṅketamakṛtvā yo'tra varttate || 155 ||

japapūjārcanaṃ homo'bhicārāya prakalpate |
evaṃ stotraṃ paṭhitvā tu saṅketamānayed dhruvam || 156 ||


naimittikārcana vidhānam

atha vakṣyāmi śāktānāṃ naimittārcanamuttamam |
atha vaiśākhamāsasya śuklapratipadīśvari || 157 ||

brāhme muhūrtte utthāya snānaṃ sandhyāmupāsya ca |
manojñe rahasi sthāne pūrvābhimukhamāsthitaḥ || 158 ||

ātmānaṃ gandhapuṣpādyairalaṃkṛtya vidhānataḥ |
kṛtvā puroditān nyāsān devatābhāvamāsthitaḥ || 159 ||

p. 395)

matsyamāṃsādividhivad bhakṣyabhojyasamanvitam |
arghyaṃ nivedya taccheṣaṃ śaktyā saha pibettataḥ || 160 ||

yauvanollāsasahito nirvikalpena cetasā |
dhyāyaṃstanmaṇḍale devīmaṣṭottarasahasrakam || 161 ||

japtvā samarpya tatpūjāṃ devatāñca samuddharet |


tatra kālādi nirṇayaḥ

evaṃ śuklapratipadaṃ samārabhya dine dine || 162 ||

kuryājjapārcanaṃ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyantamambike |
amāvasyādine dhīraḥ pūjayed bhaktakaulikān || 163 ||

tri sapta nava pañca vā vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ |
evaṃ yo māsamātrantu kuryāt sūryodayārcanam || 164 ||

devatā tasya santuṣṭā dadāti phalamīpsitam |
yatkiñcidudite sūrye maṇḍale sveṣṭadevatām || 165 ||

dhyātvā sāvaraṇāṃ samyak prajapettu vidhānataḥ |
ṣoḍaśairupacāraistu cakrapūjāpuraḥsaram || 166 ||

kuladīpān pradarśyā'tha sādhako bhāvatatparaḥ |
madhyāhne pūjayedvāpi sāyāhne vāpi pūjayet || 167 ||

sa tatphalamavāpnoti yoginīnāṃ priyo bhavet |
kāṅkṣitāṃ labhate siddhiṃ devavadvicaredbhuvi || 168 ||

p. 396)

śuklapakṣārcanavidhānam

māghaśuklapratipadi divāvasānake budhaḥ |
snātvā śuklāmbaradharaḥ sāyaṃ sandhyāmupāsya ca || 169 ||

sūryārcanoktamārgeṇa sarvadravyasamanvitaḥ |
yauvanollāsasahitaścandrasthāṃ devatāṃ smaran || 170 ||

devīṃ sampūjya vidhivaccandramaṇḍalavāsinīm |
candrāstodayaparyantaṃ japenmantramananyadhīḥ || 171 ||

evaṃ pratidinaṃ śuklacaturdaśyantamarcayet |
paurṇamāsyāṃ yathāśaktyā pūjayecchaktikaulikān || 172 ||

evaṃ yaḥ kurute bhaktyā śuklapakṣārcanaṃ sadā |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā sarvaiśvaryasamanvitaḥ || 173 ||

sarvalokaikasampūjyaḥ śivavadvicaredbhuvi |
śuklapakṣārcanaṃ yadvattadvat pakṣe sitetare || 174 ||

yaḥ karoti vidhānena sarvān kāmān samaśnute |
iha bhuktvā'khilān bhogān devavat priyadarśanaḥ || 175 ||

yoginīvīramilanaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ |

p. 397)

kṛṣṇapakṣārcanam

atha kārttikamāsasya śuklapratipadi dhruvam || 176 ||

snātvā'camya ca śuddhātmā nyāsān kṛtvā puroditān | 
prasupte jīvaloke tu muditātmā mahāniśi || 177 ||

sūryārcanoktavidhinā sarvadravyasamanvitaḥ |
ājyenānāmikāṅguṣṭhe varttiṃ prajvālya sādhakaḥ || 178 ||

pañcavarṇarajaścitre vasupatrasaroruhe |
madhusampūrṇakalase kāṃsyapātre manohare || 179 ||

dīpaṃ saṃsthāpya purata uttarābhimukhasthitaḥ |
dīpe sāvaraṇāṃ devīṃ dhyātvā vidhivadarcayet || 180 ||

yauvanollāsasahito dīpasthāṃ devatāṃ smaran |
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu japenmantramananyadhīḥ || 181 ||

evaṃ samarcayet kṛṣṇacaturdaśyantamambike |
amāvasyādine śaktyā pūjayet śaktikaulikān || 182 ||

evaṃ kṛte mahādevī prītā bhavati sarvadā 

p. 398)

sarvakāmasamṛddhātmā sarvaiśvaryasamanvitaḥ || 183 ||

sarvalokaikasammānyaḥ sañcarecca yathāsukham |
aṣṭāṣṭakārcanaṃ kuryācchaktaścedekavāsare || 184 ||


aṣṭāṣṭakārcanam

athavā'ṣṭadineṣvevāthavā dvyaṣṭadineṣu vā |
dvātriṃśaddivaseṣveva catuḥṣaṣṭidineṣu vā || 185 ||

guruṇā kārayedetat kramajñenā'pareṇa vā |
kramajñaścet svayaṃ kuryādvittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ || 186 ||

mūlāṣṭakaṃ tu brāhmyādyaścāsitāṅgādibhairavāḥ |
maṅgalādyaiśca nipuṇairaṣṭabhiḥ śaktibhistathā || 187 ||

mūlāṣṭakodbhavānīti prasiddhāni kulāgame |
akṣobhyādicatuḥṣaṣṭimithunāni samarcayet || 188 ||

pūrvoktena vidhānea yathā vibhavamarcayet |
kramalopaṃ na kurvīta sveṣṭakāyārthasiddhaye || 189 ||

gandhapuṣpākṣatādyaiśca matsyamāṃsāsavādibhiḥ |
bhakṣyabhojyādibhirnānāpadārthaiḥ ṣaḍrasānvitaiḥ || 190 ||

samyak santoṣayettāni mithunānyatibhaktitaḥ |
prauḍhāntollāsaparyantaṃ kuryāt śrīcakrapūjanam || 191 ||

p. 399)

evaṃ yaḥ kurute bhaktyā sakṛdaṣṭāṣṭakārcanam |
brahmaviṣṇumaheśādi devatābhiḥ sa pūjyate || 192 ||

kiṃ punarmānavādyaiśca sākṣācchiva ivāparaḥ |
yadarcanāccatuḥṣaṣṭi yoginīgaṇasaṃstutaḥ || 193 ||

avyāhatājñaḥ sarvatra pūjyate devavad bhuvi |
punarāvṛttirahito nivasecchivasannidhau || 194 ||

asmāt paratarā pūjā nāsti satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
kulapūjāṃ vinā cakre nādhikāraḥ kathañcana || 195 ||

vinā yantreṇa pūjā cet devatā na prasīdati |
kulapūjāṃ suniyataṃ yaḥ karoti sa kaulikaḥ || 196 ||

sarvadā samavāpnoti yoginīvīramelanam |
nīco'pi vā sakṛd bhaktyā kārayed yaḥ kulārcanam || 197 ||

sa sadgatimavāpnoti kimutā'nye dvijātayaḥ |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena sarvāvasthāsu sarvadā || 198 ||

p. 400)

kulapūjārato bhūyādabhīṣṭaphalasiddhaye |
vedaśāstroktamārgeṇa kulapūjāṃ karoti yaḥ || 199 ||

tatsamīpasthitā nityaṃ śivena saha śaṅkarī |
idaṃ satyamidaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 200 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye pañcadaśollāsaḥ || 15 ||



ṣoḍaśa ullāsaḥ 


kuladīkṣāvidhānam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi kuladīkṣāṃ susiddhaye |
adīkṣitakulo yo hi sādhanaṃ samupācaret || 1 ||

sa mahāpātakī bhūtvā rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | 
adīkṣitakulāsaṅgāt siddhihāniḥ prajāyate || 2 ||

tatkathāśravaṇaṃ cet syāttattalpāgamanaṃ yadi |
sa kulīnaḥ kathaṃ caiva pūjayet parameśvarīm || 3 ||

dīkṣitā na ca yoṣā cet kathaṃ syātkulapūjanam |
yatkule kuladīkṣā tu na syāt pāpī sa eva hi || 4 ||

kula pūjā na ceccaiva kulamantrāḥ parāṅmukhāḥ |
parayoṣā yadi syācca svayaṃ tasyā gururbhavet || 5 ||

p. 402)

nijākāntāṃ samānīya suśīlāṃ suyaśasvinīm |
kulabhaktaṃ guruṃ prārthya dīkṣayet kuladīkṣayā || 6 ||

śivaśca kaula eva syācchivāḥ syuḥ sarvadeśikāḥ |
gaṇeśo gāṇapatyaśca gaṇadīkṣāprabhuryataḥ || 7 ||

suraḥ saurāstathā caiva viṣṇurviṣṇuparāyaṇāḥ |
kulīnaḥ sarvamantrāṇāmadhikārī bhavet sadā || 8 ||

tataḥ sudīkṣataṃ kuryāt kulaṃ guruḥ prayatnataḥ |
dīkṣāyāṃ kulapūjāyāṃ śiṣyatve yadi vā gurau || 9 ||

lajjāparaṃ kulaṃ yatra vidyāpi tatra nidritā |
adhastāt dṛṣṭipātena tasya vidyā'pyadhomukhī || 10 ||

nimīlanāttato vidyā sādhakaṃ mārayed dhruvam |
pārśvāvalokanenaiva vyādhidāridryapīḍitā || 11 ||

caturdigālokanena uccāṭanagato bhavet |
etādṛśaṃ kulaṃ caiva yadi kuryāt kathañcana || 12 ||

tadā kulaguruṃ prārthya kārayeddīkṣitaṃ tataḥ |
etādṛśa kulasyāgre prayogaḥ phalado na hi || 13 ||

p. 403)

ityādi śikṣayā caiva śikṣitā kulajā satī |
parānandarasāghūrṇahṛdayā lolalocanā || 14 ||

mālālaṅkāraśobhāḍhyā raktavastravibhūṣaṇā |
tāmbūlapūritamukhī sindūrāñjanasaṃyutā || 15 ||

guruvaktrāt prayogārthaṃ sotsukā sādhakapriyā |
devīvāhaṃ devadeva upadeśaṃ samācara || 16 ||


śaktidīkṣākathanam

evamukte kule caiva devo'haṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
ityuktvā sarvaśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 17 ||

japtvā brāhmyādikāḥ śaktīḥ pūrvamantreṇa sevayet |
tadgātre deyamantrasya nyāsajālaṃ pravinyaset || 18 ||

nijaputrīvadācāryastadbhālapaṭale likhet |
śakticakraṃ tridhā'veṣṭya tatra kāmakalāṃ likhet || 19 ||

tanmadhye deyamantreṇa darbhitaṃ nāma rañjitam |
tatra devīṃ samāvāhya dhyātvā tāṃ tu prapūjayet || 20 ||

p. 404)

tatastatputrikākarṇe ṛṣichandaḥ samanvitām |
mūlavidyāṃ tridhā'vṛttyā kathayet vāmakarṇake || 21 ||

nāma kuryāttatastasyāḥ kāntaṃ sādhakasattamaḥ |
mātṛpitṛkṛtaṃ nāma varjayitvā vadettataḥ || 22 ||

adyaprabhṛti putri tvaṃ kulapūjārcane ratā |
sakulājñāṃ samādāya lajjālasyavivarjitā || 23 ||

yathopadiṣṭa vidhinā sāmarasyaṃ samācara |
ityanujñāṃ gurorlabdhvā praṇameddaṇḍavad bhuvi || 24 ||

trāhi nātha kulācārapakṣiṇīpadmanāyaka |
hṛtpādāmbhoruhacchāyāṃ dehi mūrdhni yaśodhana || 25 ||

vidhinā stavanaṃ kṛtvā gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadet |
sarvasvaṃ gurave dadyāttadardhaṃ vā dayāyuje || 26 ||

dakṣiṇāñca yathāśaktyā pradattvā gurave sudhīḥ |
stutvā natvā yathāśaktyā svakalpoktaṃ samācaret || 27 ||

p. 405)

praśastaśaktilakṣaṇāni

upadiṣṭā yadā devī tadā devī tu putrikā |
pūjārhā ca yadā devī tadā mātā na saṃśayaḥ || 28 ||

sarvathā pitṛputtrībhyāṃ mūlayogo na dṛśyate |
tatkṛtvā tena pāpena ubhau narakagāminau || 29 ||

cumbuke cānyaśāstro paśugrāme tathaiva ca |
na karttavyaṃ na karttavyaṃ na ca vākyaṃ kadācana || 30 ||

evaṃ kṛte gurau śiṣye mama śāpo bhaviṣyati |


śaktipūjāvidhānam

sāṅgāvaraṇapūjādau yadā na kṣaraye kulam || 31 ||

tadā mūrdhni guruṃ dhyātvā kulāmṛtarasena tu |
tarpayitvā kulaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ nirākulaḥ || 32 ||

niyamaḥ puruṣe jñeyo na yoṣitsu kadācana |
na nyāso yoṣitāñcāpi na dhyānaṃ na ca pūjanam || 33 ||

rātrau ṣaḍaṅgamācarya japaṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ |
sarvavidyāprayogaṃ tu tataḥ kuryāt susādhakaḥ || 34 ||

p. 405)

kulapuṣpakathanam

athātaḥ kulapuṣpasya vidhānaṃ kathyate'dhunā |
yatprāpya kuladevena labhyate vāñchitaṃ mahat || 35 ||

ṛtukāle mahādevī suṣumṇāmadhyavarttinī |
amṛtaṃ varṣate sā tu tridinaṃ pṛthivītale || 36 ||

taddine tatra vīrendro yadi vidyāṃ samuccaret |
pūrṇameva bhavettasya saphalaṃ ca manoratham || 37 ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kulaṃ vīkṣya japaṃ kuru |
ānīya pramadāṃ divyāṃ pramattāṃ yauvanānvitām || 38 ||

susnāpitāṃ sudīkṣitāṃ sunāsāṃ cāruhāsinīm |
sarvadānandahṛdayāṃ ghṛṇālajjāvivarjitām || 39 ||

gurubhaktāṃ suveśācca devatāpūjane ratām |
svakāntāṃ parakāntāṃ vā saṃsthāpya tūlikopari || 40 ||

nyāsajālaṃ pravinyasya sādhako'kṣubdhamānasaḥ |
tasyāstu madanāgāre pūjayet parameśvarīm || 41 ||

p. 407)

svayamakṣobhito bhūtvā svayambhūkusumaṃ labhet |
śivahīnā yadā śaktiḥ sarvādau varṣate hi yat || 42 ||

tadeva paramaṃ puṣpaṃ svayambhūkusumākhyakam |
taddravyaprāptimātreṇa kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale || 43 ||

svayambhūkusumaṃ dravyaṃ trailokye cāti durlabham |
svecchā ṛtumatī śaktiḥ sākṣāddevī maheśvarī || 44 ||

tasyāḥ puṣpaṃ sādhakena rakṣaṇīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
vastrālaṅkārapuṣpaiśca śaktiñca paripūjayet || 45 ||

dīkṣitāyā yathākāle puṣpaṃ bhavati nityaśaḥ |
kulapuṣpaṃ tadeva syāt sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 46 ||

kuṇḍodbhavañca golotthaṃ pūrvaiva kathitaṃ mayā |
kuṇḍodbhavaṃ siddhipradaṃ prakārāntaramucyate || 47 ||

yathāyogyaṃ samānīya śaktiṃ pūrvoditāṃ śubhām |
svakalpoktaṃ samācarya sādhako jitamānasaḥ || 48 ||

carvyaṃ coṣyaṃ nivedyātha vastrālaṅkaraṇādikam | 
pūjayedakṣataiḥ śuddhaiḥ tasyā madanamandiram || 49 ||

p. 408)

bhāvayet kāmabhāvena svayamacyutamānasaḥ |
śuddhamantrauṣadhenaiva mathayenmadanālayam || 50 ||

mathyamāne'pi tasyāṃ hi jāyate tattvamuttamam |
gṛhṇīyāttat prayatnena dravyaṃ kuṇḍodbhavaṃ śubham || 51 ||

etaccatuṣṭayaṃ dravyaṃ mantrasiddheśca kāraṇam |
eteṣāṃ dravyamadhye tu svayambhūkusumaṃ mahat || 52 ||

kvacidgandharvarājena labhyate vā navā punaḥ |
labdhvā tatparamaṃ dravyaṃ lākṣārasasamanvitam || 53 ||

kastūrīṃ kuṅkumaṃ caiva candanena sumiśritam |
raktena candanenāpi vaṭīṃ kṛtvā sugopayet || 54 ||

pūrvoktakramato vīro viśodhya mantrarājakam |
likhitvā pūjayedbhaktyā yāṃ tithiṃ prāpya sādhakaḥ || 55 ||

suptādidoṣaduṣṭā ye mantrā vidyāśca kīrttitāḥ |
prabuddhāstatprayogena yāvat sā punarāgatā || 56 ||

p. 409)

dvitīyāyāṃ gate caiva dṛśyā jātā kalāvatī |
tithikrameṇa sā devī paurṇamāsyāṃ phalapradā || 57 ||

tatra prayogamātreṇa kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ |
kṣīṇāti pūrṇatāṃ yāti yāvat sā tithirāgatā || 58 ||

tataḥ prayogaṃ vidyānāṃ mantrādīnāñca kārayet |
evaṃ yathā prabuddhā sā naiva tādṛk kadācana || 59 ||


pūjāphalakathanam

svayambhūkusumaiḥ pūjāṃ pratyahaṃ yaḥ samācaret |
yoginyaśca mahānāgā rākṣasādānavāśca ye || 60 ||

rājānaśca striyaḥ sarvā nityaṃ vaśyā bhavanti hi |
etenākṣatayogān madhumatīsiddhimālabhet || 61 ||

pratyekena labhet siddhiṃ kusumādyairyathāvidhi |
atha vakṣye ca śāktānāṃ kāmyapūjanamuttamam || 62 ||


kāmyapūjanakathanam

raktapadmairyajed devīṃ samastaraśmisaṃyutām |
vidhivallikhite yantre kulācāreṇa rātrike || 63 ||

māsamātreṇa vīrāṇāṃ mahāpātakakoṭayaḥ | 
janmāntarakṛtāḥ sarve nāśamāyānti niścitam || 64 ||

p. 410)

lakṣmīstasya gṛhe vaśyā susthirā bhavati dhruvam |
javāpuṣpaiścakrarājaṃ pūjayenmāsamātrakam || 65 ||

kulācāreṇa rātrau ca mantreṇa bhaktibhāvataḥ |
brahmahatyādi pāpāṃśca pūrvajanmakṛtānapi || 66 ||

nāśayennātra sandeho dhanavān jāyate naraḥ | 
ketakītaruṇaiḥ patraiḥ pūrvavat pūjayecchivām || 67 ||

upapātakasaṅghāṃśca māsamātreṇa nāśayet |
saubhāgyamatulaṃ tasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 68 ||

śatapatrairmanoramyaiḥ pūjayen māsamātrakam |
pūrvavat parameśāni sarvapāpaṃ vināśayet || 69 ||

campakaiḥ sumanoramyaiḥ pūjayet pūrvavacchivām |
māsamātreṇa hantyeva pātakān pūrvajanmajān || 70 ||

saubhāgyaṃ vā labhenmantrī bhagavatyāḥ prasādataḥ |
śvetapadmairmahādevīṃ pūjayed bhaktibhāvataḥ || 71 ||

pūrvavannāśayet pāpān triṃśajjanmakṛtānapi |
māsamātreṇa sakalān mokṣastasya kare sthitaḥ || 72 ||

p. 411)

bandhūkakusumairdevīṃ māsāmātreṇa pūjanāt |
trailokyaṃ vaśagaṃ tasya sarvapāpaṃ praṇāśayet || 73 ||

bilvapatraiśca jalajaiḥ sahaiva paripūjayet |
pūrvavat parameśānīṃ māsamātraṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 74 ||

samṛddhimān bhavet so'pi sarvapāpaharaḥ sadā |
mallikāmālatījātīkundaiśca śatapatrakaiḥ || 75 ||

śvetotpalaiḥ prasūnaiśca pūjayenmāsamātrakam |
kulācārakrameṇaiva pātakān śatajanmajān || 76 ||

brahmahatyādijanitān nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ |
muktistasya kare satyaṃ vācāṃpatisamo bhuvi || 77 ||

agastyabālabandhūkajavāraktotpalaiḥ samam |
pūrvakrameṇa sampūjya māsamātraṃ prayatnadhīḥ || 78 ||

pātakān pāśayet mantrī sākṣātkāmalasī bhavet |
campakaiḥ pāṭalairdevīṃ bakulairnāgakeśaraiḥ || 79 ||

kahlāraiḥ sindhuvāraiśca pūjayet pūrvavat kramāt |
saubhāgyamaṇḍalaṃ tasya māsamātreṇa niścitam || 80 ||

p. 412)

mahāpātakayukto vā yadi devīṃ prapūjayet |
śamīdūrvāṅkurāśvatthapallavairathavā'rkajaiḥ || 81 ||

māsena hanti kaluṣaṃ saptajanmakṛtaṃ dhruvam |
athātaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ likhyate siddhikāraṇam || 82 ||

brāhmyādyaṣṭakulaiḥ sārdhaṃ sādhakaḥ sthiradhīḥ śuciḥ |
catuṣpathe vā nadyāṃ vā vaṭamūle triśūlake || 83 ||

pretabhūmau bilvamūle haṭṭe vā rājaveśmani |
sindūreṇa likhenmantraṃ vipulaṃ sādhyadarbhitam || 84 ||

tataḥ sampūjya vidhivat kulaṃ kularasena ca |
tarpayitvā tadantaḥsthaḥ prajapenniśicārataḥ || 85 ||

tato lakṣapramāṇena siddhistāvadbhavanti hi |
atha rātrau puṇyagehe udyāne vā surālaye || 86 ||

ānīya kulajāṃ devīṃ mūlamantreṇa dīkṣayet |
tataḥ pūrvoktarūpeṇa kulakṣobhaṃ samācaret || 87 ||


ratnapūjāvidhānam

ratnapūjāvidhānaṃ hi kathayāmi viśeṣataḥ |
puṣpāṇi racayedvīro māṇikyaghaṭitāni ca || 88 ||

p. 413)

taistu pūjā prakarttavyā cakrarājasya pūrvavat |
nānāpuṣpaiḥ sugandhaiśca karpūrakṣodacarcitaiḥ || 89 ||

ekaviṃśatirātreṇa viṃśatirdharaṇībhujām |
dāsabhūtāḥ bhavantyeva mahārogāṃśca nāśayet || 90 ||

sūryavat kāntimānmantrī jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
muktāratnarañjitāni svarṇapuṣpāṇi sādhakaiḥ || 91 ||

taistu pūjā prakarttavyā nānāratnaiśca pūrvavat |
ekaviṃśatirātreṇa rājaputrī vaśībhavet || 92 ||

kalākāntiyuto vīro jāyate subhagaḥ kṣitau |
pravālaghaṭitaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaistu bahubhiryajet || 93 ||

pūrvavat parameśānīṃ kulācārakrameṇa tu |
puṣpaiśca vividhaiścaiva saptāhācca suniścitam || 94 ||

krūrāstasya vaśāḥ sarve vairivargāṃśca nāśayet |
tathā marakatakṣiptasvarṇapuṣpaiśca pūjayet || 95 ||

ekaviṃśatirātreṇa nānāpuṣpaiḥ kramādyajet |
vidhivat tasya varadā bhairavī bhavati sthiram || 96 ||

p. 414)

padmarāgamahāratnanirmitaiḥ svarṇasaṃyutaiḥ |
kusumairarcayeccakraṃ trisaptāhāt suniścitam || 97 ||

surāstasya vaśāḥ sarve bṛhaspatisamo bhavet |
suvarṇaracitaiḥ puṣpairvajrakeśaravarjitaiḥ || 98 ||

ekaviṃśatirātreṇa mohayejjagatīmimām |
devā daityāvaśāṃstasya jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 99 ||

indranīlamayaiḥ svarṇapuṣpaiścakraṃ samarcayet |
ekaviṃśatirātreṇa tathā nīlaiśca nīrajaiḥ || 100 ||

vairiṇo nāśamāyānti śeṣā vaśyā bhavanti hi |
gomedaghaṭitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ svarṇapuṣpairyajed budhaḥ || 101 ||

kiṃśukaiḥ kundapuṣpaiśca pūrvavat parameśvarīm |
saptāhād vairiṇo vaśyā ghātasteṣu prajāyate || 102 ||

trisaptāhānmahāpāpasañcayaṃ nāśayettataḥ |
svarṇaratnamayaiḥ puṣpairpunarnavaiḥ prapūjyate || 103 ||

tadā'śvamedhadaśakaṃ phalaṃ bhavati niścitam |
kravyādayastadvaśyā hi bhavantyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 104 ||

p. 415)

vaidūryaṃghaṭitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ pūjayeccakramuttamam |
sacampakādibhirdhīrastrailokyaṃ stambhayet kṣaṇāt || 105 ||

ekaviṃśatibhirvārairmahāpāpaharo bhavet | 
nirmālyabhūtaiḥ kusumairarcitaiḥ parameśvarīm || 106 ||

nānāratnamayaiḥ svarṇapuṣpairyadi śivāṃ yajet |
tadā devāśca munayaḥ pannagā rākṣasādayaḥ || 107 ||

sarve vaśyā bhavantyeva trisaptāhānna saṃśayaḥ | 
janmakoṭibhavaṃ pāpaṃ nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 108 ||

svarṇavarṇamayaiḥ puṣpairmāsamekaṃ prapūjayet |
tadāśvamedhadaśakaphalaṃ bhavati sarvadā || 109 ||

svarṇaratnāni puṣpāṇi yadi na syustadā śṛṇu |
pūrvoditasvapuṣpaiśca pūjā kāryā susādhakaiḥ || 110 ||

yadyat puṣpaṃ yatra yatra deyaṃ tatra mahātmanā |
tatra tatraiva dātavyaṃ yaddātavyaṃ sukhecchayā || 111 ||

p. 416)

alaṅkārasvarūpeṇa pūjayeccakranāyikām |
kevalaṃ yonipuṣpaistu trailokyaṃ stambhayedbhṛśam || 112 ||

māsamātreṇa pāpāni saptajanmabhavānyapi |
nāśayenmohayet sarvānsamudravalayāṃ dharām || 113 ||


tripurābījasādhanam

athedānīṃ pravakṣyāmi tripurābījasādhanam |
śuklāmbaradharo dhīraḥ śuklagandhādibhūṣitaḥ || 114 ||

śuklālaṅkāraracitaḥ śuklamālyānvitaḥ śuciḥ | 
śuklago brahmacārī tu śuklāsanaparigrahaḥ || 115 ||

pūjayet śuklapuṣpaistu naivedyairdhavalaistathā |
piṣṭakaṃ pāyasaṃ dugdhaṃ annaṃ bahuvidhaṃ tathā || 116 ||

śarkarā modakaṃ caiva nānāphalasamanvitam |
kṛtasaṅkalpavīrendraḥ sādhayedvāgbhavākṣaram || 117 ||


vāgbhavabījasādhanam

vāgbhavākhyāṃ japedvidyāṃ vāgīśīṃ saṃsmaran budhaḥ |
kūrparadhavalāṃ śubhrapuṣpābharaṇabhūṣitām || 118 ||

p. 417)

atyantaśubhravasanāṃ vajramauktikabhūṣaṇām |
muktāphalasamudbhūtajapamālālasatkarām || 119 ||

pustakaṃ varadānañca dadhatīmabhayapradām |
evaṃ dhyātvā prapūjyaiva vāgīśīṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 120 ||

mūlādibrahmarandhrāntaṃ sravatpīyūṣavarṣiṇīm |
tasmājjotirmayīṃ dhyāyet jihvāgre'mṛtarūpiṇīm || 121 ||

vibhāvya prajapenmantraṃ puraścaraṇasaṃkhyakam |
tataśca naṣṭahṛdayo grāmyo mūrkho'tipātakī || 122 ||

śaṭho'pi yaḥ padaṃ spaṣṭamakṣaraṃ vaktumakṣamaḥ |
jaḍo mūrkho'tidurmedhā gataprajño vinaṣṭadhīḥ || 123 ||

so'pi sañjāyate vāgmī vācaspatirivāparaḥ |
satpaṇḍitaghaṭāṭopajetā'pratihataprabhaḥ || 124 ||

sattarkapadavākyārthaśabdālaṅkārasāravit |
kumāratarasphāra vṛttālaṅkārapūrvakaiḥ || 125 ||

padagumphairmahākāvyakartā so'pi prajāyate |
vedavedāṅgavedāntasiddhāntajñānapāragaḥ || 126 ||

p. 418)

jyotiḥśastretihāsādimīmāṃsāsmṛtisāravit |
purāṇarasavādādigāruḍānekamantravit || 127 ||

pātālaśāstravijñānabhūtatantrārthatattvavit |
vicitracitrakarmādiśilpānekavicakṣaṇaḥ || 128 ||

sarvabhāṣārutajñānī samastalipikarmavit |
nānāśāstrādiśikṣādivettābhuvanaviśrutaḥ || 129 ||

sarvavāṅmayavettā ca sarvajño bhuvi jāyate |


kāmabījasādhanam

atha kāmakalāsaktaḥ sādhako raktamandire || 130 ||

raktālaṅkārasaṃyukto raktagandhānulepanaḥ |
raktavastrāvṛtaḥ samyaṅmadhye kāmakalātmanā || 131 ||

raktapuṣpaiśca vividhaiḥ naivedyai raktasannibhaiḥ |
nānopahārabalibhiḥ kuṅkumādibhirarcayet || 132 ||

mūlādibrahmarandhrāntaṃ spharaddīptisvarūpiṇīm |
bandhūkakusumākārakāntibhūṣaṇabhūṣitām || 133 ||

evaṃ sacintya vīrendraḥ pūjayet parameśvarīm |
yathoktasaṃkhyaṃ sañjapya kāmarājaṃ susādhakaḥ || 134 ||

cintayet parameśānīṃ trailokyaṃ mohayet kṣaṇāt |
rājāno vaśamāyānti pannagā rākṣasāḥ surāḥ || 135 ||

kandarpa iva vīrendro yoṣitāṃ mānahārakaḥ |
manaścintitayoṣittu dāsīvadvaśagābhavet || 136 ||

p. 419)

taddṛṣṭipathagā nārī surī vāpyathavā'surī |
vidyādharī kinnarī vā yakṣī nāgāṅganā'thavā || 137 ||

pracaṇḍatarabhūpālakanyakā siddhakanyakā |
jvalanmadanaduṣprekṣyadahanottaptamānasā || 138 ||

klinna pracalitāpāṅgā vimūḍhā madavihvalā |
niveditātmasarvasvā vaśagā tasya jāyate || 139 ||

calajjalendusaṅkāśā bālārkakiraṇāruṇā |
cintitā yoṣitāṃ yonau kṣobhayati ca tatkṣaṇāt || 140 ||

saiva sindūravarṇābhā hṛdaye cintitā satī |
sammohonmādanāropacittakarṣakarī tathā || 141 ||

p. 420)

niyojitā'thavā mūrdhni varṣantī raktabindubhiḥ |
dhāraṇāsamprayogeṇa karoti vaśagaṃ jagat || 142 ||


trailokyamohanaprayogakathanam

ākarṣayettadā śīghraṃ rambhāñcāpi tilottamām |
raktavarṇāṃ striyaṃ dhyātvā tadīyamahasā tataḥ || 143 ||

tasyā mūrdhni smaredvidyāṃ sravatpīyūṣavarṣiṇīm |
dhyāyan sammohayennārīṃ madanottaptamānasām || 144 ||

kṣaṇamātreṇa vīrendrastrailokyaṃ vaśamānayet |
etatkāmakalādhyānāt pañcakāma ivāparaḥ || 145 ||

śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ hūṃ tataḥ saṃsu krameṇa pariyojitāḥ |
yantraṃ vidadhyāt kāmastu manmathāntargato bhavet || 146 ||

kandarpasampuṭaṃ kṛtvā koṇagarbhagataṃ tataḥ |
makaradhvajasañjñantu sarvametat susādhakaḥ || 147 ||

mīnaketugataṃ kuryāt mohayejjagatīmimām |
trailokyamohano nāma prayogo'yaṃ pradarśitaḥ || 148 ||

p. 421)

śaktibījasādhanam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi śaktibījasya sādhanam |
sṛṣṭisaṃhāraparyantāṃ śarīre cintayet parām || 149 ||

sravatpīyūṣadhārābhirvaṣantīṃ viṣahāriṇīm |
hemaprabhābhāsamānāṃ vidyullatāsamaprabhām || 150 ||

sphuraccandrakalāpūrṇakapālaṃ varadābhaye |
jñānamudrāñca dadhatīṃ sākṣādamṛtarūpiṇīm || 151 ||

evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantrī hemābhairupacārakaiḥ |
pūrvasaṃkhyaṃ japedbījaṃ nānāvighnaṃ vināśayet || 152 ||

etasyāḥ smaraṇādvīro nīlakaṇṭhatvamāgataḥ |
vainateyasamo mantrī viṣabhāraṃ vināśayet || 153 ||

nāgā darśanamātreṇa jaḍībhavanti tatkṣaṇāt |
bhūtapretapiśācāṃśca nāśayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 154 ||

cāturthikajvarān sarvānapasmārāṃśca nāśayet |
duṣṭavyādhigrahāṃścaiva ḍākinīrūpikā gaṇāḥ || 155 ||

yakṣarākṣasavetālaistrinetra iva dṛśyate |

p. 422)

athavā yena vidyeyaṃ paripūrṇā vicintyate || 156 ||

nābhimaṇḍalahṛtpadmamukhamaṇḍalamadhyagā |
padmarāgamaṇisvacchā cintanāt sādhakasya ca || 157 ||

tasyāṣṭaguṇamaiśvaryamacirāt sampravarttate | 
tannāmasmaraṇānmantrī yoginīnāṃ bhavet priyaḥ || 158 ||

mātṛcakraṃ tasya kāye tena sārdhaṃ sukhī bhavet |
putravān dhanavān dhīro mantraṃ dhyātvā na saṃśayaḥ || 159 ||

vilikhya cakrarājaṃ tu paripūrṇāṃ prapūjayet |
prayacchati mahādevī khecarīsiddhimuttamām || 160 ||

catuḥṣaṣṭiyutāḥ koṭyo yogiṇīnāṃ mahojasām |
cakrametat samāsādya sthitāstā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 161 ||

pūrṇavidyājapāt sarvevaśyā bhavanti niścitam |
kṣobhayet svargabhūrloka pātālatalavāsinaḥ || 162 ||

kṣobhayantyeva vīrendrāḥ mahāvidyāprasādataḥ |
vāgbhavāt vākpatitvañca kāmāt kāmasamo bhavet || 163 ||

p. 423)

śivatvaṃ śaktibījena trikūṭe sarvamālabhet |
sarvasiddhirbhavatyeva kūṭatrayasya jāpataḥ || 164 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye ṣoḍaśollāsaḥ || 16 ||



saptadaśa ullāsaḥ 


mantraśikhāvidhānam

atha vakṣye ca mantrāṇāṃ śikhāṃ siddhipradāyikām |
yasya vijñānamātreṇa kṣipraṃ vidyā prasīdati || 1 ||

mūlarandhre tu yā śaktirbhujagākārarūpiṇī |
tatra yaśca bhramāvartto'pāna ityucyate budhaiḥ || 2 ||

nīvārayuktavidhinā kūjantī satatotthitā |
gacchantī brahmarandhre'pi praviśantī svaketane || 3 ||

yātāyātakrameṇaiva kuryāttatra manolayam |
tena mantraśikhā jātā sarvamantrapradīpikā || 4 ||

tamaḥpūrṇagṛhe yadvat na kiñcit pratibhāsate |
śikhāhīnāstathā mantrā na sidhyanti kadācana || 5 ||

śikhopadeśaḥ sarvatra goptavyaḥ sādhakena ca |
vinā'nena na sidhyanti kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 6 ||

p. 425)

caturvidhā tu yā sṛṣṭiryasyā yonau prajāyate |
punaḥ pralīyate tasyāṃ kālāgnyādiśivāntikā || 7 ||

yonimudrā parā hyeṣā vandhastasyāḥ prakīrttitaḥ |
tasyāstu bandhamātreṇa tannāsti yanna sādhayet || 8 ||

anyathā japyate yastu anyathā kurute tu yaḥ |
nāsau sidhyati mantraśca satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 9 ||

chinnā ruddhāśca ye mantrāḥ kīlitāḥ stambhitāśca ye |
dagdhāḥ santrāsitā hīnā malināstu tiraskṛtāḥ || 10 ||

bheditā bhramasaṃyuktāḥ śaptāḥ sammūrchitāśca ye |
vṛddhā bālāstathā srastāḥ prauḍhā yauvanagarvitāḥ || 11 ||

aripakṣasthitā ye ca nirvīryāḥ sattvavarjitāḥ |
aṃśakena vihīnāśca khaṇḍaśaḥ śatadhā kṛtāḥ || 12 ||

vidhinā'nena saṃyuktāḥ prabhavantyacireṇa tu |
siddhimokṣapradāḥ sarve sādhakena niyojitāḥ || 13 ||

yadyaduccāryate mantrī varṇarūpaṃ śubhāvaham |
tattat sidhyatyanāyāsād yonimudrānibandhanāt || 14 ||

p. 426)

dīkṣayitvā vidhānena abhiṣicya sahasradhā |
tato'dhikārī tantre'smin bhavet sādhakasattamaḥ || 15 ||

brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi trailokyamapi ghātayet |
nā'sau lipyati pāpena yonimudrānibandhanāt || 16 ||

na tasya pāpapuṇyaistu kṛtairdoṣairna lipyate |
kṛtvā pāpasahasrāṇi asaṃkhyātāni sarvadā || 17 ||

dahatyuccāramātreṇa yonimudrānibandhanāt |
duṣṭamantrāśca sidhyanti mantrāścānye tu kiṃ punaḥ || 18 ||

sthānasthā varadā mantrā dhyānasthāśca phalapradāḥ |
sthānadhyānaviniryuktāḥ susiddhā api vairiṇaḥ || 19 ||

sidhyanti yena tatsthānaṃ kathayāmi viśeṣataḥ |
sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tathā sakalaniṣkalam || 20 ||

kalābhinnaṃ kalātītaṃ ṣoḍhāsthānaṃ śivo'bravīt |
sakalaṃ brahmabandhasthaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ viddhi niṣkalam || 21 ||

mānasaṃ sūkṣmanāmānaṃ hṛtsthaṃ sakalaniṣkalam |
bindusthitaṃ kalābhinnaṃ kalātītaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ || 22 ||

p. 427)

kalāṅgaśūlinī saiva nādaśaktiḥ śivoditā |
eṣu sthānaṅgatā mantrāḥ sthānasthāḥ parikīrttitāḥ || 23 ||

devatāṃ hṛdi sañcintya mantrajāpaśca jāyate |
dhyānasthāścaiva te mantrā vijñeyāḥ sādhakena vā || 24 ||

tārasampuṭito vāpi duṣṭamantro'pi sidhyati |
yasya yatra dṛḍhā bhaktirjāyate satataṃ yadi || 25 ||

tasya siddho bhavenmantro yathoktajapamātrataḥ |
athātaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ likhyate siddhikāraṇam || 26 ||

brāhmyādyaṣṭakulaiḥ sārdhaṃ sādhakaḥ sthiradhīḥ śuciḥ |
catuṣpathe vā nadyāṃ vā vaṭamūle triśūlake || 27 ||

pretabhūmau bilvamūle haṭṭe vā rājaveśmani |
sindūreṇa likhed yantraṃ vipulaṃ sādhyadarbhitam || 28 ||

tatra sampūjya vidhivat kulaṃ kularasena ca |
tarpayitvā tadantaḥsthaṃ prajapenniśicārataḥ || 29 ||

p. 428)

tato lakṣapramāṇena siddhipradā bhavanti hi |
rātrau puṇyagṛhe caiva udyāne vā surālaye || 30 ||

ānīya kulajāṃ devīṃ mūlamantreṇa dīkṣayet |
tataḥ pūrvoktarūpeṇa kulakṣobhaṃ samācaret || 31 ||

evaṃ kṛtaṃ na siddhiścenmūlamantraṃ samabhyaset |
pīṭhānāṃ paramaṃ pīṭhaṃ kāmarūpaṃ mahāphalam || 32 ||

atra yā kriyate pūjā sakṛdvā sādhakena ca |
vihāya sarvapīṭhāni tasya dehe vasecchivā || 33 ||

tataḥ śataguṇaṃ proktaṃ kāmākhyāyonimaṇḍalam |
yatra koṭigaṇaiḥ sārdhamādyā vasati mardinī || 34 ||

yatpīṭhaṃ brahmaṇo vaktraṃ guptaṃ sarvasukhāvaham |
yato devyaśca devāśca munayaścaiva bhāvajāḥ || 35 ||

sarve'pyāvirbhavantyatra tena guptaṃ mahākulam |
dvividhaṃ caiva tatpīṭhaṃ guptaṃ vyaktaṃ sadā paśau || 36 ||

vyaktaṃ guptaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ durāpaṃ sādhakena vai |
vyaktaṃ sarvatra deveśa labhyate kulasundaraiḥ || 37 ||

tatrāpi lakṣamānena siddhirbhavati niścitam |
athavā'nyaprakāreṇa kathyate sādhanaṃ mahat || 38 ||

p. 429)

ānīya devīṃ tadgātre vyāpakāntaṃ pravinyaset |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhistāṃ sampūjya vidhānataḥ || 39 ||

prathamaṃ sādhakaśreṣṭho devīkūṭasya mastake |
mantraṃ vilikhya yathoktaṃ pūjayet kulavartmanā || 40 ||

prathame pīṭhadevīñca pūjayed gandhapuṣpakaiḥ |
mahābhāgāṃ tato mūladevīmāvaraṇaiḥ saha || 41 ||

lakṣaikaṃ tatra sañjapya uḍḍīyānaṃ tato viśet |
tatra pīṭhe yoganidrāṃ pūjayitvā tato yajet || 42 ||

nijeṣṭadevatāṃ tatra japellakṣaṃ samāhitaḥ |
kāmarūpaṃ tato gatvā tatra kātyāyanīṃ yajet || 43 ||

pūjayitvā tato devīṃ japellakṣaṃ samāhitaḥ |
tato jālandhare gatvā pūrṇeśīṃ prathamaṃ yajet || 44 ||

tatrāpi lakṣamānaṃ tu japtvā mantraṃ samāhitaḥ |
tataḥ pūrṇagiriṃ gatvā yajeccaṇḍīṃ samāhitaḥ || 45 ||

pūjayitvā mahādevīṃ japellakṣamananyadhīḥ |
sindūrabindupīṭhe ca kāmākhyāṃ prathamaṃ yajet || 46 ||

p. 430)

tataḥ prānte mahādevīṃ yajeddikkaravāsinīm |
evaṃ pīṭheśvarīṃ japtvā pūjayediṣṭadevatām || 47 ||

saptapīṭhe saptalakṣaṃ japtvā rātrau samāhitaḥ |
saṃkhyāpūrvaṃ tataḥ pṛcchenmahādevī kulottamam || 48 ||

taveṣṭadevataivā'haṃ vṛṇuṣva varamuttamam |
tataḥ praṇamya deveśīṃ vṛṇuyādvaramuttamam || 49 ||

yadyevaṃ naiva sā devī punaḥ pūrvoktamācaret |
akṣobhitaḥ kulācāraparicaryāparo bhavet || 50 ||

athavā sarvapīṭheṣu yajen mahiṣamardinīm |
tataḥ prasannā bhavati saiva kulavarapriyā || 51 ||

tato japtvā mūlamantraṃ sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet |
tato dhyātvā nyasedādau mātṛkāntarasañjñakam || 52 ||

pūrvavadvidhinā nyasya tenaiva nyasya mātṛkām |
akārādikṣakārāntamekaikakramayogataḥ || 53 ||

pañcāṅgañca punaḥ kṛtvā pañcadhā vyāpakaṃ caret |

p. 431)

mūrddhādicaraṇāntañca tasmānmūrddhāvasānakam || 54 ||

mūlena manunā mantrī kṛtvā sañcintayettataḥ |
dhyāyet kālīṃ mahādaityayuddharāgamahonmukhīm || 55 ||

dakṣiṇe cakrakhaḍgau ca bāṇaṃ śūlaṃ tathaiva ca |
vāme śaṅkhaṃ tathā carma dhanustarjanameva ca || 56 ||

bibhratīṃ tīvrabāṇañca mahiṣe tu niṣeduṣīm |
pitāmbaradharāṃ devīṃ pīnonnatakucadvayām || 57 ||

jaṭāmukuṭaśobhāḍhyāṃ pitṛbhūmisukhāvahām |
evaṃ dhyātvā mahādevīṃ mānasairupacārakaiḥ || 58 ||

pūjayedvidhivad bhaktyā svakīya hṛdi paṅkaje |
tataḥ so'haṃ vibhāvyātha puṣpaṃ niḥkṣipya mastake || 59 ||

ghaṭasthāpanamārabhya pīṭhapūjāvasānakam |
