kṛtvā ca pūrvavadvīraḥ sadāśivaṃ yajettataḥ || 60 ||

pūrvoktavidhinā caiva dhyātvā ca paradevatām |
yantramadhye samāvāhya mūlena parikalpitām || 61 ||

āvāhanādikaṃ caiva pūrvavacca samācaret |
sampūjya ca mahādevīṃ pūjayettadanantaram || 62 ||

devyā vāme yajet paścātpūrvoktañca guroḥkulam |
śāktaṃ japtvā ca gurvādīn nāradañca tato yajet || 63 ||

p. 432)

aṣṭapatre yajeddevīṃ durgādyāṃ dīrghapūrvikām |
durgā ca prathamā jñeyā dvitīyā varavarṇinī || 64 ||

aryā caiva tṛtīyā ca caturthī kanakaprabhā |
kṛttikā pañcamī proktā ṣaṣṭhī caivābhayapradā || 65 ||

kālī surāgiṇī caiva japtvā pūrvoditakramāt |
āyudhāni dalāgreṣu yādibhiḥ kramato yajet || 66 ||

brāhmyādyāśca tataḥ paścāt lokapālāṃstato bahiḥ |
tadastrāṇi bahirjaptvā tarpayittvā vidhānataḥ || 67 ||

tataḥ sampūjayeddevīṃ pūrvavat sādhakottamaḥ |
tarpayitvā mahādevīṃ mantrajāpaṃ samācaret || 68 ||

māturmahiṣamardinyāḥ saṅketaṃ kathayāmyataḥ |
kulācārasya saṃsiddhyai bhuktimuktisusiddhaye || 69 ||

bhāntaṃ viyat sanayanaṃ śveto mardinī ṭha dvayam |
aṣṭākṣarī samākhyātā vidyā mahiṣamardinī || 70 ||

p. 433)

sṛṣṭisthitivināśānāmādibhūtā sanātanī |
na kasmaicit pravaktavyā kathitā siddhikāriṇī || 71 ||

atyantagurubhaktāya śuddhāya sā hi prārthyate |
tadāṣṭavargā dātavyā saphalaṃ tasya sādhanam || 72 ||

praṇavādyāṃ japedvidyāṃ māyādyāṃ vā japet sudhīḥ |
vadhūbījādikāṃ vāpi kavacādyāṃ japettadā || 73 ||

sarvakāleṣu sarvatra kāmādyāṃ prajapet sudhīḥ |
vāgbhavādyāṃ japet tāṃ tu devīṃ vākyaviśuddhaye || 74 ||

sādhāraṇī prāṇavidyā hṛllekhā siddhikhecarā |
etatpūrvasthitā devī surasiddhividhāyinī || 74 ||

viśeṣataḥ kaliyuge mahāsiddheśca dāyinī |
gurūṇāṃ kulanāthānāṃ māyāpāśavimocinī || 76 ||

p. 434)

tasmādyatnena satataṃ goptavyeyaṃ navākṣarī |
aṣṭalakṣaṃ japenmantraṃ taddaśāṃśaṃ hunettataḥ || 77 ||

puraścaraṇāṣṭalakṣaṃ boddhavyaṃ sādhakena ca |
yathāśakti japaṃ kṛtvā samāpyātha stutiṃ paṭhet || 78 ||


mahiṣaghnistuti

jaya devi jagaddhātri tripurādye tridaivate |
bhaktebhyo varade devi mahiṣaghni namo'stute || 79 ||

rogavighnavināśāya kulācārasamṛddhaye |
karālavadane śyāme namaste surasundari || 80 ||

kulabhaktaprasannāsye śaṅkaraprāṇavallabhe |
raktamāṃsasamākīrṇavadane tvāṃ namāmyaham || 81 ||

iti stavane saṃstutya śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret |
visarjanaṃ vidhāyātha prayogañca tataścaret || 82 ||

etadvidyā mahāvidyā na deyā yasya kasyacit |
yadi bhāgyavaśāt labhyā kuladevī kulottamaiḥ | | 83 ||

dīkṣitā kulajābhistu siddhidā saiva nānyathā |

p. 435)

atha dūtīyajanam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi dūtīyajanamuttamam || 84 ||

śākto vā vaiṣṇavo vāpi śaivo vā'pyanyapūjakaḥ |
yasmāt kṣipraṃ bhavet siddhirnānyathā janmakoṭibhiḥ || 85 ||

mantrasya siddhikāmastu dūtīyajanamācaret |
kiṃ japanyāsavidhinā dhyānapūjādivistaraiḥ || 86 ||

yāvaddūtīṃ na pūjyeta tāvat siddhirbhavet kutaḥ |
gandharvarūpavān bhūtvā yonau sampūjya sādhakaḥ || 87 ||

ihāmutra sukhī bhūtvā devīputro bhavet kṣitau |
trikoṇeṣu trayo devā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ || 88 ||

yonimadhye yejennityaṃ caṇḍikāṃ kuladevatām |
tatra vidyāṃ jagaddhātrīṃ kulāmṛtaiḥ kulakramāt || 89 ||

sa bhavet sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ śivatulyo bhavennaraḥ |
tatra yatra kriyate pūjā japaṃ vā tarpaṇaṃ punaḥ || 90 ||

tadanantaphalaṃ kartturbhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
yuvatīyonimāsthāya sarvasiddhirbhavedyataḥ || 91 ||

p. 436)

tasmādvai yoṣitāṃ yonau pūjayet sādhakottamaḥ |
sarvālaṅkārasaṃyukto raktavastravibhūṣitaḥ || 92 ||

paramānandato rātrau pañcatattvena saṃyajet |
svakalpoktaṃ prakurvīta nyāsādikaṃ susādhakaḥ || 93 ||

mastakaṃ gajadantasya hemarūpyādinirmitam |
arghyapātre pratiṣṭhānaṃ kāryaṃ hetujalena ca || 94 ||

viśuddhakṣaumaracitāṃ hūnikāṃ cātra yojayet |
puṣpānyakāryamadhye ca uparyupari pūjayet || 95 ||

maṇḍūkaṃ pūjayedādau rudraṃ kālāgnisaṃyutam |
ādhāraśaktiṃ kūrmañca tathānantavarāhakam || 96 ||

pṛthivīñca tathā kandaṃ nālaṃ vasudalaṃ tathā |
keśarāṇi ca sampūjya karṇikāyāṃ yajettataḥ || 97 ||

dharmaṃ jñānañca vairāgyaṃ aiśvaryañca kramādyajet |
apūrvān pūjayedetān sādhakaḥ siddhihetave || 98 ||

p. 437)

ātmāntarātmaparamajñānātmānaṃ krameṇa ca |
pūjayitvā tataścaiva gururūpān samarcayet || 99 ||

advaitācārasampannāṃ gurubhaktāṃ dṛḍhavratām |
sadanuṣṭhānaniratāṃ sāttvikībhaktisaṃyutām || 100 ||

kuryāt paiśunyarahitāṃ amāyāṃ bhaktavatsalām |
cāturyaudāryadākṣiṇyakaruṇādiguṇānvitām || 101 ||

rūpayauvanasampannāṃ śīlasaubhāgyaśālinīm |
manogṛhītaviśvāsāṃ yadvā saṅketamāgatām || 102 ||

athavā tatkṣaṇāyātāṃ madanānalatāpitām |
viliptāṃ raktavastreṇa raktagandhavibhūṣitām || 103 ||

sugandhiraktakusumāṃ sarvābharaṇarājitām |
sudhūpairdhūpitāṃ tanvīṃ dūtīkarmaṇi yojayet || 104 ||

evambhūtāṃ yajedyastu prasūnatūlikopari |
tasya gātre svakalpoktaṃ nyāsajālaṃ vidhāya ca || 105 ||

ṣoḍaśairupacāraistu pūjayet sthiramānasaḥ |
japtvā ca hūnikāmadhye prasūnaiśca sugandhibhiḥ || 106 ||

candanāgurukarpūrakastūrīkuṅkumādibhiḥ |
samākīrṇe suparyaṅke pūjayet kulanāyikām || 107 ||

p. 438)

pūjayitvā sadā bhaktyā sthiracittaḥ sthirendriyaḥ |
baṭukaṃ gaṇapaṃ durgāṃ kṣetrapālaṃ tathaiva ca || 108 ||

pūrvādikramato mantrī pūjayet siddhihetave |
trikoṇe pūjayenmūrdhni kāmeśīṃ tasya madhyage || 109 ||

gaṇeśañca kulādhyakṣaṃ durgāṃ lakṣmīṃ sarasvatīm |
trikoṇe pūjayitvā tu vasantaṃ madanaṃ tathā || 110 ||

stanayoḥ pūjayet paścāt mukhe tasyāḥ kalādharam |
dakṣapādādimūrdhāntaṃ vāmamūrdhādikaṃ tathā || 111 ||

pūjayet sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ kalā vai kāmasomayoḥ |
śraddhā prītīratiścaiva dūtiḥ kāntirmanoramā || 112 ||

vimalā modinī ghorā madanonmādinī tathā |
mohinī dīpanī caiva śoṣaṇī caiva śaṅkarī || 113 ||

rañjanī ca krameṇaiva ṣoḍaśī priyadarśanā |
ṣoḍaśasvarasaṃyuktā etāḥ kāmakalā yajet || 114 ||

pūṣā ramā ca sumanā ratiḥ prītistathā dhṛtiḥ |
ṛddhiḥ saumyā marīciśca tathā caivāṃśumālinī || 115 ||

p. 439)

aṅgirā pāvanī caiva chāyā sampūrṇamaṇḍalā |
tathā tuṣṭāmṛte caiva kalāḥ somasya ṣoḍaśa || 116 ||

svaraireva prapūjyā hi sarvakāmārthasiddhaye | 
tasyāstu madanāgāre pūjayed bhagamālinīm || 117 ||

vāgbhavaṃ bhagaśabdāvate bhuge bhagini cālikhet |
athodari bhagānte ca bhagamāle bhagāvahe || 118 ||

bhagaguhye bhagayoni tato bhaganipātini |
sarvabhagavaśaṅkari bhagarūpe tataḥ param || 119 ||

nityaklinne bhagaprānte svarūpe sarvāṇyālikhet |
bhagāni me hyānayānte varade'tha samālikhet || 120 ||

rete surete'tha bhaga klinne klinnadrave tataḥ |
kledaya drāvayā'moghe bhagavicce tataḥ param || 121 ||

kṣubhaḥ kṣobhayaśabdānte sarvapadaṃ vadettataḥ |
sattvān bhageśvari brūyāt vāgbhavaṃ blūṃ jamādikam || 122 ||

bheṃ blūṃ moṃ blūṃ pradānte heṃ heṃ klinne tato vadet |
sarvāṇi bhagāni me vaśamānaya coddharet || 123 ||

badhū hrbleṃ bhuvaneśīṃ tataśca bhagamālini |
bhagamālāñca nityāñca ṅeyutāṃ manumuddharet || 124 ||

p. 440)

bhagamālāṃ mahāvidyāṃ pūjayitvā ca sādhakaḥ |
sarvānandamaye madhye cakrarājaṃ samarcayet || 125 ||

pūrvapaścimayogena nijavidyāṃ prapūjayet |
tatrāpyāvāhanaṃ nāsti jīvanyāsaṃ tathaiva ca || 126 ||

vidhivat pūjanaṃ kṛtvā svaliṅge tadanantaram |
tārañca bhuvaneśānīṃ tripurāyāstrayaṃ tataḥ || 127 ||

namaḥ śivāya vidyeyaṃ daśārṇā parikīrttitā |
anena manunā mantrī svaliṅge pūjayecchivam || 128 ||

yajettatpuruṣāghorasadyovāmeśasañjñayā |
nivṛttiñca pratiṣṭhāṃ ca vidyāṃ śāntiṃ tataḥ param || 129 ||

śāntyatītāṃ ca sampūjya ṣaḍaṅgāvaraṇaṃ yajet |
samagravidyāmuccārya tattrikoṇe prapūjayet || 130 ||

puṣpaṃ dhūpādikaṃ dattvā nirvikalpamanāḥ svayam |
sarvasaṅkṣobhaṇīṃ mudrāṃ baddhvā yoniṃ vicālayet || 131 ||

p. 441)

madhyame madhyage kṛtvā kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭharodhite |
tarjanyau daṇḍavat kṛtvā madhyamoparyanāmike || 132 ||

eṣā tu paramā mudrā sarvasaṅkṣobhaṇī matā |
kṣobhayedathavā mantrī gajahastākhyamudrayā || 133 ||

adhomukhaṃ dakṣapāṇiṃ nidhāyā'ṅguṣṭhake same |
niḥkṣipedaṅgulīḥ sarvā gajatuṇḍākṛtiryathā || 134 ||

gajahastā mahāmudrā kathitā siddhidāyikā |
uccaredbhagamālāñca drāviṇībījamuccaran || 135 ||

akṣubdhamanayoryoge mahābinduḥ prajāyate |
rajomayaṃ rajaḥ sākṣāt saṃvideva na saṃśayaḥ || 136 ||

prakṛtiḥ parameśānī bījaṃ puruṣa ucyate |
śivaśaktisamāyogo yoga eva na saṃśayaḥ || 137 ||

śītkāro mantrarūpastu vacanaṃ stavanaṃ bhavet |
āliṅganañca kastūrī karpūraṃ cumbanaṃ bhavet || 138 ||

nakhadantakṣatādīni puṣpamālādipūjanam |
līlādikaṃ dhūpadīpaṃ kucamardaṃ śivārcanam || 139 ||

p. 442)

maithunaṃ tarpaṇaṃ viddhi vīryapāto visarjanam |
kuladravyeṇa saṃśodhya śivaśaktimayo bhavet || 140 ||

vīryāmṛtaṃ paraṃbrahma hunedrāhu mukhāmbuje |
kuṇḍe triguṇasampanne śrībindunilaye pare || 141 ||

parāparavibhāgena kulīno viśvapūjitaḥ |
viviktuḥ kutsanaparo jāyate gurutalpagaḥ || 142 ||

prakṛtañca samāpyātha gṛhītvādravyamuttamam |
tarpayitvā mahādevīṃ śaktaye dakṣiṇāṃ dadet || 143 ||

suvastrābharaṇādyaiśca śaktiñca paritoṣayet |
trividhaṃ tripurāyāśca kathitaṃ pūjanaṃ mahat || 144 ||

ata-eva mahāprājño nirvikalpaḥ sadā bhavet |
tadā suvistaraiḥ stavaiḥ sarvāṇi pariśodhayet || 145 ||

nirvikalpatayā gātrayajñe doṣo na vidyate |
aśvamedhādiyajñādau vājihatyā kathaṃ bhavet || 146 ||

p. 443)

sarvabhojyavastūnāmāmiṣatvam

phalaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ caiva madhu maureyamaikṣavam |
paiṣṭībhavaṃ dhānyabhavaṃ tathā cakravinirmitam || 147 ||

sahakārabhavaṃ caiva vividhaṃ vastubhedataḥ |
mādakaṃ dharmasañchedāt varjyamāhurmanīṣiṇiḥ || 148 ||

jñānena saṃskṛte tattu mahāpātakanāśanam | 
brahmahatyāsurāpānasvarṇasteyādipātakān || 149 ||

nāśayet pūjanāccaiva nirvikalpaḥ sa mantravit |
vicārayet sadā sarvaṃ sattarkeṇa susādhakaḥ || 150 ||

payaḥ pibantīha devā gavāṃ nāśnanti śoṇitam |
mīnaṃ na bhuñjate hanta vidhavā tat kathaṃ payaḥ || 151 ||

pibanti saṃsargaduṣṭaṃ yathānyadapi vastuṣu |
jalaṃ jalacaraṃ viddhi ghaṭapūrṇaṃ samānayet || 152 ||

sthāpitaṃ taddhi saptāhaṃ jalaṃ jīvasamanvitam |
anāmiṣaṃ nāsti kiñcit sarvaṃ kṣīrādikaṃ tathā || 153 ||

śamīvṛkṣe yathā vahniḥ sadā tiṣṭhati nityaśaḥ |
sarvabhūteṣu vijñānaṃ tathā jñeyaṃ susādhakaiḥ || 154 ||

p. 444)

kāṣṭhādigharṣaṇāccaiva prakaṭo vahnirucyate |
tatkāṣṭhaṃ dahyate tena tathā brahmamayaṃ jagat || 155 ||

pāpapuṇyavinirmuktaṃ jñānametadudāhṛtam |
yajñayuktasya nānyasya anyathā pātakaṃ bhavet || 156 ||


sakalaṃ pavitraṃ vāsanā kutsitā

mādakaṃ vastu sakalaṃ varjayetkanakādikam |
dharmādharmaparijñānāt saṃskārācca pavitratā || 157 ||

viṇmūtrastrīrajo vāpi nakhāsthi sakalaṃ tathā |
vicārayenmantravittu bhrāntireva na saṃśayaḥ || 158 ||

strīrajaḥ paramaṃ dravyaṃ dehastena prajāyate |
kathaṃ taddūṣaṇaṃ yena prāpyate paramaṃ padam || 159 ||

puruṣasya yad bījaṃ bindurityabhidhīyate |
bindustu paramaṃ dravyaṃ kāyo'yaṃ śivarūpakaḥ || 160 ||

p. 445)

śivatattvena cāsthyādidūṣaṇaṃ nāsti vaindave |
pṛthivyāpastato vāyurākāśaṃ candrasūryakau || 161 ||

etaiḥ sthūlaśarīraṃ tu śukraśoṇitasambhavam |
pitṛtastrīṇi jāyante majjāsthisnāyureva ca || 162 ||

mātṛtastrīṇi jāyante tvaṅmāṃsaśoṇitāni ca |
ṣāṭkauṣikamidaṃ proktaṃ śarīraṃ paramarṣibhiḥ || 163 ||

mūlaśuddhiḥ sarvaśuddhiriti nyāyaḥ pravarttate |
śarīraṃ yadi śuddhaṃ syāttadā tatra na dūṣaṇam || 164 ||

sarvathā naiva doṣo'sti tathā śukreṣu niścitam |
tataḥ pavitraṃ dehasya kāraṇaṃ kena dūṣyate || 165 ||

jñānamārgeṇa sakalaṃ nirvikalpaṃ na dūṣaṇam |
savikalpo yadi bhavet pāpabhāgjāyate naraḥ || 166 ||

annaṃ brahmamayaṃ viddhi tena yasya tu sambhavaḥ |
nānājīvamayaṃ tattu purīṣaṃ kena dūṣyate || 167 ||

nānāvidhā hi satataṃ devatāḥ salile sthitāḥ |
tenodakena yajjātaṃ mūtraṃ kena ca dūṣitam || 168 ||

p. 446)

gomūtraprāśanaṃ viprairgomayasya ca bhakṣaṇam |
male mūtre ca yo doṣo bhrāntireva na saṃśayaḥ || 169 ||

pavitraṃ sakalaṃ caiva vāsanā kutsitā bhavet |
tato vicārya matimān sādhakastarkaśodhitaḥ || 170 ||


paramārthataḥ pūjyapūjakabhedo mithyā

māyāmūlamidaṃ sarvaṃ tato jātamidaṃ jagat |
pūjyapūjakabhedaśca mithyaiva paramārthataḥ || 171 ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena jñānamārgeṇa śodhayet |
sarvaṃ brahmamayaṃ viddhi sattarkeṇa sasādhakaḥ || 172 ||

prakṛtañca samāpyātha gṛhṇīyād dravyamuttamam |
tarpayitvā mahādevīṃ śaktyā yaddakṣiṇāṃ dadet || 173 ||

trividhaṃ tripurāyāstu kathitaṃ pūjanaṃ mahat |
vakṣye'haṃ sarvaśāktānāṃ dūtīyajanamuttamam || 174 ||


nāyikāyajanam

pīṭhapūjāvidhiṃ kṛtvā pūrvavat sādhakottamaḥ |
saṃsthāpya tatra śaktiñca pūrvavadapi pūjayet || 175 ||

p. 447)

bhaktibhāvena sampūjya nāyikāñca yathoditām |
pūjayecca tatastasyāṃ pañca kāmān samāhitaḥ || 176 ||

māyāṃ kāmaṃ tathā pūrvaṃ blūṃ strīñca yathākramāt |
kāma bījañca kandarpa manmathañca tataḥ param || 177 ||

makaraketanaṃ caiva manobhavañca ṅeyutam |
praṇavādinamo'ntañca krameṇa gandhapuṣpakaiḥ || 178 ||

arcayitvā caturdikṣu pūjayettadanantaram |
vaṭukaṃ bhairavaṃ caiva durgāñca kṣetrapālakam || 179 ||

tasyā mūrdhni trikoṇañca yantramālikhya sādhakaḥ |
mahāpretāsanaṃ madhye bālāmeva prapūjayet || 180 ||

maulau gaṇeśaṃ keśāgre kulādhyakṣaṃ lalāṭake |
durgāṃ bhruvostathā lakṣmīṃ rasanāyāṃ sarasvatīm || 181 ||

stanadvaye vasantañca madanaṃ caiva pūjayet |
mukhe sudhānidhiṃ pṛṣṭhe glūṃ bījānantaroditam || 182 ||

dakṣiṇāṃśaṃ samāśritya āśiraścaraṇāvadhi |
pūjyāḥ kāmakalāstasyāḥ nāyikā'ṅgeṣu sarvaśaḥ || 183 ||

p. 448)

tasyāṃ ṣoḍaśakāmakalāpūjanam

śraddhā prītistathā tuṣṭirdhṛtiśca tadanantaram |
bhūtiḥ kāntirmanojñā ca vimalā modinī tathā || 184 ||

moghā manobhavakarī pramadā mohinī tathā |
dīpanī śoṣaṇī caiva vaśaṅkarī tathā punaḥ || 185 ||

rañjanī caiva kāmasya kalāḥ svaravirājitāḥ |


ṣoḍaśacandrakalāpūjanam

tataścandrakalāḥ pūjyā āśiraścaraṇāvadhi || 186 ||

pūṣā vaśā sumanā ca ratiḥ prītirdhṛtistathā |
ṛddhiśca saumyā marīcirbahumāyā tataḥ param || 187 ||

madirā śaśinī chāyā tathā sampūrṇamaṇḍalā |
tuṣṭistathā'mṛtā caiva pūjyāścandrakalā imāḥ || 188 ||

pūjayenmadanāgāre raktacandanabhūṣite |
bhagamālāmanuṃ procya pūjayettadanantaram || 189 ||

vāgbhavaṃ bhuvaneśīñca kamalā vāgbhavaṃ tataḥ |
jaṃ blūñcaiva tataḥ klinne sarvāṇīti tato vadet || 190 ||

bhagānīti tataḥ paścādvaśamānaya tatparam |
strīṃ sphrīṃ kledinī bījaṃ klīṃ klīṃ ca tataḥ param || 191 ||

bhagamālinyai namaḥ svāhā pūjayeccakramadhyage |
pūjayitvā tataścakraṃ nijadevīṃ tato yajet || 192 ||

p. 449)

vidhivat pūjayitvā ca gandhaiḥ puṣpaistathā'kṣataiḥ |
dhūpairdīpaiśca naivedyairvividhaiḥ kulasādhakaḥ || 193 ||

vidhāya vandanaṃ caiva taducchiṣṭaṃ svayaṃ haret |
arcayed gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ svaśivaṃ tadanantaram || 194 ||

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya praṇavaṃ bhuvaneśvarīm |
namaḥ śabdaṃ tataḥ paścāt śivāyeti tato vadet || 195 ||

yajettat puruṣāghorasadyo vāmeśvarānapi |
nivṛttiñca pratiṣṭhāṃ ca vidyāṃ ca tadanantaram || 196 ||

śāntiñca śāntyatītāṃ ca ṣaḍaṅgāvaraṇaṃ tataḥ |
samagravidyāmuccārya trikoṇaṃ caiva pūjayet || 197 ||

avadhūteśvarī kubjā-kāmākhyā samayā api |
vajreśvarīkālike ca tathā dikkaravāsinīm || 198 ||

mahācaṇḍeśvarītāre pūjayettatra sādhakaḥ |
tadanujñāṃ tato labdhvā bhuktvā tāmbūlamuttamam || 199 ||

svaśivaṃ tatra niḥkṣipya gajahastākhyamudrayā |
prajapet kṣobharahitaścāṣṭottarasahasrakam || 200 ||

p. 450) 

śatamaṣṭottarañcāpi akṣobhasthiramānasaḥ |
japānte tajjapaṃ devyai samarpya tadanantaram || 201 ||

kṣubdhāṃ manobhavasukhaiḥ pūjayet suciraṃ vaset |
galaccandradravaṃ tasmād gṛhītvā tarpayecchivām || 202 ||

stutvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya visarjayedanantaram |
visarjanaṃ vidhāyātha śaktiñca paritoṣayet || 203 ||

vidhivaddakṣiṇāṃ dattvā annapānādibhistathā |
evaṃ yajanamātreṇa mantrasiddhirna saṃśayaḥ || 204 ||


prakārāntaradūtīyāgaḥ

athānyat sampravakṣyāmi dūtīyāgamanuttamam |
samprāpya divasaṃ pūrvadevīñca vidhivadbhage || 205 ||

pūjayitvā japenmantramayutadvitayaṃ tathā |
tasya lakṣmīrmahāvidyā bhavecca bhuvi durlabhā || 206 ||

aṣṭamītithimāsādya pakṣayorubhayorapi |
āmantrya vidhivadyoniṃ tatraiva yoginīṃ yajet || 207 ||

p. 451)

paścāt sampūjya deveśīṃ mūlamantrañca sādhakaḥ |
ṣaṭsahasraṃ divārātrau japtvā mantramananyadhīḥ || 208 ||

samprāpya mahatīṃ vidyāṃ devīputro bhavedbhuvi |


puraścaraṇakathanam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi prakārāntaramuttamam || 209 ||

yonau sampūjayeddevīṃ sarvaduḥkhanivāriṇīm |
bhagādyairbhagamāmantrya bhage bhagavatīṃ tataḥ || 210 ||

tato vai labhate siddhiṃ mantrasya lakṣamānataḥ |
dvilakṣeṇa mahāvāgmī trilakṣeṇa mahākaviḥ || 211 ||

vedalakṣeṇa vedasya sādhakaḥ pārago bhavet |
vāksiddhiśca bhavettasya pañcalakṣapramāṇataḥ || 212 ||

rasalakṣairbhavettasya aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakam |
saptalakṣeṇa vīrendro jīvanmukto bhavennaraḥ || 213 ||

ata ūrdhvaṃ na jānāmi śiva eva na saṃśayaḥ |
vidhivacchaktimāsādya sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 214 ||

sampūjya vidhivadbhaktyā yonau mūlaṃ nyasettataḥ |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā haviṣyāśī jitendriyaḥ || 215 ||

yoniṃ spṛṣṭā japenmantramekākī lakṣamānataḥ |

p. 452)

jāyate mantrasiddhistu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 216 ||

sarvasiddhiḥ kare tasya satyaṃ satyaṃ suniścitam |
athavā viṣuve caiva māsāntapakṣayorapi || 217 ||

aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ candrasūryagrahe tathā |
śmaśāne vā yathāśaktyā gurorvā śivasannidhau || 218 ||

latāmānīya yatnena yonau cakraṃ yathāvidhi |
likhanañca prakurvīta kuṅkumāgurucandanaiḥ || 219 ||

mantrañca pralikhettasyāmāvāhya parameśvarīm |
sampūjya vidhivad bhaktyā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 220 ||

japenmantraṃ divārātrau yoṣitāṃ yonideśataḥ |
lakṣamekaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ vā daśabhirhomamācaret || 221 ||

pūjāñca vidhivat kṛtvā yathāvibhavavistaraiḥ |
paritoṣya guruṃ paścānmantrasiddhiśca jāyate || 222 ||

mahāvāgmī bhavet so'pi dhanavān rājavallabhaḥ |
lakṣmīstasya sadā gehe vāṇī vaktre suniścitam || 223 ||

bhuvi śakrasamo vīro jīvanmuktaḥ prajāyate |

p. 453)

prakārāntareṇa dūtīyajanam

athavānyaprakāreṇa dūtīyajanamuttamam || 224 ||

tatra tāṃ sthāpayeddūtīṃ pūjayecca yathāvidhi |
vastrālaṅkaraṇaṃ caiva dattvā tasyai yathocitam || 225 ||

dhūpayitvā raktavastraṃ dūtīṃ kuryāt sulakṣaṇām |
padmaṃ bhūmau samālikhya ṣaṭkoṇāntargataṃ likhet || 226 ||

tasyopari gatāṃ nārīṃ nirjane bhūṣaṇānvitām |
tatastāṃ gandhapuṣpādyairnānopahāravistaraiḥ || 227 ||

devīrūpeṇa sambhāvya pūjayettāṃ yathāvidhi |
tasyā gātre nyasenmantraṃ pañcabāṇān krameṇa tu || 228 ||

svakalpoktakrameṇaiva nyāsajālaṃ pravinyaset |
dīkṣayitvā tanmūlena devībuddhyā yajettataḥ || 229 ||

yonau vā pūjayeddevīṃ yasmādyonimayī śivā |
yonau kāmeśvarīṃ caiva nābhau vajreśvarīṃ tataḥ || 230 ||

p. 454)

hṛdvaktrastanmadhyeṣu pūjayet paradevatām |
yoniṃ sadā'kṣatāṃ kṛtvā japedvidyāṃ tataḥ parām || 231 ||

ayutañca japaṃ kṛtvā sarvasiddhīśvaro bhavet |
śaktimānīya vidhivat pūjayedyonimaṇḍale || 232 ||

sampūjya nijadevīñca sāyudhāṃ saparikarām |
bhaginīṃ bhagajihvāñca bhagāsyāṃ bhagamālinīm || 233 ||

bhagadantāṃ bhagākṣīñca bhagavarṇāṃ bhagatvacām |
bhagastanīṃ bhagāṅgīñca bhagasthāṃ bhagasarpiṇīm || 234 ||

bhage bhagavatīṃ tasyāḥ sampūjya bhagamālayā |
vāgbhavaṃ kāmabījañca kamalābījameva ca || 235 ||

iti babījatrayaṃ dattvā ṅeyutāśca namo'ntikāḥ |
pūjayitvā tataḥ paścānmūlamantraṃ japettataḥ || 236 ||

ekāyutajapaṃ kṛtvā sarvasiddhiparāyaṇaḥ |
sarvaśāstrārthavettā ca sarvagaḥ sarvavit prabhuḥ || 237 ||


dūtīyāgamāhātmyakathanam

idānīṃ kathyate dūtīyāgasya mahimā bhṛśam |

p. 455)

yoṣitāṃ yonimāsthāya sarvasiddhirna saṃśayaḥ || 238 ||

etat pūjanamambāyāḥ sarvatantreṣu gopitam |
itthaṃ pūjāvidhiṃ kṛtvā sarvasiddhiyuto bhavet || 239 ||

labhate vimalāṃ vāṇīṃ kavīnāmagraṇīrbhavet |
aiśvaryamatulaṃ prāpya vaśīkuryājjagattrayam || 240 ||

siddhagandharvadevaiśca kinnarairdānavaistathā |
rākṣasairmanujairnityaṃ pūjyo bhavati sādhakaḥ || 241 ||

sarveṣāṃ vallabhaḥ so'pi yoginīnāṃ sudurlabhaḥ |
ante ca ramate devyā sākṣāt smaraharopamaḥ || 242 ||

candanāgurugandhādyairlatāḥ sampūjya yatnataḥ |
sarvapāpavinirmukto ramet kalpāyutaṃ bhuvi || 243 ||

vastrālaṅkāramālyaiśca toṣayed yadi kāminīm |
atulāṃ śriyamāpnoti sa gacchetpārvatīpadam || 244 ||

p. 456)

matsyamāṃsadadhikṣaudrapiṣṭakaiḥ pāyasādibhiḥ |
bhojayedyuvatīṃ yastu sa bhavet pṛthivīpatiḥ || 245 ||

kāyena manasā vācā sundarīṃ yaśca toṣayet |
sa bhavet sādhakaśreṣṭho mātṝṇāñca bhavet priyaḥ || 246 ||

bālikāṃ yadi paśyecca nirjane ca digambarīm |
tadā tadā prapūjyeta devīṃ dhyātvā yathāvidhi || 247 ||

sa ca sarvapriyo bhūtvā vāgmī ekakavirbhavet |
etattu sarvabhūtānāṃ tantrāṇāṃ gopitaṃ bhuvi || 248 ||

jñātvā gurumukhāt sarvaṃ śivatulyo bhavennaraḥ |
nirvikalpastu mantrajño bhaved vai sādhakaḥ sadā || 249 ||

kāmyaṃ naimiktikaṃ jñātvā caṇḍikāṃ paripūjayet |
dīkṣitasyā'dhikāro'tra nānyasyaiva kadācana || 250 ||


bhūtalipeḥ mantrasiddheḥ drāvaṇādivividhā upāyaḥ

atha sarvatra japtavyaṃ aṣṭottarasahasrakam |
amṛtatrayasaṃyogāt duṣṭamantro'pi sidhyati || 251 ||

p. 457)

evaṃ bhūtalipiścaiva hṛllekhā modinītrayam |
tryamṛtena puṭīkṛtya yo mantraṃ bhajate'cirāt || 252 ||

kramotkramācchatāvṛttyā tasya siddho bhavenmanuḥ |
pañcahrasvā sandhivarṇā vyomerāgnijalandharā || 253 ||

antyamādyaṃ dvitīyañca caturthaṃ madhyamaṃ kramāt |
pañca vargākṣarāṇi syurvāntaṃ śvetendubhiḥ saha || 254 ||

eṣā bhūtalipiḥ proktā dvicatvāriṃśadakṣaraiḥ |
puraścaryādibhirmantro yadi siddho bhavenna hi || 255 ||

upāyāstatra karttavyāḥ sapta śaṅkarabhāṣitāḥ |
drāvaṇaṃ bodhanaṃ vaśyaṃ pīḍanaṃ poṣaśoṣaṇam || 256 ||

dahanāntaṃ kramāt kuryāttataḥ siddho bhaved dhruvam |
vāruṇenaiva bījena grathanaṃ kārayenmanum || 257 ||

tanmantrayantramālikhya śihlakarpūrakuṅkumaiḥ |
uṣīrarocanākhyābhirmantraṃ saṃgrathitaṃ likhet || 258 ||

kṣīrājyamadhutoyānāṃ madhye taṃ likhitaṃ kṣipet |
pūjanājjapanādarcanād drāvitaḥ siddhido manuḥ || 259 ||

p. 458)

drāvito'pi na siddhaśced bodhanaṃ tasya kārayet |
sārasvatena bīje sampuṭīkṛtya taṃ japet || 260 ||

evaṃ kṛte na siddhiścedvaśīkuryāttataḥ param |
āraktacandanaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ haridrā madanaṃ śilā || 261 ||

etaistu mantramālikhya bhūrjapatre suśobhane |
dhāryaṃ kaṇṭhe na cet siddhiḥ pīḍanaṃ tasya kārayet || 262 ||

adharottarayogena pādāni parijapyate |
dhyāyettu devatāṃ tadvattadadhottararūpiṇīm || 263 ||

vidyāmādau ca dugdhena likhitvā'kramya cāṅghriṇā |
tathā bhūtena mantreṇa homaṃ kāryaṃ dine dine || 264 ||

pīḍito lajjayā'viṣṭaḥ siddhaḥ syādatha poṣayet |
bālāyāstritayaṃ bījamādyante tasya yojayet || 265 ||

gokṣīramadhunā likhya vidyāṃ pāṇau vidhārayet |
poṣito'yaṃ bhavet siddho na cet kurvīta śoṣaṇam || 266 ||

dvābhyāṃ ca vāyubījābhyāṃ mantraṃ kuryādvidarbhitam |

p. 459)

eṣā vidyā gale dhāryā likhitā śavabhasmanā || 267 ||

śoṣito'pi na siddhaśceddahanīyo'gnibījataḥ |
āgneyena tu bījena mantrasyaikaikamakṣaram || 268 ||

ādyantamadha ūrdhvañca yojayeddāhakarmaṇi |
brahmavṛkṣasya tailena mantramālikhya dhārayet || 269 ||

dhāraṇāt kaṇṭhadeśe tu tataḥ siddho bhavenmanuḥ |
evaṃ krameṇa kurvīta puraścaraṇamuttamam || 270 ||


puraścaryādibhirvinā mantrasiddherupāyaḥ

athānyat sampravakṣyāmi mantrasiddhestu kāraṇam |
mantrasiddhirbhavedyena puraścaryādibhirvinā || 271 ||

śivaśikhisitabhānuṃ pañcamāntyasvarāḍhyaṃ 
dvitayamidamapūrvaṃ bījamugraprabhāyāḥ |
kṣaṇamapi khamaṇīnāṃ maṇḍalāntarvibhāvya 
kṣapayati duradṛṣṭaṃ vādirāḍ jāyate saḥ || 272 ||

sa jayati ripuvargān vādirājān vivāde 
namayati ramaṇīnāṃ cittacauraścirāyuḥ |
kathayati kavirājairapyadṛṣṭaṃ sukāvyaṃ 
madhumatirapi heyaḥ kiṃpunaḥ siddhisaṅghāḥ || 273 ||

p. 460)

kulayuvati suyonau mantravarṇān vilikhya 
nikhila nigamavarṇān suptadoṣādiduṣṭān |
vidita gurukulāntarvāhyavartmā vidhijño 
manupuṭitapaṭīyān sādhayet śāntacetāḥ || 274 ||

kulapathamanusandhāyāmitāyāṃ subhūmau 
tava janani jano yastarpayettīrthatoyaiḥ |
rudhiratarasupuṣpairgandhamālyānulepai 
racita yuvativeśastaddhiyā dhyāyate saḥ || 275 ||

paricarati samastairnyāsapūrvaiḥ prasiddhai-
stava parikarajālairyonicakre prapūjya | 
suvimalakulajāṃ tvāṃ hrīṃghṛṇāvarjito yaḥ 
svayamapi racitāṅgaḥ kṣobhakṛdyoginīnām || 276 ||

paśuripukulacakraṃ saṃspṛśan madhyaśākhaṃ 
surataru suranāthaḥ śāpabhraṣṭaḥ sureśaḥ |

p. 461)

kulapatikulanāthastaddvayaṃ yojayitvā 
manupuṭitavimṛgyaṃ yojayettadbahiryaḥ || 277 ||

janani tava kalānāṃ kovidaḥ kāmarūpaḥ 
kumatirahitacittaḥsaṃlikhettāṃ tridhāreḥ |
vigatabhaya vivādadhvāntajālaḥ sudhāṃśu-
stava caraṇatalāntardhūlijālairviśālaiḥ || 278 ||

parijanitavapustaddharmabhirdeva pūjyaiḥ 
paricarati suvijño mokṣacaryādhipaḥ saḥ |
madanamadavadhūnāṃ bījamuddhṛtya śaktiṃ 
tadanu kaṭhinabījaṃ lokadhātrīṃ tadantaḥ || 279 ||

yadi japati madantarbhāvamāsādya sadyaḥ 
suranagaragatistaiḥ siddhivargaiḥ sa pūjyaḥ |
śivabhṛgumadapṛthvīśaktiyuktaṃ susiddhaṃ hariharacaturāsyasvasvabhūtiprasūtam || 280 ||

paramapuruṣasañjñaḥ kṣobhakṛt kāminīnā 
madhipatirapi vācāṃ śrīpatiḥ sārvabhaumaḥ |

p. 462)

bhṛgumadakaṭhinādhaḥ kāmabījaṃ tadagre 
bhuvanabhayavināśaḥ kṣobhiṇīṃ yojayitvā || 281 ||

japati yadi sakṛdvā cintyate vīrasiṃhaḥ 
kulayuvatikulāntaḥ kṣobhakṛt kāmabhāvāt |
madanamadatalādhaḥ śaktibījaṃ niyojya 
smaraharaharirūpī kāmarūpaḥ kuberaḥ || 282 ||

ripukulahariṇākṣīlocanāmbhojavipruṭ 
prasabhajalaniṣekāt khaṇḍitāntāghatāpaḥ |
śivabhṛgumadamūlaṃ lobhamūlaṃ samūlaṃ 
bhajati yadi gurūṇāṃ varṇamūlaṃ vimṛgyam || 283 ||

nidhipatirapi nātho gīṣpatiḥ kṣudracetā 
yadi bhavati tadetanmukhyamurvīpatitvam |

p. 463)

varaṇaraṇavivarjaṃ ghrāṇamevaṃ vivarjyaṃ 
tadupari mṛgacihnaṃ dvandvametat bhavānyāḥ || 284 ||

nikhila manu varāṇāṃ mukhyadānaikadakṣaḥ 
sa kusumaśaradharmakṣobhakṛnmantrarājaḥ |
amalaśirasi dharmaṃ vādirājaṃ svatantraṃ 
tava samanaya yuktaṃ bījamanyad bhavānyāḥ || 285 ||

dvitayamapi vimānaṃ vaktumīśo maheśaḥ 
kimiha kamalajanmā janmadharāsahasraiḥ |
iha japati ya enaṃ mantrarājaṃ svabhāgyaiḥ 
bhajati janani yuṣmatpādapadmotthabhaktyā || 286 ||

tyajati parapumāṃsaṃ mādṛśaṃ cāpi kāle 
na khalu vapuranarghyaṃ tasya kācit kadācit |
vihita gurumukhādvā vālakādvā paśorvā 
likhitamapi svabuddhyā prāpya kasmādakasmāt || 287 ||

smararipupurapāre mokṣapūryādhipāre 
paramapadavilīnaḥ sarvasaubhāgyabhogaiḥ |

p. 464)

analapuravibhāge kālikāvarṇabījaṃ 
tadapi yadi vidadhyāddigyutaṃ dantavarṇam || 288 ||

nayanayutalakāraṃ mastake nāmayuktaṃ 
tadanu vikaṭadaṃṣṭrā sotkaṭaṃ bījayuktam |
yadi japati samastaṃ guhyaguhyātiguhyaṃ 
trijagati kimihāste kleśalabhyaṃ kathañcit || 289 ||

krama paṭhitapūrvaṃ sarvamevānumadhyaṃ 
manuraparavācyaṃ tasya madhyasvarūpam |
bhajati yadi cidānandadhūkkevalo'sau 
vipina bhuvi ca madhye kautukān māṃ manuṣyaḥ || 290 ||

iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye saptadaśollāsaḥ || 17 ||



aṣṭādaśa ullāsaḥ 

siddhilakṣaṇanirūpaṇam 

atha vakṣye siddhicihnaṃ sādhakasya yathā bhavet |
akasmāt saṃlabhed gandhaṃ dhūpasya kusumasya vā || 1 ||

sadā vā priyasambhāṣyaṃ labhyate yoṣitāmapi |
svapne vṛṣaṃ gajāśvaṃ vā yadvā'rohati sādhakaḥ || 2 ||

siddhamantro bhavet so'pi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
svapne vā jāyate yoṣidvṛndaiḥ sammilanaṃ niśi || 3 ||

gajādiśailaśṛṅgeṣu vihāro rājadarśanam |
rājñāṃ tathā'ṅganānāñca darśanaṃ nṛtyagītayoḥ || 4 || 

saudhagehaṃ tathā mañcaṃ prāsādañca manoharam |
rathaṃ vā taraṇiṃ vāpi svapneṣvārohayettu yaḥ || 5 ||

samyaksiddhirbhavettasya śivaḥ so'pi na saṃśayaḥ |
dhvajañca pratimāṃ caiva rājānaṃ rudhiraṃ tathā || 6 ||

rudhirālepanaṃ svāṅge madirālepanaṃ tathā |
madirālokanaṃ māṃsaṃ striyaṃ vā cāruhāsinīm || 7 ||

p. 466)

strībhirvā veṣṭito yastu svapne bhavati sādhakaḥ |
mantrasiddhirbhavettasya satyameva na saṃśayaḥ || 8 ||

gomāṃsaṃ vāpi cānyasya bhakṣayedyo'pi māṃsakam |
labhedvā bhakṣayedvā'pi sparśayedvā kadācana || 9 ||

devatānaragandharvanārīṇāṃ darśanaṃ bhavet |
caragoruṇitamvāyā vakṣojamukhapaṅkajam || 10 ||

eteṣāṃ tu phalaṃ caiva paraṅgurvādikaṃ gurum |
teṣāṃ prasādaḥ snehañca vātsalyaṃ priyadarśanam || 11 ||


siddhicihnāni

siddhicihnaṃ bhuvi jñeyaṃ yadi na vyapate manaḥ |
yadi bhāgyavaśāccaiva kuladṛṣṭistu jāyate || 12 ||

avārdhakyaṃ bhavettasya sarvasiddhiḥ kare sthitā |
akālapanasaṃ caiva bhakṣaṇaṃ darśanaṃ tathā || 13 ||

agamyāgamanaṃ caiva guroḥ sandarśanaṃ tathā |
paramānnabhakṣaṇañca śoṇitañca viśeṣataḥ || 14 ||

divyānnaṃ vāpi bhuktvā ca siddho bhavati sādhakaḥ |

p. 467)

sādhakaṃ dravyarūpañca devatāṃ vā prapaśyati || 15 ||

khaḍgaṃ vā phalakaṃ vāpi amṛtaṃ vāpi pustikām |
siddhidravyaṃ śavaṃ caiva vasanābharaṇādikam || 16 ||

raktamālyaṃ raktapuṣpaṃ svapne prāpnoti sādhakaḥ |
siddhaḥ so'pi bhavet satyaṃ saṃśayo nāsti niścitam || 17 ||

gajaṃ vā turagaṃ vāpi śivaliṅgañca paśyati |
pīṭhādigamanaṃ tatra devatādarśanaṃ tathā || 18 ||

udyānaṃ kānanaṃ vāpi bhūdevayatidarśanam |
bālakaṃ bhikṣukaṃ caiva phalādīnāṃ tu lolupam || 19 ||

paśupakṣiphalādīnaṃ viśvāsaḥ priyadarśanaḥ |
manasā bhāvite kārye śaṅkhādidhvanirutsavaḥ || 20 ||

veṇuvīṇāravaṃ gītaṃ vicitradarśanaṃ tathā |
ajñātajanasamvādo naṣṭadravyodayaṃ tathā || 21 ||

akasmād bhāvasiddhistu alakṣitajanastutiḥ |
kṣudrasya mahatī pūjā sukṛtī ca kṛtastutiḥ || 22 ||

adṛṣṭaśāstravijñānaṃ tejasvī janavallabhaḥ |
mantralābhastathā svapne gurūṇāṃ duhitustathā || 23 ||

patnīnāṃ sādhakānāñca utkaṭotkaṭadarśanam |
asugandhiḥ sugandhiśca śraddhā puṣpodbhavastathā || 24 ||

svapne caiva puraścārī puraścaraṇakarmaṇi |
iti vijñāya vīrendraḥ prayogañca samācaret || 25 ||

p. 468)

nindyacihnāni

atha nindyāni vakṣyāmi vidyānarthakarāṇi ca |
kṛṣṇavarṇabhaṭaṃ svapne prahārastena melanam || 26 ||

maithunaṃ paranārībhirvināśo maraṇaṃ tathā |
rājyakṣobhavahnivāyujantubhirvadhadarśanam || 27 ||

gurorākṣepasampattirjantūnāṃ vyādhisādhanam |
anyamantrārcane śraddhā svadevārcananindanam || 28 ||

asiddhārthakule tatra siddhārthajapamācaret |
gaṇeśaṃ vaṭukaṃ paścāt kṣetreśaṃ yoginīstathā || 29 ||

pūjayedrātrisamaye baliṃ caiva sadā'rpayet |
kumāraṃ baṭukaṃ bhadraṃ lakṣmīṃ sucārunāyakam || 30 ||

svaśaktiṃ pañcakṣetreśaṃ yoginīṃ yogibhiḥ saha |
pūjayitvā vidhānena baliṃ paścānnivedayet || 31 ||

p. 469)

tataḥ siddho bhavenmantro nirvighnena suniścitam |
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi kāmyaprayogamuttamam || 32 ||


kāmyaprayogakathanam

rajo'vasthāṃ samālokya tanmūle sveṣṭadevatām |
pūjayitvā mahārātrau tridinaṃ prajapenmanum || 33 ||

lakṣapīṭhaphalaṃ caiva labhate sādhakottamaḥ |
vetālapādukāsiddhiṃ khaṅgasiddhintathaiva ca || 34 ||

añjanaṃ tilakaṃ caiva prāpnoti kṣobhavarjitaḥ |
kulāgāraṃ puṣpitāyā dṛṣṭvā yo japate naraḥ || 35 ||

ayutaikapramāṇena sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |
kevalaṃ guptabhāvena sa tu vidyānidhirbhavet || 36 ||

saṃskṛtāḥ prākṛtāścaiva laukikā vaidikāstathā |
vaśamāyānti te sarve sādhakasya na cānyathā || 37 ||

athavā muktakeśaśca bhuvi bhuktvā susaṃyataḥ |
prajapya cā'yutaṃ prājñaḥ etadeva phalaṃ labhet || 38 ||

nagnāṃ paralatāṃ paśyan ayutaṃ yastu sādhakaḥ |
prajaped yo bhavet sadyo vidyāyāvallabhaḥ svayam || 39 ||

p. 470)

tasya darśanamātreṇa vādinaḥ kuṇṭhatāṃ gatāḥ |
gadyapadyamayī vāṇī sabhāyāṃ tasya jāyate || 40 ||

tasya vākyaparicayājjaḍībhavanti vāgminaḥ |
tannāmnā sudhiyaḥ sarve praṇamanti mudānvitāḥ || 41 ||

havirbhuktvā'thavā pūrvakrameṇaiva phalaṃ bhavet | 
athavā dhanakāmastu mahadaiśvaryakāmukaḥ || 42 ||

bṛhaspatisamo yastu bhavituṃ kāmayennaraḥ | 
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā kulamāmantrya tattvavit || 43 ||

maithunaṃ yaḥ karotyeva sa tu sarvaphalaṃ labhet |
athavā hastamāropya sthiradhīḥ pūtamānasaḥ || 44 ||

kavitvaṃ labhate so'pi amṛtatvañca gacchati |
pṛthvīmṛtumatīṃ vīkṣya sahasraṃ yadi nityaśaḥ || 45 ||

tadā vādī susiddhānte tataḥ kṣititalaṃ viśet |
mahācīnakrame devīṃ dhyātvā devīṃ prapūjayet || 46 ||

taddrumodbhavapuṣpeṇa pūjayed bhaktibhāvataḥ |
sa bhavet kuladevaśca kuladrumagataḥ śuciḥ || 47 ||

p. 471)

brahmatarormūlapadme devīṃ dhyātvā yathāvidhi |
tatsudhādhārasāreṇa tarpayenmātṛkānane || 48 ||

padmaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathā vilvaṃ khañjanaṃ śikhanaṃ tathā |
cāmaraṃ ravivimbañca tilapuṣpaṃ sarovaram || 49 ||

trisṛtaṃ vīkṣya japtvā ca śataśaḥ śuddhabhāvataḥ |
mukhaprasādaṃ suvacaḥ sulocanaṃ suhāsyakam || 50 ||

suveśaṃ subhagaṃ gandhaṃ sujanaṃ sukhameva ca |
labhate ca yathāsaṃkhyaṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 51 ||


mahācīnakramaḥ

mahācīnadrumalatā veṣṭitena ca yat phalam |
tasyāpi ṣoḍaśāṃśena phalaṃ nārhanita te śavāḥ || 52 ||

śavāsanādhikaphalaṃ latāgṛhapraveśanam |
latārateṣu japtavyaṃ mahāpātakamuktaye || 53 ||

dhanārthī prajapedvidyāṃ parayoṣit samāgame |
latābhāve samutsārya svaśukraṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 54 ||

akṣubdhaṃ prajapenmantraṃ dharmakāmārthasiddhaye |
iti cīnakrameṇaiva tārā śīghraphalapradā || 55 ||

p. 472)

guruvākyakrameṇaiva tripurā bhuktimuktidā |
mahācīnakrameṇaiva kālikā phaladāyiṇī || 56 ||

saptasaptatibhedā yā śrīvidyā kathitā bhuvi |
tāsāntu samatā jñeyā guptasādhanasādhane || 57 ||

cattvāriṃśat prakārā yā bhairavī viditā bhuvi |
tāsāṃ tu samatā jñeyā guptasādhanasādhane || 58 ||

yā yā vidyā mahācaṇḍāstāsāmevaṃ vidhirmataḥ |
mahācīnakrameṇaiva chinnamastā tu siddhidā || 59 ||

sarvā yāścaṇḍikā vidyāstāsāṃ caivaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
yathā kālī tathā tārā yathā bālā tathonmukhī || 60 ||

yathā kālī tathā durgā yathā durgā tathā jaṭā |
yathā'sau tripurā bālā tathā tripurabhairavī || 61 ||

sundarī ca tathā jñeyā marddinī ca tathā parā |
anyā yā yā mahāvidyā tathā bhavati niścitam || 62 ||

p. 473)

mantraṃ yantraṃ pṛthagbhāvāt sādhanenaikatāṃ bhajet |
etatyantrañca mantrañca putrebhyo'pi na darśayet || 63 ||

anyathā pretarājasya bhavanaṃ yāti niścitam |
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi kāmyasādhanamuttamam || 64 ||

saṃyojya mūlamantrānte kramotkrameṇa mātṛkām |
āmantrayettataḥ paścāt pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā || 65 ||

prātareva jalaṃ dhīro bhāvayenmātṛkāmayam |
tadambupānato mantrī vāgmī drutaṃ kavirbhavet || 66 ||

trimāsānnā'tra sandehaḥ ṣaṇmāsāt vākpatirbhavet |
kāmyakarttā mahāvāgmī bhavet śrutidharā naraḥ || 67 ||

aṣṭamāt sarvamantrāṇāṃ pārago viśvapūjitaḥ |
sarvajñatāṃ labhedvīro nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 68 ||

dvivarṣādaṣṭasiddhiśca trivarṣāttu śivo bhavet |
pradīpakalikākāraṃ jihvāyāṃ mūlamantrakam || 69 ||

devatāṃ śuklavarṇāñca vibhāvya yo japenmanum |

p. 474)

trimāsādūrdhvato mantrī pūrvavat phalamāpnuyāt || 70 ||

tattatprakārabhedena tattatphalamavāpnuyāt |
prātareva hi japtavyamaṣṭottarasahasrakam || 71 ||

athavā'nyaprakāraṃ tu vakṣyāmi tantravartmanā |
balidānādikaṃ sarvaṃ vidhāya pūrvavad budhaḥ || 72 ||

śmaśānālayamāgatya muktakeśo digambaraḥ |
japedayutasaṅkhyaṃ tu sarvakāmārthasiddhaye || 73 ||

mahācīnadrumalatāmajjābhirvipraputrakam |
sahasraṃ devīmabhyarcya śmaśāne sādhakottamaḥ || 74 ||

tadā rājyamavāpnoti yadi sa na palāyitaḥ |
svagātrarudhirāktaiśca bilvapatraiḥ sahasraśaḥ || 75 ||

śmaśāne'bhyarcya devīntu vāgīśasamatāṃ vrajet |
śmaśānaśayano vīraḥ śavāsanagataḥ śuciḥ || 76 ||

asakṛt prajapenmantraṃ sarvasiddhirbhavettataḥ |
kavirvāgmī dhanī dakṣaḥ sarvayoṣitpriyaḥ sukhī || 77 ||

p. 475)

jāyate nātra sandehaścaṇḍikāsādhakaḥ svayam |
kṣudraviṭcuarabhūtāni devīṃ dhyātvā vināśayet || 78 ||

nābhimātrajale sthitvā devīmarkagatāṃ smaran |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā sādhako labhate śriyam || 79 ||

retoyuktena puṣpeṇa arkasyeha sahasraśaḥ |
śmaśāne'bhyarcya devīntu sarvasiddhiṃ tu vindati || 80 ||

dhanavān balavān vāgmī sarvayoṣitpriyo bhavet |
sukhī syānnātra sandeho mahākālavaco yathā || 81 ||

cchan yo taṃ śā ga ne ṣi śma yet ntraṃ ka ge lpa ma pra bha |
raktacandanadigdhāṅgīṃ raktapuṣpairalaṃkṛtām || 82 ||

ta jāṃ ku ge vīṃ pū pra bha javāpuṣpairviśeṣataḥ |
mantraṃ proccārayettatra ne ga ve ge śa liṃ pra bha || 83 ||

kuṇḍagolodbhavaiḥ puṣpaiḥ svayambhūkusumena ca |
pūjayitvā tatra devīṃ yo vai dhyāyecca devatām || 84 ||

tatra gatvā labhet siddhiṃ yadi vā na bhayaṃ vrajet |
aṣṭamyāñca caturdaśyāṃ pūjayitvā yathāvidhi || 85 ||

arghyaṃ dattvā tataḥ paścāt javāpuṣpañca varvarām |
candanañcā'rkakusumaṃ tathā śvetāparājitām || 86 ||

arghyaṃ dattvā viśeṣeṇa devīmarkagatāṃ smaran |

p. 476)

aṣṭottaraśataṃ jāpyaṃ yāvajjīvanasaṃkhyayā || 87 ||

ājñāsiddhimavāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
prātaḥ snānarato mantrīṃ nityamaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 88 ||

japettasyā'śu sidhyanti dhanadhānyādisampadaḥ |
anena vidhinā mantrī grahakṣudraripūn jayet || 89 ||

athocyate padāghātaṃ devyāstantravidhānataḥ |
svakalpoktaṃ vidhāyā'tha sādhako vijitendriyaḥ || 90 ||

sarvadravyasamāyukto devatādhyānatatparaḥ |
vivāde japakāle vā yatkiñcit kulasādhane || 91 ||

tatrādau cakramālikhya sindūrarajasā'plutam |
rātrau prapūjayed gehe sthitvā padātmikāṃ parām || 92 ||

japtvā'yutapramāṇena tathā cāmantrayettataḥ |
ehi mātarjagaddhātri sañcāre caraṇākule || 93 ||

śubhāśubhamaye rātre kathayasva jagatpate |
evaṃ nimantrayitvā tu saptavārān susaṃyataḥ || 94 ||

bhaktibhāvena sampūjya dattvā ca balimuttamam |
tato vīkṣeccakrarājaṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 95 ||

p. 477)

padāghātaṃ samālokya jñātavyaṃ tacchubhāśubham |
sādhakābhimukhaṃ cihnaṃ ubhayoryadi caikataḥ || 96 ||

savyasya dakṣiṇasyāpi śubhaṃ bhavati niścitam |
viparītaphalaṃ dadyādviparīte suniścitam || 97 ||

padamadhye śubhā rekhā śubhadā śubhayogataḥ |
viparītaphalaṃ tattu viparītañca niścitam || 98 ||

kṣudraṃ kṣudraphalaṃ proktaṃ dīrghaṃ dīrghaphalapradam |
athātaḥ pādukāmantraṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ || 99 ||

pādukāṃ pūjayāmyadya ādyantamaṇḍitāṃ vadet |
sarvabhūte mahāśaktiḥ pādāgre yā sthitā yataḥ || 100 ||

atastau caraṇau pūjyau sarvatra bhūtimicchatā |
ādyantarahitā devī pādukādisvarūpiṇī || 101 ||

yoṣitāṃ yonimāsthāya sarvasiddhipradāyinī |
svatantreyaṃ mahāvidyā na prakāśyā kadācana || 102 ||

p. 478)

saṃhārakramayogena devī saṃhārarūpiṇī |
sṛṣṭikrameṇa deveśīmahāsṛṣṭipravarttinī || 103 ||

sthitikrameṇa sā devī sthititattvapravarttinī |
brahmaviṣṇusvarūpā sā mahādevasvarūpiṇī || 104 ||

ādye madhumatīsiddhiḥ saṃhārakramayogataḥ |
ante kṣetreṇa siddhiḥ syāccheṣe gandharvayonayaḥ || 105 ||

samudāye mahāmokṣaṃ dadāti paramā kalā |
pādukā paramā vidyā saptākṣarasamanvitā || 106 ||

etāṃ vinā mahāvidyā naiva sarvaphalapradā |
kathiteyaṃ mahāvidyā yatnād gopyā sadaiva hi || 107 ||

lokasnehādidānīṃ tu kathitañca sudurlabham |
etattantrañca mantrañca prayogañca tathā param || 108 ||

atibhaktāya putrāya prāṇadāya na darśayet |
anyathā pretarājasya bhavanaṃ yāti niścitam || 109 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye aṣṭādaśollāsaḥ || 98 ||




ekonaviṃśa ullāsaḥ 


ṣaṭkarmavidhānam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi ṣaṭkarmavidhimuttamam |
śāntivaśyastambhanāni vidveṣoccāṭane tataḥ || 1 ||

māraṇaṃ karmaṣaṭkañca teṣāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate |


śāntyādiṣaṭkarmalakṣaṇam

rogakṛtyā grahādīnāṃ nirāsaḥ śāntirīritā || 2 ||

vaśyaṃ janānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vidheyatvamudāhṛtam |
pravṛttirodhaḥ sarveṣāṃ stambhanaṃ tadudāhṛtam || 3 ||

snigdhānāṃ dveṣajananaṃ mitho vidveṣaṇaṃ matam |
sadvidyāsiddhikālādīn jñātvā karmāṇi sādhayet || 4 ||

pūrvoktabalimantreṇa baliṃ dadyāccatuṣpathe |
rātrāveva prayogādau śivāyai ca susiddhaye || 5 ||

p. 481)

tattatkarmasu mantrayantrakālanirṇayaḥ

dikkāladevatādīṃśca(jñātvā)vidveṣe vyoma kīrttitam |
uccāṭane smṛto vāyurbhūyo'gnirmāraṇe mataḥ || 6 ||

svacchaṃ viyan marut kṛṣṇo rakto'gniviśadaṃ payaḥ |
pītā bhūmiḥ pañcabhūta rūpametadudīritam || 7 ||

vṛttaṃ divastat ṣaḍbindulāñkṣitaṃ mātariśvanaḥ | 
trikoṇaṃ svastikopetaṃ vahnerardhendusaṃyutam || 8 ||

ambhojamambhaso bhūmeścaturasraṃ savajrakam |
tattadbhūtasamābhāni maṇḍalāni vidurbudhāḥ || 9 ||

varṇāḥ svairañjitānyāhuḥ svasvanāmāvṛtānyapi |
śabdasparśarūparasagandhā bhūtaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 10 ||

bhūtānāmudayaṃ dvayorapi budhaḥ saṃlakṣayet pakṣayo-
statrādho vasudhodayaṃ niśitadhīrghrāṇasya daṇḍaspṛśi |
devedhaḥspṛśi vāriṇo hutavahasyordhvaṃ gate tūdayaṃ 
tiryak saṃspṛśi mārutasya paritaḥ spṛṣṭe marudvartmanaḥ || 11 ||

vaśyastambhanayoḥ praśasta udayo bhūmerjalasyodayaḥ 

p. 482)

śastaḥ śāntikapauṣṭikādiṣu śubheṣvahnāya vahneḥ punaḥ | 
śatrormāraṇadāraṇādikaraṇeṣūccāṭanocchvāsayā-
rvāyoḥ śāntikanirviṣīkaraṇayorvyomno hi taścodayaḥ || 12 ||

tattadbhūtodaye samyak tattanmaṇḍalasaṃyute |
yantraṃ kuryāt rocanayā guṇḍikayā haridrayā || 13 ||

gṛhadhūmacitāṅgāraviṣairatha krameṇa tu |
śītāṃśusalilakṣauṇīvyomavāyuhavirbhujām || 14 ||

varṇāḥ syurmantrabījāni ṣaṭkarmasu yathākramam |
vaśyādau lekhanī dūrvā ākarṣe śikhipucchikā || 15 ||

vidveṣoccāṭane kākapakṣeṇāpi ca māraṇam |


ṣaṭkarmadevatānirūpaṇam

ratirvāṇī ramā jyeṣṭhā durgā kālī yathākramāt || 16 ||

ṣaṭkarmadevatāḥ proktāḥ karmādau tāḥ prapūjayet |

p. 483)

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi ṣaṭkarmakāla nirṇayam || 17 ||


ṣaṭkarmakālanirṇayaḥ

pañcamī ca dvitīyā ca caturthī saptamī tathā |
budhejyaravisaṃyuktā śāntikarmaṇi pūjitā || 18 ||

gurucandrayutā ṣaṣṭhī caturthī ca trayodaśī |
navamī pauṣṭike śastā aṣṭamī navamī tathā || 19 ||

daśamyekādaśī caiva bānuśukradine tathā |
ākarṣaṇe tvamāvasyā navamī pratipattathā || 20 ||

paurṇamāsī mandabhānuyuktā vidveṣakarmaṇi |
kṛṣṇā caturdaśī tadvadaṣṭamī mandavārakāḥ || 21 ||

uccāṭane tithiḥ śastā pradoṣe ca viśeṣataḥ | 
caturdaśyaṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tvamāvāsyā tathaiva ca || 22 ||

mandārārkadinopetā śastā māraṇakarmaṇi |
budhacandradinopetā pañcamī daśamī tathā || 23 ||

paurṇamāsī ca vijñeyā tithiḥ stambhanakarmaṇi |
śubhagrahodaye kuryāt śubhāni ca śubhodaye || 24 ||

p. 484)

raudrakarmāṇi riktārke mṛtyuyoge ca māraṇam |
īśaścandrendranir-ṛtivāyvagnīnāṃ diśo matāḥ || 25 ||

sūryodayaṃ samārabhya ghaṭikādaśakaṃ kramāt |
ṛtavaḥ syurvasantādyā ahorātraṃ dine dine || 26 ||

vasantagrīṣmavarṣākhyāśaraddhemantaśaiśirāḥ |
hemantaḥ śāntike prokto vasanto vaśyakarmaṇi || 27 ||

śiśiraḥ stambhane jñeyo vidveṣe grīṣma īritaḥ |
prāvṛḍuccāṭane jñeyaḥ śaranmāraṇa karmaṇi || 28 ||


ṣaṭkarmasu āsanādinirṇayaḥ

gomeṣayoḥ śāntike ca vyāghrasya khaḍgino'pi vā |
vaśye ca stambhane śastā gajacarmaṇi dveṣake || 29 ||

uccāṭane bhallūkasya meṣasya māraṇe tathā |
mahiṣāśvayorvā carmaṇi sādhayet sādhakottamaḥ || 30 ||

padmākhyaṃ svastikaṃ bhūyo vikaṭaṃ kukkuṭaṃ punaḥ |
vajraṃ bhadrakamityāhurāsanāni manīṣiṇaḥ || 31 ||

p. 485)

aṅguṣṭhau ca nibadhnīyāddhastābhyāṃ vyutkrameṇa tu |
ūrvorupari vīrendraḥ kṛtvā pādatale ubhe || 32 ||

padmāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarveṣāmeva pūjitam |
pūjājapavidhānena aṅguṣṭhaṃ dhāraṇañcaret || 33 ||

jānūrvorantare samyak kṛtvā pādatale ubhe |
ṛjukāyaḥ sukhāsīnaḥ svastikaṃ tat pracakṣate || 34 ||

sīvanyāmātmanaḥ pārśve gulphau niḥkṣipya pādayoḥ |
etadvā svastikaṃ proktaṃ savye nyasyetaraṃ karam || 35 ||

padmāsanaṃ samāsthāya jānūrvorantare karau |
niveśya bhūmau saṃsthāpya romasthaḥ kukkuṭāsanaḥ || 36 ||

gulphau ca vṛṣaṇasyādhaḥ sīvanyāḥ pārśvayoḥ kṣipet |
pārśvapādau ca pāṇibhyāṃ dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā suniścalaḥ || 37 ||

bhadrāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarvavyādhiviṣāpaham |

p. 486)

ūrvoḥ pādau kramānnyasya jānvoḥ pratyammukhāṅgulau || 38 ||

karau vidadhyādākhyātaṃ vajrāsanamanuttamam |
svasvakalpavidhānena pūjādīṃśca samācaret | 39 ||


mudrāvidhānam

ṣaṇmudrāḥ kramaśo jñeyāḥ padmapāśagadāhvayāḥ |
muṣalāśanikhaḍgādyāḥ śāntikādiṣu karmasu || 40 ||

hastau tu sammukhau kṛtvā sannatāvunnatāṅgulau |
talāntamilitāṅguṣṭhau kṛtvaiṣā padmamudrikā || 41 ||

vāmamuṣṭistu tarjanyāṃ dakṣamuṣṭistu tarjanīm |
saṃyojyāṅguṣṭhakāgrābhyāṃ tarjanyagre kṣipettataḥ || 42 ||

eṣā pāśāhvayā mudrā vidvadbhiḥ parikīrttitā |
anyonyābhimukhau hastau kṛtvā ca grathitāṅgulau || 43 ||

aṅgulyau madhyame bhūyaḥ saṃlagne ca prasārite |
gadāmudreyamākhyātā vidvadbhiḥ parikīrttitā || 44 ||

p. 487)

muṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tu hastābhyāṃ vāmasyopari dakṣiṇam |
kuryānmuṣalamudreyaṃ sarvavighnavināśinī || 45 ||

anāmikādvayaṃ veṣṭya cākuñcya tarjanīdvayam |
kaniṣṭhāṃ madhyamāṃ caiva jyeṣṭhāṅguṣṭhena ca kramāt || 46 ||

vajramudreyamākhyātā vidvadbhiḥ parikīrttitā |
kaniṣṭhānāmikāṃ baddhvā sāṅguṣṭhenaiva saṃyutā || 47 ||

śeṣāṅgulī tu prasṛte saṃspṛṣṭe khaḍgamudrikā |
sitaraktapītamiśrā kṛṣṇadhūmrā prakīrttitāḥ || 48 ||

ṣaṭkarmadevatāvarṇāḥ kathitāstu yathākramāt |
yadvarṇadevatā yatra tadvarṇañcopahāraṇam || 49 ||

puṣpaṃ caiva tathā jñeyaṃ sādhakānāṃ susiddhaye |
mantreṇāntaritān kuryānmāraṇāni yathāvidhi || 50 ||


ṣaṭkarmasu vinyāsāḥ

grathanañca vidarbhaśca sampuṭaṃ rodhanaṃ tathā |

p. 488)

yogaḥ pallava ityete vinyāsāḥ ṣaṭsu karmasu || 51 ||


grathanam

yogāstrāntaritān kṛtvā sādhyavarṇān yathāvidhi |
grathanaṃ tadvijānīyāt praśastaṃ śāntikarmaṇi || 52 ||


vidarbhaḥ

mantrārṇadvayamadhyasthaṃ sādhyanāmākṣaraṃ likhet |
vidarbha eva vijñeyo mantribhirvaśyakarmaṇi || 53 ||


sampuṭaḥ

ādāvante ca mantrasyasyānnyāsaḥ sampuṭaḥ smṛtaḥ |
eṣa syāt stambhane śasta ityukto mantravedibhiḥ || 54 ||


rodhanam yogaḥ pallavaḥ

nāmna ādyanta madhyeṣu mantraḥ syādrodhanaṃ matam |
vidveṣasya vidhāne tu praśasto nyāsa uttamaḥ || 55 ||

mantrasyānte bhavennāma yoga proccāṭane mataḥ |
ante nāmno bhavenmantraḥ pallavo māraṇe smṛtaḥ || 56 ||

p. 489)

ṣaṭkarmasu mālāvidhānam

muktābhiḥ sphāṭikairvāpi samāyojyā'kṣasūtrakaiḥ |
śāntikarmaṇi miśraṃ vā japenmantrasya siddhaye || 57 ||

pravālaraktamaṇibhirvaśyapauṣṭikayorjapet |
mantratulyāṅgamaṇibhirjapedākṛṣṭakarmaṇi || 58 ||

sādhyakeśasūtraprotaisturaṅgadaśanodbhavaiḥ |
akṣamālāṃ samālokya vidveṣoccāṭane japet || 59 ||

mṛtasya yuddhaśūnyasya daśanairgardabhasya ca |
kṛtvā'kṣamālāṃ japtavyaṃ śatrumāraṇamicchatā || 60 ||

sāmānyā kathitā mālā viśeṣāḥ pūrvasūcitāḥ |
anāmikāmadhyabhāge mālāṃ saṃsthāpya sādhakaḥ || 61 ||

śāntikastambhavaśyeṣu vṛddhāgreṇa pracālayet |
tarjanyāṅguṣṭhayogena vidveṣoccāṭayorjapet || 62 ||

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena māraṇe japa īritaḥ |
mātṛkayā'thavā kuryād yathātantravidhānataḥ || 63 ||

p. 490)

sarveṣu ca prayogeṣu dvisahasraṃ japañcaret |
athavānyaprakāreṇa kathyate sādhanaṃ mahat || 64 ||


ākarṣaṇavidhānam

mahācīnadrume bījaṃ likhitvā kuṅkumena ca |
tatpārśve sādhyamālikhya tāḍayed divyadṛṣṭibhiḥ || 65 ||

tatra gacchati kāmārttā yatra deśe sa pūjakaḥ |
gorocanādibhirdravyaiḥ svīyacandraṃ samālikhet || 66 ||

atīva sundarīṃ ramyāṃ tanmadhye pratimāṃ śubhām |
jvalantīṃ nāmasahitāṃ kāmabījavidarbhitām || 67 ||

cintayettu tato devīṃ yojanānāṃ sahasrataḥ |
adṛṣṭapūrvā sā śaktiḥ drutamāyāti durlabhā || 68 ||

rājakanyā'tha cārvaṃgī bhayalajjāvivarjitā |
āyāti sādhakaṃ samyak mantramūḍhā satī śubhā || 69 ||

cakramadhyagato bhūtvā sādhakaścintayed yadā |

p. 491)

udyatsūryasahasrābhamātmānamaruṇaṃ tathā || 70 ||

sādhyamapyaruṇībhūtaṃ cintayet sādhakottamaḥ |
anena kramayogena yuvā kandarparūpavān || 71 ||

sarvasaubhāgyasubhagaḥ sarvalokavaśaṅkaraḥ |
sarvaraktopacārastu mudrānihitavigrahaḥ || 72 ||

cakraṃ prapūjayedyastu yasya nāmavidarbhitam |
sa bhaveddāsavat satyaṃ dhaneśo vāpi vākpatiḥ || 73 ||

cakramadhyagataṃ kṛtvā nāma yasyāstu yoṣitaḥ |
adṛṣṭā vā satī vāpi yogamudrādharā'pi vā || 74 ||

haṭhādānayate śīghraṃ yakṣiṇīṃ rājakanyakām |
nāginīmapsarāṃ vāpi gandharvīṃ vā surāṅganām || 75 ||

vidyādharīṃ divyarūpāmṛṣikanyāṃ ripustriyaḥ |
madanodbhavasantāpāṃ sphurajjaghanasustanīm || 76 ||

kāmabāṇaprabhinnāntaḥkaraṇāṃ lolacakṣuṣīm |
mahākāmakalādhyānāt sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 77 ||

kṣobhayet svargabhūlokapātālatalayoṣitaḥ |
rocanābhāgamekaṃ tu bhāgamekaṃ tu kuṅkumam || 78 ||

atha bhāgadvayaṃ caiva candanairmardayet samam |

p. 492)

ekatra tilakaṃ kuryāt trilokavaśakārakam || 79 ||

aṣṭottaraśatāvṛttyā mantrayitvā vaśaṃ nayet |
rājānaṃ nagaraṃ grāmaṃ yatkiṃcidanyaducyate || 80 ||

sādhakenaiva niyataṃ tat sarvaṃ tasya vaśyagam |
yonipuṣpairliṅgapuṣpairmiśrīkṛtya ca sādhakaḥ || 81 ||

añjalitrayadānena rājānaṃ vaśamānayet |


puttalīprayogaḥ

rahasyasthānake mantrī likhedrocanayā bhuvi || 82 ||

cāruśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |
pratimāṃ sundarīṃ ramyāṃ vilikhya sumanoharām || 83 ||

tadbhālagalahṛnnābhiyonimaṇḍale yoṣitām |
yasyā nāma mahāvidyāmaṅkuśāntarvidarbhitam || 84 ||

kāmaṃ vahnisamārūḍhaṃ adharottarasaṃyutam |
bindunādakalākrāntamaṅkuśaṃ parikīrttitam || 85 ||

sarvasandhiṣu dehasya kāmabījaṃ samālikhet |
svarāntaṃ pṛthivīsaṃsthaṃ ratibindusamanvitam || 86 ||

kathitaṃ kāmabījañca sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
nīladāḍimbapuṣpābhaṃ cintayeddehasandhiṣu || 87 ||

p. 493)

tadāśābhimukho bhūtvā svayaṃ devīsvarūpakaḥ |
mudrāṃ tu kṣobhiṇīṃ baddhvā mantramaṣṭaśataṃ japet || 88 ||

niyojya madanāgāre calasūrya navātmake |
tato'pyadhikasarvāṅgīṃ kāmabāṇaiḥ supīḍitām || 89 ||

ananyamānasāṃ premabhramamāṇāṃ madālasām |
evamākarṣayennārīṃ yojanānāṃ śatairapi || 90 ||

īśāne'ṅkuśamāsādya tatra yantraṃ vilikhya ca |
aṣṭamīrātrimārabhya caturdaśyāṃ samāpayet || 91 ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ martyo mantrayitvā svayaṃ yataḥ |
tāmbūlapūritamukho muktakeśo jitendriyaḥ || 92 ||

madirāghūrṇanayanaḥ parayoṣitsamāyutaḥ |
gandhacandanapuṣpaistu digvāsāḥ kulabhūṣaṇaḥ || 93 ||

yannāmnā darbhitaṃ yantraṃ pūjayedvīrasādhakaḥ |
sā samāyāti kāmārttā yatra devaḥ sa pūjakaḥ || 94 ||

p. 494)

anyaprayogakathanam

atha cedrātrisamaye svakulaṃ tūlikopari |
vāmabhāge samāsīnaṃ raktavastravibhūṣitam || 95 ||

gandhapuṣpairdhūpadīpairveṣṭitaṃ sumanoharam |
sarvaśṛṅgāraveśāḍhyaṃ sphuraccakitalocanam || 96 ||

kucadvandva jitāmitraviśālakarikumbhakam |
lalāṭe mantramālikhya sādhyanāmavidarbhitam || 97 ||

tatskandhe bhujamāropya bhaṅgyādhṛtakucācalaḥ |
tāmbūlapūritamukhaḥ kulaṃ tadabhisaṃhitam || 98 ||

kulākulajapaṃ kṛtvā samānayati tatkṣaṇāt |
yannāmnā likhitaṃ yantraṃ tāñca nayati tatkṣaṇāt || 99 ||

śatayojanabāhyasthāṃ nadīparvatamadhyagām |
dvīpāntarasahasreṣu rakṣitāṃ nigaḍādibhiḥ || 100 ||

payodharakṣubdhamadhyāṃ cañcalotpalalocanām |
nitambavimbaviddhvastasphurajjaghanamaṇḍalām || 101 ||

sādhakākṛṣṭahṛdayāṃ vivarāntaḥprasarpiṇīm |
kavāṭalohasannaddhapūrikāvivarāntare || 102 ||

sādhakāntaḥ samāsīnāṃ devatāmiva cāriṇīm |
evamākṛṣṭisiddhiścet sādhakaḥ kauliko bhavet || 103 ||

p. 495)

kenāpi vyapadeśena samāsādya baliṃ kulāt |
rātrau vīravaro vāpi divyo vā sādhakottamaḥ || 104 ||

nijaṃ kulaṃ samādāya tatkulasthāna eva ca |
vilikhya mālinīṃ devīmāraktavasanojjvalām || 105 ||

nijavāmorumadhye tu nijamantravidarbhitam |
maṇinā sādhyanāmānaṃ vilikhya tasya madhyataḥ || 106 ||

svayaṃ kāmakalārūpastatrā'vāhya maheśvarīm |
kāmarājena saṃyuktāṃ devaṃ dhyātvā susādhakaḥ || 107 ||

pūjayitvā tato japyamaṣṭottarasahasrakam |
yannāmnā dīyate caiva sā vaśyaṃ pratipadyate || 108 ||

etadvidhānayoge tu prabhavanti hi devatāḥ |
yoginyo'psarasaścāpi ke pare narakiṅkarāḥ || 109 ||

svayaṃ divyadharo vāpi kimvā'yaṃ vīrasādhakaḥ |
iti cet jñāyate lokaistadā bhraṣṭo bhaviṣyati || 110 ||

tasmād yatnād gopitavyaṃ na prakāśyaṃ kadācana |

p. 496)

anayākṛṣṭikarmajña iti cet pratipadyate || 111 ||

niścitaṃ nimiṣe hānistadā tasya prajāyate |
yadi svapnāvatīṃ vidyāṃ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 112 ||

rātrau ca divase vāpi japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
japtvā vighnaṃ kadāciddhi pathi tasya na vidyate || 113 ||

atiguptena karttavyaṃ vīreṇa kulasādhanam |
niḥsaṅgena sadā kuryāt kulākṛṣṭiṃ viśeṣataḥ || 114 ||

kulagehapraveśārthaṃ rātrau yāmagate budhaḥ |
jalamadhye ca durmārge ārāme vā śmaśānake || 115 ||

śūnyāgāre tathā bilvamūle vā janavarjite |
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu yo japtvā gacchati dhruvam || 116 ||

tasya vighnaṃ na jāyeta sādhane sarvathā punaḥ |
athavā sa sarvakāryeṣu japtvā cāṣṭasahasrakam || 117 ||

gacchedyadi sadā dhīrastadā vighnaṃ na jāyate |
na ca vighnaṃ tatastatra tatra tatra japāntaram || 118 ||

p. 497)

gamanaṃ tasya jāyeta tasya vighnaṃ na varttate |
athavā mātṛkāṃ sarvāṃ likhitvā cakrabāhyataḥ || 119 ||

bhūrjapatre svarṇapatre raupye'tha tāmrapatrake |
sādhyanāmānvitaṃ kṛtvā dhārayedvāmabāhuke || 120 ||

so'vadhyaḥ sarvajantūnāṃ vyāghrādīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
athavā mātṛkāyuktāṃ svasañjñāṃ cakramaṇḍitām || 121 ||

karpūrakuṅkumādyaiśca ajarāmaratāṃ labhet |
anena vidhinā vāpi rocanāgurucandanaiḥ || 122 ||

vidarbhitañca nāmnā tu yasmin kasminnupasthitam |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ vāpi sakalaṃ jalamaṇḍalam || 123 ||

vaśīkuryānmahāvīraḥ so'tikrāntaḥ na saṃśayaḥ |
kāmarājena bījena mahākāmakalātmanā || 124 ||

ekaikāntaritaṃ kṛtvā sādhyanāmākṣaraṃ likhet |
balirasyā likhedevaṃ veṣṭayenmātṛkākṣaraiḥ || 125 ||

hemamadhyagataṃ kṛtvā dhārayedvāmake bhuje |
śikhāyāmathavā vastre śarīre yatra kutra vā || 126 ||

karoti dāsabhūtaṃ hi trailokyaṃ sacarācaram |
sammohayati rājānaṃ vājinaṃ duṣṭakuñjaram || 127 ||

caurān dasyūn tathā caiva tathā mantrābhicārakam |
cakravajrāṇi vetālaṃ durdamaṃ rākṣasaṃ tathā || 128 ||

p. 498)

bhūtapretāpiśācāṃśca dhāritā cakrarūpiṇī |
yena vīreṇa tatsarvaṃ sa kuryāddāsavadvaśam || 129 ||

tanmadhye ca gatāṃ pṛthvīṃ saśailavanakānanām |
catuḥsamudraparyantāṃ jvalantīṃ cintayettataḥ || 130 ||

ṣaṇmāsadhyānayogena jāyate madanopamaḥ |
dṛṣṭyā'karṣayate lokaṃ dṛṣṭyaiva kurute vaśam || 131 ||

dṛṣṭyā saṅkṣobhayennārīṃ dṛṣṭyaivā'paharedviṣam |
dṛṣṭyā karṣati vāgīśaṃ dṛṣṭyā sarvavilocanam || 132 ||

dṛṣṭyā caturvidhastrīṇāṃ nāśayedviṣamajvarān |
etat prapūjanaṃ rātrau cakraṃ sindūramiśritam || 133 ||

karoti mahadākarṣaṃ dūrasthāyāśca yoṣitaḥ |
madhye dikṣu vidikṣvevaṃ yadā devīṃ prapūjayet || 134 ||

dikpatikramayogena tadā sarvaṃ jagattrayam |
bhūrjapatre vilikhyaiva rocanāgurukuṅkumaiḥ || 135 ||

p. 499)

tanmadhye nagaraṃ deśaṃ maṇḍalaṃ khaṇḍameva vā |
svanāmadarbhitaṃ kṛtvā yadi bhūmau nidhāpayet || 136 ||

cakrametanmahābhāge puraḥkṣobhanamuttamam |
dhārayedvāmahastena kaṇṭhe vā bhujamūlake || 137 ||

śikhāyāmathavā vastre dhārayedyatra kutra vā |
rocanā kuṅkumāktañca lākṣālaktakasaṃyutam || 138 ||

arkakṣīreṇa saṃyuktaṃ dhūstūrakarasaṃ tathā |
kastūrīdravasaṃyuktaṃ ekīkṛtya tataḥ param || 139 ||

cakrametat samālikhya yasya nāmnā susādhakaḥ |
tasya vyāghragajavyādhiripusarpaviṣādikam || 140 ||

cauragrahajalāriṣṭaśākinīḍākinībhayam |
bhayaṃ na vidyate kutra paramañcābhicārakam || 141 ||

nityaṃ saṃsādhayed devīṃ kālamṛtyuṃ vināśayet |
na śakto hiṃsituṃ tasya romaikamapi sarvathā || 142 ||

p. 500)

athavā madhyagāṃ devīṃ trikoṇodaragāṃ tathā |
adhastānnāmasaṃyuktāṃ rocanākuṅkumānvitām || 143 ||

nidhāpayecca saptāhāddāsavat kiṅkaro bhavet |
tāmbūladhūpamudakaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ dadhi || 144 ||

dugdhaṃ ghṛtaṃ dantapatraṃ vastraṃ karpūrameva vā |
kastūrīṃ ghusṛṇaṃ cailāṃ lavaṅgaṃ jātipatrakam || 145 ||

phalaṃ vā vastu yaccānyat sakalaṃ vā kadācana |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā yasmai kasmai prayacchati || 146 ||

sa vaśyo jāyate satyaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
striyaśca sakalā vaśyā dāsībhūtā bhavanti hi || 147 ||

haṭhākarṣaṇametattu kathitaṃ nānyathā bhavet |
pītadravyeṇa saṃlikhya dhārayedindradiggatam || 148 ||

nāmnā sarvāṅgabhūto'pi mūko bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
sahasravadanaścāpi mūko bhavati niścitam || 149 ||

mahānīlarasenāpi nāma saṃyojya pūrvavat |
dakṣiṇābhimukho vahnau dagdhvā mārayate'cirāt || 150 ||

p. 501)

mahiṣāśvapurīṣābhyāṃ gomūtrairnāma cāṅkitam |
kṛtvā śarāvamadhyasthaṃ vidviṣṭaṃ sarvajantuṣu || 151 ||

yutaṃ rocanayā nāma kākapakṣeṇa saṃlikhet |
nīlakarpaṭake samyak nīlasūtreṇa veṣṭayet || 152 ||

lambamānastadākāśe paramuccāṭanaṃ bhavet |
dugdhalākṣārocanābhirmahānīlarasena ca || 153 ||

likhitvā dhārayeccakraṃ sarvavarṇān vaśaṃ nayet |
anenaiva vidhānena jalamadhye viniḥkṣipet || 154 ||

saubhāgyamatulaṃ prāpya snānapānānna saṃśayaḥ |
etanmadhyagatāṃ pṛthvīṃ nāgarīṃ vā surāṅganām || 155 ||

saptāhāt kṣobhayennityaṃ jvālāmālāṃ vicintayan |
likhitvā pītavarṇena svīyacakraṃ yadā'rcayet || 156 ||

pūrvāśābhimukho bhūtvā stambhayet sarvavādinaḥ |
sindūreṇa likheccakraṃ pūjayeduttarāmukhaḥ || 157 ||

yadatra dāso vaśago loko bhavati sarvadā |
cakraṃ gairikayā'likhya pūjayet paścimāmukhaḥ || 158 ||

p. 502)

sarvāṅganāvaśyakārī mohakārī sadā bhavet |
dakṣiṇābhimukho bhūtvā nityaṃ cakraṃ yadā'rcayet || 159 ||

yasya nāmnā tasya nityaṃ mahāhānistu jāyate |
cakraṃ vahnimukho bhūtvā rātrau sampūjayet sudhīḥ || 160 ||

stambhavidveṣaṇavyādhiśatrūccāṭanakārakam |
rocanāliptitaṃ cakraṃ dugdhamadhye vaśaṅkaram || 161 ||

kṣiptaṃ gomūtramadhye tu śatrūccāṭanakārakam |
tailasthaṃ cakrarājaṃ tu vidveṣaṇakaraṃ param || 162 ||

jvalajjvalanamadhyasthaṃ sarvaśatruvināśanam |
ekāyutaṃ japaṃ kuryāt prayogeṣu ca sādhakaḥ || 163 ||

trikoṇaṃ kuṇḍamāsādya samyak śāstravidhānataḥ |
tasmin homaṃ prakurvīta saṃskṛte havyavāhane || 164 ||

prakṣālya gavyadugdhena saṃśodhya lavaṇaṃ sudhīḥ |
cūrṇitaṃ juhuyānmantrī saptāhādvaśayejjanān || 165 ||

p. 503)

dadhimadhvājyasaṃsiktaiḥ saindhavairjuhuyāt punaḥ |
vaśayet sakalān devān gandharvān kiṃ suparṇakān || 166 ||

viśuddhaṃ lavaṇaprasthaṃ vibhaktaṃ pañcadhā pṛthak |
yasya nāmnā sa vaśyaḥ syādanenaiva na saṃśayaḥ || 167 ||

śuddhaṃ lavaṇamādāya juhuyānmadhurānvitam |
jalaṃ pañcāśataṃ dadyādvaśaṃ nayati vāñchitam || 168 ||


dakṣiṇakālikārcanavidhānam

dakṣiṇāyā viśeṣañca kathayāmi viśeṣataḥ |
kūrcalajjādvayaṃ cānte ādyābījaṃ tathaiva ca || 169 ||

yojayitvā japedvidyāmayutaṃ vaśamānayet |


kālikādhyānam

nāgayajñopavītāñca candrārdhadhṛtamastakām || 170 ||

jaṭājūṭasamāyuktāṃ mahākālasamīpagām |
aṣṭottaraśatāmantrya pūrvadravyeṇa sādhakaḥ || 171 ||

tenaiva tilakaṃ kṛtvā vaśayejjagatīmimām |
śatābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā rocanātilakena tu || 172 ||

rājānaṃ sādhakaśreṣṭho vaśamānayati kṣaṇāt |

p. 504)

ādye caikaṃ tu bījānāṃ tathaivānte ca ekakam || 173 ||

dakṣiṇe kālike ceti sādhyaṃ saṃyojya mantravit |
svāhāntaṃ mantramuccārya bhavedākarṣaṇaṃ mahat || 174 ||

lohitāṅgulihastāṃ ca ekaśūladharāṃ tathā |
mahākālāgra āsīnāṃ dhyātvā cākarṣaṇañcaret || 175 ||

sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva pātālatalagaṃ tathā |
ākarṣayati mantrajñaḥ kimanyat bhuvi yoṣitaḥ || 176 ||

śeṣañca vaśavat kuryāduccāṭanamathocyate |
piṅgākṣīṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāñca kartrīkharparadhāriṇīm || 177 ||

dhyātvā coccāṭayecchatrūn pūrvavajjapamācaret |
ante ca ṭhadvayaṃ dattvā prajapenmanumuttamam || 178 ||

kapilāṃ dvibhujāṃ kartrīkapālasavyadakṣiṇam |

p. 505)

utthitāṃ sannatāṃ dhyāyet sādhako ripumāraṇe |


dhyānānusāreṇa phalakathanam

viśeṣaḥ kathitaḥ kālyāḥ sāmānyamatha vakṣyate |
uccāṭayati piṅgākṣī saṃdrāvayati kekarā || 180 ||

vidrāvayati muktāsyā santatrāsayati ghūrṇitā |
vikṣobhayati saṃkṣubdhā sampātayati sannatā || 181 ||

saṅkocayati vitrastā prabuddhā vā prabodhayet |
yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ jano dhyāyet tatra tatra smaredapi || 182 ||

atra sarvatra kartavyaṃ bhāvamātrasya cihnitam |
uccāṭanādi sarvatra maithunānte samācaret || 183 ||

dhūstūrakāṣṭhayogena citāvahnau ca mantravit |
ulūkakākapakṣaiśca homācchatrūn vināśayet || 184 ||

uccāṭanārthaṃ śatrūṇāṃ homaṃ kuryācca mantravit |
pūrvoktena vidhānena citākāṣṭhahutāśane || 185 ||

p. 506)

ulūkakākapakṣābhyāṃ kṛtvā homaṃ vinirdaśet |
vidveṣaṇavidhānena anyonyakalahānvitam || 186 ||

ulūkapakṣahomena garbhapāto bhavet striyāḥ |
sahasreṇa pramāṇena kuryāddhomaṃ yathāvidhi || 187 ||

śmaśānāṅgāramādāya maṅgale vāsare niśi |
kṛṣṇavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya badhnīyāt raktatantubhiḥ || 188 ||

śatābhimantritaṃ tacca niḥkṣipedarimandire |
saptāhābhyantare tasya dhruvamuccāṭanaṃ bhavet || 189 ||

mahācīnadrumarasenāktaṃ piṇḍaṃ vidhāya ca |
yannāmnā dīyate tacca so'cirānmṛtyumarhati || 190 ||

narāsthini likhenmantraṃ kṣārayukta haridrayā |
sahasraṃ parisañjapya niśāyāṃ ravivāsare || 191 ||

kṣipyate yasya gehe tu tasya mṛtyustrimāsataḥ |
kṣetre tu śasyahāniḥ syājjavahānisturaṅgame || 192 ||

p. 507)

dhanahānirdhanāgāre grāmamadhye tu tatkṣayaḥ |
dveṣyadveṣakayornāmni tayordveṣo mahān bhavet || 193 ||


puttalīprayogakathanam

mantraṃ kṛṣṇatṛtīyādi prajapedyāvadaṣṭamīm |
puttalīḥ pañca kurvīta sāṅgopāṅgāḥ śubhāḥ samāḥ || 194 ||

ekā sādhyadrumeṇa syādanyā piṣṭamayī tathā |
cakrihastamṛdā'nyā syādanyāmadhumayī tathā || 195 ||

lavaṇaṃ pañcasambhūtaṃ cūrṇitaṃ dugdhaśodhitam |
prokṣayecca tathā kṣīradadhyājyamadhubhiḥ kramāt || 196 ||

guḍājyamadhubhiḥ samyak miśritenāmbunā tataḥ |
kurvīta puttalīṃ saumyāṃ sarvāvayavaśobhitām || 197 ||

puttalyāṃ jīvamādhāya mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ param |
raktavastradharāṃ śuddhāṃ puttalīṃ dāruṇā kṛtām || 198 ||

adhomukhīṃ hunet kuṇḍe piṣṭajāmāsanādadhaḥ |
mṛṇmayīṃ pratimāṃ pādadeśe nyasyettathātmanaḥ || 199 ||

madhūcchiṣṭamayīṃ vyomni kuṇḍasyordhvaṃ pralambayet |
lavaṇena kṛtāṃ paścāt pratimāṃ saṃspṛśan japet || 200 ||

nijamantraṃ yathānyāyamaṣṭottarasahasrakam |

p. 508)

agnimādāya sandīpya sādhyanakṣatradārubhiḥ || 201 ||

tasminnabhyarcya mantroktāṃ devatāṃ rūpyapatrake |
kuśītarājidūrvābhirdattvā'rghyaṃ praṇamet sudhīḥ || 202 ||

mantrairetaiḥ prayogādāvante ca yatamānasaḥ |
tvarānandana śatrughna niśāyāṃ havyavāhana || 203 ||

haviṣā mantradattena tṛpto bhava mayā saha |
jātavedo mahādeva taptajāmbūnadaprabha || 204 ||

svāhāpate viśvabhakṣa lavaṇaṃ daha śatruhan |
oṃ durge sarvaviparyāsi grastaṃ yuktaṃ tvayā jagat || 205 ||

mahādevi namastubhyaṃ varade varadā bhava |
tamomayi mahādevi mahādevasya suvrate || 206 ||

kriyā me puruṣaṃ hutvā vaśamānaya dehi me |
oṃ durge durge durgāsi durgasaṃrodhanākule || 207 ||

śaṅkhacakradhare devi duṣṭaśatrubhayaṅkari |
namaste daha me śatruṃ vaśamānaya caṇḍike || 208 |

śākambhari mahādevi śaraṇaṃ me bhavā'naghe |
bhadrakāli bhavābhītibhadrasiddhipradāyini || 209 ||

sapatnān me hana hana daha tāpaya śoṣaya |
prāṇāsiśaktirudrādyairutkramyotkṛtya cānaya || 210 ||

mahādevi mahākāli rakṣāsthānasthite'mbike |
sādhyaṃ saṃhṛtya nirvartyaṃ puttalīṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 211 ||

p. 509)

mūlamantraṃ samuccārya juhuyādedhite'nale |
prathamo dakṣapādaśca tatkarastadanantaram || 212 ||

śivadūtīyamākhyātaṃ vāmahastaṃ tataḥ param |
madhyādūrdhvaṃ pañcamaḥ syādardhoṃ'śaḥ ṣaṣṭha īritaḥ || 213 ||

saptamo vāmabhāgaḥ syādevaṃ bhāgakramaḥ smṛtaḥ |
sapta sapta vibhāgo vā prayogeṣu yathāvidhi || 214 ||

hutvaivamarcayedagniṃ praṇameddaṇḍavattataḥ |
yajamāno dhanairdhānyaiḥ prīṇayed gurumātmanaḥ || 215 ||

anena manunā mantrī vaśayedasurān surān |
kiṃ punarmanujān bhūpān sadyo'pyasya vimohitāḥ || 216 ||

māraṇe pūrvasamproktaṃ puttalīnāṃ catuṣṭayam |
niveśayad yathāpūrvaṃ sādhakendro yathāvidhi || 217 ||

aparāṃ vakṣyamāṇena vidhānena prakalpayet |
varāhapārāvataviṭ tiladrumaiśca rāmaṭhaiḥ || 218 ||

ripornakhaiśca keśaiśca tathā vāmāṅghrireṇujaiḥ |
snuhikṣīreṇa sampiṣṭaiḥ pūrvoktalavaṇānvitaiḥ || 219 ||

p. 510)

vidhāya puttalīḥ samyak prāṇasthāpanamācaret |
japapūjādikaṃ kuryāt prāguktavartmanā sudhīḥ || 220 ||

tataḥ pūrvodite bhāge rātrau prajvalite'nale |
durgāṃ vā bhadrakālīṃ vā samārādhya yathāvidhi || 221 ||

dhārayenniśitaṃ śastraṃ savyahastena sādhakaḥ |
vāmaṃ pādaṃ samāruhya dakṣiṇaṃ dhruvamānasaḥ || 222 ||

chittvā chittvā hunenmantrī nirāhāro jitendriyaḥ |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīṃ samārabhya yāvat kṛṣṇacaturdaśī || 223 ||

anenaiva vidhānena homaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
dvisaptāhaprayogeṇa mārayet ripumātmanaḥ || 224 ||

krūre roge grahe sarpe tarjanyā saṃspṛśan japet |
smṛtvā śūladharāṃ devīṃ devāṃśca tatkṣaṇājjayet || 225 ||

darbhitaṃ sādhyanāmānaṃ samuddiṣṭañca saṃlikhet |
kulālamṛttikāyāṃ tu pratimāyāṃ hṛdi nyaset || 226 ||

p. 511)

kṛtaprāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ tāṃ pūjitāṃ kusumādibhiḥ |
vidhāyāgniṃ japenmantramaṣṭottarasaharakam || 227 ||

saṃkhyāsampūrṇamātreṇa vaśamāyāti vāñchitam |
abhyarcya ca tataḥ paścāt tīkṣṇatailena mantravit || 228 ||

kṛtvāyutaṃ vidhāyārghyaṃ tīkṣṇatailena ca punaḥ |
teṣu sampātayed bhūyaḥ spṛṣṭvā tānyayutaṃ japet || 229 ||

vedhayet parasenāyāṃ kṣaṇānnaṣṭā diśo daśa |
prāpnuyānnaṣṭasañjñāyāṃ palāyanaparāyaṇaḥ || 230 ||

japitvā siddhaguñjānāṃ kuḍavañcānalodare |
niḥkṣipet śatrusenāyāṃ gūḍhaṃ rātrisamāgame || 231 ||

jvaramārīmahārāgaiḥ pīḍitaḥ sainyanāyakaḥ |
parasparavirodhena gacchedvigataceṣṭayā || 232 ||

śilāsaṃstambhane mantrī karaikasurasambhavaiḥ |
sādhyapādarajoyukto homāduccāṭayet ripūn || 233 ||

kare dārumayīṃ kṛtvā pratimāmatiśobhanām |
śaptāṃ pratiṣṭhitaprāṇāṃ chedayedaṅgaśaḥ puraḥ || 234 ||

kākolūkavasāyuktamaṣṭottarasahasrakam |

p. 512)

kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ śmaśāne havyavāhane || 235 ||

juhuyājjāyate śaturyamagehaṃ dinatrayāt |
unmattasamidhāṃ homānmattāḥ syuḥ śatravaḥ kṣaṇāt || 236 ||

ulūkakākayoḥ pakṣaṃ savasāraktasaṃyutam |
juhuyānniśi kāntāre śatru kālātithirbhavet || 237 ||

śatroḥ pratikṛtiṃ mantrī pratiṣṭhitasamīraṇām |
viṣaliptaviliptāṅgīmabhyukṣya nikṣipejjale || 238 ||

jvarākrānto bhavecchīghraṃ dugdhasekāt sukhaṃ nayet |


prakārāntaraprayogakathanam

tarjanītriśikhaṃ dorbhyāṃ dhārayantīṃ bhayaṅkarīm || 239 ||

raktāṃ dhyātvā ravervimbe prajapedayutaṃ manum |
mārayedavivādena ripūn bandhusamanvitān || 240 ||

p. 513)

khaḍgakheṭadarāṃ kruddhāṃ siṃhasthāṃ ravimaṇḍale |
dhyātvā mantraṃ japenmantrī pūrvavannāśayedarīn || 241 ||

cāpabāṇadharāṃ bhīmāṃ siṃhasthāṃ jvalanopamām |
sṛjantīṃ bāṇanivahān dhārayantīṃ ca tarjanīm || 242 ||

dhyātvā japennijaṃ mantramayutaṃ jalamadhyataḥ |
ripuñca parasenāñca śīghramuccāṭayed dhruvam || 243 ||

pretapiṇḍaṃ samādāya golakaṃ kārayettataḥ |
sādhyanāmāṅkitaṃ kṛtvā śatrusadṛśaputtalīm || 244 ||

jīvaṃ tatra nidhāyaiva citāgnau prakṣipettataḥ |
ekāyutaṃ japaṃ kṛtvā trirātrānmāraṇaṃ bhavet || 245 ||

pūrvavat puttalīṃ kṛtvā japtvā ca pūrvavartmanā |
mahājvālā bhavettasya taptatāmraśalākayā || 246 ||

guhyadvāre pravinyasya saptāhānmaraṇaṃ ripoḥ |
kuṇḍalinyā mukhe kṣiptvā vairiṇaṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 247 ||

tasmāt kālāgnimadhye ca prakṣipya dahanañcaret |
prātareva vibhāvyātha japedayutasaṃkhyakam || 248 ||

trisaptāhaprayogeṇa ripuryāti yamālayam |

p. 514)

bhūrjapatre svarṇapatre nimbapatre vilikhya ca || 249 ||

pūjāṃ kṛtvā dahedagnau tadā śatrunikṛntanam |
vahnipatre riporvidyāṃ likhitvā patrake'male || 250 ||

puṭāntaṃ mantramudbhāvya paṭe kṛtvā vidarbhya ca |
sahasrajanmabhirjaptvā vidyā na hi phalapradā || 251 ||

patrābhāve kule pātre raktacandananirmitam |
vahnibījaṃ puṭaṃ kṛtvā dṛṣṭyā dagdhvā vicintya ca || 252 ||

vāmahastena juhuyālluptaṃ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
padmapatre yasya mantraṃ puṭīkṛtya ca dīpanaiḥ || 253 ||

mṛṇmadhye nikṣipettoye tadvadbodho bhaviṣyati |
vāṅmāyākamalābījaṃ dīpanaṃ parikīrtitam || 254 ||

mṛtpātre tu mṛtaṃ likhya mṛdā tīrtha puṭīkṛtam |
jale ca nikṣipenmantraṃ punarnava itīritaḥ || 255 ||

p. 515) 

yanmanuṃ taṃ manuṃ dagdhaṃ navoditaśaśī yathā |
kārayedakṣavṛkṣeṇā'pyedāyudha pañcakam || 256 ||

śaṅkhakhaḍgarathāṅgāni śārṅgakaumodakī kramāt |
pañcagavyeṣu nikṣipya tāni spṛṣṭvā japenmanum || 257 ||

avaṭān pañca nikhaneddikṣu purvoditakramāt |
avaṭeṣu ca pūrṇeṣu pañcagavyena sādhakaḥ || 258 ||

adharāṇi prajanāni pañcaghoṣapuraḥsaram |
nivasettasya madhyādi pūjāṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi || 259 ||

bālukābhiḥ samāpūrya mṛdbhiḥ kuryāt samantataḥ |
baliñca vikirettatra teṣāṃ mantrī yathākramāt || 260 ||

dikpatibhyo baliṃ dattvā brāhmaṇān bhojayettataḥ |
dīnāṃśca kṛpaṇādīṃśca toṣayed bhojanādibhiḥ || 261 ||

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt yathāvittānusārataḥ |
yatreyaṃ vihitā rakṣā deśe vā nagare'pi vā || 262 ||

grāme gehe tathā kṣetre vardhante sampadaḥ sadā |
agnyutpātādayo doṣā bhūtapretādi saṃyutāḥ || 263 ||

abhicārakṛtāḥ kṛtyaripucaurādyupadravāḥ |
nekṣantāṃ tāṃ diśaṃ bhītyā tarjitā devatājñayā || 264 ||

p. 516) 

yasmācca kathitaṃ granthaṃ ṣaṭkarmavidhimuttamam |
goptavyañca prayatnena nānyathā narakaṃ vrajet || 265 ||

hṛdi kālīṃ yajedyatnāt dhyānaṃ tasyāḥ pracakṣate |


kālikādhyānam

dhyāyet kālīṃ karālāsyāṃ sudaṃṣṭrāṃ bhīmalocanām || 266 ||

sphuranmarakataśreṇīṃ karakāñcīṃ digambarīm |
vīrāsanasamāsīnāṃ mahākāloparisthitām || 267 ||

śrutimūlasamākīrṇāṃ sṛkkaṇīṃ caṇḍanādinīm |
muṇḍamālāgaladraktacarcitāṃ pīvarastanīm || 268 ||

madirāmoditāsphālakampitākhilamedinīm |
vāme khaḍgamuṇḍakhaṇḍadhāriṇīṃ dakṣiṇe kare || 269 ||

varābhayayutāṃ ghoravadanāṃ lolajihvikām |
śakuntapakṣisaṃyuktāṃ bālacandravibhūṣitām || 270 ||

śivābhirghorarāvābhiḥ sevitāṃ pralayoditām |
caṇḍahāsacaṇḍanādacaṇḍasphoṭaiśca bhairavaiḥ || 271 ||

gṛhītvā narakaṅkālaṃ jayaśabdaparāyaṇaiḥ |
sevitāśeṣasiddhaughasevitaiḥ sevitāṃ sadā || 272 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye ekonaviṃśollāsaḥ || 19 ||


viṃśa ullāsaḥ


lukīvidyāvivecanam

atha cātrasamāsena lukī vidyā pracakṣyate |
bhaumavāre niśīthe ca catuṣpathagato naraḥ || 1 ||

digvāsā muktakeśaśca makārapañcakaiḥ saha |
svakalpokta vidhānena sampūjya nijadevatām || 2 ||

māṣabhaktabaliṃ dadyāt sadagdhamīnaśodhitam |
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu japtvā stutvā ca sādhakaḥ || 3 ||

so'darśano bhavatyāśu ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogataḥ |


vetālasiddhikathanam

atha vakṣye ca vetāla siddhiṃ sarvottamottamām || 4 ||

bilvavṛkṣodbhavaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ śmaśāne sādhakottamaḥ |
bhaumavāre madhyarātrau gatvā kulayugānvitaḥ || 5 ||

khanitvā kāṣṭhaṃ lakṣaṃ vai japenmahiṣamardinīm |
sahasraṃ homayedvīrastatraiva pitṛkānane || 6 ||

p. 519)

kāṣṭhamuddhṛtya tenaiva daṇḍaṃ pādukacihnitam |
kṛtvā durgāṣṭamīrātrau śmaśāne niḥkṣipettataḥ || 7 ||

tasyopari śavaṃ kṛtvā nijadevīṃ prapūjya ca |
śavāsanagato vīro japedaṣṭasahasrakam || 8 ||

tato mātṛbaliṃ dadyāt kāṣṭhamāmantrayettataḥ |
spheṃ spheṃ daṇḍa mahābhāga yoginīhṛdayapriya || 9 ||

mama hastasthito deva mamājñāṃ paripālaya |
evamāmantrya vetālaṃ yatra yatra prapūjyate || 10 ||

tatra tūrṇaṃ vihāyātha punarāyāti kaulikam |
gaccha gaccha mahābhāge pāduke varavarṇini || 11 ||

matpādasparśamātreṇa sa yāti śatayojanam |
evamāmantrya pādukāṃ dadyāt pādatale tataḥ || 12 ||

yatrecchā vartate tasya tatra gacched yathāsukham |


khaḍgasiddhikathanam

atha vakṣye khaḍgasiddhiṃ sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca || 13 ||

aṣṭalauhaṃ samādāya pañcāśadaṅgulaṃ matam |
kṛtvā tatra mūlamantraṃ likhitvā prajapenmanum || 14 ||

pūrvasaṃkhyaṃ pūrvamantraṃ japtvā ca tadanantaram |
tat sahasraṃ tato hutvā mahāśavakalevare || 15 ||

p. 520)

khanitvā jīvavṛkṣāgre baddhvā śuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
kulāṣṭamyāmardharātre citāmadhye samāhitaḥ || 16 ||

prīti pūrvaṃ samāmantrā hunet pitṛvane tataḥ |
madhuratrayasaṃyuktaṃ bilvapatreṇa saṃyutam || 17 ||

pādādimūrdhaparyantaṃ homānte balimāharet |
balyante sā mahāmāyā devī mahiṣamardinī || 18 ||

āyāti varapūrṇāsyā varahastā hasonmukhī |
gṛdhravadatiśabdena khaḍgamuttolya dhārayet || 19 ||

ghoradaṃṣṭre mahākāli karavālasvarūpiṇi |
kāṃ īṃ ūṃ kuru kalyāṇi vipakṣādapi vistaram || 20 ||

tata evaṃ samāmantrya yamuddiśya kṣipennaraḥ |
chittvā chitvā punaśchittvā gato'pyākṛṣyate punaḥ || 21 ||


phetkāriṇīsiddhikathanam

phetkāriṇyāṃ mahāsiddhiṃ vakṣyāmi tantravartmanā |
kulamīnaṃ kuladravyaṃ kulamadyaṃ kuleśvaraṃ || 22 ||

p. 521)

kulasthāne samānīya dattvā devyai prayatnataḥ |
vidhivat pūjanaṃ kṛtvā sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 23 ||

aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu japtvā bhūmitale sthitaḥ |
bhūmau phūtkāramātreṇa vivaraṃ tatra jāyate || 24 ||

śatayojanadūre vā yatra sādhyasthitirbhavet |
tatraiva gamanaṃ tasya bhūtalāntaḥ prasarpiṇaḥ || 25 ||

evaṃ vivaramadhye ca gavākṣairapi sādhakaḥ |
kāyasaṅkocamāsādya gacchatyavikalo naraḥ || 26 ||


khecarīsiddhikathanam

khecarākhyāṃ mahāsidhiṃ pravakṣye'hamataḥ param |
yādikṣāntaṃ caturbhāge sindūrarajasā likhet || 27 ||

svīyacakraṃ mātṛkārṇaiḥ saṃveṣṭya caiva madhyataḥ |
pūjayet rātrisamaye kulācārakrameṇa tu || 28 ||

tatkṣaṇāt sādhakaśreṣṭhaḥ khecaro bhavati dhruvam |
evaṃ ṣaṇmāsayogena siddho bhavati sādhakaḥ || 29 ||

p. 522)

atha ṣaḍaṅgadevatā

oṃkārasphaṭikaśyāmanīlakṛṣṇāruṇārcciṣaḥ |
varadābhayadhāriṇyaḥ pradhānatanavaḥ striyaḥ || 30 ||

devatāṅge ṣaḍaṅgaḥ syāt sakalīkaraṇaṃ bhavet |
añjaliñcārghyavat kṛtvā paramīkaraṇaṃ bhavet || 31 ||

amṛtīkaraṇe devi mudrā syādanurūpiṇī |
hṛdayaṃ madhyamānāmā tarjanībhiḥ smṛtaṃ śiraḥ || 32 ||

madhyamātarjanībhyāṃ syādaṅguṣṭhena śikhā smṛtā |
daśabhiḥ kavacaṃ proktaṃ tisṛbhirnetramīritam || 33 ||

proktāṅgulībhyāṃ mantraṃ syāttatastālatrayaṃ śive |
diśastenaiva badhnīyāt choṭikābhiḥ samantataḥ || 34 ||


atha kālikākalpaṃ

śiva uvāca -

saṅkaṭe rājabhavane kārāgāreṣu bandhane |
smaraṇāt kālikā devī sarvabandhavimocinī || 35 ||

citsvarūpā mahākālī viṣṇurūpā maheśvarī |
brahmāṇī devagandharvasevitā śivagehinī || 36 ||

p. 523)

brahmaviṣṇu prasūteyaṃ śivamāyātmikā parā |
sādhakasya kṣaṇaṃ dhyānāt pratyakṣā suravanditā || 37 ||

prabhāvaṃ kālikādevyā vaktuṃ na śakyate priye |
śatakoṭimukhenāpi pañcabhiśca kathaṃ priye || 38 ||

asmākaṃ jananī devī bhaktyā svadehagāminī |
ādau mātā tathā guptā māgurītyabhidhīyate || 39 ||

mama sāmrājyasāhāyyaṃ mamaiva siddhidāyinī |
mama prabhutvamāpannā mama mātā yaśasvinī || 40 ||

tayā vinā jagadaddhātri spandituṃ naiva śakyate |
ityuktvā śaṅkaro mohāt mūrcchitaḥ punarudgataḥ || 41 ||

śivadehād ghorarūpo bhairavaḥ samajījanat |
jāto'sau śaṅkaro devyāḥ pādapadmaṃ samāśritaḥ || 42 ||

āliṅgya pārvatī tena rūpeṇa vapurucyate |

p. 524)

kevalaṃ dhyānayogena sarvasiddhimupālabhet || 43 ||

sampūjya lakṣamātraṃ tu japtvā tridaśatāriṇīm |
prayogaprabhavā devī prasanna bhaktavatsalā || 44 ||

arkapuṣpasahasreṇa pūjitā varadāyinī |
sahasraṃ parijapyaitat sandhyāyāṃ ravivāsare || 45 ||

dhanavān kāmabhogīśo bhavet sarvajanapriyaḥ |
sahasraṃ parijapyeta vipulāṃ kavitāṃ labhet || 46 ||

mahadaiśvaryamāpnoti dharmapatnīrato niśi |
raktapuṣpaśatenāpi maṇḍite kujavāsare || 47 ||

brahmapuṣpaśatenāpi raktacandanacarcitaḥ |
sampūjya rājasammānaṃ kujavāre niśārdhake || 48 ||

dharmakṣetre padmapuṣpaiḥ sahasraiḥ paripūjayet |
ravau vāre trayodaśyāṃ haviṣyāśī jitendriyaḥ || 49 ||

sahasraṃ parijapyeta vipulāṃ kavitāṃ labhet |
putradātā niśābhāge dharapatnīsāhayavān || 50 ||

sañjapennijaśayyāyāṃ niśi saṃyatamānasaḥ |
dīrghamāyurvaśakaraṃ putraṃ saṃlabhate'cirāt || 51 ||

p. 525)

rocanāprasthamātraṃ tu mantreṇānena mantritam |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ mantraṃ dhārayettilakaṃ yadi || 52 ||

rājāno'pi hi dāsatvaṃ bhajante kiṃ pare janāḥ |
paranārīratā ye vā japanti paścimāmukhāḥ || 53 ||

sahasraṃ kālikā mantraṃ dhanāḍhyāste bhavanti hi |
catuṣpathe sahasrārdhaṃ muktakeśo digambaraḥ || 54 ||

aṣṭavāraṃ japedyo'pi tasya prītā ca kālikā |
acalāṃ kamalāṃ labdhvā sukhaṃ prāpnoti bhūtale || 55 ||

karpūrapūitamukho nijanārīsamāgame |
japettu kālikādhyānādaṣṭādhikaśataṃ niśi || 56 ||

suprītā kālikā svapne darśanaṃ pradadāti ca |
matsyāśī nityamāṃsāśi rātrau paścimadiṅmukhaḥ || 57 ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ kṛtvā yo japedardharātrakam |
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyaṃ tacchaturyamavallabhaḥ || 58 ||

p. 526)

arkāraṇye japedvyāghracarmaṇi prativāsaram |
aṣṭau dināni labhyeta kanyāṃ śīlaguṇānvitām || 59 ||

arkavṛkṣasya mūle tu yo japettridinaṃ niśi |
sahasrārdhapramāṇena dhanadena samo bhavet || 60 ||

sahasraṃ parijapyeta niśāyāṃ maṅgale niśi |
catuṣpathe mahārogānmukto rogī bhaved dhruvam || 61 ||

bhagaliṅgasya saṃyogī paścimābhimukhī janaḥ |
tasya dāridryasaṃnyāsaṃ karoti japamastake || 62 ||

nijarāmābhigamane prakṣālya bhagaliṅgakam |
tajjalaṃ tāmrapātreṣu saṃsthāpya tarpaṇaṃ caret |
mūlamantreṇa deveśi labhate mahatīṃ śriyam || 63 |


saptaśatīprayogavidhānam

prathamacaritasya brahma-ṛṣirgāyatrīcchando mahākālīdevatā nandā śaktī raktadantikā bījamagnistattvaṃ mahākālīprītyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ |

p. 527)

madhyamacaritasya viṣṇur-ṛṣimahālakṣmīrdevatā'nuṣṭupchandaḥ śākambharī śaktirdurgā bījaṃ sūryastattvaṃ mahālakṣmīprītyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ |

uttamacaritasya rudra ṛṣiḥ sarasvatī devatā uṣṇikchando bhīmā śaktirbhrāmarī bījaṃ vāyustattvaṃ sarasvatīprītyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ |

daśavaktrā daśabhujā daśapādāñjanaprabhā |
viśālayā rājamānā triṃśallocanamālayā || 64 ||

prasphurat kuladaṃṣṭrābhā bhīmarūpā bhayaṅkarī |
khaḍgabāṇagadāśūlaśaṅkhacakrabhuśuṇḍibhṛt || 65 ||

parighaṃ kārmukaṃ śīrṣaṃ niścyotadrudhiraṃ dadhau |
madhukaiṭabhayoryuddhe yaiṣā sā tāmasī śivā || 66 ||

śvetānanā nīlabhujā suśvetastanamaṇḍalā |
raktamadhyā raktadehā nīlajaṅghorujānukā || 67 ||

citrānulepanā kāntā sarvasaubhāgyaśālinī |
aṣṭādaśabhujā pūjyā sā sahasrabhujā raṇe || 68 ||

p. 528)

āyudhānyatra vakṣyante dakṣiṇādhaḥ karakramāt |
akṣamālāṃ tu muṣalaṃ bāṇāsiṃ kuliśaṃ gadām || 69 ||

cakraṃ triśūlaṃ paraśuṃ śaṅkhakheṭaka pāśakam |
śaktidaṇḍacarmapāśaṃ pānapātraṃ kamaṇḍalum || 70 ||

alaṅkṛtabhujāmebhirāyudhaiḥ parameśvarīm |
smartavyā stutikālādau mahiṣāsuramardinī || 71 ||

ityeṣā rājasī mūrtiḥ sarvadevamayī matā |
evaṃ dhyātvā naro nityaṃ labhetepsitamātmanaḥ || 72 ||

gaurīdehāt samutpannā yā sattvaikaguṇāśrayā |
sākṣāt sarasvatī proktā śumbhāsuranibarhiṇī || 73 ||

dadhau cāṣṭabhujā bāṇaṃ muṣalaṃ śūlacakrakam |
śaṅkhaghaṇṭālāṅgalañca kārmukañca tathā param || 74 ||

dhyeyā sā stutikālādau vadhe śumbhaniśumbhayoḥ |


atha mantroddhāraḥ

vārāhītantre -

tāraṃ vāṇīṃ tato lajjāṃ vahnijāyāṃ tataḥ param |
punastārañca vārāhī caṇḍīmantraḥ samīritaḥ || 75 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye viṃśollāsaḥ || 20 ||



ekaviṃśa ullāsaḥ


avadhūtāśramanirūpaṇam

athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi avadhūtakramaṃ śṛṇu |
caturthāśramiṇāṃ madhye avadhūtāśramo mahān || 1 ||

kevalaṃ kulayogena tasya mokṣaḥ prajāyate |
yadi na syāttadā caiva śṛṇu yat kathayāmi te || 2 ||

jñāna bhāve ca sampanne samprārthyaṃ nijakaulikīm |
tadājñayā vimuktaḥ syāttāṃ sampūjya kulāntare || 3 ||

kuṇḍalīśaktivivare tadā yogaṃ samabhyaset |
nirdvandvo nirahaṅkāraḥ śuddhanāḍījitendriyaḥ || 4 ||

mṛdvāsane samāsīnaḥ ṣaṭkasaṃyamane rataḥ |
baddhapadmāsano yogī yogaṃ yuñjīta yatnataḥ || 5 ||

ūrvorupari vīrendraḥ kṛtvā pādatale ubhe |

p. 531)

aṅguṣṭhau tu nibadhnīyāddhastābhyāṃ vyutkrameṇa tu || 6 ||

padmāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarveṣāmeva pūjitam |


mulādhāracakrakathanam

gudāttu dvyaṅgulādūrdhvamadhomeḍhrāttu dvyaṅgulam || 7 ||

ekāṅgulaṃ tu tanmadhye dehamadhyaṃ prakītitam |
kandamasti śarīre'smin puṇyāpuṇyavivarjitaḥ || 8 ||

tantupañjaramadhyastho yathā bhramati sūtikaḥ |
jīvastu mūlacakre'smin adhaḥprāṇaścaratyasau || 9 ||

prāṇārūḍho bhavejjīvaḥ sarvadeheṣu sarvadā |
tatra nāḍyaḥ samutpannāḥ sahasrāṇāṃ dvisaptatiḥ || 10 ||

merudaṇḍabahiḥ pārśve candrasūryātmike śubhe |
iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva vāmadakṣiṇataḥ sthite || 11 ||

kandamadhyād dvayormadhye suṣumnā supratiṣṭhitā |
pṛṣṭhamadhyagatā sā tu saha mūrdhni vyavasthitā || 12 ||

p. 532)

ṛjvībhūtā tu vajrākhyā jvalantī viśvadhāriṇī |
muktimārge sadā guptā meḍhramūrdhni vyavasthitā || 13 ||

tasyāścāntargatā nāḍī citrākhyā yogivallabhā |
pañcavarṇojjvalā devī pañcabhūtanivāsinī || 14 ||

pañcadevairyutā devī pañcatattvaprakāśinī |
pūrayitvā tu vicinnā citrā sā granthirūpiṇī || 15 ||

tayaiva grathitaṃ sarvaṃ mūlādi padmapañcakam |
tasyā madhye brahmanāḍī mṛṇālatanturūpiṇī || 16 ||

brahmarandhraṃ tu tanmadhye haravaktraṃ sadāśivam |
gudameḍrāntare granthi suṣumnā sandhiruttamā || 17 ||

ādhāre ca gudasthāne paṅkajañca caturdalam |
suvarṇābhaṃ vādisāntaṃ hemavarṇaṃ suśobhanam || 18 ||

kalikārūpakaṃ padmaṃ pṛthivīpadminīyutam |
taruṇāruṇasaṅkāśāṃ śūlakhaṭvāṅgakau tataḥ || 19 ||

vāme khaḍgaṃ surākumbhaṃ dadhānāmugradaṃṣṭriṇīm |

p. 533)

dugdhābhāṃ saṃsthitāṃ dhyāyet ḍākinīṃ locanatrayām || 20 ||

karṇikāyāṃ sthitā yoniḥ kāmākhyā yogivallabhā |
apānākhyaṃ hi kandarpaṃ ādhāre ca trikoṇake || 21 ||

svayambhūliṅgaṃ tanmadhye paścimābhimukhaṃ bhavet |
bandhūkapuṣpasaṅkāśaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham || 22 ||

bhramantaṃ yonimadhye ca śiśiraṃ śaśabhṛtsamam |
tasyordhve kuṇḍalī śaktiraṣṭadhā kuṇḍalākṛtiḥ || 23 ||

aṣṭaprakṛtirūpā sā nābhestiryagadho gatā |
adhovaktrasthitādevī ūrdhvapucchā'tiśobhanā || 24 ||

akārādikṣakārāntā kuṇḍalītyabhidhīyate |
sā ca vidyullatākārā mṛṇālatantusannibhā || 25 ||

p. 534)

parisphurati sarvātmā suptā'hisadṛśākṛtiḥ |
brahmadvāramukhaṃ nityaṃ mukhenāvṛtya tiṣṭhati || 26 ||

yena mārgeṇa gantavyaṃ brahmadvāraṃ nirāmayam |
mukhena śvāsaṃ praviṣṭā brahmarandhraṃ mukhaṃ tadā || 27 ||

mastake maṇivadbhinnasvayambhūliṅgaveṣṭinī |
nānyaḥ panthā dvitīyo'sti śarīre paramasya ca || 28 ||

mūlādhāre kāmarūpaṃ pīṭhaṃ paramadurlabham |
adhovaktrāṇi padmāni mūlādīni yathākramāt || 29 ||


mūlādhāracakram tadhyānaphalam

gudāttu dvyaṅgulādūrdhvamadho meḍhrāttu dvyaṅgulāt |
caturaṅgulavistāraṃ kandamūlaṃ khagāṇḍavat || 30 ||

ekāṅgulaṃ tu tanmadhye caturasraṃ trikoṇakam |
evaṃ dhyātvā ca vīrendraḥ sthiracittaḥ sthirendriyaḥ || 31 ||

ākuñcayed gudamūlaṃ cibukaṃ hṛdayopari |
nava dvārāṇi saṃyamya kukṣimāpūrya vāyunā || 32 ||

śabdabījena tāṃ devīṃ dṛḍhavibhrāmayettataḥ |
vāyunā bhidya tadvaktraṃ ūrdhvavaktraṃ tu kārayet || 33 ||

p. 538)

udghāṭayet kapāṭaṃ tu yathā kuñcikayā dṛḍham |
ullāsojjvalakārasya śikhā yāti samujjvalā || 34 ||

mūlacakraṃ tato bhittvā brahmadvāraṃ vibhedayet |
ūrdhvaṃ bhittvā tu liṅgaṃ vai itaraṃ puṣkaraṃ tataḥ || 35 ||

mūlādhāre santataṃ dhyānayogāt
stambhakṣobhāvutplutirdārdurīva |
bhūmityāgaḥ khecaratvaṃ krameṇa
nṝṇāmete ṣaḍguṇāḥ sambhavanti || 36 ||

kāntiprakarṣo vapuṣo'pi
nādavyaktiḥ pradīptiḥ jaṭharānalasya |
laghutvamaṅgasya nijendriyāṇāṃ
paṭutvamārogyamadīnatā ca || 37 ||

svādhiṣṭhānacakram taddhyānaphalam

mūlādipadmaṣaṭkañca kalikāsadṛśaṃ śubham |
svādhiṣṭhānaṃ mahāpadmaṃ liṅgamūle rasacchadam || 38 ||

p. 536)

bandhūka puṣpasaṅkāśaṃ sadā jalasamanvitam |
sindūraprasphuradvarṇairbādilāntaiśca maṇḍitam || 39 ||

śūlaṃ vajraṃ tathā padmaṃ ḍamaruṃ karapaṅkajaiḥ |
dadhānāṃ śyāmavarṇāñca rākiṇīṃ tritayānvitām || 40 ||

raktadhātvekanāthāṃ tāṃ cintayettatra sādhakaḥ |
vicintya sthiracittena adhiṣṭhānaṃ prabhedayet || 41 ||

iha vetti nidhāya mānasaṃ svaṃ
vividhañcāśrutaśāstrajālamuccaiḥ |
avadhūtajarāmayaḥ sa martyaḥ
suciraṃ jīvati vītamṛtyubhītiḥ || 42 ||

vapuṣo'śucitā janasya śaśvat
paramāṃ śuddhimihātanoti puṃsām |
śaradambujapelavasya dehe
dṛḍharuddho ghanatāñca śītaraśmiḥ || 43 ||


maṇipūracakram taddhyānaphalam

tadūrdhve savyadakṣābhyāṃ cintayet sādhakottamaḥ |
padmamadhye sthitaṃ śuddhaṃ vidyutābhaṃ trikoṇakam || 44 ||

p. 537)

tanmadhye cintayed devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ haṃsavāhanam |
raktavarṇaṃ caturvaktraṃ dadhatañca trilocanam || 45 ||

cintayet kūrcapadme ca akṣamālāṃ kamaṇḍalum |
brahmasattvākṣamoṅkāraṃ sthitaṃ nābheradhaḥ sadā || 46 ||

nābhau nīlanibhaṃ padmaṃ maṇipūraṃ daśāsrakam |
vidyutpuñjasphuradvarṇairḍādiphāntaiśca maṇḍitam || 47 ||

vahninā saṃyutaṃ tatra lākinīṃ māṃsadhātugām |
mānasaṃ saṃvibhāvyātha bhedayettadanantaram || 48 ||

sthāne'sminnihitātmanaḥ sukṛtinaḥ pātālasiddhiṃ parāṃ
khaḍgasyāpratimasya sādhanamapi syādīpsitañca kṣitau |
rūpaṃ bhūmivisarjanaṃ parapure śaktaḥ praveṣṭuṃ jarā-
hāniścākhiladuḥkharogaśamanaṃ kālasya vā vañcanam || 49 ||


anāhatacakram taddhyānaphalam

anāhataṃ hṛdi dhyāyet piṅgābhaṃ dvādaśacchadam |
drutasvarṇaprabhāvarṇaiḥ kādiṭhāntaiśca maṇḍitam || 50 ||

p. 538)

medaḥsthāṃ kākinīṃ tatra pītābhāṃ mattarūpiṇīm |
