abhayaṃ ḍamaruṃ śūlaṃ pāśañca karapaṅkajaiḥ || 51 ||

dadhānāṃ cārurupāñca nānābharaṇabhūṣitām |
tatraivāṅguṣṭhamātmānaṃ pradīpakalikopamam || 52 ||

tatra sañcintayed devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ nirañjanam |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ vāṇīlakṣmīvibhūṣitam || 53 ||

vainateyasamārūḍhaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam |
pītāmbaradharaṃ śāntaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitam || 54 ||

pūrṇaśailaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ tatraiva paricintayet |
prabhedayettataḥ paścāt sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 55 ||

etasmin satataṃ niviṣṭamanasaḥ sthāne vimānasthitāḥ 
kṣubhyantyadbhutarūpakāntikalitā divyastriyo yoginaḥ |
jñānañcāpratimaṃ trikālaviṣayaṃ kṣobhaḥ purasya śruti-
rdūrādeva ca darśanañca khagatiḥ syādyoginīmelanam || 56 ||


viśuddhacakram taddhyānaphalam

viśuddhaṃ ṣoḍaśārañca dhūmrābhaṃ kaṇṭhadeśake |

p. 539)

tadante vyomabījañca śuklaṃ haimagajasthitam || 57 ||

taruṇāruṇasaṅkāśaiḥ svaraiśca parimaṇḍitam |
ākāśasahitaṃ padmaṃ śākinīṃ paricintayet || 58 ||

asthisaṃsthāṃ caturbāhuṃ pañcavaktrāṃ trilocanām |
pāśāṅkuśau pustakañca jñānamudrāñca dhāriṇīm || 59 ||

daṃṣṭriṇīmugrarūpāñca sadā madhumadākulām |
ardhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ nānāmaṇivibhūṣitam || 60 ||

candracūḍaṃ trinayanaṃ varābhayakaraṃ śubham |
dhyātvaivaṃ cakrarājaṃ tu bhedayet sādhakottamaḥ || 61 ||

sthāne'tra saṃsaktamanā manuṣya-
strikāladarśī vigatādhirogaḥ |
jitvā jarāmañjananīlakeśaḥ
kṣitau ciraṃ jīvati vītamṛtyuḥ || 62 ||

iha sthāne cittaṃ satatamavadhāyāttapavano
yadi kruddho yogī calayati samastaṃ tribhuvanam |
na ca brahmā viṣṇurna ca hariharo naiva khamaṇi-
stadīyaṃ sāmarthyaṃ śamayitumalaṃ nāpi gaṇapaḥ || 63 ||

p. 540)

ājñācakram taddhyānaphalam

dvidalaṃ hakṣavarṇābhyāṃ śubhābhyāṃ parimaṇḍitam |
vidyutkoṭiprabhaṃ cakraṃ bhruvorūrdhve manonmanī || 64 ||

binduyuktaṃ sarvavarṇaṃ sarvapadmeṣu cintayet |
yonimadhye sthitaṃ liṅgamitaraṃ taruṇāruṇam || 65 ||

jālandharaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ hākinīṃ tatra cintayet |
bindusthāṃ hākinīṃ śukramedomajjāsvarūpiṇīm || 66 ||

akṣamālāñca ḍamaruṃ kapālaṃ pustakaṃ tathā |
cāpaṃ mudrāṃ dadhānāñca śuklāṃ netratrayānvitām || 67 ||

padmamadhye'ntarātmānaṃ prabhārūpaṃ hi kāraṇam |
oṅkārajyotiṣaṃ kalpapradīpābhaṃ jaganmayam || 68 ||

bālasūryapratīkāśāṃ sadā bindumadakṣaram |
tataḥ saṅkṣobhaṇadvāre dhyāyet padmaṃ suśobhanam || 69 ||

p. 541)

dhyānayoganiratasya jāyate pūrvajanmakṛtakarmaṇāṃ smṛtiḥ |
tatra bindunilaye ca dūrato darśanaśravaṇayoḥ samarthatā || 70 ||

iha sannihitasvacittavṛttiḥ pratimāyāḥ pratijalpanaṃ karoti |
gamanañca pure pareṣāṃ punarutthānamapyaho mṛtasya || 71 ||

nirālambāṃ mudrāṃ nijagurumukhenaiva viditā-
miha sthāne kṛtvā sthiraniśitadhīḥ sādhakavaraḥ |
sadā'bhyāsāt paśyatyamaranilayānantarakhilā-
muḍuśreṇīṃ viṣṇorapi padamuḍūnāmapi patim || 72 ||

bhedānte padmaṣaṭkaṃ ca prasphuṭañcordhvavaktrakam |
bhavatyeva na sandeho'pyatha syāt sādhakasya ca || 73 ||

tadūrdhve cārdhacandre ca bhānumaṇḍalamuttamam |
makārabindurūpeṇa tadūrdhve candramaṇḍalam || 74 ||

bindorupari nādaṃ hi śuddhasphaṭikasannibham |
cetasā samprapaśyanti nādānte vṛṣabhadhvajam || 75 ||

śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ kapardaśaśibhūṣaṇam |

p. 542)

vyāghrāmbaraṃ tu tanmadhye adhomukhaṃ saśaktikam || 76 ||

sthāne hyatra parītañca varadābhayapāṇikam |
prasannavadanaṃ śāntaṃ sarpayajñopavītinam || 77 ||

nādopari mahādevaṃ paśyanti jñānacakṣuṣā |
nānyat paśyanti tatraiva antaraṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam || 78 ||

puratrayaviniṣkrānto yatra vāyuḥ pralīyate |
tatra saṃsthaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā taddhyānamīśvaraṃ viduḥ || 79 ||

vibhāvya sādhakaśreṣṭho bhedayettadanantaram |
śaṅkhinīnālaṃ saṃsthāpyavyāptiśūnyaṃ vibharti yaḥ || 80 ||

amṛtodadhisaṅkāśaṃ śatayojanavistṛtam |
candanodyānamadhyasthaṃ vedikāṃ tu tadantare || 81 ||

tanmadhye sphaṭikaṃ dhyāyet paścimānanamambujam |
sravantamamṛtaṃ nityaṃ devyaṅge kamalāntare || 82 ||

p. 543)

sahasrārapadmavarṇanam

sahasrārapadmaṃ visargādadhastā-
dadho vaktramāraktakiñjalkapuñjam |
kuraṅgeṇa hīnastriśṛṅgastadantaḥ
sphuradraśmijālaḥ sudhāṃśuḥ samāste || 83 ||

tadantargataṃ brahmarandhraṃ susūkṣmaṃ
yadādhārabhūtaṃ suṣumṇākhyanāḍyāḥ |
tadetat padaṃ divyamatyantaguhyaṃ
surairapyagamyaṃ sugopyaṃ suyatnāt || 84 ||

etat kailāsasañjñaṃ paramakulapadaṃ bindurūpī svarūpī
yatrāstre devedavo bhavabhayatimiradhvaṃsahaṃso maheśaḥ |
bhūtānāmādidevo rasavisarasitāṃ santatāmantaraṅge
saudhīṃ dhārāṃ vimuñcannabhimata phalado yogināṃ yogagamyaḥ || 85 ||

sthānasyāsya jñānamātreṇa puṃsāṃ
saṃsāre'smin sambhavo naiva bhūyaḥ |
bhūtagrāmaṃ santatanyāsayogāt
kartuṃ hartuṃ syācca śaktiḥ samagrā || 86 ||

sthāne pare haṃsanivāsabhūte-
kailāsanāmnīha vidhāya cetaḥ |

p. 544)

yogī gatavyādhiradhaḥkṛtādhi-
vādhaściraṃ jīvati mṛtyumuktaḥ || 87 ||

sthāne'smin kṣayavṛddhibhāvarahitā nityoditā'dhomukhī
bālādityanibhaprabhāśaśabhṛtaḥ sā'ste kalā ṣoḍaśī |
bālāgrasya vikhaṇḍitasya śatadhā caikena bhāgena yā
sūkṣmatvāt sadṛśī nirantaragalatpīyūṣadhārādharā || 88 ||

etasyāḥ parataḥ sthitā bhagavatī bhūtādhidevādhipā
nirvāṇākhyakalā'rdhacandrakuṭilā sā ṣoḍaśāntargatā |
bālāgrasya sahasradhā vigalitasyaikena bhāgena yā
sūkṣmatvāt sadṛśī trilokajananī yā dvādaśārkaprabhā || 89 ||

nirvāṇākhyakalāpadoparigatā nirvāṇaśaktiḥ parā
koṭyādityasamaprabhā'tigahanā bālāgrabhāgasya yā |
koṭyaṃśena samā samastajananī nityoditā nirmalā
nityānandapadasthaloruvigaladdhārā nirālambanā || 90 ||

p. 545)

etasyāḥ parataḥ parātparataraṃ nirvāṇaśakteḥ padaṃ
śaivaṃ śāśvatamaprameyamamalaṃ nityoditaṃ niṣkriyam |
tadviṣṇoḥ padamityuśanti sudhiyaḥ kecit padaṃ brahmaṇaḥ
keciddhaṃsapadaṃ nirañjanapadaṃ kecinnirālambanam || 91 ||

āropyā'ropya śaktiṃ kamalajanilayādātmanā sākameṣu
sthāneṣvājñāvasāneṣvavahitahṛdayaścintayitvā krameṇa |
nītvā nādāvasānaṃ khagata kulamahāpadmasadmāntarasthāṃ
dhyāyeccaitanyarūpāmabhimataphalasamprāptaye śaktimādyām || 92 ||

sākṣāllākṣārasābhaṃ gaganagatamahāpadmasadmasthahaṃsāṃ
pītvā divyāmṛtaughaṃ punarapi ca viśenmadhyadeśaṃ kulasya |
cakre cakre krameṇāmṛtarasavisaraistarpayet devatāstā
hākinyādyāḥ samastāḥ kamalajapadagāṃ tarpayet kuṇḍalīṃ tām || 93 ||

kuṇḍalī kuṇḍalākārānakrībhūtā nivāsinī |
iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnānilayaṃ vrajet || 94 ||

śaktiṃ bhairavasaṃyogādamṛtānandamānayet |

p. 546)

candramārgeṇa vāyuñca pibettañca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 95 ||

kumbhakañca yathāśaktyā suryamārgeṇa recayet |
sūryamārge pibedvāyuṃ candramārgeṇa recayet || 96 ||

śūnyañca prativimbacandrasadṛśaṃ sūkṣmātisūkṣmaṃ paraṃ
sarvaṃ vyāpya tamomayaṃ jagati saddhāmaprakāśaṃ param |
dṛśyādṛśyavināśabhedasakalaṃ jyotirmayaṃ sarvato
dhyātvā tacca padaṃ tu sādhakavaraiḥ dūrīkṛtaścāntakaḥ || 97 ||

evamabhyasyamānasya ahanyahani niścitam |
jarāmaraṇaduḥkhādyairmucyate bhavasāgarāt || 98 ||


yonimudrābandhaphalakathanam

caturvidhā tu yā sṛṣṭiryasyāṃ yonau prajāyate |
punaḥ pralīyate yasyāṃ kālāgnyādiśivāntakam || 99 ||

yonimudrā parā hyeṣā bandhastasyāḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
tasyāstu bandhamātreṇa tannāsti yanna sādhayet || 100 ||

p. 547)

anyathā japyate yastu anyathā kurute tu yaḥ |
nāsau tatphalapātrañca satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 101 ||

chinnā bandhāśca ye mantrāḥkīlitāḥ stambhitāśca ye |
dagdhāḥ santrāsitā hīnā malināśca tiraskṛtāḥ || 102 ||

bheditā bhramasaṃyuktāḥ suptā sammurcchitāśca ye |
vṛddhā bālāstathā prauḍhāstathā yauvanagarvitāḥ || 103 ||

aripakṣe sthitā ye ca nirvīryāḥ sattvavarjitāḥ |
aṃśakena vihīnāśca khaṇḍaśaḥ śatadhā kṛtāḥ || 104 ||

vidhinānena saṃyuktāḥ prabhavantyacireṇa tu |
siddhimokṣapradāḥ sarve sādhakena niyojitāḥ || 105 ||

yadyaduccarate mantrī varṇarūpaṃ śubhāśubhaṃ |
tattat sidhyatyasandeho yonimudrānibandhanāt || 106 ||

dīkṣayitvā vidhānena abhiṣicya sahasradhā |
tato'dhikārī bhavati tantre'smin sādhakottamaḥ || 107 ||

brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi trailokyamapi ghātayet |
nāsau lipyati pāpena yonimudrānibandhanāt || 108 ||

p. 548)

tasmādabhyasanaṃ nityaṃ kartavyaṃ puṇyakāṅkṣibhiḥ |
ayāsājjāyate siddhirabhyāsānmokṣamāpnuyāt || 109 ||

samvittiṃ labhate'bhyāsāt yogo'bhyāsāt pravartate |
mantrāṇāṃ siddhirabhyāsādabhyāsādvāyusādhanam || 110 ||

kālavañcanamabhyāsāttathā mṛtyuñjayo bhavet |
etadbhedaṃ vijānāti sa yāti paramaṃ padam || 111 ||

tadaṣṭadhā tu jīvo'sau bahiryāti dine dine |
dineśāṅgulimānena tadardhañcopavāsataḥ || 112 ||

triguṇaṃ ratikāle ca dviguṇaṃ bhojanād bahiḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ vahedvāyustriguṇaṃ yadi daivatam || 113 ||

nyūnaṃ dhatte tataḥ prāṇaḥ śarīraṃ parimuñcati |
śarīrasamatāṃ nītvā nyūnaṃ vā sādhakottamaḥ || 114 ||

kumbhayitvā adhovāyuṃ kuṇḍalīmukhavartmani |
yojayitvā tato jīvaṃ kuṇḍalyā sahitaṃ sudhīḥ || 115 ||

gamāgamaṃ kārayitvā siddho bhavati nāparaḥ |
pīyate khādyate yattu tatsarvaṃ kuṇḍalīmukhe || 116 ||

hutvā siddhimavāpnoti na ca bandhena bādhyate |

p. 549)

avadhūtācārakathanam

bhikṣā kāryā na ca svārthaṃ kuṇḍalyāḥ prakṛteḥ punaḥ || 117 ||

re mātardehi me bhikṣāṃ kuṇḍalīṃ tarpayāmyaham |
avadhūtāśrame sthitvā bhairavānandatatparaḥ || 118 ||

bhairavo'haṃ na cānyo'smi na cānyo matparaḥ kvacit |
tantramantrārcanaṃ sarvaṃ mayi jātaṃ na cānyathā || 119 ||

śivo'haṃ bhairavānando matto'haṃ kulanāyakaḥ |
raktamālyāmbaradharo hetuyuktaḥ sadā bhavet || 120 ||

evaṃ bhikṣāṃ vrajan bhikṣurbhairavānandatatparaḥ |
yena kenāpi veśena yena kenā'pyalakṣitaḥ || 121 ||

yatra kutrāśrame tiṣṭhan kulayogīśvaraḥ sadā |
unmattamukajaḍavat virale lokamadhyage || 122 ||

p. 550)

kvacicchiṣṭaḥ kvacidbhraṣṭaḥ kvacid bhūtapiśācavat |
nānāveśadharo yogī vicarettu mahītale || 123 ||

sarvasparśo yathā vāyuryathākāśaśca sarvagaḥ |
sarvabhakṣo yathā vahnistathā yogī sadā śuciḥ || 124 ||

tathā mlecchagṛhānnādi yogīhastagataṃ śuci |
kṣīyate na ca pāpena badhyate na ca janmanā || 125 ||

yadvanmantrabalopetaḥ krīḍan sarpairna daṃśyate |
tathā jñānaparo yogī krīḍannindriyapannagaiḥ || 126 ||

pūjayanti mahādevīṃ kulāṅgakṛṣṭimātrataḥ |
gandhaṃ puṣpañca tāmbulaṃ naivedyaṃ yatra yadbhavet || 127 ||

manasā tat samutsṛjya bāhyataḥ kulavāriṇā |
ātmanyeva samāyojya devīrūpakuleśvaraḥ || 128 ||

p. 551)

na pūjā nāpi tannāma na niṣṭhā na vratādikam |
pūrṇo'haṃ bhairavaścāhaṃ nityānando'hamavyayaḥ || 129 ||

nirañjanasvarūpo'haṃ nirvikāro hyahaṃ prabhuḥ |
sarvaśāstrābhiyukto'haṃ sarvamantrārthapāsyāḥ || 130 ||

asmatparataro deśo na jāto na janiṣyati |
ānandarūpavān bhūtvā sarveṣāṃ priyakāraka || 131 ||

na yogī na bhogī na vātmā na kāṅkṣī
na vīro na dhīro na vā sādhakendraḥ |
sadānandapūrṇo dharaṇyāṃ vivekī
cirājjātadhūto dvitīyo maheśaḥ || 132 ||

śrutau kuṇḍale'sṛg gale muṇḍamālā
kare pānapātraṃ mukhe hanta hālā |
parityaktakarmā layanyastadharmā
virakto'vadhūto dvitīyo maheśaḥ || 133 ||

p. 552)

vāme rāmā ramaṇakuśalā dakṣiṇe pānapātraṃ
madhye nyastaṃ maricasahitaṃ śūkarasyoṣṇamāṃsam |
skandhe vīṇā lalitasubhagā sadgurūṇāṃ prapañcaḥ
kaulo dharmaḥ paramagahano yogināmapyagamyaḥ || 134 ||


iti śrīmajjñānānandaparamahaṃsaviracite kaulāvalīnirṇaye ekaviṃśollāsaḥ samāptaḥ || 21 ||
